"""独立的正式 TEST 区块入口（用于训练已跑满但收尾崩溃的 run；**不重训、不覆盖曲线**）。

用法（服务器，GPU1）：
  cd models_v1 && CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1 python eval_stgeo_tb.py \
      --dataset LEVIR-CD-256 --dataset_root /share_datasets/CD/LEVIR-CD-256 \
      --test_list .../list/test.txt --ckpt_dir <ckpt_dir> \
      --vmamba_pretrained_weight_path ... --stgeo sup [--stgeo_sup_small 16 ...]
输出的 `=== TEST RESULTS === … === END TEST RESULTS ===` 与 train_stgeo.py **逐字同格式**，
可直接 append 到该 run 的 train_log.txt，使 `[ACTUAL-OPT-STEPS]/[PROTOCOL-VERSION]` 落在最后完整区块内。
"""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
import sys

import torch

_HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if _HERE not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, _HERE)

from model.casa_vmamba_stgeo_net import CASAVMambaSTGeoNet  # noqa: E402
from model.layers.st_geodesic import STGEO_FORM  # noqa: E402
from model.layers.stgeo_sup import STGEO_SUP_FORM  # noqa: E402
from model.metric_tool import ConfuseMatrixMeter  # noqa: E402
from model.utils import BCEDiceLoss  # noqa: E402

import dataset.Transforms as myTransforms  # noqa: E402
import dataset.dataset as myDataLoader  # noqa: E402

PROTOCOL_VERSION = "exp1_stgeo_exact80k_v1"
MEAN6 = [0.406, 0.456, 0.485, 0.406, 0.456, 0.485]
STD6 = [0.225, 0.224, 0.229, 0.225, 0.224, 0.229]


def log(m):
    print(m, flush=True)


def build_loader(ds_root, list_path, bs, nw):
    tf = myTransforms.Compose([myTransforms.Normalize(mean=MEAN6, std=STD6),
                               myTransforms.Scale(256, 256), myTransforms.ToTensor()])
    ds = myDataLoader.Dataset(file_root=ds_root, list_path=list_path, transform=tf)
    return torch.utils.data.DataLoader(ds, batch_size=bs, shuffle=False,
                                       num_workers=nw, pin_memory=True)


@torch.no_grad()
def evaluate(loader, model):
    model.eval()
    meter = ConfuseMatrixMeter(n_class=2)
    losses = []
    for img, target in loader:
        pre, post = img[:, 0:3].cuda(), img[:, 3:6].cuda()
        target = target.cuda().float()
        out = model(pre.float(), post.float(), target)
        losses.append(float(BCEDiceLoss(out, target)))
        meter.update_cm(pr=(out > 0.5).long().cpu().numpy(), gt=target.cpu().numpy())
    return sum(losses) / max(1, len(losses)), meter.get_scores()


def sha16(p):
    if not p or not os.path.isfile(p):
        return ""
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(p, "rb") as f:
        for c in iter(lambda: f.read(1 << 20), b""):
            h.update(c)
    return h.hexdigest()[:16]


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--dataset", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--dataset_root", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--test_list", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--ckpt_dir", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--vmamba_pretrained_weight_path", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--stgeo", default="sup")
    ap.add_argument("--stgeo_sigmas", default="1.0,2.0,4.0")
    ap.add_argument("--stgeo_mid", type=int, default=32)
    ap.add_argument("--stgeo_dim", type=int, default=192)
    ap.add_argument("--stgeo_pool", default="max_shape")
    ap.add_argument("--stgeo_xscale", type=int, default=0)
    ap.add_argument("--stgeo_sup_small", type=int, default=16)
    ap.add_argument("--stgeo_sup_lambda", type=float, default=0.2)
    ap.add_argument("--rep_mode", default="full")
    ap.add_argument("--str_dim", type=int, default=192)
    ap.add_argument("--backbone_lr_ratio", type=float, default=0.1)
    ap.add_argument("--num_workers", type=int, default=4)
    ap.add_argument("--gpu_id", type=int, default=0)
    args = ap.parse_args()

    torch.cuda.set_device(args.gpu_id)
    model = CASAVMambaSTGeoNet(
        vmamba_pretrained_path=args.vmamba_pretrained_weight_path,
        stgeo=args.stgeo, stgeo_sigmas=tuple(float(s) for s in args.stgeo_sigmas.split(",")),
        stgeo_mid=args.stgeo_mid, stgeo_dim=args.stgeo_dim,
        stgeo_pool=args.stgeo_pool, stgeo_xscale=bool(args.stgeo_xscale),
        stgeo_sup_small=args.stgeo_sup_small, stgeo_sup_lambda=args.stgeo_sup_lambda,
        rep_mode=args.rep_mode, str_dim=args.str_dim).float().cuda()
    rep = model.stgeo_report()
    pb = model.param_breakdown()
    st = model.pretrain_load_stats() or {}

    cands = [f for f in os.listdir(args.ckpt_dir) if f.startswith("best_F1=") and f.endswith(".pth")]
    assert len(cands) == 1, f"best ckpt 必须恰好 1 个，实际 {cands}"
    best_name = cands[0]
    best_f1 = float(best_name[len("best_F1="):-len(".pth")])
    ck = torch.load(os.path.join(args.ckpt_dir, best_name), map_location="cpu",
                    weights_only=False)
    model.load_state_dict(ck.get("state_dict", ck))
    lastp = os.path.join(args.ckpt_dir, "last.pth")
    cur_iter, best_epoch = 0, -1
    if os.path.isfile(lastp):
        ckl = torch.load(lastp, map_location="cpu", weights_only=False)
        cur_iter = int(ckl.get("actual_steps", 0))
        best_epoch = ckl.get("best_epoch", -1)
    log(f"[EVAL-ONLY] ckpt={best_name} best_f1={best_f1:.4f} actual_steps={cur_iter} "
        f"best_epoch={best_epoch}")

    # ---- 折叠等价性（TF32 off + deterministic）
    prev_tf32 = torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32
    prev_det = torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic
    torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32 = False
    torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32 = False
    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
    torch.manual_seed(16)
    pre_f = torch.randn(8, 3, 256, 256).cuda()
    post_f = torch.randn(8, 3, 256, 256).cuda()
    ld = build_loader(args.dataset_root, args.test_list, 16, args.num_workers)
    img_r, _ = next(iter(ld))
    pre_r, post_r = img_r[:, 0:3].cuda(), img_r[:, 3:6].cuda()
    model.eval()
    with torch.no_grad():
        y_tr = model(pre_f, post_f)
        y_tr_r = model(pre_r.float(), post_r.float())
    model.switch_to_deploy()
    model.eval()
    with torch.no_grad():
        y_dp = model(pre_f, post_f)
        y_dp_r = model(pre_r.float(), post_r.float())
    fold_err = float((y_tr - y_dp).abs().max())
    dis = float(((y_tr > 0.5) != (y_dp > 0.5)).float().mean())
    fold_err_r = float((y_tr_r - y_dp_r).abs().max())
    dis_r = float(((y_tr_r > 0.5) != (y_dp_r > 0.5)).float().mean())
    flips = ((y_tr_r > 0.5) != (y_dp_r > 0.5))
    n_flip = int(flips.sum())
    margin = float((y_tr_r - 0.5).abs()[flips].max()) if n_flip else 0.0
    knife = bool(n_flip > 0 and margin < fold_err_r)
    deploy_params = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())

    test_loader = build_loader(args.dataset_root, args.test_list, 16, args.num_workers)
    _, sv = evaluate(test_loader, model)

    log("=== TEST RESULTS ===")
    log("[MODEL] CASA-VMamba-STGeo (VMamba-Tiny + SPD(2)/H2 geodesic evidence + TAR/DCR)")
    log(f"[ARCH] casa_vmamba_stgeo")
    log(f"[STGEO] {args.stgeo} form={rep.get('form')} sigmas={args.stgeo_sigmas} "
        f"mid={args.stgeo_mid} dim={args.stgeo_dim} pool={args.stgeo_pool} "
        f"xscale={args.stgeo_xscale} sup_small={args.stgeo_sup_small} "
        f"sup_lambda={args.stgeo_sup_lambda}")
    if args.stgeo == "sup":
        log(f"[STGEO-SUP] lambda={args.stgeo_sup_lambda} small={args.stgeo_sup_small} "
            f"form={STGEO_SUP_FORM} dropped_at_deploy=1")
    else:
        log(f"[STGEO-GAMMA] 0.000000e+00 (eval-only)")
    log(f"[REP-MODE] {args.rep_mode}")
    log(f"[BACKBONE-LR-RATIO] {args.backbone_lr_ratio}")
    log("[DATA-CONTRACT] legacy_6ch_reverse_v1")
    log("[DEPLOY-NUMERICS] tf32=off deterministic=on (STR T2 protocol)")
    log(f"[PRETRAIN-SHA256] {sha16(args.vmamba_pretrained_weight_path)}")
    log(f"[PRETRAIN-LOAD] retained={st.get('retained')}/{st.get('pretrained_keys')} "
        f"worst_diff={st.get('worst_diff'):.3e}")
    log(f"[PARAM-BREAKDOWN] backbone={pb['backbone']} stgeo={pb['stgeo']} tar={pb['tar']} "
        f"dcr={pb['dcr']} head={pb['head']} total={pb['total']} "
        f"non_backbone={pb['non_backbone']}")
    log(f"[REPARAM-MAX-ABS-ERROR] {fold_err:.3e} (train vs deploy, fixed random batch)")
    log(f"[REPARAM-ARGMAX-DISAGREE] {dis:.3e} (0.5-binarization; hard gate: must be 0)")
    log(f"[REPARAM-REAL-MAX-ABS-ERROR] {fold_err_r:.3e} (real batch 16)")
    log(f"[REPARAM-REAL-ARGMAX-DISAGREE] {dis_r:.3e} (operational gate: must be 0)")
    log(f"[FOLD-KNIFE-EDGE] n_flip={n_flip} flipped_margin_max={margin:.3e} "
        f"max_abs_diff={fold_err_r:.3e} verdict="
        f"{'NO_FLIP' if n_flip == 0 else ('KNIFE_EDGE_ARTIFACT' if knife else 'NEEDS_REVIEW')}")
    log(f"[DEPLOY-PARAMS] total={deploy_params / 1e6:.6f} M")
    log(f"Recall={sv['recall']:.4f} | Precision={sv['precision']:.4f} | OA={sv['OA']:.4f} | "
        f"F1={sv['F1']:.4f} | IoU={sv['IoU']:.4f} | Kappa={sv['Kappa']:.4f}")
    log(f"[BEST-F1] {best_f1:.4f} (epoch {best_epoch})")
    log(f"[ACTUAL-OPT-STEPS] {int(cur_iter)}")
    log(f"[PROTOCOL-VERSION] {PROTOCOL_VERSION}")
    log("=== END TEST RESULTS ===")
    log(f"[EVAL-ONLY-META] {json.dumps({'best_ckpt': best_name, 'deploy_params': deploy_params})}")
    torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32 = prev_tf32
    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = prev_det


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/main.py
================================================================================
import sys

from model.trainer import Trainer

sys.path.insert(0, '.')

import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
import torch.backends.cudnn as cudnn
from torch.nn.parallel import gather
import torch.optim.lr_scheduler

import dataset.dataset as myDataLoader
import dataset.Transforms as myTransforms
from model.metric_tool import ConfuseMatrixMeter
from model.utils import BCEDiceLoss, init_seed, adjust_learning_rate

import os, time
import numpy as np
from argparse import ArgumentParser



@torch.no_grad()
def val(args, val_loader, model):
    model.eval()

    salEvalVal = ConfuseMatrixMeter(n_class=2)

    epoch_loss = []

    total_batches = len(val_loader)
    print(len(val_loader))
    for iter, batched_inputs in enumerate(val_loader):

        img, target = batched_inputs
        pre_img = img[:, 0:3]
        post_img = img[:, 3:6]

        start_time = time.time()

        if args.onGPU == True:
            pre_img = pre_img.cuda()
            target = target.cuda()
            post_img = post_img.cuda()

        pre_img_var = torch.autograd.Variable(pre_img).float()
        post_img_var = torch.autograd.Variable(post_img).float()
        target_var = torch.autograd.Variable(target).float()

        # run the mdoel
        output = model(pre_img_var, post_img_var)
        loss = BCEDiceLoss(output, target_var)

        pred = torch.where(output > 0.5, torch.ones_like(output), torch.zeros_like(output)).long()

        # torch.cuda.synchronize()
        time_taken = time.time() - start_time

        epoch_loss.append(loss.data.item())

        # compute the confusion matrix
        if args.onGPU and torch.cuda.device_count() > 1:
            output = gather(pred, 0, dim=0)
        # salEvalVal.addBatch(pred, target_var)
        f1 = salEvalVal.update_cm(pr=pred.cpu().numpy(), gt=target_var.cpu().numpy())
        if iter % 5 == 0:
            print('\r[%d/%d] F1: %3f loss: %.3f time: %.3f' % (iter, total_batches, f1, loss.data.item(), time_taken),
                  end='')

    average_epoch_loss_val = sum(epoch_loss) / len(epoch_loss)
    scores = salEvalVal.get_scores()

    return average_epoch_loss_val, scores


def train(args, train_loader, model, optimizer, epoch, max_batches, cur_iter=0, lr_factor=1.):
    # switch to train mode
    model.train()

    salEvalVal = ConfuseMatrixMeter(n_class=2)
    epoch_loss = []

    for iter, batched_inputs in enumerate(train_loader):

        img, target = batched_inputs
        pre_img = img[:, 0:3]
        post_img = img[:, 3:6]

        start_time = time.time()

        # adjust the learning rate
        lr = adjust_learning_rate(args, optimizer, epoch, iter + cur_iter, max_batches, lr_factor=lr_factor)

        if args.onGPU == True:
            pre_img = pre_img.cuda()
            target = target.cuda()
            post_img = post_img.cuda()

        pre_img_var = torch.autograd.Variable(pre_img).float()
        post_img_var = torch.autograd.Variable(post_img).float()
        target_var = torch.autograd.Variable(target).float()

        # run the model
        output = model(pre_img_var, post_img_var)
        loss = BCEDiceLoss(output, target_var)

        pred = torch.where(output > 0.5, torch.ones_like(output), torch.zeros_like(output)).long()

        optimizer.zero_grad()
        loss.backward()
        optimizer.step()

        epoch_loss.append(loss.data.item())
        time_taken = time.time() - start_time
        res_time = (max_batches * args.max_epochs - iter - cur_iter) * time_taken / 3600

        if args.onGPU and torch.cuda.device_count() > 1:
            output = gather(pred, 0, dim=0)

        # Computing F-measure and IoU on GPU
        with torch.no_grad():
            f1 = salEvalVal.update_cm(pr=pred.cpu().numpy(), gt=target_var.cpu().numpy())

        if iter % 5 == 0:
            print('\riteration: [%d/%d] f1: %.3f lr: %.7f loss: %.3f time:%.3f h' % (
                iter + cur_iter, max_batches * args.max_epochs, f1, lr, loss.data.item(),
                res_time),
                  end='')

    average_epoch_loss_train = sum(epoch_loss) / len(epoch_loss)
    scores = salEvalVal.get_scores()

    return average_epoch_loss_train, scores, lr


def trainValidateSegmentation(args):
    os.environ['CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES'] = str(args.gpu_id)
    
    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = True

    init_seed(args.seed)

    args.savedir = args.savedir + '_' + args.file_root + '_iter_' + str(args.max_steps) + '_lr_' + str(args.lr) + '/'

    if args.file_root == 'LEVIR':
         args.file_root = './levir_cd_256'
    elif args.file_root == 'WHU':
        args.file_root = './whu_cd_256'
    elif args.file_root == 'CLCD':
        args.file_root = './clcd_256'
    elif args.file_root == 'OSCD':
        args.file_root = 'oscd_256'
    else:
        raise TypeError('%s has not defined' % args.file_root)

    if not os.path.exists(args.savedir):
        os.makedirs(args.savedir)


    model = Trainer(args.model_type).float()
    if args.onGPU:
        model = model.cuda()

    # mean = [0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5]
    # std = [0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5]

    mean = [0.406, 0.456, 0.485, 0.406, 0.456, 0.485]
    std = [0.225, 0.224, 0.229, 0.225, 0.224, 0.229]

    # compose the data with transforms
    trainDataset_main = myTransforms.Compose([
        myTransforms.Normalize(mean=mean, std=std),
        myTransforms.Scale(args.inWidth, args.inHeight),
        myTransforms.RandomCropResize(int(7. / 224. * args.inWidth)),
        myTransforms.RandomFlip(),
        myTransforms.RandomExchange(),
        myTransforms.ToTensor()
    ])

    valDataset = myTransforms.Compose([
        myTransforms.Normalize(mean=mean, std=std),
        myTransforms.Scale(args.inWidth, args.inHeight),
        myTransforms.ToTensor()
    ])

    train_data = myDataLoader.Dataset(file_root=args.file_root, mode="train", transform=trainDataset_main)

    trainLoader = torch.utils.data.DataLoader(
        train_data,
        batch_size=args.batch_size, shuffle=True,
        num_workers=args.num_workers, pin_memory=True, drop_last=False
    )

    test_data = myDataLoader.Dataset(file_root=args.file_root, mode="test", transform=valDataset)
    testLoader = torch.utils.data.DataLoader(
        test_data, shuffle=False,
        batch_size=args.batch_size, num_workers=args.num_workers, pin_memory=True)


    max_batches = len(trainLoader)
    print('For each epoch, we have {} batches'.format(max_batches))

    if args.onGPU:
        cudnn.benchmark = True

    args.max_epochs = int(np.ceil(args.max_steps / max_batches))
    start_epoch = 0
    cur_iter = 0
    max_F1_val = 0

    if args.resume is not None:
        args.resume = args.savedir + 'checkpoint.pth.tar'
        if os.path.isfile(args.resume):
            print("=> loading checkpoint '{}'".format(args.resume))
            checkpoint = torch.load(args.resume)
            start_epoch = checkpoint['epoch']
            cur_iter = start_epoch * len(trainLoader)
            # args.lr = checkpoint['lr']
            model.load_state_dict(checkpoint['state_dict'])
            print("=> loaded checkpoint '{}' (epoch {})"
                  .format(args.resume, checkpoint['epoch']))
        else:
            print("=> no checkpoint found at '{}'".format(args.resume))

    logFileLoc = args.savedir + args.logFile
    if os.path.isfile(logFileLoc):
        logger = open(logFileLoc, 'a')
    else:
        logger = open(logFileLoc, 'w')
        logger.write(
            "\n%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\t%s" % ('Epoch', 'Kappa (val)', 'IoU (val)', 'F1 (val)', 'R (val)', 'P (val)', 'OA (val)'))
    logger.flush()

    optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(model.parameters(), args.lr, (0.9, 0.99), eps=1e-08, weight_decay=1e-4)

    for epoch in range(start_epoch, args.max_epochs):
        lossTr, score_tr, lr = \
            train(args, trainLoader, model, optimizer, epoch, max_batches, cur_iter)
        cur_iter += len(trainLoader)

        torch.cuda.empty_cache()

        # evaluate on validation set
        if epoch == 0:
            continue

        lossVal, score_val = val(args, testLoader, model)
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()
        logger.write("\n%d\t\t%.4f\t\t%.4f\t\t%.4f\t\t%.4f\t\t%.4f\t\t%.4f" % (epoch, score_val['Kappa'], score_val['IoU'],
                                                                       score_val['F1'], score_val['recall'],
                                                                       score_val['precision'], score_val['OA']))
        logger.flush()

        torch.save({
            'epoch': epoch + 1,
            'arch': str(model),
            'state_dict': model.state_dict(),
            'optimizer': optimizer.state_dict(),
            'lossTr': lossTr,
            'lossVal': lossVal,
            'F_Tr': score_tr['F1'],
            'F_val': score_val['F1'],
            'lr': lr
        }, args.savedir + 'checkpoint.pth.tar')

        # save the model also
        model_file_name = args.savedir + 'best_model.pth'
        if epoch % 1 == 0 and max_F1_val <= score_val['F1']:
            max_F1_val = score_val['F1']
            torch.save(model.state_dict(), model_file_name)

        print("Epoch " + str(epoch) + ': Details')
        print("\nEpoch No. %d:\tTrain Loss = %.4f\tVal Loss = %.4f\t F1(tr) = %.4f\t F1(val) = %.4f" \
              % (epoch, lossTr, lossVal, score_tr['F1'], score_val['F1']))
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()

    state_dict = torch.load(model_file_name)
    model.load_state_dict(state_dict)

    loss_test, score_test = val(args, testLoader, model)
    print("\nTest :\t Kappa (te) = %.4f\t IoU (te) = %.4f\t F1 (te) = %.4f\t R (te) = %.4f\t P (te) = %.4f" \
          % (score_test['Kappa'], score_test['IoU'], score_test['F1'], score_test['recall'], score_test['precision']))
    logger.write("\n%s\t\t%.4f\t\t%.4f\t\t%.4f\t\t%.4f\t\t%.4f" % ('Test', score_test['Kappa'], score_test['IoU'],
                                                                   score_test['F1'], score_test['recall'],
                                                                   score_test['precision'], score_test['OA']))
    logger.flush()
    logger.close()


if __name__ == '__main__':
    parser = ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument('--file_root', default="LEVIR", help='Data directory | LEVIR | WHU | CLCD | OSCD ')
    parser.add_argument('--inWidth', type=int, default=256, help='Width of RGB image')
    parser.add_argument('--inHeight', type=int, default=256, help='Height of RGB image')
    parser.add_argument('--max_steps', type=int, default=80000, help='Max. number of iterations')
    parser.add_argument('--num_workers', type=int, default=4, help='No. of parallel threads')
    parser.add_argument('--model_type', type=str, default='small', help='select vit model type | tiny | small')
    parser.add_argument('--batch_size', type=int, default=16, help='Batch size')
    parser.add_argument('--step_loss', type=int, default=100, help='Decrease learning rate after how many epochs')
    parser.add_argument('--lr', type=float, default=2e-4, help='Initial learning rate')
    parser.add_argument('--lr_mode', default='poly', help='Learning rate policy, step or poly')
    parser.add_argument('--seed', default=16, help='initialization seed number')
    parser.add_argument('--savedir', default='./results', help='Directory to save the results')
    parser.add_argument('--resume', default=None, help='Use this checkpoint to continue training | '
                                                       './results_ep100/checkpoint.pth.tar')
    parser.add_argument('--logFile', default='trainValLog.txt',
                        help='File that stores the training and validation logs')
    parser.add_argument('--onGPU', default=True, type=lambda x: (str(x).lower() == 'true'),
                        help='Run on CPU or GPU. If TRUE, then GPU.')
    parser.add_argument('--gpu_id', default=0, type=int, help='GPU id number')

    args = parser.parse_args()
    print('Called with args:')
    print(args)

    trainValidateSegmentation(args)

# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/profile_CASA.py

"""Measure full-batch training memory and throughput for CASA variants."""
import argparse
import gc
import json
import os
import subprocess
import time
from pathlib import Path

import torch

from model.casa_net import CASANet
from train_CASA import PROTOCOL, GUIDE_WEIGHT, make_loader, training_losses, make_optimizer


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--dataset-root", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--pretrained", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--modules", nargs="+", choices=("none", "hyperbolic", "qkv"),
                        default=("none", "hyperbolic", "qkv"))
    args = parser.parse_args()
    torch.set_num_threads(4)
    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = True
    torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32 = True
    torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32 = False
    image, target = next(iter(make_loader(args.dataset_root, "train")))
    image, target = image.cuda().float(), target.cuda().float()
    results = {}
    for variant in args.modules:
        torch.manual_seed(2333)
        model = CASANet(args.pretrained, variant).cuda().train()
        optimizer = make_optimizer(model)
        torch.cuda.reset_peak_memory_stats()
        times = []
        for step in range(6):
            torch.cuda.synchronize()
            start = time.time()
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            logits, auxiliary = model(image[:, :3], image[:, 3:], return_aux=True)
            loss, main_loss, auxiliary_loss = training_losses(logits, auxiliary, target)
            loss.backward()
            torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), float("inf"), error_if_nonfinite=True, foreach=True)
            optimizer.step()
            torch.cuda.synchronize()
            times.append(time.time() - start)
        model.context.collect_diag = True
        with torch.no_grad():
            model.predict(image[:, :3], image[:, 3:])
        used = subprocess.check_output(
            ["nvidia-smi", "--query-compute-apps=pid,used_memory", "--format=csv,noheader,nounits"],
            text=True)
        process_mib = next(float(row.split(",")[1]) for row in used.splitlines()
                           if int(row.split(",")[0]) == os.getpid())
        context_mib = process_mib - torch.cuda.memory_reserved() / 1024**2
        result = dict(protocol=PROTOCOL, batch_size=image.shape[0],
                      training_outputs=4, training_prediction_format="logits",
                      guide_weight=GUIDE_WEIGHT, encoder_lr_mult=1.0,
                      inference_method="predict", inference_outputs=1,
                      grid_sizes=list(model.context.grid_sizes),
                      contexts_per_scale=list(model.context.tokens_per_scale),
                      joint_contexts=sum(model.context.tokens_per_scale),
                      compression_power=model.context.compression_power,
                      support_power=model.context.support_power,
                      self_reference=model.context.self_reference,
                      qk_geometry=model.context.geometry, value_geometry=model.context.value_geometry,
                      heads=model.context.heads if variant != "none" else 0,
                      qkv_dim=model.context.qk.out_channels if variant != "none" else 0,
                      hyperbolic_radius=model.context.radius_mode if variant == "hyperbolic" else None,
                      context_projection_weight_decay=1e-4, context_projection_weight_decay_mode="decoupled",
                      body_weight_decay_mode="coupled_l2", context_other_weight_decay=0.0,
                      optimizer_groups=[dict(name=g["group_name"], weight_decay=g["weight_decay"],
                                             decoupled_weight_decay=g["decoupled_weight_decay"])
                                        for g in optimizer.param_groups],
                      peak_allocated_mib=torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated() / 1024**2,
                      peak_reserved_mib=torch.cuda.max_memory_reserved() / 1024**2,
                      context_overhead_mib=context_mib, process_memory_mib=process_mib,
                      seconds_per_step=sum(times[2:]) / len(times[2:]), loss=float(loss.detach()),
                      params=sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters()), diagnostics=model.diagnostics())
        results[variant] = result
        print(f"[PROFILE] {variant} {json.dumps(result)}", flush=True)
        del model, optimizer, logits, auxiliary, loss, main_loss, auxiliary_loss
        gc.collect()
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    args.output.write_text(json.dumps(results, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/profile_MOE.py
================================================================================
"""Measure full-batch training and batch-one deployed MoE execution on GPU 1."""
import argparse
import gc
import json
import os
import statistics
import subprocess
import time
from pathlib import Path

import torch

from model.casa_net import CASANet
from model.layers.candidate_context import MODEL_VARIANTS
from train_CASA import (PROTOCOL, make_loader, make_optimizer, training_losses,
                        task_gradient_diagnostics, loss_function, SpatialMetrics)


def process_memory_mib():
    output = subprocess.check_output(
        ["nvidia-smi", "--query-compute-apps=pid,used_memory", "--format=csv,noheader,nounits"],
        text=True)
    for row in output.splitlines():
        pid, memory = row.split(",")
        if int(pid) == os.getpid():
            return float(memory)
    return None


def profile_variant(variant, image, target, pretrained, training_steps, inference_steps):
    torch.manual_seed(2333)
    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(2333)
    image, target = image.cuda().float(), target.cuda().float()
    model = CASANet(pretrained, variant).cuda().train()
    optimizer = make_optimizer(model)
    torch.cuda.reset_peak_memory_stats()
    timings = []
    for step in range(training_steps):
        torch.cuda.synchronize()
        started = time.perf_counter()
        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        logits, auxiliary = model(image[:, :3], image[:, 3:], return_aux=True)
        loss, main, guide = training_losses(logits, auxiliary, target)
        if not torch.isfinite(loss):
            raise FloatingPointError(f"{variant}: non-finite profiling loss")
        if step == 2 and variant != "none":
            task_gradient_diagnostics(model, main)
        loss.backward()
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), float("inf"),
                                      error_if_nonfinite=True, foreach=True)
        optimizer.step()
        torch.cuda.synchronize()
        timings.append(time.perf_counter() - started)
    # Formal epoch evaluation collects full-batch context and spatial diagnostics.
    model.eval()
    model.context.collect_diag = True
    with torch.no_grad():
        validation_logits, _ = model(image[:, :3], image[:, 3:], return_aux=True)
        loss_function(validation_logits, target)
        spatial = SpatialMetrics(image.device)
        spatial.update(validation_logits[0] > 0, target)
        spatial.scores()
    model.context.collect_diag = False
    del validation_logits, spatial
    result = dict(
        protocol=PROTOCOL, physical_gpu=1, variant=variant, batch_size=image.shape[0],
        training_outputs=4, inference_outputs=1, training_steps=training_steps,
        peak_allocated_mib=torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated() / 1024**2,
        peak_reserved_mib=torch.cuda.max_memory_reserved() / 1024**2,
        process_memory_mib=process_memory_mib(),
        seconds_per_step=statistics.mean(timings[3:]),
        memory_scope="training including task-gradient and full-batch epoch diagnostics",
        training_step_seconds=timings,
        loss=float(loss.detach()), main_loss=float(main.detach()), guide_loss=float(guide.detach()),
        params=sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters()),
        trainable_params=sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad),
        context_params=sum(p.numel() for p in model.context.parameters()),
        configuration=model.context_metadata(),
        optimizer_groups=[dict(name=g["group_name"], weight_decay=g["weight_decay"],
                               decoupled_weight_decay=g["decoupled_weight_decay"])
                          for g in optimizer.param_groups],
        precision=dict(tensor="float32", cudnn_tf32=True, matmul_tf32=False),
        device_name=torch.cuda.get_device_name(),
    )
    if result["process_memory_mib"] is not None:
        result["context_overhead_mib"] = result["process_memory_mib"] - torch.cuda.memory_reserved() / 1024**2
    a, b = image[:1, :3].clone(), image[:1, 3:].clone()
    del optimizer, logits, auxiliary, loss, main, guide, image, target
    model.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
    model = model.eval().switch_to_deploy()
    gc.collect()
    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    # One image pair models the final inference API, excluding deep supervision heads.
    with torch.inference_mode():
        for _ in range(10):
            model.predict(a, b)
        torch.cuda.synchronize()
        persistent_bytes = torch.cuda.memory_allocated()
        torch.cuda.reset_peak_memory_stats()
        events = []
        for _ in range(inference_steps):
            start = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)
            end = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)
            start.record()
            prediction = model.predict(a, b)
            end.record()
            events.append((start, end))
        torch.cuda.synchronize()
        milliseconds = [start.elapsed_time(end) for start, end in events]
        if not torch.isfinite(prediction).all():
            raise FloatingPointError(f"{variant}: non-finite deployed prediction")
    result["deploy"] = dict(
        params=sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters()),
        trainable_params=sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad),
        batch_size=1, measured_iterations=inference_steps,
        median_ms=statistics.median(milliseconds), mean_ms=statistics.mean(milliseconds),
        p90_ms=sorted(milliseconds)[max(0, int(.9 * len(milliseconds)) - 1)],
        peak_allocated_mib=torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated() / 1024**2,
        incremental_peak_mib=(torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated() - persistent_bytes) / 1024**2,
        memory_scope="deployed model, one image pair and inference temporaries",
    )
    del model, prediction, events, a, b
    gc.collect()
    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    return result


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--dataset-root", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--pretrained", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--modules", nargs="+", choices=MODEL_VARIANTS, default=MODEL_VARIANTS)
    parser.add_argument("--training-steps", type=int, default=6)
    parser.add_argument("--inference-steps", type=int, default=50)
    parser.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=32)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if os.environ.get("CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES") != "1":
        raise RuntimeError("Run with CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1 for this GPU-1 exploration")
    if args.training_steps < 4 or args.inference_steps < 1:
        parser.error("training-steps must be >= 4 and inference-steps must be >= 1")
    torch.set_num_threads(4)
    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = True
    torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32 = True
    torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32 = False
    image, target = next(iter(make_loader(args.dataset_root, "train", batch_size=args.batch_size)))
    results = {}
    args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    for variant in args.modules:
        results[variant] = profile_variant(variant, image, target, args.pretrained,
                                           args.training_steps, args.inference_steps)
        args.output.write_text(json.dumps(results, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        print(f"[PROFILE] {variant} {json.dumps(results[variant])}", flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/run4_dry_run.py

"""R4 真实数据 3-step dry-run（方案 §7 T7/T8）。

用途：在**不启动任何正式 80K** 的前提下，用真实 A/B/label 走原 `BCEDiceLoss` 与原 Adam
参数组跑 3 次优化，逐项核验：

  * T8：A/B/label 几何映射同步（在每个几何变换之后逐一断言 img/label 空间尺寸一致）；
        label 按 `gray>=128` 二值化（与原始 PNG 逐像素比对）；
        loss 有限；batch32 显存可容纳（R8）；
  * T7：第 1 步 `gamma.grad` 有限非零；γ 开门后第 2/3 步 `diff.weight.grad` 非零；
        encoder/TAR/DCR/head 均仍有梯度；backbone:new 参数组 LR 比例 = 0.1。

纪律：只写 `--output-dir`（独立目录，例 `.../CASA-TViM/Run4_DRY/<variant>`）；
不保存 best、不写 `train_log.txt` 之外的任何历史产物、不触碰 Run1–Run3 / Diag1 文件。

    python run4_dry_run.py --variant E6_FET1 --device cuda:0 --steps 3 \
      --dataset-root /share_datasets/CD/SYSU-CD-256 \
      --train-list /share_datasets/CD/SYSU-CD-256/list/train.txt \
      --output-dir /home/yqwang/outputs/CASA-CD/CASA-TViM/Run4_DRY/E6_FET1
"""
import argparse
import json
import os
import sys
import time

import numpy as np
import torch

_MODELS_ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if _MODELS_ROOT not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, _MODELS_ROOT)

from model.casa_tvim_str_net import CASATViMSTRNet          # noqa: E402
from model.utils import BCEDiceLoss, init_seed               # noqa: E402
import dataset.dataset as myDataLoader                       # noqa: E402
import dataset.Transforms as myTransforms                    # noqa: E402

MEAN = [0.406, 0.456, 0.485, 0.406, 0.456, 0.485]
STD = [0.225, 0.224, 0.229, 0.225, 0.224, 0.229]

VARIANTS = {
    "M1_R4CTRL": dict(fine_tap=0),
    "E6_FET1": dict(fine_tap=1),
}


class _Probe(object):
    """插在几何变换之间的探针：img/label 空间尺寸必须始终一致。

    注意布局差异：几何变换阶段 img 是 numpy HWC、label 是 HW；`ToTensor` 之后
    img 是 CHW、label 是 [1,H,W]。比较的是**空间**尺寸，不是前两维。
    """

    def __init__(self, name, rec):
        self.name = name
        self.rec = rec

    @staticmethod
    def _spatial(t):
        if hasattr(t, "dim") and t.dim() == 3:      # torch CHW / [1,H,W]
            return int(t.shape[-2]), int(t.shape[-1])
        return int(t.shape[0]), int(t.shape[1])     # numpy HWC / HW

    def __call__(self, img, label):
        ih, iw = self._spatial(img)
        lh, lw = self._spatial(label)
        self.rec.append({"stage": self.name, "img": [ih, iw], "label": [lh, lw],
                         "sync": bool(ih == lh and iw == lw)})
        return [img, label]


def build_train_transform(probe_rec, size=256):
    return myTransforms.Compose([
        myTransforms.Normalize(mean=MEAN, std=STD),
        myTransforms.Scale(size, size),
        _Probe("after_scale", probe_rec),
        myTransforms.RandomCropResize(int(7. / 224. * size)),
        _Probe("after_crop", probe_rec),
        myTransforms.RandomFlip(),
        _Probe("after_flip", probe_rec),
        myTransforms.RandomExchange(),
        _Probe("after_exchange", probe_rec),
        myTransforms.ToTensor(),
        _Probe("after_tensor", probe_rec),
    ])


def build_eval_transform(size=256):
    return myTransforms.Compose([
        myTransforms.Normalize(mean=MEAN, std=STD),
        myTransforms.Scale(size, size),
        myTransforms.ToTensor(),
    ])


def geom_probe_pass(args, n=4):
    """几何同步探针：在主进程里用**训练增广链**逐样本走一遍，记录每个几何变换后的空间尺寸。

    不能依赖 DataLoader 的 worker 进程（probe_rec 不会被写回父进程），
    因此显式 num_workers=0 地逐样本取样。
    """
    rec = []
    tf = build_train_transform(rec)
    ds = myDataLoader.Dataset(file_root=args.dataset_root, list_path=args.train_list, transform=tf)
    for i in range(min(n, len(ds))):
        _ = ds[i]
    return rec


def check_label_gray128(args, n=4):
    """label 张量必须逐位等于 (raw_gray >= 128)；同时统计 raw 中的中间灰度。

    必须用**独立**的确定性 eval 链数据集（不能复用带随机增广的训练 Dataset，
    否则 crop/flip 会让比较失去意义）。
    """
    import cv2
    tf = build_eval_transform()
    ds = myDataLoader.Dataset(file_root=args.dataset_root, list_path=args.train_list, transform=tf)
    rows, ok = [], True
    n_inter = 0
    for i in range(min(n, len(ds))):
        img, lab = ds[i]
        raw = cv2.imread(ds.gts[i], 0)
        n_inter += int(((raw > 0) & (raw < 255)).sum())
        ref = torch.from_numpy((raw >= 128).astype("int64")).unsqueeze(0)
        same = (lab.shape == ref.shape) and torch.equal(lab, ref)
        ok = ok and same
        rows.append({"idx": i, "same_as_gray128": bool(same),
                     "unique": sorted(set(int(v) for v in lab.flatten().tolist()))[:5],
                     "raw_min": int(raw.min()), "raw_max": int(raw.max()),
                     "raw_intermediate_px": int(((raw > 0) & (raw < 255)).sum())})
        _ = img
    return ok, rows, n_inter


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--variant", type=str, required=True, choices=sorted(VARIANTS))
    ap.add_argument("--device", type=str, default="cuda:0")
    ap.add_argument("--steps", type=int, default=3)
    ap.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=32)
    ap.add_argument("--dataset-root", type=str, required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--train-list", type=str, required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--output-dir", type=str, required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--tinyvim-pretrained-weight-path", type=str, default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=16)
    ap.add_argument("--lr", type=float, default=2e-4)
    ap.add_argument("--backbone-lr-ratio", type=float, default=0.1)
    args = ap.parse_args()

    cfg = VARIANTS[args.variant]
    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)
    log_path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, "dry_run_log.txt")
    logf = open(log_path, "a", encoding="utf-8")

    def log(msg):
        print(msg, flush=True)
        logf.write(msg + "\n")
        logf.flush()

    device = args.device
    if device.startswith("cuda") and not torch.cuda.is_available():
        device = "cpu"
    log(f"[DRY] variant={args.variant} fine_tap={cfg['fine_tap']} device={device} steps={args.steps} "
        f"batch={args.batch_size} seed={args.seed} out={args.output_dir}")
    log("[DRY] this run never writes best_F1=*.pth and never touches Run1-Run3 / Diag1 artifacts")

    init_seed(args.seed)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- model
    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)
    model = CASATViMSTRNet(
        args.tinyvim_pretrained_weight_path, caacp=True, rep_mode="full", str_dim=96,
        caacp_score_mode="rank", frh=False, caacp_residual_mode="current",
        fs_tar=False, fine_tap=bool(cfg["fine_tap"])).float().to(device)
    if cfg["fine_tap"]:
        log(f"[DRY] FET gamma_init={model.fet_gamma():.1e} "
            f"params={model.fine_evidence_tap.param_report()['train_new']}")

    backbone = [p for n, p in model.named_parameters() if n.startswith("encoder.")]
    new = [p for n, p in model.named_parameters() if not n.startswith("encoder.")]
    opt = torch.optim.Adam(
        [{"params": backbone, "lr": args.lr * args.backbone_lr_ratio,
          "lr_scale": args.backbone_lr_ratio, "name": "backbone"},
         {"params": new, "lr": args.lr, "lr_scale": 1.0, "name": "new"}],
        args.lr, (0.9, 0.99), eps=1e-08, weight_decay=1e-4)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- data
    tf = build_train_transform([])
    ds = myDataLoader.Dataset(file_root=args.dataset_root, list_path=args.train_list, transform=tf)
    loader = torch.utils.data.DataLoader(ds, batch_size=args.batch_size, shuffle=True,
                                         num_workers=2, pin_memory=True, drop_last=False)
    log(f"[DRY] train samples={len(ds)} batches/epoch={len(loader)}")

    it = iter(loader)
    losses, gamma_grads, diff_grads, lr_rows = [], [], [], []
    loss_finite = []
    shapes = None
    model.train()
    t0 = time.time()
    for step in range(args.steps):
        img, target = next(it)
        pre, post = img[:, 0:3].to(device), img[:, 3:6].to(device)
        tgt = target.to(device).float()
        out = model(pre.float(), post.float(), tgt)
        loss = BCEDiceLoss(out, tgt)
        opt.zero_grad()
        loss.backward()
        if cfg["fine_tap"]:
            g = model.fine_evidence_tap.gamma.grad
            gamma_grads.append(None if g is None else float(g.item()))
            dw = model.fine_evidence_tap.diff.weight.grad
            diff_grads.append(None if dw is None else float(dw.abs().sum().item()))
        lr_rows.append([float(pg["lr"]) for pg in opt.param_groups])
        losses.append(float(loss.item()))
        loss_finite.append(bool(torch.isfinite(loss).item()))
        shapes = {"img": list(img.shape), "out": list(out.shape), "target": list(tgt.shape)}
        opt.step()
        log(f"[DRY] step={step + 1} loss={losses[-1]:.6f} finite={loss_finite[-1]} "
            f"gamma.grad={gamma_grads[-1] if gamma_grads else 'n/a'} "
            f"|Wdiff.grad|_1={diff_grads[-1] if diff_grads else 'n/a'} "
            f"lr={['%.3e' % x for x in lr_rows[-1]]}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------- gradient coverage
    zero_grad_params = []
    for n, p in model.named_parameters():
        if not p.requires_grad:
            continue
        if p.grad is None or float(p.grad.abs().sum().item()) == 0.0:
            if n.startswith("fine_evidence_tap.diff"):
                continue                     # γ=0 时 W_diff 梯度为 0 是预期（T7 只要求 γ 开门后非零）
            zero_grad_params.append(n)
    if cfg["fine_tap"]:
        zero_grad_params = [n for n in zero_grad_params if not n.startswith("fine_evidence_tap.diff")]
    log(f"[DRY] zero-grad params (excluding FET diff while gamma==0): {zero_grad_params[:8]} "
        f"(n={len(zero_grad_params)})")

    # ------------------------------------------------------- label contract
    lab_ok, lab_rows, n_inter = check_label_gray128(args)
    log(f"[DRY] label gray>=128 check: ok={lab_ok} rows={json.dumps(lab_rows)}")

    probe_rec = geom_probe_pass(args)
    geom_sync = bool(probe_rec) and all(r["sync"] for r in probe_rec)
    log(f"[DRY] geometry sync probes={len(probe_rec)} all_sync={geom_sync}")
    for r in probe_rec:
        if not r["sync"]:
            log(f"[DRY][GEOM-FAIL] {r}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------- batch32 memory
    batch32_ok = True
    peak_mb = None
    if device.startswith("cuda"):
        try:
            torch.cuda.reset_peak_memory_stats()
            big_img, big_tgt = next(iter(torch.utils.data.DataLoader(
                myDataLoader.Dataset(file_root=args.dataset_root, list_path=args.train_list,
                                     transform=build_eval_transform()),
                batch_size=32, shuffle=False, num_workers=2, pin_memory=True)))
            pre = big_img[:, 0:3].to(device).float()
            post = big_img[:, 3:6].to(device).float()
            tgt = big_tgt.to(device).float()
            model.train()
            out = model(pre, post, tgt)
            loss = BCEDiceLoss(out, tgt)
            opt.zero_grad()
            loss.backward()
            opt.step()
            peak_mb = torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated() / (1024 ** 2)
            log(f"[DRY] batch32 forward+backward OK peak_allocated={peak_mb:.0f} MB "
                f"(two processes/GPU must fit -> R8)")
            del out, loss, pre, post, tgt, big_img, big_tgt
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
        except torch.cuda.OutOfMemoryError as e:
            batch32_ok = False
            log(f"[DRY][OOM] batch32 does not fit on this GPU: {e}")

    report = {
        "variant": args.variant,
        "fine_tap": int(cfg["fine_tap"]),
        "device": device,
        "actual_steps": int(args.steps),
        "wrote_best": False,
        "losses": losses,
        "loss_finite": loss_finite,
        "gamma_grad_step1": bool(gamma_grads and gamma_grads[0] not in (None, 0.0)),
        "gamma_grads": gamma_grads,
        "diff_grad_step23": bool(len(diff_grads) >= 3 and all(x not in (None, 0.0) for x in diff_grads[1:3])),
        "diff_grads": diff_grads,
        "lr_rows": lr_rows,
        "lr_ratio_ok": all(abs(r[0] / r[1] - args.backbone_lr_ratio) < 1e-9 for r in lr_rows),
        "zero_grad_params": zero_grad_params,
        "geom_sync": geom_sync,
        "geom_probes": probe_rec,
        "label_gray128": bool(lab_ok),
        "label_rows": lab_rows,
        "raw_intermediate_px": n_inter,
        "batch_size": args.batch_size,
        "batch32_ok": bool(batch32_ok),
        "peak_memory_mb": peak_mb,
        "shapes": shapes,
        "elapsed_s": round(time.time() - t0, 1),
        "dataset_root": args.dataset_root,
        "train_list": args.train_list,
    }
    with open(os.path.join(args.output_dir, "dry_run.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)
    log(f"[DRY] wrote {os.path.join(args.output_dir, 'dry_run.json')}")

    hard_fail = (not all(loss_finite) or not geom_sync or not lab_ok or not batch32_ok
                 or (cfg["fine_tap"] and not report["gamma_grad_step1"])
                 or (cfg["fine_tap"] and not report["diff_grad_step23"])
                 or not report["lr_ratio_ok"] or bool(zero_grad_params))
    log(f"[DRY] RESULT={'FAIL' if hard_fail else 'PASS'}")
    logf.close()
    return 1 if hard_fail else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

================================================================================
# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/run7_dry_run.py
================================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Run7 HFAE 真实数据 dry-run（3 步 + 机制自检 + 折叠等价 + batch32 显存探针）。

**只读语义**：绝不写 `best_F1=*.pth`，不触碰 Run1–Run6 / Diag1 任何产物；只写自己的 log 与 summary。

    python models/_legacy/run7_dry_run.py --variant E9_HFAE_A \
      --dataset-root /share_datasets/CD/SYSU-CD-256 \
      --train-list /share_datasets/CD/SYSU-CD-256/list/train.txt \
      --tinyvim-pretrained-weight-path .../tinyvim_s_1000e.pth \
      --device cuda:0 --output-dir .../Run7_DRY/SYSU-CD-256/E9_HFAE_A
"""
import argparse
import copy
import json
import os
import sys
import time

import numpy as np
import torch

_MODELS = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if _MODELS not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, _MODELS)

import dataset.dataset as myDataLoader                                    # noqa: E402
import dataset.Transforms as myTransforms                                  # noqa: E402
from model.casa_tvim_str_net import CASATViMSTRNet                         # noqa: E402
from model.str_hf_edge import HF_EDGE_TAPS, edge_target_from_label, hf_edge_loss  # noqa: E402
from model.utils import BCEDiceLoss, init_seed                             # noqa: E402

# variant -> (hf_edge, hf_edge_form)
VARIANTS = {
    "M1_R7CTRL": (0, "none"),
    "E9_HFAE_A": (1, "aux_only"),
    "E10_HFAE_B": (1, "aux_inject"),
}
MEAN6 = [0.406, 0.456, 0.485, 0.406, 0.456, 0.485]
STD6 = [0.225, 0.224, 0.229, 0.225, 0.224, 0.229]


def build_transform():
    return myTransforms.Compose([myTransforms.Normalize(mean=MEAN6, std=STD6),
                                 myTransforms.Scale(256, 256), myTransforms.ToTensor()])


def make_model(variant, args, device):
    hf_edge, form = VARIANTS[variant]
    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)
    m = CASATViMSTRNet(
        args.tinyvim_pretrained_weight_path, caacp=True, rep_mode="full", str_dim=96,
        caacp_score_mode="rank", frh=False, caacp_residual_mode="current", fs_tar=False,
        fine_tap=False, hf_edge=bool(hf_edge), hf_edge_form=form,
        hf_edge_taps=args.hf_edge_taps, hf_edge_lambda=args.hf_edge_lambda).float().to(device)
    return m


def npar(m):
    return sum(p.numel() for p in m.parameters())


def check_label_gray128(args, n=4):
    """label 契约：Transforms.Normalize 必须按 gray>=128 二值化（CDD 为 JPEG 有中间灰度）。"""
    import cv2
    rows, ok = [], True
    with open(args.train_list, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        names = [ln.strip() for ln in f if ln.strip()][:n]
    for nm in names:
        p = os.path.join(args.dataset_root, "label", nm)
        raw = cv2.imread(p, 0)
        if raw is None:
            rows.append({"name": nm, "missing": True}); ok = False; continue
        inter = int(((raw > 0) & (raw < 255)).sum())
        rows.append({"name": nm, "raw_min": int(raw.min()), "raw_max": int(raw.max()),
                     "intermediate_px": inter})
    return ok, rows


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--variant", required=True, choices=sorted(VARIANTS))
    ap.add_argument("--dataset-root", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--train-list", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--tinyvim-pretrained-weight-path", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--output-dir", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--device", default="cuda:0")
    ap.add_argument("--steps", type=int, default=3)
    ap.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=32)
    ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=16)
    ap.add_argument("--lr", type=float, default=2e-4)
    ap.add_argument("--backbone-lr-ratio", type=float, default=0.1)
    ap.add_argument("--hf-edge-taps", default="1_4_1_8")
    ap.add_argument("--hf-edge-lambda", type=float, default=0.2)
    args = ap.parse_args()

    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)
    log_path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, "dry_run_log.txt")
    logf = open(log_path, "a", encoding="utf-8")

    def log(msg):
        print(msg, flush=True)
        logf.write(msg + "\n"); logf.flush()

    device = args.device
    if device.startswith("cuda") and not torch.cuda.is_available():
        device = "cpu"
    hf_edge, form = VARIANTS[args.variant]
    log(f"[DRY] variant={args.variant} hf_edge={hf_edge} form={form} device={device} "
        f"steps={args.steps} batch={args.batch_size} seed={args.seed} "
        f"taps={args.hf_edge_taps} lambda={args.hf_edge_lambda}")
    log("[DRY] never writes best_F1=*.pth; never touches Run1-Run6 / Diag1 artifacts")

    init_seed(args.seed)
    model = make_model(args.variant, args, device)
    rep = model.hf_edge_report()
    log(f"[DRY] train_params={npar(model)} hf_edge_report={json.dumps(rep, default=str)}")

    # ---------------- epoch-0 与 CTRL 逐位比对（同一 real batch，未训练前）
    ck = None
    if hf_edge:
        ck = make_model("M1_R7CTRL", args, device).eval()

    backbone = [p for n, p in model.named_parameters() if n.startswith("encoder.")]
    new = [p for n, p in model.named_parameters() if not n.startswith("encoder.")]
    opt = torch.optim.Adam(
        [{"params": backbone, "lr": args.lr * args.backbone_lr_ratio,
          "lr_scale": args.backbone_lr_ratio, "name": "backbone"},
         {"params": new, "lr": args.lr, "lr_scale": 1.0, "name": "new"}],
        args.lr, (0.9, 0.99), eps=1e-08, weight_decay=1e-4)

    ds = myDataLoader.Dataset(file_root=args.dataset_root, list_path=args.train_list,
                              transform=build_transform())
    loader = torch.utils.data.DataLoader(ds, batch_size=args.batch_size, shuffle=True,
                                         num_workers=2, pin_memory=True, drop_last=False)
    log(f"[DRY] train samples={len(ds)} batches/epoch={len(loader)}")
    it = iter(loader)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- epoch-0 门
    img0, tgt0 = next(it)
    pre0 = img0[:, 0:3].to(device).float(); post0 = img0[:, 3:6].to(device).float()
    t0v = tgt0.to(device).float()
    model.eval()
    with torch.no_grad():
        y_exp = model(pre0, post0)
        if ck is not None:
            y_ctrl = ck(pre0, post0)
            eq0 = torch.equal(y_ctrl, y_exp)
            d0 = float((y_ctrl - y_exp).abs().max())
        else:
            eq0, d0 = True, 0.0
    log(f"[DRY] epoch-0 real-batch torch.equal(CTRL,{args.variant})={eq0} max|d|={d0:.3e}")
    if ck is not None:
        del ck
    if hf_edge and not eq0:
        log("[DRY][P0-FAIL] epoch-0 与 CTRL 不是逐位相同 —— 零初始化/注入路径有问题")
        logf.close(); return 2

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 3 步训练
    model.train()
    losses, auxes, tap_grads, head_grads = [], [], [], []
    step_rows = []
    for step in range(args.steps):
        img, target = next(it)
        pre = img[:, 0:3].to(device).float(); post = img[:, 3:6].to(device).float()
        tgt = target.to(device).float()
        out = model(pre, post, tgt)
        loss = BCEDiceLoss(out, tgt)
        aux = None
        if hf_edge:
            aux = hf_edge_loss(model.aux_edge_logits(), tgt, args.hf_edge_lambda)
            loss = loss + aux
        opt.zero_grad(); loss.backward()
        tg = float(model.hf_edge_tap_grad_norm()) if hf_edge else 0.0
        hg = float(model.hf_edge_head_grad_norm()) if hf_edge else 0.0
        opt.step()
        finite = bool(torch.isfinite(loss).item())
        losses.append(float(loss.item()))
        if aux is not None:
            auxes.append(float(aux.item()))
        tap_grads.append(tg); head_grads.append(hg)
        step_rows.append({"step": step + 1, "loss": losses[-1], "finite": finite,
                          "aux": (float(aux.item()) if aux is not None else None),
                          "tap_grad_norm": tg, "head_grad_norm": hg,
                          "lr": [float(pg["lr"]) for pg in opt.param_groups]})
        log(f"[DRY] step={step+1} loss={losses[-1]:.6f} finite={finite} "
            f"aux={step_rows[-1]['aux']} tap_grad={tg:.4e} head_grad={hg:.4e} "
            f"lr={['%.3e' % x for x in step_rows[-1]['lr']]}")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 真实 batch 折叠门
    # 与项目既有折叠协议一致（D0 审计 / eval.py）：TF32 off + cudnn deterministic。
    # 不这么做会把 reparam 舍入误差放大到 1e-3 量级，导致个别元素跨过 0.5（实测 4.8e-7）。
    torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32 = False
    torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32 = False
    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
    model.eval()
    with torch.no_grad():
        y_tr = model(pre0, post0)
    dep = copy.deepcopy(model); dep.eval(); dep.switch_to_deploy()
    with torch.no_grad():
        y_dp = dep(pre0, post0)
    dis = float(((y_tr > 0.5) != (y_dp > 0.5)).float().mean())
    err = float((y_tr - y_dp).abs().max())
    dep_params = npar(dep)
    log(f"[DRY] real-batch fold (TF32 off, deterministic): disagreement={dis} "
        f"max|d|={err:.3e} deploy_params={dep_params}")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 梯度覆盖
    zero_grad = [n for n, p in model.named_parameters()
                 if p.requires_grad and (p.grad is None or float(p.grad.abs().sum()) == 0.0)]
    log(f"[DRY] zero-grad params n={len(zero_grad)} first={zero_grad[:8]}")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- label 契约
    lab_ok, lab_rows = check_label_gray128(args)
    log(f"[DRY] label gray>=128 check: ok={lab_ok} rows={json.dumps(lab_rows)}")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- batch32 显存
    peak_mb = None
    if device.startswith("cuda"):
        try:
            torch.cuda.reset_peak_memory_stats()
            model.train()
            out = model(pre0, post0, t0v)
            loss = BCEDiceLoss(out, t0v)
            if hf_edge:
                loss = loss + hf_edge_loss(model.aux_edge_logits(), t0v, args.hf_edge_lambda)
            opt.zero_grad(); loss.backward(); opt.step()
            peak_mb = torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated() / (1024 ** 2)
            log(f"[DRY] batch{args.batch_size} forward+backward OK peak_allocated={peak_mb:.0f} MB")
            del out, loss
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
        except torch.cuda.OutOfMemoryError:
            log("[DRY][FAIL] batch32 OOM -> 同卡 2 并发不可行（不许降 batch，改同卡串行）")
            peak_mb = -1.0

    summary = {
        "variant": args.variant, "hf_edge": hf_edge, "hf_edge_form": form,
        "device": device, "steps": args.steps, "batch_size": args.batch_size,
        "seed": args.seed, "hf_edge_taps": args.hf_edge_taps,
        "hf_edge_lambda": args.hf_edge_lambda,
        "train_params": npar(model), "deploy_params": dep_params,
        "losses": losses, "aux_losses": auxes, "tap_grad_norms": tap_grads,
        "head_grad_norms": head_grads, "steps_detail": step_rows,
        "epoch0_real_batch_equal_vs_ctrl": bool(eq0), "epoch0_max_abs_diff": d0,
        "fold_disagreement_real": dis, "fold_max_abs_diff_real": err,
        "zero_grad_params": zero_grad, "label_gray128_ok": lab_ok, "label_rows": lab_rows,
        "peak_allocated_MB_batch32": peak_mb,
        "hf_edge_report": rep,
        "gates": {
            "all_losses_finite": bool(all(s["finite"] for s in step_rows)),
            "epoch0_bitwise_identity": bool(eq0),
            "fold_disagreement_zero": bool(dis == 0.0),
            "tap_grad_nonzero_step1": bool((not hf_edge) or tap_grads[0] > 0),
            "deploy_within_5M": bool(dep_params <= 5_000_000),
        },
    }
    with open(os.path.join(args.output_dir, "dry_run_summary.json"), "w",
              encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(summary, f, indent=2, default=str)
    ok = all(summary["gates"].values())
    log(f"[DRY] GATES={json.dumps(summary['gates'])} -> {'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'}")
    logf.close()
    return 0 if ok else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

================================================================================
# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/run8_dry_run.py
================================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Run8 真实数据 dry-run（3 步 + 机制自检 + epoch-0 恒等 + 折叠等价 + batch32 显存探针）。

**只读语义**：绝不写 `best_F1=*.pth`，不触碰 Run1–Run7 / Diag1 任何产物；只写自己的 log 与 summary。

    python models/_legacy/run8_dry_run.py --variant CTRL_R8 \
      --dataset-root /share_datasets/CD/SYSU-CD-256 \
      --train-list /share_datasets/CD/SYSU-CD-256/list/train.txt \
      --vmamba-pretrained-weight-path .../vssm_tiny_0230_ckpt_epoch_262.pth \
      --device cuda:0 --output-dir .../Run8_DRY/SYSU-CD-256/CTRL_R8

变体口径（与 `train_scripts/CASA-TViM/Run8/run_all.sh` 的 `variant_args()` 必须一致）：
  CTRL_R8   : hqkv=0,  hgsl=0                            （同期对照）
  H1_HYP    : hqkv=1 hyperbolic, hgsl=0                   （几何+双分支+注入）
  H2_EUC    : hqkv=1 euclidean,  hgsl=0                   （同式欧氏对照：曲率是否必要）
  H3_HYP_GS : hqkv=1 hyperbolic, hgsl=1 detach_score=1    （+几何监督损失）
"""
import argparse
import copy
import json
import os
import sys
import time

import torch

_MODELS = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if _MODELS not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, _MODELS)

import dataset.dataset as myDataLoader                                    # noqa: E402
import dataset.Transforms as myTransforms                                  # noqa: E402
from model.casa_vmamba_net import CASAVMambaSTRNet                         # noqa: E402
from model.hgsl_loss import HGSL_STAGES_DEFAULT, hgsl_loss                 # noqa: E402
from model.utils import BCEDiceLoss, init_seed                              # noqa: E402

# variant -> dict(hqkv, geometry, hgsl, hgsl_detach_score, inject_mode)
VARIANTS = {
    "CTRL_R8":   dict(hqkv=0, geometry="hyperbolic", hgsl=0, hgsl_detach_score=1,
                      inject_mode="none"),
    "H1_HYP":    dict(hqkv=1, geometry="hyperbolic", hgsl=0, hgsl_detach_score=1,
                      inject_mode="dcr1"),
    "H2_EUC":    dict(hqkv=1, geometry="euclidean", hgsl=0, hgsl_detach_score=1,
                      inject_mode="dcr1"),
    "H3_HYP_GS": dict(hqkv=1, geometry="hyperbolic", hgsl=1, hgsl_detach_score=1,
                      inject_mode="dcr1"),
}
MEAN6 = [0.406, 0.456, 0.485, 0.406, 0.456, 0.485]
STD6 = [0.225, 0.224, 0.229, 0.225, 0.224, 0.229]


def build_transform():
    return myTransforms.Compose([myTransforms.Normalize(mean=MEAN6, std=STD6),
                                 myTransforms.Scale(256, 256), myTransforms.ToTensor()])


def make_model(variant, args, device):
    v = VARIANTS[variant]
    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)
    m = CASAVMambaSTRNet(
        vmamba_pretrained_path=args.vmamba_pretrained_weight_path,
        hqkv=bool(v["hqkv"]), str_dim=args.str_dim,
        geometry=v["geometry"], branch=args.hqkv_branch,
        hgsl=bool(v["hgsl"]), hgsl_detach_score=bool(v["hgsl_detach_score"]),
        inject_mode=v["inject_mode"],
        hqkv_rank=args.hqkv_rank, hqkv_lite_dim=args.str_dim,
        hqkv_detail_window=args.hqkv_detail_window,
        hqkv_global_window=args.hqkv_global_window,
        hqkv_attn_stages=tuple(int(s) for s in args.hqkv_attn_stages.split(",") if s != ""),
        rep_mode="full", fs_tar=False,
        hqkv_r0=args.hqkv_r0, hqkv_a=args.hqkv_a,
    ).float().to(device)
    return m


def npar(m):
    return sum(p.numel() for p in m.parameters())


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--variant", required=True, choices=sorted(VARIANTS))
    ap.add_argument("--dataset-root", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--train-list", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--vmamba-pretrained-weight-path", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--output-dir", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--device", default="cuda:0")
    ap.add_argument("--steps", type=int, default=3)
    ap.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=32)
    ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=16)
    ap.add_argument("--lr", type=float, default=2e-4)
    ap.add_argument("--backbone-lr-ratio", type=float, default=0.1)
    ap.add_argument("--str-dim", type=int, default=192)
    ap.add_argument("--hqkv-rank", type=int, default=32)
    ap.add_argument("--hqkv-detail-window", type=int, default=2)
    ap.add_argument("--hqkv-global-window", type=int, default=4)
    ap.add_argument("--hqkv-attn-stages", default="1,2,3")
    ap.add_argument("--hqkv-branch", default="both")
    ap.add_argument("--hqkv-r0", type=float, default=0.5)
    ap.add_argument("--hqkv-a", type=float, default=0.5)
    ap.add_argument("--hgsl-lambda", type=float, default=0.2)
    args = ap.parse_args()

    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)
    log_path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, "dry_run_log.txt")
    logf = open(log_path, "a", encoding="utf-8")

    def log(msg):
        print(msg, flush=True)
        logf.write(msg + "\n"); logf.flush()

    device = args.device
    if device.startswith("cuda") and not torch.cuda.is_available():
        device = "cpu"
    v = VARIANTS[args.variant]
    log(f"[DRY] variant={args.variant} spec={json.dumps(v)} device={device} "
        f"steps={args.steps} batch={args.batch_size} seed={args.seed} "
        f"str_dim={args.str_dim} rank={args.hqkv_rank}")
    log("[DRY] never writes best_F1=*.pth; never touches Run1-Run7 / Diag1 artifacts")

    init_seed(args.seed)
    model = make_model(args.variant, args, device)
    st = model.pretrain_load_stats() or {}
    log(f"[DRY] PRETRAIN-LOAD retained={st.get('retained')}/{st.get('pretrained_keys')} "
        f"worst_diff={st.get('worst_diff')} missing_new={len(st.get('missing_new', []))} "
        f"dropped={len(st.get('dropped_intentional', []))}")
    # ⚠️ 这条是 Run8 的 P0：VMamba 官方 222 个 key 必须**逐位**加载（worst_diff == 0），
    # 否则"换骨干"就变成"随机初始化一个 43M 的大网络"，与预训练无关。
    pre_ok = (st.get("worst_diff") == 0.0 and st.get("retained", 0) > 0)
    log(f"[DRY] P0 pretrain bitwise load: {'PASS' if pre_ok else 'FAIL'}")
    pb = model.param_breakdown()
    log(f"[DRY] params breakdown={json.dumps(pb)}")
    if model.hqkv_op is not None:
        log(f"[DRY] hqkv_report={json.dumps(model.hqkv_report(), default=str)}")

    # ---------------- epoch-0 与 CTRL 逐位比对（同一 real batch，未训练前）
    ck = None
    if v["hqkv"]:
        ck = make_model("CTRL_R8", args, device).eval()

    backbone = [p for n, p in model.named_parameters() if n.startswith("encoder.")]
    new = [p for n, p in model.named_parameters() if not n.startswith("encoder.")]
    opt = torch.optim.Adam(
        [{"params": backbone, "lr": args.lr * args.backbone_lr_ratio,
          "lr_scale": args.backbone_lr_ratio, "name": "backbone"},
         {"params": new, "lr": args.lr, "lr_scale": 1.0, "name": "new"}],
        args.lr, (0.9, 0.99), eps=1e-08, weight_decay=1e-4)

    ds = myDataLoader.Dataset(file_root=args.dataset_root, list_path=args.train_list,
                              transform=build_transform())
    loader = torch.utils.data.DataLoader(ds, batch_size=args.batch_size, shuffle=True,
                                         num_workers=2, pin_memory=True, drop_last=False)
    log(f"[DRY] train samples={len(ds)} batches/epoch={len(loader)}")
    it = iter(loader)

    img0, tgt0 = next(it)
    pre0 = img0[:, 0:3].to(device).float(); post0 = img0[:, 3:6].to(device).float()
    t0v = tgt0.to(device).float()
    model.eval()
    with torch.no_grad():
        y_exp = model(pre0, post0)
        if ck is not None:
            y_ctrl = ck(pre0, post0)
            eq0 = torch.equal(y_ctrl, y_exp)
            d0 = float((y_ctrl - y_exp).abs().max())
        else:
            eq0, d0 = True, 0.0
    log(f"[DRY] epoch-0 real-batch torch.equal(CTRL,{args.variant})={eq0} max|d|={d0:.3e}")
    if ck is not None:
        del ck
        if device.startswith("cuda"):
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    if v["hqkv"] and not eq0:
        log("[DRY][P0-FAIL] epoch-0 与 CTRL 不是逐位相同 —— "
            "γ 零初始化 / 注入路径 / fork_rng 顺序有问题")
        logf.close(); return 2

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 3 步训练
    model.train()
    losses, hgsls, step_rows = [], [], []
    for step in range(args.steps):
        img, target = next(it)
        pre = img[:, 0:3].to(device).float(); post = img[:, 3:6].to(device).float()
        tgt = target.to(device).float()
        out = model(pre, post, tgt)
        loss = BCEDiceLoss(out, tgt)
        hg = None
        if v["hgsl"]:
            smaps = model.hgsl_score_maps()
            hg = hgsl_loss(smaps, tgt, args.hgsl_lambda, stages=HGSL_STAGES_DEFAULT)
            if hg is None:
                log("[DRY][P0-FAIL] hgsl=1 但 score_maps 为空")
                logf.close(); return 2
            loss = loss + hg
        opt.zero_grad(); loss.backward()
        # 机制自检：γ 是否真的拿到梯度（否则"注入"这一路等于没接上）
        gdet = (float(model.hqkv_op.gamma_detail.grad.abs().sum())
                if (model.hqkv_op is not None and model.hqkv_op.gamma_detail.grad is not None)
                else 0.0)
        opt.step()
        finite = bool(torch.isfinite(loss).item())
        losses.append(float(loss.item()))
        if hg is not None:
            hgsls.append(float(hg.item()))
        step_rows.append({"step": step + 1, "loss": losses[-1], "finite": finite,
                          "hgsl": (float(hg.item()) if hg is not None else None),
                          "gamma_detail_grad": gdet,
                          "lr": [float(pg["lr"]) for pg in opt.param_groups]})
        log(f"[DRY] step={step+1} loss={losses[-1]:.6f} finite={finite} "
            f"hgsl={step_rows[-1]['hgsl']} gamma_detail_grad={gdet:.4e} "
            f"lr={['%.3e' % x for x in step_rows[-1]['lr']]}")
    if model.hqkv_op is not None:
        diag = model.hqkv_diag()
        log(f"[DRY] hqkv_diag={json.dumps(diag, default=str)}")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 真实 batch 折叠门
    torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32 = False
    torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32 = False
    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
    model.eval()
    with torch.no_grad():
        y_tr = model(pre0, post0)
    dep = copy.deepcopy(model); dep.eval(); dep.switch_to_deploy()
    with torch.no_grad():
        y_dp = dep(pre0, post0)
    dis = float(((y_tr > 0.5) != (y_dp > 0.5)).float().mean())
    err = float((y_tr - y_dp).abs().max())
    dep_params = npar(dep)
    # ---- 刀锋像素定位（与 tools/archive/run7_fold_knife_edge.py 的判据**同一定义**）
    # `fold_disagreement_zero` 是**硬门**；但当它非零时不能直接判"实现错误"，必须先区分：
    #   KNIFE_EDGE_ARTIFACT ⇔ 存在翻转 且 **所有**翻转像素的 |p_train − 0.5| 都 < 本次折叠的最大绝对误差
    #   ⇒ 翻转由「该像素在阈值上没有 margin、二值化本就不适定」造成，而非折叠实现错误。
    # Run7 已用这个判据把 4 次折叠门异常全部判定为 KNIFE_EDGE_ARTIFACT（预注册 §7.6 有披露）。
    flip = (y_tr > 0.5) != (y_dp > 0.5)
    n_flip = int(flip.sum())
    margin_tr = (y_tr - 0.5).abs()
    fm = float(margin_tr[flip].max()) if n_flip else None
    knife_edge = bool(n_flip > 0 and fm is not None and fm < err)
    verdict = ("NO_FLIP" if n_flip == 0 else
               ("KNIFE_EDGE_ARTIFACT" if knife_edge else "NEEDS_REVIEW"))
    log(f"[DRY] real-batch fold (TF32 off, deterministic): disagreement={dis} "
        f"max|d|={err:.3e} deploy_params={dep_params}")
    log(f"[DRY] fold knife-edge: n_flip_px={n_flip} flipped_margin_max={fm} "
        f"max_abs_diff={err:.3e} verdict={verdict}")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 梯度覆盖
    zero_grad = [n for n, p in model.named_parameters()
                 if p.requires_grad and (p.grad is None or float(p.grad.abs().sum()) == 0.0)]
    log(f"[DRY] zero-grad params n={len(zero_grad)} first={zero_grad[:8]}")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- batch32 显存
    peak_mb = None
    if device.startswith("cuda"):
        try:
            torch.cuda.reset_peak_memory_stats()
            model.train()
            out = model(pre0, post0, t0v)
            loss = BCEDiceLoss(out, t0v)
            if v["hgsl"]:
                hg2 = hgsl_loss(model.hgsl_score_maps(), t0v, args.hgsl_lambda)
                if hg2 is not None:
                    loss = loss + hg2
            opt.zero_grad(); loss.backward(); opt.step()
            peak_mb = torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated() / (1024 ** 2)
            log(f"[DRY] batch{args.batch_size} forward+backward OK peak_allocated={peak_mb:.0f} MB")
            del out, loss
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
        except torch.cuda.OutOfMemoryError:
            log("[DRY][FAIL] batch32 OOM -> 同卡 2 并发不可行（不许降 batch，改同卡串行）")
            peak_mb = -1.0

    summary = {
        "variant": args.variant, "spec": v, "device": device, "steps": args.steps,
        "batch_size": args.batch_size, "seed": args.seed,
        "str_dim": args.str_dim, "hqkv_rank": args.hqkv_rank,
        "param_breakdown": pb,
        "train_params": npar(model), "deploy_params": dep_params,
        "losses": losses, "hgsl_losses": hgsls, "steps_detail": step_rows,
        "epoch0_real_batch_equal_vs_ctrl": bool(eq0), "epoch0_max_abs_diff": d0,
        "fold_disagreement_real": dis, "fold_max_abs_diff_real": err,
        "fold_knife_edge": {"n_flip_px": n_flip, "flipped_margin_max": fm,
                            "max_abs_diff": err, "verdict": verdict},
        "zero_grad_params": zero_grad,
        "peak_allocated_MB_batch32": peak_mb,
        "pretrain_load": {k: (v2 if k != "loaded_keys" else len(v2))
                          for k, v2 in st.items()},
        "hqkv_report": (model.hqkv_report() if model.hqkv_op is not None else None),
        "hqkv_diag": (model.hqkv_diag() if model.hqkv_op is not None else None),
        "gates": {
            "pretrain_bitwise_load": bool(pre_ok),
            "all_losses_finite": bool(all(s["finite"] for s in step_rows)),
            "epoch0_bitwise_identity": bool(eq0),
            "fold_disagreement_zero": bool(dis == 0.0),
            "fold_knife_edge_artifact": bool(dis == 0.0 or knife_edge),
            "_fold_gate_note": ("`fold_disagreement_zero` 是硬门；`fold_knife_edge_artifact` 是"
                                "Run7 预注册 §7.6 认可的等价判据（翻转像素全部无阈值 margin）。"
                                "两者任一为真即算过门，但**必须在报告里写明走的是哪一条**。"),
            "gamma_gets_gradient": bool((not v["hqkv"]) or
                                        any(s["gamma_detail_grad"] > 0 for s in step_rows)),
            "non_backbone_params_small": bool(pb["non_backbone"] <= 4_000_000),
        },
    }
    with open(os.path.join(args.output_dir, "dry_run_summary.json"), "w",
              encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(summary, f, indent=2, default=str)
    ok = all(summary["gates"].values())
    log(f"[DRY] GATES={json.dumps(summary['gates'])} -> {'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'}")
    logf.close()
    return 0 if ok else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

================================================================================
# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/smoke_test.py
================================================================================
"""Smoke test for CASA-CD (baseline / CASAA / SAA / ORACLE modes, no real data needed).

Builds ChangeViT-Tiny, loads the pretrained DeiT-Tiny weights, and runs the mechanism
test suite (Run1 设计文档 §8.8 + Run2 决策文档 §13):

  T0 qkv sliced equivalence: CASAAAttention 的切片投影（full X 只算 Q、压缩上下文只算
     K/V）必须与整段 fused qkv 后切片数学等价（<1e-6）。
  T1 full-network smoke:   3-step forward+backward on random 256x256 bi-temporal input,
                           params + FLOPs (per mode).
  T2 baseline equivalence: with --mode baseline, new vit.forward_pair(x1, x2) must be
                           identical (eval, <1e-6) to vit(x1), vit(x2).
  T3 pretrained keys:      CASAA must not add any missing/unexpected key vs baseline
                           (qkv/proj in-place inheritance of DeiT-Tiny weights).
  T4 CASAA mechanism:      output shape, token budget (N=256, K=64, Kc=32, Kb=32),
                           T1/T2 swap symmetry of routing, gradients finite & nonzero.
  T5 oracle router:        GT patch occupancy -> 256 scores；TopK 正确；不进入梯度；
                           T1/T2 swap 不改变 routing（DIAGNOSTIC-ONLY）。

Usage:
    python smoke_test.py --model_type tiny --pretrained_weight_path <deit_tiny.pth> \
        [--gpu_id 0] [--mode all|baseline|casaa|saa|oracle]
"""
import os
import sys
import argparse
from functools import partial

import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F

_MODELS_ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if _MODELS_ROOT not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, _MODELS_ROOT)

from model.trainer import Trainer
from model.encoder import DinoVisionTransformer
from model.layers import MemEffAttention, CASAAAttention, rank_normalize_per_image
from model.layers.block import NestedTensorBlock as Block
from model.utils import BCEDiceLoss
from model.psd_detail import PSDDetail
from model.resnet import resnet18
from model.mobile_detail import MobileDetail
from torchvision.models import mobilenet_v3_small

CASAA_LAYERS = [8, 9, 10, 11]
CASAA_KEEP = 0.25
CASAA_SHARE = 0.5


def measure_params(model):
    return sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())


def measure_effective_params(model):
    """Params excluding unused ResNet head (layer4 + fc + avgpool never run in forward)."""
    total = measure_params(model)
    dead = 0
    for name, mod in model.named_modules():
        if name in ("encoder.resnet.layer4", "encoder.resnet.fc", "encoder.resnet.avgpool"):
            dead += sum(p.numel() for p in mod.parameters())
    return total - dead


def measure_flops(model, size=256):
    from fvcore.nn import flop_count

    model.eval()
    pre = torch.randn(1, 3, size, size).cuda()
    post = torch.randn(1, 3, size, size).cuda()
    label = torch.zeros(1, 1, size, size).cuda()
    with torch.no_grad():
        counts, unsupported = flop_count(model, (pre, post, label))
    return sum(counts.values()), len(unsupported)


def build_trainer(pretrained_path, mode, device, router=None, vit_depth=12):
    if mode == "oracle":
        trainer_mode, router = "casaa", "oracle"
    elif mode == "detail":
        trainer_mode, router = "casaa", "detail"
    elif mode == "detail_fused":
        trainer_mode, router = "casaa", "detail_fused"
    else:
        trainer_mode = mode
        if router is None:
            router = "content" if mode == "saa" else "change"
    return Trainer("tiny", pretrained_path=pretrained_path, resnet_pretrained=False,
                   mode=trainer_mode, casaa_layers=CASAA_LAYERS, casaa_keep_ratio=CASAA_KEEP,
                   casaa_change_share=CASAA_SHARE, casaa_router=router,
                   vit_depth=vit_depth).float().to(device)


def build_vit(casaa_layers):
    return DinoVisionTransformer(
        img_size=256, patch_size=16, embed_dim=192, depth=12, num_heads=6,
        mlp_ratio=4, block_fn=partial(Block, attn_class=MemEffAttention),
        num_register_tokens=0, casaa_layers=casaa_layers,
        casaa_keep_ratio=CASAA_KEEP, casaa_change_share=CASAA_SHARE, casaa_router="change")


def load_vit_keys(vit, pretrained_path):
    """Same load procedure as Encoder.__init__; returns (missing, unexpected) sets."""
    sd = torch.load(pretrained_path, map_location="cpu")["model"]
    for k in ["pos_embed", "patch_embed.proj.weight"]:
        sd.pop(k, None)
    msg = vit.load_state_dict(sd, strict=False)
    return set(msg.missing_keys), set(msg.unexpected_keys)


def t0_qkv_sliced_equivalence(device):
    """Smoke-0（Run2 §13）：切片 qkv 投影必须与整段 qkv 后切片数学等价。"""
    print("[T0] qkv sliced projection equivalence (full X -> Q only, context -> K/V only)")
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    B, N, C, H = 2, 256, 192, 6
    x1 = torch.randn(B, N, C, device=device)
    x2 = torch.randn(B, N, C, device=device)

    def ref_forward_pair(attn, xa, xb):
        """参考实现：整段 fused qkv 后切片（Run1 旧逻辑）。"""
        with torch.no_grad():
            Ca, Cb = attn.compute_contexts(xa, xb)
            Kref = Ca.shape[1]
            qkv1 = attn.qkv(xa).reshape(B, N, 3, H, C // H).permute(2, 0, 3, 1, 4)
            qkv2 = attn.qkv(xb).reshape(B, N, 3, H, C // H).permute(2, 0, 3, 1, 4)
            qkv1c = attn.qkv(Ca).reshape(B, Kref, 3, H, C // H).permute(2, 0, 3, 1, 4)
            qkv2c = attn.qkv(Cb).reshape(B, Kref, 3, H, C // H).permute(2, 0, 3, 1, 4)
            q1, k1, v1 = qkv1[0] * attn.scale, qkv1[1], qkv1[2]
            q2, k2, v2 = qkv2[0] * attn.scale, qkv2[1], qkv2[2]
            _, k1c, v1c = qkv1c
            _, k2c, v2c = qkv2c
            y1 = ((q1 @ k1c.transpose(-2, -1)).softmax(-1) @ v1c).transpose(1, 2).reshape(B, N, C)
            y2 = ((q2 @ k2c.transpose(-2, -1)).softmax(-1) @ v2c).transpose(1, 2).reshape(B, N, C)
        return attn.proj_drop(attn.proj(y1)), attn.proj_drop(attn.proj(y2))

    for router in ("change", "content"):
        attn = CASAAAttention(dim=C, num_heads=H, qkv_bias=True, router=router).to(device).eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            y1, y2 = attn.forward_pair(x1, x2)
