"""Native pyramid, matching common initialization, task gradients and folding."""
import torch

from model.casa_net import CASANet
from train_CASA import context_decay_names, training_losses, make_optimizer, verify_deployment


def main():
    torch.set_num_threads(4)
    torch.manual_seed(2333)
    a,b=torch.randn(1,3,256,256),torch.randn(1,3,256,256)
    target=torch.randint(0,2,(1,1,256,256)).float()
    reference=None
    for variant in ('none','hyperbolic','qkv'):
        torch.manual_seed(2333)
        model=CASANet(module=variant).eval()
        count=sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())
        with torch.no_grad():
            encoded=model.encoder(a)[1:]
            assert [tuple(x.shape[1:]) for x in encoded]==[(24,64,64),(32,32,32),(96,16,16),(320,8,8)]
            logits,aux=model(a,b,return_aux=True)
            outputs=model(a,b)
            assert len(logits)==len(outputs)==len(aux['guide_logits'])==4
            assert all(x.shape==target.shape for x in logits)
            assert all(torch.equal(z.sigmoid(),p) for z,p in zip(logits,outputs))
            assert torch.equal(model.predict(a,b),outputs[0])
            assert all(torch.isfinite(x).all() for x in model(a,a))
            if reference is None:
                reference=outputs[0]
            else:
                assert torch.equal(reference,outputs[0]), 'common initialization differs'
        model.train()
        optimizer=make_optimizer(model)
        context_ids={id(p) for p in model.context.parameters()}
        projection_ids={id(p) for n,p in model.named_parameters() if n in context_decay_names(model)}
        grouped=[id(p) for group in optimizer.param_groups for p in group['params']]
        assert len(grouped)==len(set(grouped))==len(list(model.parameters()))
        for group in optimizer.param_groups:
            assert group['lr_scale']==1.
            assert all(group['weight_decay']==(0. if id(p) in context_ids and id(p) not in projection_ids
                                               else 1e-4) for p in group['params'])
        for _ in range(3):
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            logits,aux=model(a,b,return_aux=True)
            loss,main,guide=training_losses(logits,aux,target)
            assert torch.allclose(loss,main+.2*guide)
            loss.backward()
            assert all(torch.isfinite(p.grad).all() for p in model.parameters() if p.grad is not None)
            optimizer.step()
        assert model.context.guide.weight.grad.abs().sum()>0
        if variant!='none':
            for layer in (model.context.qk,model.context.v,model.context.out,
                          model.context.local_dw,model.context.local_out):
                assert layer.weight.grad.abs().sum()>0
            assert (model.context.gamma_context.abs().sum(-1)>0).all()
            assert (model.context.gamma_detail.abs().sum(-1)>0).all()
        model.eval()
        _,report=verify_deployment(model,torch.cat((a,b),1))
        assert max(report['per_scale_max_diff'])<2e-4 and report['binary_disagreement']==0
        deployed=model.switch_to_deploy()
        print(variant,'train/deploy params',count,sum(p.numel() for p in deployed.parameters()),
              'guide/task gradients and folding PASS')


if __name__=='__main__':
    main()

================================================================================
# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/test_CASA_resume.py
================================================================================
"""Optimizer/RNG continuation and deployment backend-state checks."""
import copy
import random

import numpy as np
import torch
from torch import nn

from train_CASA import (PROTOCOL, make_optimizer, restore_training_state,
                        rng_state, strict_fold_precision, update_learning_rate,
                        learning_rate, context_decay_names)


class SmallModel(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.module = "qkv"
        self.encoder = nn.Linear(3, 3)
        self.body = nn.Linear(3, 3)
        self.context = nn.ModuleDict({"qk": nn.Linear(3, 3, bias=False),
                                      "norm": nn.LayerNorm(3),
                                      "v": nn.Linear(3, 3, bias=False),
                                      "out": nn.Linear(3, 3, bias=False),
                                      "guide": nn.Linear(3, 3)})

    def forward(self, x):
        x = self.context["norm"](self.encoder(x))
        return self.context["out"](self.context["qk"](self.body(x)) + self.context["v"](x)) + self.context["guide"](x)


def step(model, optimizer):
    x = torch.randn(4, 3) + random.random() + float(np.random.random())
    optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
    model(x).square().mean().backward()
    optimizer.step()


def backend_state():
    return (torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark, torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32,
            torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32, torch.get_float32_matmul_precision())


def check_decay_step():
    model = SmallModel().double()
    optimizer = make_optimizer(model)
    with torch.no_grad():
        for parameter in model.parameters():
            parameter.fill_(1.0)
            parameter.grad = torch.zeros_like(parameter)
    optimizer.step()
    lr, decay, eps = 5e-4, 1e-4, 1e-8
    expected_context = 1 - lr * decay
    expected_body = 1 - lr * decay / (decay + eps)
    torch.testing.assert_close(model.context["qk"].weight,
        torch.full_like(model.context["qk"].weight, expected_context), rtol=0, atol=1e-12)
    torch.testing.assert_close(model.body.weight,
        torch.full_like(model.body.weight, expected_body), rtol=0, atol=1e-12)
    torch.testing.assert_close(model.encoder.weight,
        torch.full_like(model.encoder.weight, expected_body), rtol=0, atol=1e-12)
    assert torch.equal(model.context["norm"].weight, torch.ones_like(model.context["norm"].weight))
    assert torch.equal(model.context["guide"].bias, torch.ones_like(model.context["guide"].bias))


def main():
    check_decay_step()
    torch.manual_seed(2333)
    np.random.seed(2333)
    random.seed(2333)
    model = SmallModel()
    optimizer = make_optimizer(model)
    decay = {id(p): group["weight_decay"] for group in optimizer.param_groups for p in group["params"]}
    scales = {id(p): group["lr_scale"] for group in optimizer.param_groups for p in group["params"]}
    decay_names = context_decay_names(model)
    decoupled = {id(p): group["decoupled_weight_decay"]
                 for group in optimizer.param_groups for p in group["params"]}
    for name, parameter in model.named_parameters():
        expected_decay = 0.0 if name.startswith("context.") and name not in decay_names else 1e-4
        assert decay[id(parameter)] == expected_decay
        assert scales[id(parameter)] == 1.0
        assert decoupled[id(parameter)] == (name in decay_names)
        if name.startswith("context.norm.") or name == "context.guide.bias":
            assert decay[id(parameter)] == 0.0
        elif name.endswith(".weight"):
            assert decay[id(parameter)] == 1e-4
    for count in (0, 185, 199, 200, 20000, 39999):
        rate = learning_rate(5e-4, 1, count, 40000)
        update_learning_rate(optimizer, rate)
        assert optimizer.param_groups[0]["lr"] == rate
        assert all(group["lr"] == rate for group in optimizer.param_groups[1:])
    for _ in range(4):
        step(model, optimizer)
    checkpoint = copy.deepcopy(dict(
        epoch=2, step=4, state_dict=model.state_dict(), optimizer=optimizer.state_dict(),
        manifest=dict(protocol=PROTOCOL, module="qkv", actual_steps=6, encoder_lr_mult=1.0), rng_state=rng_state()))
    for _ in range(2):
        step(model, optimizer)
    restored = SmallModel()
    restored_optimizer = make_optimizer(restored)
    epoch, count, exact_rng = restore_training_state(checkpoint, restored, restored_optimizer, 6, 2)
    assert (epoch, count, exact_rng) == (2, 4, True)
    for _ in range(2):
        step(restored, restored_optimizer)
    assert all(torch.equal(a, b) for a, b in zip(model.parameters(), restored.parameters()))
    assert all(int(state["step"]) == 6 for state in restored_optimizer.state.values())
    checkpoint.pop("rng_state")
    epoch, count, exact_rng = restore_training_state(checkpoint, restored, restored_optimizer, 6, 2)
    assert (epoch, count, exact_rng) == (2, 4, False)
    assert all(torch.equal(restored.state_dict()[key], value)
               for key, value in checkpoint["state_dict"].items())
    before = backend_state()
    try:
        with strict_fold_precision():
            assert backend_state() == (False, False, False, "highest")
            raise ValueError("exercise context exit")
    except ValueError:
        pass
    assert backend_state() == before
    print("Encoder LR, decoupled context/coupled body decay, optimizer/RNG continuation and backend restoration PASS")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/test_context_geometry.py
================================================================================
"""Lorentz geometry checked against Poincare and Klein coordinate identities."""
import torch

from model.layers.lorentz_ops import (
    acosh1p_squared, lift, normalize_sum, pairwise_squared_distance,
)


def poincare_distance_reference(space_q, space_k):
    """FP64 reference using direct chords in the Poincare ball."""
    q, k = space_q.double(), space_k.double()
    time_q = torch.sqrt(1 + (q * q).sum(-1, keepdim=True))
    time_k = torch.sqrt(1 + (k * k).sum(-1, keepdim=True))
    ball_q, ball_k = q / (time_q + 1), k / (time_k + 1)
    chord = torch.cdist(ball_q, ball_k, compute_mode="donot_use_mm_for_euclid_dist")
    denominator = torch.sqrt(
        (1 - ball_q.square().sum(-1)).unsqueeze(-1)
        * (1 - ball_k.square().sum(-1)).unsqueeze(-2))
    return (2 * torch.asinh(chord / denominator)).square()


def klein_centroid_reference(points, weights):
    """Einstein barycenter in Klein coordinates, converted through the ball."""
    time, space = points[..., :1], points[..., 1:]
    klein = space / time
    weighted_mass = weights.unsqueeze(-1) * time.unsqueeze(-3)
    center = (weighted_mass * klein.unsqueeze(-3)).sum(-2) / weighted_mass.sum(-2)
    ball = center / (1 + torch.sqrt(1 - center.square().sum(-1, keepdim=True)))
    denominator = 1 - ball.square().sum(-1, keepdim=True)
    return torch.cat(((1 + ball.square().sum(-1, keepdim=True)) / denominator,
                      2 * ball / denominator), dim=-1)


def lift_radius_and_gradient():
    directions = torch.randn(7, 16, dtype=torch.float64)
    directions = directions / directions.norm(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
    lengths = torch.tensor([0., .01, .25, 1., 3., 10., 30.],
                           dtype=torch.float64, requires_grad=True)
    point = lift(lengths[:, None] * directions)
    unit = point[:, 0].square() - point[:, 1:].square().sum(-1)
    torch.testing.assert_close(unit, torch.ones_like(unit), atol=5e-13, rtol=0)
    radius = torch.asinh(point[:, 1:].norm(dim=-1))
    torch.testing.assert_close(radius, torch.asinh(lengths), atol=2e-15, rtol=2e-15)
    gradient = torch.autograd.grad(radius[1:].sum(), lengths)[0][1:]
    expected = 1 / torch.sqrt(1 + lengths.detach()[1:].square())
    torch.testing.assert_close(gradient, expected, atol=1e-14, rtol=1e-13)
    assert (gradient > 0).all(), "large-radius spatial inputs lost radial gradients"
    assert radius.max() - radius.min() > 4
    print("Lorentz unit norm, variable radius and non-clipped radial gradients PASS")


def centroid_identities():
    space = torch.randn(2, 4, 7, 16, dtype=torch.float64) * .7
    points = lift(space)
    weights = torch.rand(2, 4, 3, 7, dtype=torch.float64).softmax(-1)
    actual = normalize_sum(weights @ points)
    expected = klein_centroid_reference(points, weights)
    torch.testing.assert_close(actual, expected, atol=2e-13, rtol=2e-13)

    constant = points[..., :1, :].expand_as(points)
    actual = normalize_sum(weights @ constant)
    expected = points[..., :1, :].expand(-1, -1, 3, -1)
    torch.testing.assert_close(actual, expected, atol=2e-13, rtol=2e-13)

    selected = torch.nn.functional.one_hot(torch.tensor([1, 4, 6]), 7).double()
    actual = normalize_sum(selected @ points)
    torch.testing.assert_close(actual, points[..., [1, 4, 6], :], atol=2e-13, rtol=2e-13)

    opposite = torch.stack((space[..., 0, :], -space[..., 0, :]), dim=-2)
    center = normalize_sum(lift(opposite).mean(-2))
    origin = torch.zeros_like(center)
    origin[..., 0] = 1
    torch.testing.assert_close(center, origin, atol=1e-14, rtol=0)
    print("Lorentz centroids agree with Klein barycenters, constants and one-hot weights PASS")


def distance_references():
    q = torch.randn(2, 4, 11, 16, dtype=torch.float64) * 1.2
    k = torch.randn(2, 4, 7, 16, dtype=torch.float64) * .8
    actual = pairwise_squared_distance(lift(q), lift(k))
    expected = poincare_distance_reference(q, k)
    torch.testing.assert_close(actual, expected, atol=2e-12, rtol=2e-12)

    radial_q = torch.tensor([0., .1, .8, 2., 4.], dtype=torch.float64)
    radial_k = torch.tensor([.03, .4, 1.3, 3.], dtype=torch.float64)
    a = torch.zeros(5, 16, dtype=torch.float64)
    b = torch.zeros(4, 16, dtype=torch.float64)
    a[:, 0], b[:, 0] = radial_q.sinh(), radial_k.sinh()
    expected = (radial_q[:, None] - radial_k[None, :]).square()
    actual = pairwise_squared_distance(lift(a), lift(b))
    torch.testing.assert_close(actual, expected, atol=2e-11, rtol=2e-11)
    print("FP64 squared distances agree with Poincare chords and radial geodesics PASS")


def float32_distance_range():
    lengths = torch.tensor([0., 1e-4, .05, .5, 1., 3., 10., 30.])
    q = torch.randn(2, 4, len(lengths), 16)
    k = torch.randn_like(q)
    q = q / q.norm(dim=-1, keepdim=True) * lengths[None, None, :, None]
    k = k / k.norm(dim=-1, keepdim=True) * lengths[None, None, :, None]
    cases = [(q, k), (q, q), (q, q + torch.randn_like(q) * 1e-3)]
    maximum_error = 0.
    for left, right in cases:
        actual = pairwise_squared_distance(lift(left), lift(right))
        expected = poincare_distance_reference(left, right)
        maximum_error = max(maximum_error, float((actual.double() - expected).abs().max()))
        assert torch.isfinite(actual).all() and (actual >= 0).all()
        torch.testing.assert_close(actual.double(), expected, atol=2e-3, rtol=3e-5)
    print(f"FP32 spatial norm 0..30 squared-distance max absolute error={maximum_error:.3e} PASS")


def small_distance_limit():
    z = torch.cat((torch.tensor([0.], dtype=torch.float64),
                   torch.logspace(-14, -2, 25, dtype=torch.float64),
                   torch.tensor([1e-3 - 1e-9, 1e-3, 1e-3 + 1e-9], dtype=torch.float64)))
    z.requires_grad_()
    actual = acosh1p_squared(z)
    expected = 4 * torch.asinh(torch.sqrt(z.detach() / 2)).square()
    torch.testing.assert_close(actual, expected, atol=3e-15, rtol=2e-13)
    derivative = torch.autograd.grad(actual.sum(), z)[0]
    assert derivative[0] == 2 and torch.isfinite(derivative).all()
    positive_z = z.detach()[1:]
    expected_derivative = (4 * torch.asinh(torch.sqrt(positive_z / 2))
                           / torch.sqrt(positive_z * (positive_z + 2)))
    torch.testing.assert_close(derivative[1:], expected_derivative, atol=2e-12, rtol=2e-12)
    print("Squared acosh analytic value, derivative and branch-boundary limits PASS")


def distance_and_centroid_gradients():
    q = (torch.randn(1, 2, 3, dtype=torch.float64) * .6).requires_grad_()
    k = (torch.randn(1, 3, 3, dtype=torch.float64) * .6).requires_grad_()
    assert torch.autograd.gradcheck(lambda a, b: pairwise_squared_distance(lift(a), lift(b)),
                                    (q, k), eps=1e-6, atol=2e-6, rtol=2e-4)
    logits = torch.randn(1, 2, 3, dtype=torch.float64, requires_grad=True)
    assert torch.autograd.gradcheck(lambda points, scores: normalize_sum(scores.softmax(-1) @ lift(points)),
                                    (k, logits), eps=1e-6, atol=2e-6, rtol=2e-4)

    for radius in (0., .5, 3., 30.):
        source = torch.randn(1, 2, 5, 16)
        source = source / source.norm(dim=-1, keepdim=True) * radius
        for perturbation in (0., 1e-6, .01):
            a = source.clone().requires_grad_()
            b = (source + perturbation * torch.randn_like(source)).requires_grad_()
            distance = pairwise_squared_distance(lift(a), lift(b))
            distance.sum().backward()
            assert torch.isfinite(a.grad).all() and torch.isfinite(b.grad).all()
            if radius == 0 and perturbation == 0:
                assert distance.count_nonzero() == 0
                assert a.grad.count_nonzero() == 0 and b.grad.count_nonzero() == 0
    print("Noncoincident distance/centroid gradcheck and FP32 coincident gradients PASS")


def euclidean_origin_limit():
    q = torch.randn(2, 3, 5, 16, dtype=torch.float64)
    k = torch.randn(2, 3, 7, 16, dtype=torch.float64)
    euclidean = torch.cdist(q, k, compute_mode="donot_use_mm_for_euclid_dist").square()
    scale = 1e-4
    intrinsic = pairwise_squared_distance(lift(scale * q), lift(scale * k)) / scale ** 2
    torch.testing.assert_close(intrinsic, euclidean, atol=2e-5, rtol=2e-6)
    print("Lorentz spatial lift has the Euclidean metric at the origin PASS")


def main():
    torch.set_num_threads(4)
    torch.manual_seed(4701)
    lift_radius_and_gradient()
    centroid_identities()
    distance_references()
    float32_distance_range()
    small_distance_limit()
    distance_and_centroid_gradients()
    euclidean_origin_limit()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/test_data_pipeline.py
================================================================================
"""RGB/date ordering, synchronized augmentation and stable binary loss checks."""
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

import cv2
import numpy as np
import torch
from torch.nn import functional as F

from dataset import casa_transforms as transforms
from train_CASA import (MEAN, STD, learning_rate, loss_function, make_loader,
                        mask_boundary, training_losses, SpatialMetrics)


def main():
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
        root = Path(directory)
        for name in ("A", "B", "label", "list"):
            (root / name).mkdir()
        a = np.full((256, 256, 3), (17, 83, 211), dtype=np.uint8)
        b = np.full_like(a, (199, 67, 29))
        mask = np.zeros((256, 256), dtype=np.uint8)
        mask[100:180, 130:210] = 255
        cv2.imwrite(str(root / "A" / "pair.png"), cv2.cvtColor(a, cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR))
        cv2.imwrite(str(root / "B" / "pair.png"), cv2.cvtColor(b, cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR))
        cv2.imwrite(str(root / "label" / "pair.png"), mask)
        (root / "list" / "test.txt").write_text("pair.png\n")
        image, target = next(iter(make_loader(root, "test", batch_size=1, workers=0)))
        expected = (torch.tensor([17, 83, 211, 199, 67, 29]) / 255 - torch.tensor(MEAN)) / torch.tensor(STD)
        assert torch.allclose(image[0, :, 0, 0], expected, atol=1e-6)
        assert torch.equal(target[0, 0], torch.from_numpy((mask >= 128).astype(np.int64)))

    mask = np.zeros((32, 32), dtype=np.uint8)
    mask[5:19, 12:25] = 255
    pair = np.stack((mask, mask, mask, 255-mask, 255-mask, 255-mask), -1)
    with patch.object(transforms.random, "random", return_value=0.0), \
            patch.object(transforms.random, "randrange", return_value=1):
        image, target = transforms.Compose([
            transforms.RandomFlip(), transforms.RandomRotate90(), transforms.RandomExchange()
        ])(pair, mask)
    assert np.array_equal(image[:, :, 0], 255-target)
    assert np.array_equal(image[:, :, 3], target)
    with patch.object(transforms.random, "random", return_value=0.0), \
            patch.object(transforms.random, "randint", side_effect=[1, 2, 3, 4]):
        cropped, crop_target = transforms.RandomCropResize(8)(pair, mask)
    assert np.array_equal(crop_target, cv2.resize(mask[1:-2, 3:-4], (32, 32), interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST))
    assert np.array_equal(cropped, cv2.resize(pair[1:-2, 3:-4], (32, 32)))

    logits = torch.tensor([[[[-100.0, 100.0]]]], requires_grad=True)
    target = torch.tensor([[[[1.0, 0.0]]]])
    loss = loss_function((logits,), target)
    loss.backward()
    assert torch.isfinite(loss) and torch.isfinite(logits.grad).all()
    assert (logits.grad.abs() > 0.4).all()
    moderate = torch.tensor([[[[-2.0, 1.0]]]])
    probability = moderate.sigmoid()
    expected = F.binary_cross_entropy(probability, target) + 1 - (
        2 * (probability * target).sum() + 1e-5) / (probability.sum() + target.sum() + 1e-5)
    assert torch.allclose(loss_function((moderate,), target), expected)
    assert learning_rate(5e-4, 1, 185, 40145) == 5e-4 * .9 * 186 / 200 + 5e-5

    for value in (0.0, 1.0):
        constant = torch.full((1, 1, 16, 16), value)
        assert mask_boundary(constant).count_nonzero() == 0
    target = torch.zeros(1, 1, 16, 16)
    target[:, :, 4:12, 5:13] = 1
    edge = mask_boundary(target)
    assert edge[0, 0, 3:13, 4:14].sum() == 64
    assert edge[:, :, 0].sum() == 0 and edge[:, :, -1].sum() == 0
    assert F.adaptive_max_pool2d(edge, (4, 4)).sum() > 0
    aux = dict(guide_logits=tuple(torch.zeros(1, 1, side, side, requires_grad=True)
                                 for side in (8, 4, 2, 1)))
    outputs = tuple(torch.zeros_like(target, requires_grad=True) for _ in range(4))
    total, main_loss, guide = training_losses(outputs, aux, target)
    assert torch.equal(total, main_loss + .2 * guide)
    total.backward()
    assert all(z.grad is not None and torch.isfinite(z.grad).all() and z.grad.abs().sum() > 0
               for z in (*outputs, *aux["guide_logits"]))

    metric = SpatialMetrics(torch.device("cpu"))
    metric.update(target > 0, target)
    scores = metric.scores()
    assert scores["boundary_1px"]["F1"] == 1 and scores["boundary_2px"]["F1"] == 1
    assert scores["interior"]["recall"] == 1 and scores["interior"]["target_pixels"] == 16
    shifted = torch.roll(target, shifts=1, dims=-1)
    metric = SpatialMetrics(torch.device("cpu"))
    metric.update(shifted > 0, target)
    assert metric.scores()["boundary_1px"]["F1"] == 1
    metric = SpatialMetrics(torch.device("cpu"))
    metric.update(torch.zeros_like(target).bool(), target)
    assert metric.scores()["boundary_1px"]["F1"] == 0
    assert metric.scores()["interior"]["recall"] == 0
    small = torch.zeros_like(target)
    small[..., 8, 8] = 1
    metric = SpatialMetrics(torch.device("cpu"))
    metric.update(torch.zeros_like(small).bool(), small)
    assert metric.scores()["interior"]["target_pixels"] == 0
    assert metric.scores()["interior"]["recall"] is None
    print("RGB/date order, synchronized augmentation, stable losses, boundary targets/metrics and warmup PASS")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/test_hyperbolic_context.py

"""Occupancy radii, pooled-content keys, subtypes and independent adoption."""
import torch

from model.layers.hyperbolic_context import HyperbolicContext, subtype_assignments
from model.layers.pyramid_context import channel_norm, pool_features, project_tokens
from model.layers.lorentz_ops import pairwise_squared_distance


def compression_and_geometry():
    op = HyperbolicContext().double()
    p = torch.full((1, 1, 8, 8), .5, dtype=torch.double, requires_grad=True)
    appearance = torch.linspace(.05, .95, 64, dtype=torch.double).reshape_as(p).requires_grad_()
    x = torch.randn(1, 64, 8, 8, dtype=torch.double, requires_grad=True)
    weights, support = subtype_assignments(p, appearance, 1)
    assert weights.shape == (1, 1, 4, 64)
    assert torch.allclose(weights.sum(-1), torch.ones_like(weights[..., 0]))
    assert torch.allclose(support, torch.full_like(support, .5))
    prototype = pool_features(x, weights, 1)
    assert not torch.allclose(prototype[:, 0], prototype[:, 1])
    loss = prototype[:, 0].square().mean()
    gradients = torch.autograd.grad(loss, (x, p, appearance))
    assert all(torch.isfinite(g).all() and g.abs().sum() > 0 for g in gradients)
    sparse = torch.full_like(p, .05)
    sparse[..., 0, 0] = .6
    sparse_weights, _ = subtype_assignments(sparse, torch.full_like(p, .5), 1)
    assert .69 < sparse_weights[0, 0, 0, 0] < .70
    q = torch.randn(1, op.heads, 12, op.head_dim, dtype=torch.double)
    occupancy = torch.linspace(0, 1, 12, dtype=torch.double).reshape(1, 1, 12, 1)
    points = op.geometric_point(q, occupancy)
    radius = torch.asinh(points[..., 1:].norm(dim=-1))
    assert torch.allclose(radius, torch.asinh(1 + occupancy[..., 0]).expand_as(radius), atol=1e-12)
    assert torch.allclose(points, op.geometric_point(3 * q, occupancy), atol=1e-12)
    assert torch.allclose(points[..., 0].square() - points[..., 1:].square().sum(-1), torch.ones_like(radius), atol=2e-14)
    # K and V depend on the same P, without a separate region-scatter statistic.
    mean = torch.randn(1, 1, 64, dtype=torch.double)
    delta = torch.randn_like(mean)
    two = torch.cat((mean + delta, mean - delta), 1).mean(1, keepdim=True)
    large = torch.cat((mean + 5 * delta, mean - 5 * delta), 1).mean(1, keepdim=True)
    assert torch.allclose(op.project_keys(two)[0], op.project_keys(large)[0], atol=1e-12)
    assert torch.allclose(project_tokens(op.v, two, op.heads), project_tokens(op.v, large, op.heads), atol=1e-12)
    print('Occupancy radius, scatter invariance, sparse targets, two subtypes and gradients PASS')


def independent_adoption():
    op = HyperbolicContext().double()
    feature = torch.randn(1, 64, 2, 2, dtype=torch.double)
    p = op.guide(feature).sigmoid()
    query = op.project_queries(feature, p).flatten(2, 3)
    prototypes = torch.randn(1, 448, 64, dtype=torch.double)
    key, p_key = op.project_keys(prototypes)
    value = project_tokens(op.v, prototypes, op.heads)
    support = torch.ones(1, 448, dtype=torch.double)
    attention = op.attention(query, key, support, 0)
    update, gate, *_ = op.readout(feature, attention, value, p, p_key)
    with torch.no_grad():
        op.scale_bias.add_(7.)
    shifted = op.attention(query, key, support, 0)
    shifted_update, shifted_gate, *_ = op.readout(feature, shifted, value, p, p_key)
    assert torch.allclose(shifted, attention, atol=1e-14)
    assert torch.allclose(shifted_gate, gate, atol=1e-14)
    assert torch.allclose(update, shifted_update, atol=1e-14)
    # A single available fine-scale prototype is not divided by 256 at readout.
    rare = torch.zeros_like(attention)
    rare[..., 0] = 1
    with torch.no_grad():
        op.gate.weight.zero_()
        op.gate.bias.zero_()
    _, rare_gate, *_ = op.readout(feature, rare, value, p, p_key)
    assert torch.equal(rare_gate, torch.full_like(rare_gate, .5))
    radial_flat = HyperbolicContext.geometric_point(query[..., 1:], torch.full_like(query[..., :1], .5))
    assert (pairwise_squared_distance(query, key) - pairwise_squared_distance(radial_flat, key)).abs().max() > 1e-4
    print('Common logit shift invariance, rare-match adoption and radial sensitivity PASS')


def module():
    op = HyperbolicContext()
    sizes = (32, 16, 8, 4)
    tar = [torch.randn(1, 64, n, n, requires_grad=True) for n in sizes]
    output, _ = op(tar)
    assert all(torch.equal(a, b) for a, b in zip(tar, output))
    with torch.no_grad():
        op.gamma_context.fill_(.1)
        op.gamma_detail.fill_(.1)
    constant = torch.randn(1, 64, 1, 1)
    fields = [constant.expand(1, 64, n, n).clone() for n in sizes]
    output, _ = op(fields)
    assert all(torch.allclose(a, b, atol=3e-6) for a, b in zip(fields, output))
    zero = [torch.zeros_like(x) for x in tar]
    output, _ = op(zero)
    assert all(torch.equal(a, b) for a, b in zip(zero, output))
    op.collect_diag = True
    output, _ = op(tar)
    grads = torch.autograd.grad(output[0].square().mean(), tar, retain_graph=True)
    assert all(torch.isfinite(g).all() and g.abs().sum() > 0 for g in grads)
    sum(o.square().mean() for o in output).backward()
    for name, parameter in op.named_parameters():
        assert parameter.grad is not None and torch.isfinite(parameter.grad).all(), name
        assert parameter.grad.abs().sum() > 0, name
    for index, stage in enumerate(op.last_diag.values()):
        assert stage['contexts'] == 448 and stage['finite']
        assert 0 < stage['context_mass_mean'] < 1
        if sizes[index] > op.grid_sizes[index]:
            assert stage['subtype_separation_rms'] > 0
        assert stage['adoption_source'] == 'learned_value_correction_gate'
    print('Identity, constant/zero fields, all parameter and cross-scale gradients PASS')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    torch.set_num_threads(4)
    torch.manual_seed(2333)
    compression_and_geometry()
    independent_adoption()
    module()

# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/test_pyramid_context.py
================================================================================
"""Compression fidelity, constant fields and cross-scale task gradients."""
import torch

from model.layers.euclidean_context import EuclideanContext
from model.layers.pyramid_context import assignments, pool_features, highpass, regions


def compression_semantics():
    feature = torch.randn(1, 64, 8, 8, dtype=torch.double, requires_grad=True)
    probability = torch.zeros(1, 1, 8, 8, dtype=torch.double)
    probability[..., 0, 0] = 1
    weights, confidence = assignments(probability, 1)
    prototype = pool_features(feature, weights, 1)
    assert weights[0, 0, 0, 0] > .9999
    assert torch.allclose(prototype[:, 0], feature[..., 0, 0], atol=3e-4)
    assert torch.allclose(confidence, torch.ones_like(confidence), atol=1e-9, rtol=0), "sparse confident target lost prior mass"
    for value in (0., .01, .5, .99, 1.):
        weights, confidence = assignments(torch.full_like(probability, value), 1)
        assert torch.allclose(weights.sum(-1), torch.ones_like(weights[..., 0]))
        prototype = pool_features(feature, weights, 1)
        assert torch.allclose(prototype[:, 0], prototype[:, 1], atol=1e-12)
        expected = torch.tensor([[max(value**2, 1e-6), max((1-value)**2, 1e-6)]], dtype=torch.double)
        assert torch.allclose(confidence, expected, atol=1e-12)
    logits = torch.randn_like(probability, requires_grad=True)
    weights, _ = assignments(logits.sigmoid(), 2)
    pooled = pool_features(feature, weights, 2)
    grads = torch.autograd.grad(pooled.square().sum(), (feature, logits))
    assert all(torch.isfinite(g).all() and g.abs().sum() > 0 for g in grads)
    sparse = torch.full_like(probability,.05)
    sparse[...,0,0]=.6
    sharpened,_=assignments(sparse,1)
    assert .69 < sharpened[0,0,0,0] < .70
    operator = EuclideanContext().double()
    dense = operator.project_queries(feature)
    expected = torch.einsum("brtp,bhrpd->bhrtd", weights, regions(dense, 2)).flatten(2, 3)
    actual = operator.pool_keys(dense, pooled, weights, 2)
    assert torch.allclose(actual, expected, atol=1e-12), "pool/linear equivalence failed"
    print("sparse targets, unsupported classes, assignment gradients and pool/linear equivalence PASS")


def contexts():
    sizes = (32,16,8,4)
    for context_type in (EuclideanContext,):
        op = context_type()
        tar = [torch.randn(1,64,n,n,requires_grad=True) for n in sizes]
        output, aux = op(tar)
        assert all(torch.equal(x,y) for x,y in zip(output,tar))
        assert len(aux['guide_logits']) == 4
        with torch.no_grad():
            op.gamma_context.fill_(.1)
            op.gamma_detail.fill_(.1)
        constant = torch.randn(1,64,1,1)
        fields = [constant.expand(1,64,n,n).clone() for n in sizes]
        output, _ = op(fields)
        assert all(torch.allclose(x,y,atol=3e-6,rtol=1e-6) for x,y in zip(output,fields)), "constant field changed"
        assert highpass(torch.ones(1,64,8,8)).abs().max() == 0
        op.collect_diag = True
        output, _ = op(tar)
        gradients = torch.autograd.grad(output[0].square().mean(), tar, retain_graph=True)
        assert all(torch.isfinite(g).all() and g.abs().sum() > 0 for g in gradients), "cross-scale gradient missing"
        sum(x.square().mean() for x in output).backward()
        for name, parameter in op.named_parameters():
            assert parameter.grad is not None and torch.isfinite(parameter.grad).all(), name
            assert parameter.grad.abs().sum() > 0, name
        for i in range(4):
            diag=op.last_diag[f'stage{i+1}']
            assert diag['finite'] and diag['contexts']==224
            assert diag['context_residual_to_feature_rms']>0
            if i<2:
                assert diag['local_residual_to_feature_rms']>0
        # Identical distances and class confidence should not prefer the larger source grid.
        query=op.project_queries(torch.zeros(1,64,1,3)).flatten(2,3)
        key=op.project_queries(torch.zeros(1,64,1,224)).flatten(2,3)
        attention=op.attention(query,key,torch.ones(1,224),0)
        mass=torch.stack([p.sum(-1) for p in attention.split(op.tokens_per_scale,-1)],-1)
        assert torch.allclose(mass,torch.full_like(mass,.2),atol=1e-6)
        # With no matching prototype, the self candidate receives nearly all mass.
        remote=op.project_queries(torch.full((1,64,1,224),100.)).flatten(2,3)
        remote_attention=op.attention(query,remote,torch.ones(1,224),0)
        assert remote_attention.sum(-1).max()<1e-4
        # Uniformly ambiguous pairs have half the total prior of confident pairs.
        uncertain=op.attention(query,key,torch.full((1,224),.25),0).sum(-1)
        confident=op.attention(query,key,torch.tensor([1.,1e-6]).repeat(112)[None],0).sum(-1)
        assert torch.all(uncertain < confident)
        print(context_type.__name__, 'identity, constant preservation, all parameter/cross-scale gradients PASS')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    torch.set_num_threads(4)
    torch.manual_seed(2333)
    compression_semantics()
    contexts()

"""R4-FET1 验收测试 T0–T9（方案 §7）：逐项硬断言，不接受“基本一致”。

运行（服务器，GPU0 独占）：

    cd models
    python test_run4_fine_tap.py \
      --tinyvim-pretrained-weight-path ../pretrained_weight/tinyvim_s_1000e.pth \
      --device cuda:0 \
      --real-dataset-root /share_datasets/CD/SYSU-CD-256 \
      --real-test-list /share_datasets/CD/SYSU-CD-256/list/test.txt --limit 16 \
      --dry-run-json /home/yqwang/outputs/CASA-CD/CASA-TViM/Run4_DRY/E6_FET1/dry_run.json

设计纪律：
  * 每个断言独立计分；任一 FAIL ⇒ 进程退出码 1（FAIL STOP）。
  * 需要 CUDA selective-scan 内核的测试（全模型 forward）在无内核环境标 SKIP-NO-KERNEL，
    **在目标服务器上必须实测通过**，SKIP 不计入 PASS。
  * 不修改任何历史权重/日志；T9 的注入实验全部在临时目录进行。
"""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile

import torch

_MODELS_ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if _MODELS_ROOT not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, _MODELS_ROOT)

from model.casa_tvim_str_net import CASATViMSTRNet                       # noqa: E402
from model.str_fine_tap import FineEvidenceTap1x1, FET_FORM, FET_FUSE, FET_SOURCE  # noqa: E402
from train import (measure_params, measure_trainable_params, measure_flops,  # noqa: E402
                   source_code_identity, PROTOCOL_VERSION, SOURCE_IDENTITY_FILES)

EXPECT = {
    "ctrl_train": 5_084_017,
    "ctrl_deploy": 4_880_190,
    "fet_train": 5_097_938,
    "fet_deploy": 4_894_110,
    "fet_train_inc": 13_921,
    "fet_deploy_inc": 13_920,
    "fet_mac": 64 * 64 * 144 * 96,      # 56,623,104
    "budget": 5_000_000,
}

# T0：与未改动的 M1 原生 smoke（T-CA-2）逐字一致的预训练加载基线。
# 61 个键按 Slim 结构有意丢弃（Stage4=Localx3+final TViM），不是加载缺失。
EXPECT_RETAINED = 744
EXPECT_PRETRAINED_KEYS = 805

FOLD_FP64_TOL = 1e-10
FOLD_FP32_TOL = 2e-5
WHOLE_FP32_TOL = 2e-4


class Harness(object):
    """逐项验收记录器（FAIL STOP 语义）。"""

    def __init__(self, verbose=True):
        self.rows = []
        self.verbose = verbose

    def check(self, tid, name, ok, detail=""):
        status = "PASS" if ok else "FAIL"
        self.rows.append((tid, name, status, detail))
        if self.verbose:
            print(f"[{status}] {tid} {name}" + (f" :: {detail}" if detail else ""), flush=True)
        return bool(ok)

    def skip(self, tid, name, detail):
        self.rows.append((tid, name, "SKIP", detail))
        print(f"[SKIP] {tid} {name} :: {detail}", flush=True)
        return True

    def summary(self):
        print("\n=== R4 T0-T9 SUMMARY ===")
        for tid, name, status, detail in self.rows:
            print(f"{status:4s} | {tid:3s} | {name}" + (f" | {detail}" if detail else ""))
        n_fail = sum(1 for r in self.rows if r[2] == "FAIL")
        n_skip = sum(1 for r in self.rows if r[2] == "SKIP")
        n_pass = sum(1 for r in self.rows if r[2] == "PASS")
        print(f"--- PASS={n_pass} FAIL={n_fail} SKIP={n_skip} ---")
        return n_fail


def _sha256(path, nbytes=None):
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1 << 20), b""):
            h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest() if nbytes is None else h.hexdigest()[:nbytes]


def build_model(fine_tap, pretrained_path, device, seed=16):
    """严格复刻 train.py 的构造序列（encoder → head → tar → decoder → FET）。"""
    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    model = CASATViMSTRNet(
        pretrained_path, caacp=True, rep_mode="full", str_dim=96,
        caacp_score_mode="rank", frh=False, caacp_residual_mode="current",
        fs_tar=False, fine_tap=bool(fine_tap)).float()
    return model.to(device)


def _kernel_ok(fn):
    """执行 fn；若因缺少 selective-scan 内核失败则返回 ('NOKERNEL', msg)。"""
    try:
        return True, fn()
    except RuntimeError as e:
        if "selective scan kernel" in str(e) or "mamba" in str(e):
            return "NOKERNEL", str(e)
        raise


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- T0
def t0_identity(h, args, device):
    path = args.tinyvim_pretrained_weight_path
    if path is None or not os.path.isfile(path):
        h.check("T0", "pretrained weight present", False, f"missing {path}")
        return None
    base = os.path.basename(path)
    h.check("T0", "pretrained file is TinyViM-S 1000e (never a Run1 best ckpt)",
            base == "tinyvim_s_1000e.pth", base)
    sha_full = _sha256(path)
    print(f"[T0] pretrained sha256={sha_full}", flush=True)
    if args.source_identity_json and os.path.isfile(args.source_identity_json):
        with open(args.source_identity_json, encoding="utf-8") as f:
            expected = json.load(f)
        expected = expected.get("source_code_sha256", expected)
        cur = source_code_identity()
        bad = [k for k in cur if expected.get(k) not in (None, cur[k])]
        h.check("T0", "source code identity matches Run4 manifest", not bad,
                f"files={len(cur)} mismatched={bad}" if bad else f"files={len(cur)} all match")
    else:
        h.skip("T0", "source code identity vs manifest", "--source-identity-json not given (record manually)")

    model = build_model(1, path, device)
    ls = model.encoder.load_stats() or {}
    ok_load = (ls.get("retained") == EXPECT_RETAINED
               and ls.get("pretrained_keys") == EXPECT_PRETRAINED_KEYS
               and float(ls.get("worst_diff", 1.0)) == 0.0)
    h.check("T0", f"ImageNet backbone exact-loaded (retained=={EXPECT_RETAINED}/"
                  f"{EXPECT_PRETRAINED_KEYS}, worst_diff==0; 61 keys intentionally dropped by Slim)",
            ok_load,
            f"retained={ls.get('retained')}/{ls.get('pretrained_keys')} worst_diff={ls.get('worst_diff')} "
            f"dropped_intentional={len(ls.get('dropped_intentional', []))}")
    h.check("T0", "no FET key came from the pretrained file",
            all("fine_evidence_tap" not in k for k in (ls.get("loaded_keys") or [])),
            f"new_modules={len(ls.get('missing_new', []))}")
    return model, sha_full


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- T1
def t1_ctrl_params(h, args, device):
    model = build_model(0, args.tinyvim_pretrained_weight_path, device)
    tr = measure_params(model)
    ok_tr = (tr == EXPECT["ctrl_train"])
    h.check("T1", f"fine_tap=0 train params == {EXPECT['ctrl_train']:,}", ok_tr, f"got {tr:,}")
    h.check("T1", "fine_tap=0 registers no FET module/param",
            getattr(model, "fine_evidence_tap", None) is None
            and not any(k.startswith("fine_evidence_tap") for k in model.state_dict()))
    n_keys = len(model.state_dict())
    model.switch_to_deploy()
    dp = measure_params(model)
    h.check("T1", f"fine_tap=0 deploy params == {EXPECT['ctrl_deploy']:,}", dp == EXPECT["ctrl_deploy"], f"got {dp:,}")
    h.check("T1", "fine_tap=0 deploy graph carries no FET key",
            not any(k.startswith("fine_evidence_tap") for k in model.state_dict()),
            f"train_keys={n_keys} deploy_keys={len(model.state_dict())} (fold removes TAR/DCR branch keys by design)")
    return True


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- T2
def t2_epoch0(h, args, device):
    m0 = build_model(0, args.tinyvim_pretrained_weight_path, device)
    r0 = torch.get_rng_state().clone()
    m1 = build_model(1, args.tinyvim_pretrained_weight_path, device)
    r1 = torch.get_rng_state().clone()
    h.check("T2", "global CPU RNG state identical after both constructions", torch.equal(r0, r1))

    s0, s1 = m0.state_dict(), m1.state_dict()
    extra = sorted(k for k in s1 if k not in s0)
    missing = sorted(k for k in s0 if k not in s1)
    h.check("T2", "new keys are exactly fine_evidence_tap.*", not missing
            and all(k.startswith("fine_evidence_tap.") for k in extra), f"new={extra} missing={missing}")
    bad = [k for k in s0 if not torch.equal(s0[k], s1[k])]
    h.check("T2", "all pre-existing tensors bitwise identical (torch.equal)", not bad,
            f"n_keys={len(s0)} mismatched={bad[:4]}")
    gamma = m1.fet_gamma()
    dw = float(m1.fine_evidence_tap.diff.weight.abs().sum().item())
    h.check("T2", "gamma == 0 and W_diff == 0", gamma == 0.0 and dw == 0.0, f"gamma={gamma} |W_diff|_1={dw}")

    if args.device.startswith("cuda"):
        x = torch.randn(2, 3, 256, 256, device=device)
        m0.eval(); m1.eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            y0 = m0(x, x)
            y1 = m1(x, x)
        h.check("T2", "epoch-0 forward bitwise identical (torch.equal, max_abs==0)", torch.equal(y0, y1),
                f"max_abs={(y0 - y1).abs().max().item():.3e} disagree={((y0 > .5) != (y1 > .5)).float().mean().item():.3e}")
    else:
        h.skip("T2", "epoch-0 forward bitwise identical", "needs CUDA selective-scan kernel")
    return m1


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- T3
def t3_shapes(h, args, device):
    """FET 局部（无需内核）+ 全模型（需内核）形状契约。"""
    tap = FineEvidenceTap1x1(48, 96)
    for B in (1, 2, 16):
        P = torch.randn(B, 48, 64, 64)
        Q = torch.randn(B, 48, 64, 64)
        with torch.no_grad():
            Z = torch.cat([P, Q, (Q - P).abs()], dim=1)
            T = tap(P, Q)
        h.check("T3", f"local FET shapes B={B}",
                list(P.shape) == [B, 48, 64, 64] and list(Z.shape) == [B, 144, 64, 64]
                and list(T.shape) == [B, 96, 64, 64],
                f"P={list(P.shape)} Z={list(Z.shape)} T={list(T.shape)}")

    if not args.device.startswith("cuda"):
        h.skip("T3", "full-model shape contract", "needs CUDA selective-scan kernel")
        return
    model = build_model(1, args.tinyvim_pretrained_weight_path, device)
    model.eval()
    seen = {}

    def pre_hook(mod, inp):
        seen["P"], seen["Q"] = inp[0].shape, inp[1].shape

    def out_hook(mod, inp, out):
        seen["T"] = out.shape

    def head_hook(mod, inp):
        seen["Rprime"] = inp[0].shape

    hs = [model.fine_evidence_tap.register_forward_pre_hook(pre_hook),
          model.fine_evidence_tap.register_forward_hook(out_hook),
          model.head.register_forward_pre_hook(head_hook)]
    with torch.no_grad():
        y = model(torch.randn(2, 3, 256, 256, device=device), torch.randn(2, 3, 256, 256, device=device))
    for hh in hs:
        hh.remove()
    ok = (list(seen.get("P", [])) == [2, 48, 64, 64] and list(seen.get("Q", [])) == [2, 48, 64, 64]
          and list(seen.get("T", [])) == [2, 96, 64, 64] and list(seen.get("Rprime", [])) == [2, 96, 64, 64]
          and list(y.shape) == [2, 1, 256, 256])
    h.check("T3", "full-model shapes P/Q[2,48,64,64] T/R'[2,96,64,64] out[2,1,256,256]", ok, str(seen))


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- T4
def t4_fold(h, args, device):
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    tap = FineEvidenceTap1x1(48, 96)
    with torch.no_grad():
        tap.gamma.fill_(0.3)
        tap.diff.weight.normal_(0.0, 0.05)
        tap.pq.bias.add_(torch.randn(96) * 0.02)
    P = torch.randn(4, 48, 64, 64)
    Q = torch.randn(4, 48, 64, 64)
    D = (Q - P).abs()

    # (1) FP64 代数恒等式：两侧都在 FP64 上求值，检验 γ[W_pq,W_Δ] 拼核的正确性
    Wpq64 = tap.pq.weight.detach().double()
    bpq64 = tap.pq.bias.detach().double()
    Wd64 = tap.diff.weight.detach().double()
    g64 = tap.gamma.detach().double().reshape(())
    train_ref = g64 * (torch.nn.functional.conv2d(torch.cat([P, Q], dim=1).double(), Wpq64, bpq64)
                       + torch.nn.functional.conv2d(D.double(), Wd64, None))
    W, b = tap.get_equivalent_kernel_bias()
    h.check("T4", "fused kernel is 96x144x1x1 (FP64)", list(W.shape) == [96, 144, 1, 1] and W.dtype == torch.float64,
            f"shape={list(W.shape)} dtype={W.dtype}")
    fused_ref = torch.nn.functional.conv2d(torch.cat([P, Q, D], dim=1).double(), W, b.reshape(-1))
    err64 = (train_ref - fused_ref).abs().max().item()
    h.check("T4", f"FP64 algebraic fold max_abs < {FOLD_FP64_TOL:g}", err64 < FOLD_FP64_TOL, f"max_abs={err64:.3e}")

    # (2) FP32 子模块等价：训练图模块 vs 折叠后模块
    train_f32 = tap(P, Q)
    tap.switch_to_deploy()
    fused_out = tap(P, Q)
    err32 = (train_f32 - fused_out).abs().max().item()
    h.check("T4", f"FP32 submodule train<->deploy max_abs < {FOLD_FP32_TOL:g}", err32 < FOLD_FP32_TOL,
            f"max_abs={err32:.3e} disagree={((train_f32 > .5) != (fused_out > .5)).float().mean().item():.3e}")
    keys = set(tap.state_dict())
    h.check("T4", "pq/diff/gamma removed; single Conv(144->96,k1) remains",
            keys == {"fused.weight", "fused.bias"} and tap.fused.kernel_size == (1, 1)
            and tap.fused.in_channels == 144 and tap.fused.out_channels == 96, f"keys={sorted(keys)}")
    rep = tap.param_report()
    h.check("T4", f"deploy param increment == {EXPECT['fet_deploy_inc']:,}", rep["fused"] == EXPECT["fet_deploy_inc"],
            f"got {rep['fused']:,}")
    n_fold = tap.fold_count
    tap.switch_to_deploy()
    out2 = tap(P, Q)
    h.check("T4", "second switch_to_deploy is idempotent (no re-fold, same output)",
            tap.fold_count == n_fold and torch.equal(out2, fused_out), f"fold_count={tap.fold_count}")


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- T5
def t5_train_deploy(h, args, device, model=None):
    if not args.device.startswith("cuda"):
        h.skip("T5", "whole-model train<->deploy equivalence", "needs CUDA selective-scan kernel")
        return
    prev = (torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32, torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32,
            torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic)
    torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32 = False
    torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32 = False
    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
    try:
        m = build_model(1, args.tinyvim_pretrained_weight_path, device)
        with torch.no_grad():                        # 非平凡：γ、W_diff 开门
            m.fine_evidence_tap.gamma.fill_(0.3)
            m.fine_evidence_tap.diff.weight.normal_(0.0, 0.02)
        m.eval()
        torch.manual_seed(16)
        pre_fix = torch.randn(8, 3, 256, 256, device=device)
        post_fix = torch.randn(8, 3, 256, 256, device=device)
        real = _load_real_batch(args, device)
        with torch.no_grad():
            y_tr = m(pre_fix, post_fix)
            y_tr_real = m(real[0], real[1]) if real is not None else None
        m.switch_to_deploy()
        m.eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            y_dp = m(pre_fix, post_fix)
            y_dp_real = m(real[0], real[1]) if real is not None else None
        e = (y_tr - y_dp).abs().max().item()
        d = ((y_tr > 0.5) != (y_dp > 0.5)).float().mean().item()
        h.check("T5", f"random batch max_abs < {WHOLE_FP32_TOL:g} and binary disagreement == 0",
                e < WHOLE_FP32_TOL and d == 0.0, f"max_abs={e:.3e} disagree={d:.3e}")
        if y_tr_real is not None:
            er = (y_tr_real - y_dp_real).abs().max().item()
            dr = ((y_tr_real > 0.5) != (y_dp_real > 0.5)).float().mean().item()
            margin = (y_tr_real - 0.5).abs().min().item()
            h.check("T5", "real test batch (b=16) max_abs < 2e-4 and binary disagreement == 0",
                    er < WHOLE_FP32_TOL and dr == 0.0,
                    f"max_abs={er:.3e} disagree={dr:.3e} min|p-0.5|={margin:.3e}")
        else:
            h.skip("T5", "real test batch equivalence", "no --real-dataset-root/--real-test-list")
        dp = measure_params(m)
        h.check("T5", f"deploy graph total params == {EXPECT['fet_deploy']:,}", dp == EXPECT["fet_deploy"], f"got {dp:,}")
    finally:
        (torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32, torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32,
         torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic) = prev


def _load_real_batch(args, device):
    if not (args.real_dataset_root and args.real_test_list):
        return None
    if not (os.path.isdir(args.real_dataset_root) and os.path.isfile(args.real_test_list)):
        return None
    import dataset.dataset as myDataLoader
    import dataset.Transforms as myTransforms
    tf = myTransforms.Compose([
        myTransforms.Normalize(mean=[0.406, 0.456, 0.485, 0.406, 0.456, 0.485],
                               std=[0.225, 0.224, 0.229, 0.225, 0.224, 0.229]),
        myTransforms.Scale(args.inWidth if hasattr(args, "inWidth") else 256, 256),
        myTransforms.ToTensor(),
    ])
    ds = myDataLoader.Dataset(file_root=args.real_dataset_root, list_path=args.real_test_list, transform=tf)
    n = min(int(args.limit), len(ds))
    imgs = torch.stack([ds[i][0] for i in range(n)])
    return imgs[:, 0:3].to(device), imgs[:, 3:6].to(device)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- T6
def t6_params_flops(h, args, device):
    m = build_model(1, args.tinyvim_pretrained_weight_path, device)
    tr, trn = measure_params(m), measure_trainable_params(m)
    ctrl = build_model(0, args.tinyvim_pretrained_weight_path, device)
    ctrl_tr = measure_params(ctrl)
    h.check("T6", f"train-graph params == {EXPECT['fet_train']:,}", tr == EXPECT["fet_train"], f"got {tr:,}")
    h.check("T6", f"FET train increment == {EXPECT['fet_train_inc']:,}", tr - ctrl_tr == EXPECT["fet_train_inc"],
            f"got {tr - ctrl_tr:,}")
    h.check("T6", "FET new params are trainable (no frozen backbone trick)", trn == tr, f"trainable={trn:,}")
    m.switch_to_deploy()
    dp, dpn = measure_params(m), measure_trainable_params(m)
    h.check("T6", f"deploy params == {EXPECT['fet_deploy']:,} and <= 5,000,000",
            dp == EXPECT["fet_deploy"] and dp <= EXPECT["budget"], f"got {dp:,}")
    h.check("T6", f"FET deploy increment == {EXPECT['fet_deploy_inc']:,}", dp - EXPECT["ctrl_deploy"] == EXPECT["fet_deploy_inc"],
            f"got {dp - EXPECT['ctrl_deploy']:,}")
    h.check("T6", "train/deploy params printed separately (train > 5M is allowed, deploy is the hard gate)",
            tr > EXPECT["budget"] >= dp, f"train={tr:,} deploy={dp:,}")
    mac = 64 * 64 * 144 * 96
    h.check("T6", f"FET fused MAC == {EXPECT['fet_mac']:,} (fvcore convention 1 MAC = 1 FLOP)",
            mac == EXPECT["fet_mac"], f"manual={mac:,} (0.0566231 G)")
    if args.device.startswith("cuda"):
        ok, res = _kernel_ok(lambda: measure_flops(m, size=256))
        if ok is True:
            flops, unsup = res
            h.check("T6", "deploy fvcore FLOPs measured (record [DEPLOY-FLOPS] + unsupported_ops)",
                    flops > 0, f"{flops:.4f} G unsupported_ops={unsup}")
        else:
            h.skip("T6", "deploy fvcore FLOPs", res)
    else:
        h.skip("T6", "deploy fvcore FLOPs", "needs CUDA")


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- T7
def t7_gradients(h, args, device):
    if not args.device.startswith("cuda"):
        h.skip("T7", "3-step gradient path", "needs CUDA selective-scan kernel")
        return
    from model.utils import BCEDiceLoss
    m = build_model(1, args.tinyvim_pretrained_weight_path, device)
    m.train()
    backbone = [p for n, p in m.named_parameters() if n.startswith("encoder.")]
    new = [p for n, p in m.named_parameters() if not n.startswith("encoder.")]
    opt = torch.optim.Adam([{"params": backbone, "lr": 2e-5, "lr_scale": 0.1, "name": "backbone"},
                            {"params": new, "lr": 2e-4, "lr_scale": 1.0, "name": "new"}],
                           2e-4, (0.9, 0.99), eps=1e-8, weight_decay=1e-4)
    torch.manual_seed(16)
    pre = torch.randn(4, 3, 256, 256, device=device)
    post = torch.randn(4, 3, 256, 256, device=device)
    tgt = (torch.rand(4, 1, 256, 256, device=device) > 0.7).float()
    lrs, gammas = [], []
    finite = True
    for step in range(3):
        out = m(pre, post, tgt)
        loss = BCEDiceLoss(out, tgt)
        opt.zero_grad()
        loss.backward()
        g = m.fine_evidence_tap.gamma.grad
        gammas.append(None if g is None else float(g.item()))
        lrs.append([round(pg["lr"], 12) for pg in opt.param_groups])
        if not torch.isfinite(loss):
            finite = False
        dw = m.fine_evidence_tap.diff.weight.grad
        if step >= 1 and (dw is None or float(dw.abs().sum().item()) == 0.0):
            finite = False
        opt.step()
    h.check("T7", "loss finite for 3 steps, no NaN", finite)
    h.check("T7", "step-1 gamma.grad finite and non-zero", gammas[0] is not None
            and gammas[0] != 0.0 and abs(gammas[0]) < 1e3, f"gamma.grad[0]={gammas[0]}")
    h.check("T7", "after gamma opens, W_diff gradient non-zero at steps 2 and 3",
            all(x is not None and x != 0.0 for x in gammas[1:]),
            f"gamma.grad={['%.3e' % x if x is not None else None for x in gammas]}")
    grp_ok = all(abs(l[0] / l[1] - 0.1) < 1e-9 for l in lrs)
    h.check("T7", "per-group lr ratio backbone:new == 0.1 preserved by the scheduler", grp_ok, f"lrs={lrs[0]}")
    miss = [n for n, p in m.named_parameters()
            if p.requires_grad and (p.grad is None or float(p.grad.abs().sum().item()) == 0.0)
            and not n.startswith("fine_evidence_tap.diff")]
    h.check("T7", "encoder/TAR/DCR/head all still receive gradients", not miss, f"zero-grad params={miss[:5]}")


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- T8
def t8_dry_run(h, args, device):
    if not args.dry_run_json:
        h.skip("T8", "real-data 3-step dry-run", "run models/_legacy/run4_dry_run.py then pass --dry-run-json")
        return
    if not os.path.isfile(args.dry_run_json):
        h.check("T8", "dry_run.json present", False, args.dry_run_json)
        return
    with open(args.dry_run_json, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        d = json.load(f)
    h.check("T8", "actual_steps == 3 (dry-only, no best ckpt written)",
            int(d.get("actual_steps", -1)) == 3 and not d.get("wrote_best", True),
            f"actual_steps={d.get('actual_steps')} wrote_best={d.get('wrote_best')}")
    h.check("T8", "loss finite on every step", all(bool(x) for x in d.get("loss_finite", [])),
            f"losses={d.get('losses')}")
    h.check("T8", "A/B/label share the same geometry mapping and label is gray>=128 binarized",
            bool(d.get("geom_sync")) and bool(d.get("label_gray128")),
            f"geom_sync={d.get('geom_sync')} label_gray128={d.get('label_gray128')}")
    h.check("T8", "gamma received gradient on step 1 and W_diff on steps 2-3",
            bool(d.get("gamma_grad_step1")) and bool(d.get("diff_grad_step23")))
    h.check("T8", "batch32 resource probe recorded", bool(d.get("batch32_ok")), f"bs={d.get('batch_size')}")


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- T9
def t9_eval_injection(h, args, device):
    """用真实 eval.py 做错误组合注入：必须非零退出，不允许 strict=False 静默成功。"""
    tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="r4_t9_")
    try:
        ctrl = build_model(0, args.tinyvim_pretrained_weight_path, "cpu")
        fet = build_model(1, args.tinyvim_pretrained_weight_path, "cpu")
        fet_deploy = build_model(1, args.tinyvim_pretrained_weight_path, "cpu")
        fet_deploy.switch_to_deploy()
        arch_fet = {"arch": "casa_tvim_str", "backbone": "tinyvim_s_slim", "caacp": 1,
                    "caacp_score_mode": "rank", "caacp_residual_mode": "current",
                    "frh": 0, "fs_tar": 0, "fine_tap": 1,
                    "fine_tap_form": FET_FORM, "fine_tap_source": FET_SOURCE, "fine_tap_fuse": FET_FUSE,
                    "rep_mode": "full", "str_dim": 96,
                    "protocol_version": PROTOCOL_VERSION,
                    "source_code_sha256": source_code_identity()}
        arch_ctrl = dict(arch_fet, fine_tap=0, fine_tap_form="none",
                         fine_tap_source="none", fine_tap_fuse="none")
        cases = []

        def make_case(name, arch, state_dict, fine_tap_flag, expect_marker=None):
            d = os.path.join(tmp, name)
            os.makedirs(d, exist_ok=True)
            torch.save(state_dict, os.path.join(d, "best_F1=0.5000.pth"))
            if arch is not None:
                with open(os.path.join(d, "arch.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    json.dump(arch, f)
            cases.append((name, d, fine_tap_flag, expect_marker))

        make_case("cli_fine_tap0_on_fet_ckpt", arch_fet, fet.state_dict(), 0, "[ARCH-MISMATCH]")
        make_case("missing_sidecar_with_fine_tap1", None, fet.state_dict(), 1, "[ARCH-MISSING]")
        make_case("wrong_fine_tap_form", dict(arch_fet, fine_tap_form="pq_abs_3x3_v1"),
                  fet.state_dict(), 1, "[ARCH-MISMATCH]")
        make_case("fet_ckpt_but_arch_says_ctrl", arch_ctrl, fet.state_dict(), 1, "[ARCH-MISMATCH]")
        make_case("missing_fet_weights", arch_fet, ctrl.state_dict(), 1, None)
        make_case("deploy_state_misused_as_train_ckpt", arch_fet, fet_deploy.state_dict(), 1, None)
        # R4 fail-closed：FET checkpoint 的身份字段必须齐备，缺一即拒绝
        no_proto = {k: v for k, v in arch_fet.items() if k != "protocol_version"}
        make_case("fet_sidecar_without_protocol_version", no_proto, fet.state_dict(), 1, "[ARCH-MISMATCH]")
        no_src = {k: v for k, v in arch_fet.items() if k != "source_code_sha256"}
        make_case("fet_sidecar_without_source_code_sha256", no_src, fet.state_dict(), 1, "[ARCH-MISMATCH]")

        # T9 只检验 sidecar/strict-load 门；用结构合法但不含任何权重的假预训练文件，
        # 避免 FileNotFoundError 掩盖真正的判定（真实 T0 已单独核验预训练身份）。
        dummy_pre = os.path.join(tmp, "dummy_tinyvim_s_1000e.pth")
        torch.save({"model_ema": {}}, dummy_pre)
        pre_arg = str(args.tinyvim_pretrained_weight_path) if args.tinyvim_pretrained_weight_path \
            else dummy_pre

        for name, d, flag, marker in cases:
            cmd = [sys.executable, os.path.join(_MODELS_ROOT, "eval.py"),
                   "--arch", "casa_tvim_str", "--ckpt_dir", d, "--fine_tap", str(flag),
                   "--dataset", "SYSU-CD-256",
                   "--caacp", "1", "--caacp_score_mode", "rank", "--caacp_residual_mode", "current",
                   "--rep_mode", "full", "--str_dim", "96",
                   "--dataset_root", os.path.join(tmp, "nonexistent_data"),
                   "--test_list", os.path.join(tmp, "nonexistent_list.txt"),
                   "--pretrained_weight_path", pre_arg,
                   "--onGPU", "False"]
            p = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=_MODELS_ROOT)
            out = (p.stdout or "") + (p.stderr or "")
            if marker is None:
                # 必须死在 strict load（键缺失/多余/形状不符），不能是别的原因
                load_fail = any(s in out for s in ("Missing key(s)", "Unexpected key(s)",
                                                   "size mismatch", "Error(s) in loading state_dict"))
                ok = p.returncode != 0 and load_fail
                tag = "strict-load" if load_fail else "OTHER-REASON"
            else:
                ok = p.returncode != 0 and marker in out
                tag = "ok" if marker in out else "MISSING"
            h.check("T9", f"reject: {name}", ok,
                    f"rc={p.returncode} marker={tag} "
                    f"tail={out.strip().splitlines()[-1][:120] if out.strip() else ''}")

        # 正向对照：正确的组合不得被 arch 检查误杀
        d = os.path.join(tmp, "correct_combo")
        os.makedirs(d, exist_ok=True)
        torch.save(fet.state_dict(), os.path.join(d, "best_F1=0.5000.pth"))
        with open(os.path.join(d, "arch.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(arch_fet, f)
        cmd = [sys.executable, os.path.join(_MODELS_ROOT, "eval.py"),
               "--arch", "casa_tvim_str", "--ckpt_dir", d, "--fine_tap", "1",
               "--dataset", "SYSU-CD-256",
               "--caacp", "1", "--caacp_score_mode", "rank", "--caacp_residual_mode", "current",
               "--rep_mode", "full", "--str_dim", "96",
               "--dataset_root", os.path.join(tmp, "nonexistent_data"),
               "--test_list", os.path.join(tmp, "nonexistent_list.txt"),
               "--pretrained_weight_path", pre_arg,
               "--onGPU", "False"]
        p = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=_MODELS_ROOT)
        out = (p.stdout or "") + (p.stderr or "")
        h.check("T9", "positive control: correct combo passes the arch gate (strict load succeeds)",
                "[ARCH-MISMATCH]" not in out and "[ARCH-MISSING]" not in out
                and "size mismatch" not in out and "Missing key(s)" not in out
                and "Unexpected key(s)" not in out,
                f"rc={p.returncode}")

        # 训练后 checkpoint（可选）：裸 state_dict 必须 strict 加载
        if args.checkpoint and os.path.isfile(args.checkpoint):
            ck = torch.load(args.checkpoint, map_location="cpu", weights_only=False)
            sd = ck.get("state_dict", ck)
            m = build_model(1, args.tinyvim_pretrained_weight_path, "cpu")
            res = m.load_state_dict(sd, strict=True)
            h.check("T9", "post-train checkpoint strict-loads into the fine_tap=1 train graph",
                    not res.missing_keys and not res.unexpected_keys, f"{args.checkpoint}")
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(tmp, ignore_errors=True)


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--tinyvim-pretrained-weight-path", type=str, default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--device", type=str, default="cuda:0")
    ap.add_argument("--real-dataset-root", type=str, default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--real-test-list", type=str, default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=16)
    ap.add_argument("--checkpoint", type=str, default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--source-identity-json", type=str, default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--dry-run-json", type=str, default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--skip-fold-heavy", type=int, default=0)
    args = ap.parse_args()

    device = args.device
    if device.startswith("cuda") and not torch.cuda.is_available():
        print(f"[WARN] {device} unavailable, falling back to cpu (heavy tests will SKIP)")
        device = "cpu"
        args.device = "cpu"

    h = Harness()
    print(f"[R4] protocol={PROTOCOL_VERSION} device={device} "
          f"pretrained={args.tinyvim_pretrained_weight_path}", flush=True)
    print("[R4] code identity: " + json.dumps(source_code_identity()), flush=True)

    t0_identity(h, args, device)
    t1_ctrl_params(h, args, device)
    t2_epoch0(h, args, device)
    t3_shapes(h, args, device)
    t4_fold(h, args, device)
    t5_train_deploy(h, args, device)
    t6_params_flops(h, args, device)
    t7_gradients(h, args, device)
    t8_dry_run(h, args, device)
    t9_eval_injection(h, args, device)

    n_fail = h.summary()
    return 1 if n_fail else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
