#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Run7 HFAE（高频边缘辅助监督）T0–T12 验收测试。

本地（无 GPU / 无 selective_scan kernel）会**跳过**需要前向的检查并明确打印 SKIP；
服务器（casacd 环境）应全 PASS。

    python models/_legacy/test_run7_hf_edge.py                 # 全量
    python models/_legacy/test_run7_hf_edge.py --device cuda:0
"""
import argparse
import copy
import os
import sys
import tempfile

import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn as nn

_MODELS = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if _MODELS not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, _MODELS)

from model.casa_tvim_str_net import CASATViMSTRNet                       # noqa: E402
from model.str_hf_edge import (HFEdgeHead, HF_EDGE_TAPS, HF_EDGE_TAP_KEYS,  # noqa: E402
                               hf_edge_loss, edge_target_from_label)
from model.utils import BCEDiceLoss                                      # noqa: E402

BASE_DEPLOY = 4_880_190
EXP_B_DEPLOY = 4_890_366          # 实测值（见 Run7 README §2）；T8 用区间断言容错
DEPLOY_LIMIT = 5_000_000

RESULTS = []


def rec(tid, name, ok, detail="", skipped=False):
    tag = "SKIP" if skipped else ("PASS" if ok else "FAIL")
    RESULTS.append((tid, name, tag, detail))
    print(f"[{tag}] {tid} {name}" + (f"  | {detail}" if detail else ""), flush=True)


def build(**kw):
    torch.manual_seed(16)
    return CASATViMSTRNet(None, caacp=True, rep_mode="full", str_dim=96,
                          caacp_score_mode="rank", frh=False,
                          caacp_residual_mode="current", fs_tar=False, **kw)


def npar(m):
    return sum(p.numel() for p in m.parameters())


def kernel_ok():
    try:
        import selective_scan_cuda_oflex  # noqa: F401
        return True
    except Exception:
        return False


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- tests
def t0_legacy_regression(dev, has_kernel):
    """T0：hf_edge=0 与基线逐位同构（taps/head 都不存在）。"""
    m = build()
    ok = (m.hf_edge is False and m.hf_edge_head is None and m.hf_edge_form == "none"
          and m._hf_handles == [] and m.hf_edge_report() is None)
    m.eval(); m.switch_to_deploy()
    dp = npar(m)
    ok = ok and dp == BASE_DEPLOY
    rec("T0", "legacy path (hf_edge=0) unchanged", ok,
        f"deploy_params={dp} (expect {BASE_DEPLOY})")


def t1_tensor_contract(dev, has_kernel):
    """T1：tap / head / target 形状契约。"""
    if not has_kernel:
        rec("T1", "tensor contract", False, "no selective_scan kernel; run on server",
            skipped=True)
        return
    m = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="aux_inject").to(dev).eval()
    B = 2
    pre = torch.randn(B, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
    post = torch.randn(B, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
    with torch.no_grad():
        out = m(pre, post)
    logits = m.aux_edge_logits()
    spec = HF_EDGE_TAPS[m.hf_edge_taps]
    # 边缘头把双时相配成**一张**变化边缘图 ⇒ logits/feat 均为 B 尺寸
    ok = (out.shape == (B, 1, 256, 256)
          and logits is not None and logits.shape == (B, 1, spec[0][3], spec[0][3])
          and m._hf_edge_feat.shape == (B, m.hf_edge_head.mid, spec[0][3], spec[0][3]))
    tgt = edge_target_from_label(torch.zeros(B, 1, 256, 256, device=dev), spec[0][3])
    ok = ok and tgt.shape == logits.shape
    rec("T1", "tensor contract", ok,
        f"out={tuple(out.shape)} logits={tuple(logits.shape)} feat={tuple(m._hf_edge_feat.shape)} "
        f"target={tuple(tgt.shape)}")


def t2_rng_pretrain(dev, has_kernel):
    """T2：同 seed 构造 → 既有 state_dict 交集逐位相同；新参数零初始化（inject）。"""
    c = build()
    a = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="aux_only")
    b = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="aux_inject")
    sd_c = c.state_dict()
    ok, details = True, []
    for tag, m in (("A", a), ("B", b)):
        sd = m.state_dict()
        common = set(sd_c) & set(sd)
        diff = [k for k in common if not torch.equal(sd_c[k], sd[k])]
        new = sorted(set(sd) - set(sd_c))
        ok = ok and not diff
        details.append(f"{tag}: common={len(common)} diff={len(diff)} new={len(new)}")
        if tag == "B":
            ok = ok and m.hf_edge_report()["inject_zero_init"] is True
    # 负对照：把 inject 置非零，自检必须报 False
    b.hf_edge_head.inject.weight.data.fill_(0.01)
    neg = b.hf_edge_report()["inject_zero_init"] is False
    ok = ok and neg
    rec("T2", "RNG discipline + zero-init", ok, " | ".join(details) + f" | neg_ctrl={neg}")


def t3_epoch0_forward(dev, has_kernel):
    """T3：epoch-0 前向逐位恒等（synthetic 8 + 真实 16 由 T7 侧覆盖）。"""
    if not has_kernel:
        rec("T3", "epoch-0 bitwise identity", False, "no kernel; run on server", skipped=True)
        return
    c = build().to(dev).eval()
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    pre = torch.randn(8, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
    post = torch.randn(8, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
    with torch.no_grad():
        yc = c(pre, post)
    ok, det = True, []
    for tag, kw in (("aux_only", {}), ("aux_inject", {})):
        m = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form=tag).to(dev).eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            y = m(pre, post)
        eq = torch.equal(yc, y)
        ok = ok and eq
        det.append(f"{tag}: equal={eq} max|d|={float((y-yc).abs().max()):.3e}")
    rec("T3", "epoch-0 bitwise identity", ok, " | ".join(det))


def t4_grad_chain(dev, has_kernel):
    """T4：辅助损失 → 高频分支的梯度链非空。"""
    if not has_kernel:
        rec("T4", "aux-loss gradient chain", False, "no kernel; run on server", skipped=True)
        return
    m = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="aux_only").to(dev).train()
    pre = torch.randn(4, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
    post = torch.randn(4, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
    tgt = (torch.rand(4, 1, 256, 256, device=dev) > 0.9).float()
    out = m(pre, post)
    main = BCEDiceLoss(out, tgt)
    aux = hf_edge_loss(m.aux_edge_logits(), tgt, 0.2)
    loss = main + aux
    loss.backward()
    ok = (torch.isfinite(loss) and float(aux) > 0
          and m.hf_edge_tap_grad_norm() > 0
          and m.hf_edge_tap_grad_nonzero() > 0
          and m.hf_edge_head_grad_norm() > 0)
    head_grad = any(p.grad is not None and float(p.grad.abs().sum()) > 0
                    for p in m.head.parameters())
    enc_grad = any(p.grad is not None and float(p.grad.abs().sum()) > 0
                   for n, p in m.named_parameters() if n.startswith("encoder."))
    ok = ok and head_grad and enc_grad
    rec("T4", "aux-loss gradient chain", ok,
        f"aux={float(aux):.5f} tap_grad={m.hf_edge_tap_grad_norm():.4e} "
        f"nonzero={m.hf_edge_tap_grad_nonzero()} head_grad={m.hf_edge_head_grad_norm():.4e} "
        f"main_head_grad={head_grad} encoder_grad={enc_grad}")


def t5_mechanism_selfcheck(dev, has_kernel):
    """T5：机制自检——**只回传辅助损失**时，高频分支必须拿到非零梯度。

    首版用「加 aux 的 tap 梯度 vs 只主损失的 tap 梯度」做对照，**判据是错的**：
    高频分支本来就由**主损失**驱动（它同时喂主输出），两者总范数几乎相同
    （实测 0.17833 vs 0.17849），无法隔离 aux 的贡献。正确做法是**只用 aux 回传**：
    若此时 tap 梯度 > 0，才证明「边缘监督自己就能到达高频分支」。

    同时报告：主损失单独回传的 tap 梯度（对照读数）、关闭开关时无 tap 可查。
    """
    if not has_kernel:
        rec("T5", "mechanism self-check (HF-branch grad)", False, "no kernel", skipped=True)
        return
    pre = torch.randn(4, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
    post = torch.randn(4, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
    tgt = (torch.rand(4, 1, 256, 256, device=dev) > 0.9).float()

    # (a) 只回传 aux
    m1 = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="aux_only").to(dev).train()
    _ = m1(pre, post)
    aux = hf_edge_loss(m1.aux_edge_logits(), tgt, 0.2)
    aux.backward()
    g_aux_only = m1.hf_edge_tap_grad_norm()
    head_g_aux_only = m1.hf_edge_head_grad_norm()
    main_head_grad_after_aux = any(p.grad is not None and float(p.grad.abs().sum()) > 0
                                   for p in m1.head.parameters())

    # (b) 只回传主损失（对照）
    m2 = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="aux_only").to(dev).train()
    out2 = m2(pre, post)
    BCEDiceLoss(out2, tgt).backward()
    g_main_only = m2.hf_edge_tap_grad_norm()

    # (c) 关闭开关
    m3 = build().to(dev).train()
    g_disabled = m3.hf_edge_tap_grad_norm()

    ok = (g_aux_only > 0 and head_g_aux_only > 0 and g_disabled == 0.0
          and not main_head_grad_after_aux)
    rec("T5", "mechanism self-check (aux-only reaches HF branch)", ok,
        f"tap_grad_aux_only={g_aux_only:.4e} head_grad_aux_only={head_g_aux_only:.4e} "
        f"tap_grad_main_only={g_main_only:.4e} disabled={g_disabled} "
        f"main_head_touched_by_aux={main_head_grad_after_aux}")


def t6_numeric_gate(dev, has_kernel):
    """T6：TF32 off + deterministic 下 epoch-0 数值门 max|Δ| < 2e-4 且无 NaN。"""
    if not has_kernel:
        rec("T6", "numeric gate", False, "no kernel", skipped=True)
        return
    torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32 = False
    torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32 = False
    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
    c = build().to(dev).eval()
    a = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="aux_inject").to(dev).eval()
    torch.manual_seed(1)
    pre = torch.randn(4, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
    post = torch.randn(4, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
    with torch.no_grad():
        yc, ya = c(pre, post), a(pre, post)
    d = float((yc - ya).abs().max())
    ok = d < 2e-4 and torch.isfinite(ya).all()
    rec("T6", "numeric gate", ok, f"max|d|={d:.3e} finite={bool(torch.isfinite(ya).all())}")


def t7_fold_gate(dev, has_kernel):
    """T7：train↔deploy 二值 disagreement 必须为 0（真实 16 张优先，缺数据退化为 synthetic）。

    机制上两种形态都**不修改主输出路径**（aux_only 推理丢弃监督头；aux_inject 保留零初始化注入），
    因此折叠后主输出应与训练图逐位一致。
    """
    if not has_kernel:
        rec("T7", "fold equivalence (train<->deploy)", False, "no kernel", skipped=True)
        return
    ok, det = True, []
    for tag in ("aux_only", "aux_inject"):
        m = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form=tag).to(dev).eval()
        torch.manual_seed(2)
        pre = torch.randn(16, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
        post = torch.randn(16, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
        with torch.no_grad():
            y_tr = m(pre, post)
        d = copy.deepcopy(m); d.eval(); d.switch_to_deploy()
        with torch.no_grad():
            y_dp = d(pre, post)
        dis = float(((y_tr > 0.5) != (y_dp > 0.5)).float().mean())
        err = float((y_tr - y_dp).abs().max())
        ok = ok and dis == 0.0 and err < 2e-4
        det.append(f"{tag}: dis={dis} max|d|={err:.3e} deploy_params={npar(d)}")
    rec("T7", "fold equivalence (train<->deploy)", ok, " | ".join(det))


def t8_params_flops(dev, has_kernel):
    """T8：deploy 参数门（≤5M）与两形态的增量。"""
    c = build(); a = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="aux_only")
    b = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="aux_inject")
    train = {"CTRL": npar(c), "A": npar(a), "B": npar(b)}
    dep = {}
    for tag, m in (("CTRL", c), ("A", a), ("B", b)):
        m.eval(); m.switch_to_deploy(); dep[tag] = npar(m)
    ok = (dep["CTRL"] == BASE_DEPLOY and dep["A"] == BASE_DEPLOY
          and abs(dep["B"] - EXP_B_DEPLOY) <= 64 and all(v <= DEPLOY_LIMIT for v in dep.values()))
    rec("T8", "deploy param gate", ok,
        f"train={train} deploy={dep} A_delta={dep['A']-BASE_DEPLOY:+d} "
        f"B_delta={dep['B']-BASE_DEPLOY:+d} limit={DEPLOY_LIMIT}")


def t9_dry_run(dev, has_kernel):
    """T9：真实数据 3 步 dry-run（由 models/_legacy/run7_dry_run.py 承担；此处检查可调用性）。"""
    script = os.path.join(_MODELS, "run7_dry_run.py")
    ok = os.path.isfile(script)
    rec("T9", "real-data 3-step dry-run entrypoint exists", ok,
        f"{script} (full dry-run runs on server with the dataset)")


def t10_sidecar_negative(dev, has_kernel):
    """T10：fail-closed 负例——开关/形态不自洽必须被模型层断言拒绝。"""
    cases = []
    try:
        build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="none"); cases.append(("hf_edge=1+form=none", False))
    except AssertionError:
        cases.append(("hf_edge=1+form=none", True))
    try:
        build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="aux_only", fine_tap=True)
        cases.append(("hf_edge=1+fine_tap=1", False))
    except AssertionError:
        cases.append(("hf_edge=1+fine_tap=1", True))
    try:
        build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="aux_only", hf_edge_taps="bogus")
        cases.append(("bad taps", False))
    except AssertionError:
        cases.append(("bad taps", True))
    try:
        build(hf_edge=False, hf_edge_form="aux_only"); cases.append(("hf_edge=0+form!=none", False))
    except AssertionError:
        cases.append(("hf_edge=0+form!=none", True))
    ok = all(v for _n, v in cases)
    rec("T10", "sidecar fail-closed negatives", ok,
        " ".join(f"{n}={'rejected' if v else 'ACCEPTED(!)'}" for n, v in cases))


def t11_resume(dev, has_kernel):
    """T11：resume/protocol-exact 由 models/_legacy/run7_dry_run.py + train.py 侧车校验承担。"""
    ok = os.path.isfile(os.path.join(_MODELS, "train.py"))
    rec("T11", "resume protocol-exact entrypoint", ok,
        "train.py [PROTOCOL-MISMATCH] strict resume gate (runs on server)")


def t12_hook_idempotency(dev, has_kernel):
    """T12（R7 hotfix §7.7 回归）：tap hook 注册必须幂等，且重复注册在数值上中性。

    背景：波二（E9_HFAE_A）实测单步耗时随累计 forward 数线性上升 ⇒ 4× 劣化。根因是
    `_ensure_hf_hooks()` 把幂等标记打在 `RemovableHandle` 上、却到 `mod._forward_hooks`
    的 **hook 函数**里查该标记（两者不是同一对象），于是每次 forward 重复注册 1 个 hook/模块。
    本测试从三个方向锁死该类缺陷：
      (a) 连续 forward 后 hook 数恒为 1/模块、handle 数恒定（旧代码会线性增长）；
      (b) 手工制造重复注册 ⇒ 新增的 fail-closed 判据必须抛 RuntimeError（而非静默变慢）；
      (c) 重复注册在**数值上是中性的**——同一权重、同一输入下输出与 aux logits 逐位相同
          （这同时证明：修复前已产出的波一/波二部分结果与修复版语义同一，差异只在速度）。
    """
    if not has_kernel:
        rec("T12", "hook idempotency (regression §7.7)", False,
            "no selective_scan kernel; run on server", skipped=True)
        return
    handles = []
    for form in ("aux_only", "aux_inject"):
        m = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form=form).to(dev).eval()
        pre = torch.randn(2, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
        post = torch.randn(2, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
        counts = []
        with torch.no_grad():
            for _ in range(25):
                m(pre, post)
                counts.append(tuple(sorted(m.hf_edge_hook_counts().items())))
        handles.append((form, counts, len(m._hf_handles)))

    # (a) hook 数/ handle 数在 25 次 forward 后必须恒定
    n_taps = len(HF_EDGE_TAP_KEYS["1_4_1_8"])
    ok_a = all(all(c == tuple((k, 1) for k in sorted(HF_EDGE_TAP_KEYS["1_4_1_8"]))
                   for c in counts) and nh == n_taps
               for _f, counts, nh in handles)

    # (b) 重复注册必须被 fail-closed 判据拒绝
    m = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="aux_only").to(dev).eval()
    with torch.no_grad():
        m(torch.randn(2, 3, 256, 256, device=dev), torch.randn(2, 3, 256, 256, device=dev))
    key0, key1 = HF_EDGE_TAP_KEYS["1_4_1_8"]
    dup = (lambda k: (lambda mo, i, o: m._hf_taps.__setitem__(k, o)))(key0)
    dup._hfae_key = key0                      # 冒充"我们自己的"重复 hook
    m._hf_hook_targets()[0].register_forward_hook(dup)
    try:
        m._ensure_hf_hooks()
        ok_b, det_b = False, "duplicate accepted(!)"
    except RuntimeError:
        ok_b, det_b = True, "RuntimeError raised"

    # (c) 重复注册的数值中性：模拟旧缺陷（未打标记的重复 hook）后输出必须逐位相同
    m = build(hf_edge=True, hf_edge_form="aux_only").to(dev).eval()
    pre = torch.randn(2, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
    post = torch.randn(2, 3, 256, 256, device=dev)
    with torch.no_grad():
        out0 = m(pre, post).clone()
        log0 = m.aux_edge_logits().clone()
    for key, mod in zip((key0, key1), m._hf_hook_targets()):
        for _ in range(20):
            mod.register_forward_hook(
                (lambda k: (lambda mo, i, o: m._hf_taps.__setitem__(k, o)))(key))
    with torch.no_grad():
        out1 = m(pre, post)
        log1 = m.aux_edge_logits()
    d_out = (out0 - out1).abs().max().item()
    d_log = (log0 - log1).abs().max().item()
    ok_c = torch.equal(out0, out1) and torch.equal(log0, log1)

    ok = ok_a and ok_b and ok_c
    rec("T12", "hook idempotency (regression §7.7)", ok,
        f"(a)counts_const={ok_a} (b){det_b} (c)bitwise_neutral={ok_c} "
        f"max|d_out|={d_out:.2e} max|d_logits|={d_log:.2e}; "
        f"forms={[f'{f}:{len(set(c))}distinct' for f, c, _n in handles]}")


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--device", default="cuda:0" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    a = ap.parse_args()
    dev = a.device
    has_kernel = kernel_ok()
    print(f"=== Run7 HFAE T0-T12 | device={dev} | selective_scan={has_kernel} ===")
    for fn in (t0_legacy_regression, t1_tensor_contract, t2_rng_pretrain, t3_epoch0_forward,
               t4_grad_chain, t5_mechanism_selfcheck, t6_numeric_gate, t7_fold_gate,
               t8_params_flops, t9_dry_run, t10_sidecar_negative, t11_resume,
               t12_hook_idempotency):
        try:
            fn(dev, has_kernel)
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            rec(fn.__name__, "exception", False, f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    n_fail = sum(1 for _t, _n, tag, _d in RESULTS if tag == "FAIL")
    n_skip = sum(1 for _t, _n, tag, _d in RESULTS if tag == "SKIP")
    print(f"\n=== SUMMARY: {len(RESULTS)} checks, {n_fail} FAIL, {n_skip} SKIP ===")
    return 1 if n_fail else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/test_run8_cpu_precheck.py

"""Run8 CPU 侧预检（**本机可跑**，不依赖 CUDA / selective_scan）。

覆盖：
  C1 双曲算子的数学性质（非负、有界、A/B 对称、全零退化）
  C2 **退化定理复现**：固定半径双曲距离 vs 余弦 ⇒ 逐像素同序（T3 的 CPU 版）
  C3 **非退化性**：detail 与 global 两分支的排序必须显著不同（本轮的核心卖点）
  C4 HQKV 的形状 / 参数预算 / γ 零初始化
  C5 HGSL 损失：形状、有限、对小目标不塌成全 0
  C6 网络构建 + 参数分布（无预训练，纯 CPU）
  C7 关掉 HQKV（CTRL）与打开（EXP）的参数差 = HQKV 参数量

本脚本**不写入任何正式名产物**，只打印。
"""
import os
import sys

import torch

_HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if _HERE not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, _HERE)

from model.layers.caacp_hqkv import CAACPHQKV, BranchSATAttention  # noqa: E402
from model.layers.hyperbolic import (  # noqa: E402
    branch_rank_disagreement, cosine_change_score, euclidean_change_score,
    fixed_radius_change_score, lorentz_change_score,
)
from model.hgsl_loss import downsample_gt_occupancy, hgsl_loss  # noqa: E402

torch.manual_seed(0)
FAILS = []
CHECKS = []


def check(name, cond, detail=""):
    CHECKS.append((name, bool(cond), detail))
    if not cond:
        FAILS.append(name)
    print(f"  [{'PASS' if cond else 'FAIL'}] {name}  {detail}")


print("=" * 78)
print("C1 双曲算子数学性质")
print("=" * 78)
B, C, H, W = 2, 32, 16, 16
xa = torch.randn(B, C, H, W)
xb = torch.randn(B, C, H, W)
s_h = lorentz_change_score(xa, xb, branch="detail")
s_e = euclidean_change_score(xa, xb, branch="detail")
s_c = cosine_change_score(xa, xb)
check("C1.1 Lorentz 分数形状", tuple(s_h.shape) == (B, 1, H, W), f"{tuple(s_h.shape)}")
check("C1.2 分数 finite", bool(torch.isfinite(s_h).all()))
check("C1.3 分数 ∈ [0,1]", float(s_h.min()) >= 0.0 and float(s_h.max()) <= 1.0,
      f"[{float(s_h.min()):.4f},{float(s_h.max()):.4f}]")
s_swap = lorentz_change_score(xb, xa, branch="detail")
check("C1.4 A/B 交换对称", float((s_h - s_swap).abs().max()) < 1e-6,
      f"max_abs={float((s_h-s_swap).abs().max()):.2e}")
s_same = lorentz_change_score(xa, xa, branch="detail")
# 容差说明（实测根因，不是"放松标准"）：`acosh` 在自变量 1 附近**条件数发散**。
# 即使 u_A 与 u_B 在数学上相同，float32 下分别做 normalize + dot 后 `z−1` 只能精确到
# ~1e-7；而 `d ≈ sinh(r)·sqrt(2(z−1))`，于是 `d ≈ 1e-3`、`s ≈ 1e-3`。
# 实测 `s_same.max() ≈ 6.9e-4`，完全符合该误差模型。因此容差定 2e-3 并在此如实记录。
check("C1.5 相同时相 ⇒ 分数≈0（容差 2e-3，见下方根因）",
      float(s_same.max()) < 2e-3, f"max={float(s_same.max()):.2e}")
print(f"    根因：acosh 在 z→1 处条件数发散；float32 下 z−1 的 ~1e-7 误差被 sqrt 放大到 "
      f"~1e-3。可接受，但**必须在预注册里披露**（否则真实数据上的极小变化会被这个底噪主导）。")
z = torch.zeros(B, C, H, W)
s_zero = lorentz_change_score(z, z, branch="detail")
check("C1.6 全零输入无 NaN", bool(torch.isfinite(s_zero).all()))

print()
print("=" * 78)
print("C2 退化定理复现（固定半径双曲距离 == 余弦排序）")
print("=" * 78)
s_fix = fixed_radius_change_score(xa, xb)
dg = branch_rank_disagreement(s_fix, s_c)
check("C2.1 固定半径 vs 余弦：逐像素 rank 全同", dg["frac_rank_diff"] == 0.0,
      f"frac_rank_diff={dg['frac_rank_diff']}, tau={dg['kendall_tau']:.6f}")
check("C2.2 Kendall τ = 1（浮点容差 1e-5）", abs(dg["kendall_tau"] - 1.0) < 1e-5,
      f"{dg['kendall_tau']:.9f}")

print()
print("=" * 78)
print("C3 非退化性：detail vs global 两分支排序必须不同（本轮核心卖点）")
print("=" * 78)
s_d = lorentz_change_score(xa, xb, branch="detail")
s_g = lorentz_change_score(xa, xb, branch="global")
d_dg = branch_rank_disagreement(s_d, s_g)
check("C3.1 detail vs global rank 分歧 > 0", d_dg["frac_rank_diff"] > 0.0,
      f"frac_rank_diff={d_dg['frac_rank_diff']:.4f}, tau={d_dg['kendall_tau']:.4f}")
d_hc = branch_rank_disagreement(s_d, s_c)
check("C3.2 detail(变半径) vs 余弦：排序必须改变（非退化）",
      d_hc["frac_rank_diff"] > 0.0,
      f"frac_rank_diff={d_hc['frac_rank_diff']:.4f}, tau={d_hc['kendall_tau']:.4f}")
d_he = branch_rank_disagreement(s_d, s_e)
check("C3.3 双曲 vs 同式欧氏：排序有差异", d_he["frac_rank_diff"] > 0.0,
      f"frac_rank_diff={d_he['frac_rank_diff']:.4f}, tau={d_he['kendall_tau']:.4f}")
print(f"    ⚠️ 记录读数（不设阈值）：Kendall τ(H, E) = {d_he['kendall_tau']:.4f}。"
      f"τ 越接近 1 ⇒ 曲率本身带来的排序增量越小，Stage D 的 H-vs-E 对照必须如实报告这一点。")

print()
print("=" * 78)
print("C4 HQKV 形状 / 参数预算 / γ 零初始化")
print("=" * 78)
DIMS = (96, 192, 384, 768)
op = CAACPHQKV(stage_dims=DIMS, rank=32, lite_dim=192)
rep = op.param_report()
print(f"  per-stage: {rep['per_stage']}")
print(f"  total_params = {rep['total_params']:,}  ({rep['total_params']/1e6:.4f} M)")
check("C4.1 γ 零初始化", rep["gamma_zero_init"], f"gammas={rep['gammas']}")
check("C4.2 HQKV 参数 < 1.0M", rep["total_params"] < 1_000_000,
      f"{rep['total_params']:,}")
_RES = (64, 32, 16, 8)          # 256² 下的 1/4、1/8、1/16、1/32
fA = [torch.randn(2, d, r, r) for d, r in zip(DIMS, _RES)]
fB = [torch.randn(2, d, r, r) for d, r in zip(DIMS, _RES)]
out, diag = op(fA, fB)
check("C4.3 输出形状 = (B, lite_dim, 64, 64)", tuple(out.shape) == (2, 192, 64, 64),
      f"{tuple(out.shape)}")
check("C4.4 γ=0 ⇒ 输出恒为 0（epoch-0 恒等的前提）",
      float(out.abs().max()) == 0.0, f"max={float(out.abs().max()):.3e}")
check("C4.5 每个 stage 都有 diag", len(diag["stages"]) == 4, f"{list(diag['stages'])}")
for k, v in diag["stages"].items():
    print(f"    {k}: s_d_mean={v['score_detail_mean']:.4f} s_g_mean={v['score_global_mean']:.4f} "
          f"contrib={v.get('contrib_norm', 0):.3f}")

print()
print("=" * 78)
print("C5 HGSL 损失")
print("=" * 78)
label = torch.zeros(2, 1, 256, 256)
label[0, :, 100:104, 100:104] = 1.0          # 4×4 = 16px 小目标（tiny_16_63 档）
label[1, :, 10:74, 10:74] = 1.0              # 64×64 大目标
occ32 = downsample_gt_occupancy(label, 32)
check("C5.1 max 池化保底：4px 小目标在 1/32 上不被抹掉", float(occ32[0].sum()) > 0,
      f"sum={float(occ32[0].sum())}")
occ32a = downsample_gt_occupancy(label, 32, mode="avg")
check("C5.2 对照：avg 池化会削弱小目标（故不作默认）",
      float(occ32a[0].sum()) <= float(occ32[0].sum()),
      f"avg_sum={float(occ32a[0].sum()):.4f}")
smaps = {i: torch.rand(2, 1, 256 // (4 * 2 ** i), 256 // (4 * 2 ** i))
         for i in range(4)}
l = hgsl_loss(smaps, label)
check("C5.3 HGSL 有限且 > 0", l is not None and torch.isfinite(torch.tensor(float(l))) and float(l) > 0,
      f"loss={float(l):.6f}")
check("C5.4 λ=0.2 生效（loss ≤ 0.2·2.0）", float(l) <= 0.4, f"{float(l):.6f}")

print()
print("=" * 78)
print("C6 网络构建 + 参数分布（无预训练，CPU）")
print("=" * 78)
try:
    from model.casa_vmamba_net import CASAVMambaSTRNet
    net_exp = CASAVMambaSTRNet(vmamba_pretrained_path=None, hqkv=True, hgsl=True,
                               hgsl_detach_score=True)
    pb = net_exp.param_breakdown()
    print(f"  backbone(VSSM-T)      {pb['backbone']:>12,}  ({pb['backbone']/1e6:8.3f} M)")
    print(f"  CAACP-HQKV            {pb['hqkv']:>12,}  ({pb['hqkv']/1e6:8.4f} M)")
    print(f"  TAR                   {pb['tar']:>12,}  ({pb['tar']/1e6:8.4f} M)")
    print(f"  DCR                   {pb['dcr']:>12,}  ({pb['dcr']/1e6:8.4f} M)")
    print(f"  head                  {pb['head']:>12,}")
    print(f"  TOTAL                 {pb['total']:>12,}  ({pb['total']/1e6:8.3f} M)")
    print(f"  **非骨干合计**         {pb['non_backbone']:>12,}  ({pb['non_backbone']/1e6:8.4f} M)")
    net_ctrl = CASAVMambaSTRNet(vmamba_pretrained_path=None, hqkv=False)
    pbc = net_ctrl.param_breakdown()
    delta = pb["total"] - pbc["total"]
    check("C6.1 CTRL/EXP 参数差 == HQKV 参数量", delta == pb["hqkv"],
          f"delta={delta:,} hqkv={pb['hqkv']:,}")
    check("C6.2 CTRL 非骨干参数 < 3M（用户要求尽量小）",
          pbc["non_backbone"] < 3_000_000,
          f"{pbc['non_backbone']/1e6:.4f} M")
    check("C6.3 VMamba-T 参数量落在 [28M, 45M]（口径待权重实测对账）",
          28e6 < pb["backbone"] < 45e6, f"{pb['backbone']/1e6:.3f} M")
    print(f"    ⚠️ 口径提示：项目文档写「官方 VMamba-T ≈30M」，但按官方 v2 配置"
          f"（depths=[2,2,9,2], dims=[96,192,384,768], ssm_ratio=2.0, mlp_ratio=4.0）"
          f"实测为 {pb['backbone']/1e6:.3f} M。论文引用必须以**权重实测**为准，"
          f"不得沿用未核对的 30M。")
except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
    import traceback
    traceback.print_exc()
    check("C6 网络构建", False, f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}")

print()
print("=" * 78)
print("C7 分支特化 / 几何对照的可构造性")
print("=" * 78)
try:
    for geom in ("hyperbolic", "euclidean"):
        n = CASAVMambaSTRNet(vmamba_pretrained_path=None, hqkv=True, geometry=geom)
        check(f"C7 geometry={geom} 可构造", n is not None)
    check("C7 三个几何候选的共同前缀一致",
          True, "hyperbolic / euclidean（同式）")
except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
    check("C7", False, f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}")

print()
print("=" * 78)
print(f"结果：{len(CHECKS)-len(FAILS)}/{len(CHECKS)} PASS, {len(FAILS)} FAIL")
if FAILS:
    print("FAILED:", ", ".join(FAILS))
print("=" * 78)
sys.exit(1 if FAILS else 0)

# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/test_st_geodesic.py

"""ST-GEO 数学与实现验收（纯 CPU，无外部数据；FAIL STOP）。

对应 st_geodesic.py 的命题 1/2/3：
  T1  生产实现（测地极坐标闭式）== eigh 参考的 AIM 距离
  T1b **反例守卫**：把 z=tanh(u)e^{2iθ} 当等距盘坐标的错误公式必须被判为不一致
  T2  对比度不变性（随机 SPD）：d_shape == 0、d_scale == 2√2|log c|
  T2c 对比度不变性（强各向异性条纹图，最坏数值情形）⇒ d_shape == 0
  T3  偏置不变性（图像域）⇒ 两项均为 0
  T4  分解恒等式 d²_AIM = d²_shape + 2(log σ₁/σ₂)²（命题 3）
  T5  对称性   T6 平坦区无 NaN/valid=0   T7 解析特例 ⇒ √2·u
  T8  梯度链非零有限   T9 双时相同一变换 ⇒ 距离不变
  T10 γ 零初始化 ⇒ 模块输出恒为 0      T11 抗伪变化曲线（|ΔI| 涨、d_shape 平）
  T12 真实结构变化必须有响应
用法：python test_st_geodesic.py
"""
import math
import sys

import torch

from model.layers.st_geodesic import (
    STGeoEvidence, det_scale_distance, poincare_shape_distance, shape_disk_coords,
    spd_geodesic_evidence, tr_based_shape_distance,
)

FAILED = []


def check(name, cond, detail=""):
    print(f"[{'PASS' if cond else 'FAIL'}] {name} {detail}")
    if not cond:
        FAILED.append(name)


def disk_distance_wrong(zx1, zy1, zx2, zy2):
    """**错误**做法（仅用于 T1b 反例）：把 z = tanh(u)e^{2iθ} 当作等距盘坐标。"""
    dx, dy = zx1 - zx2, zy1 - zy2
    num2 = dx * dx + dy * dy
    den = 1.0 - (zx1 * zx2 + zy1 * zy2)
    cross = zx1 * zy2 - zy1 * zx2
    den2 = den * den + cross * cross
    ratio = torch.sqrt((num2 / den2.clamp_min(1e-12)).clamp(0.0, 1.0))
    return math.sqrt(2.0) * torch.atanh(ratio.clamp(max=1.0 - 1e-7))


def aim_reference(j1, j2):
    """参考实现：‖log(J1^{-1/2} J2 J1^{-1/2})‖_F（eigh，慢但权威）。"""
    evals, evecs = torch.linalg.eigh(j1)
    inv_sqrt = (evecs @ torch.diag_embed(evals.clamp_min(1e-30).rsqrt())
                @ evecs.transpose(-1, -2))
    m = inv_sqrt @ j2 @ inv_sqrt
    ev = torch.linalg.eigvalsh(0.5 * (m + m.transpose(-1, -2))).clamp_min(1e-30)
    return ev.log().pow(2).sum(dim=-1).sqrt()


def spd_from_ab(a, b, c):
    m = torch.zeros(a.shape[0], 2, 2, dtype=a.dtype)
    m[:, 0, 0] = a
    m[:, 0, 1] = b
    m[:, 1, 0] = b
    m[:, 1, 1] = c
    return m


def rand_spd(n, gen, scale=1.0):
    x = torch.randn(n, 2, 2, generator=gen)
    m = x @ x.transpose(-1, -2) + 0.05 * torch.eye(2)
    return m * scale


def edge_image(n=1, h=48, w=48):
    """强各向异性合成图：单方向正弦条纹（κ→1，条件数最坏）。"""
    y, x = torch.meshgrid(torch.arange(h, dtype=torch.float32),
                          torch.arange(w, dtype=torch.float32), indexing="ij")
    img = torch.sin(2 * math.pi * x / 6.0)
    return img.view(1, 1, h, w).repeat(n, 3, 1, 1)


def main():
    gen = torch.Generator().manual_seed(7)

    # ---------------- T1 生产实现 vs eigh 参考 ----------------
    j1 = rand_spd(256, gen)
    j2 = rand_spd(256, gen)
    za = shape_disk_coords(j1[:, 0, 0:1], j1[:, 0, 1:2], j1[:, 1, 1:2])
    zb = shape_disk_coords(j2[:, 0, 0:1], j2[:, 0, 1:2], j2[:, 1, 1:2])
    d_prod = poincare_shape_distance(za[0], za[1], za[2], zb[0], zb[1], zb[2])

    def det1(j):
        det = (j[:, 0, 0] * j[:, 1, 1] - j[:, 0, 1] ** 2).clamp_min(1e-30).sqrt()
        return spd_from_ab(j[:, 0, 0] / det, j[:, 0, 1] / det, j[:, 1, 1] / det)

    d_ref = aim_reference(det1(j1), det1(j2))
    err = (d_prod[:, 0] - d_ref).abs().max().item()
    check("T1 production closed form == AIM reference (eigh)",
          err < 2e-3, f"max|d|={err:.3e}")

    # ---------------- T1b 错误公式必须被判为不一致（反例守卫）----------------
    d_wrong = disk_distance_wrong(za[0], za[1], zb[0], zb[1])
    err_wrong = (d_wrong[:, 0] - d_ref).abs().max().item()
    check("T1b wrong disk formula is inconsistent (guard)", err_wrong > 0.1,
          f"max|d|={err_wrong:.3e} (should be large)")

    # ---------------- T2 对比度不变性（随机 SPD）----------------
    c = 1.7
    jb = j1 * (c * c)
    zb2 = shape_disk_coords(jb[:, 0, 0:1], jb[:, 0, 1:2], jb[:, 1, 1:2])
    d_shape_c = poincare_shape_distance(za[0], za[1], za[2], zb2[0], zb2[1], zb2[2])
    d_scale_c = det_scale_distance(j1[:, 0, 0:1], j1[:, 0, 1:2], j1[:, 1, 1:2],
                                   jb[:, 0, 0:1], jb[:, 0, 1:2], jb[:, 1, 1:2])
    check("T2a J -> c^2 J => d_shape == 0 (exact, FP32)",
          float(d_shape_c.max()) == 0.0, f"max={float(d_shape_c.max()):.3e}")
    want = 2.0 * math.sqrt(2.0) * abs(math.log(c))
    check("T2b J -> c^2 J => d_scale == 2sqrt2|log c|",
          abs(float(d_scale_c.mean()) - want) < 1e-5,
          f"got={float(d_scale_c.mean()):.6f} want={want:.6f}")

    # ---------------- T2c 最坏情形：强各向异性条纹图 + 对比度 ----------------
    e = edge_image(2)
    _, pe0 = spd_geodesic_evidence(e, e * 1.35, sigma_d=1.0)
    _, pe1 = spd_geodesic_evidence(e, e * 0.55, sigma_d=1.0)
    kappa_edge = float(pe0["kappa_a"].max())
    worst = max(float(pe0["d_shape"].max()), float(pe1["d_shape"].max()))
    check("T2c anisotropic (kappa->1) worst case: contrast => d_shape == 0",
          worst == 0.0, f"max kappa={kappa_edge:.4f} max d_shape={worst:.3e}")

    # ---------------- T3 偏置不变性 ----------------
    img = torch.randn(2, 3, 64, 64, generator=gen) * 0.5
    _, p1 = spd_geodesic_evidence(img, img + 0.35, sigma_d=1.0)
    check("T3 image + offset => d_shape == 0 and d_str == 0",
          float(p1["d_shape"].abs().max()) == 0.0 and float(p1["d_str"].abs().max()) == 0.0,
          f"shape={float(p1['d_shape'].abs().max()):.2e} "
          f"str={float(p1['d_str'].abs().max()):.2e}")

    # ---------------- T4 分解恒等式（命题 3）----------------
    jj1 = rand_spd(128, gen, scale=0.7)
    jj2 = rand_spd(128, gen, scale=1.9)
    a1 = shape_disk_coords(jj1[:, 0, 0:1], jj1[:, 0, 1:2], jj1[:, 1, 1:2])
    a2 = shape_disk_coords(jj2[:, 0, 0:1], jj2[:, 0, 1:2], jj2[:, 1, 1:2])
    ds = poincare_shape_distance(a1[0], a1[1], a1[2], a2[0], a2[1], a2[2])
    dsc = det_scale_distance(jj1[:, 0, 0:1], jj1[:, 0, 1:2], jj1[:, 1, 1:2],
                             jj2[:, 0, 0:1], jj2[:, 0, 1:2], jj2[:, 1, 1:2])
    d_full = (ds * ds + dsc * dsc).sqrt()
    d_ref_full = aim_reference(jj1, jj2).view_as(d_full)
    e4 = (d_full - d_ref_full).abs().max().item()
    check("T4 d_full == d_shape (+) d_scale == d_AIM(J1,J2)", e4 < 3e-3,
          f"max|d|={e4:.3e}")

    # ---------------- T4b τ 参考实现与生产实现一致 ----------------
    a1d = det1(jj1)
    a2d = det1(jj2)
    d_tau = tr_based_shape_distance(a1d[:, 0, 0:1], a1d[:, 0, 1:2], a1d[:, 1, 1:2],
                                    a2d[:, 0, 0:1], a2d[:, 0, 1:2], a2d[:, 1, 1:2])
    # τ 写法在强各向异性下病态，这里只要求两者在 1e-2 内一致（并记录差异）
    e4b = (ds - d_tau).abs().max().item()
    check("T4b production == tau-based reference (<=1e-2)", e4b < 1e-2,
          f"max|d|={e4b:.3e}")

    # ---------------- T5 对称性 ----------------
    ds_sw = poincare_shape_distance(a2[0], a2[1], a2[2], a1[0], a1[1], a1[2])
    check("T5 symmetry", float((ds - ds_sw).abs().max()) < 1e-6)

    # ---------------- T6 平坦区 ----------------
    flat = torch.zeros(1, 3, 32, 32)
    ev_f, p_f = spd_geodesic_evidence(flat, flat, sigma_d=1.0)
    check("T6 flat input: finite, valid=0, d_shape=0",
          bool(torch.isfinite(ev_f).all()) and float(p_f["valid"].max()) == 0.0
          and float(p_f["d_shape"].abs().max()) == 0.0)

    # ---------------- T7 解析特例 ----------------
    u = 0.8
    jA = spd_from_ab(torch.tensor([math.exp(u)]), torch.tensor([0.0]),
                     torch.tensor([math.exp(-u)]))
    jB = spd_from_ab(torch.tensor([1.0]), torch.tensor([0.0]), torch.tensor([1.0]))
    aa = shape_disk_coords(jA[:, 0, 0:1], jA[:, 0, 1:2], jA[:, 1, 1:2])
    bb = shape_disk_coords(jB[:, 0, 0:1], jB[:, 0, 1:2], jB[:, 1, 1:2])
    d7 = poincare_shape_distance(aa[0], aa[1], aa[2], bb[0], bb[1], bb[2]).item()
    check("T7 diag(e^u,e^-u) vs I => sqrt2*u", abs(d7 - math.sqrt(2.0) * u) < 1e-4,
          f"got={d7:.6f} want={math.sqrt(2.0)*u:.6f}")

    # ---------------- T8 梯度 ----------------
    img_a = torch.randn(1, 3, 48, 48, generator=gen).requires_grad_(True)
    img_b = torch.randn(1, 3, 48, 48, generator=gen)
    ev, _ = spd_geodesic_evidence(img_a, img_b, sigma_d=1.0)
    ev.sum().backward()
    g = img_a.grad
    check("T8 finite non-zero gradient wrt input",
          g is not None and bool(torch.isfinite(g).all()) and float(g.abs().sum()) > 0)

    # ---------------- T9 同一变换作用于双时相 ----------------
    x = torch.randn(1, 1, 40, 40, generator=gen).repeat(1, 3, 1, 1)
    y = torch.randn(1, 1, 40, 40, generator=gen).repeat(1, 3, 1, 1)
    _, p0 = spd_geodesic_evidence(x, y, sigma_d=1.0)
    _, p90 = spd_geodesic_evidence(x.transpose(2, 3), y.transpose(2, 3), sigma_d=1.0)
    d0, d90 = float(p0["d_shape"].mean()), float(p90["d_shape"].mean())
    check("T9 identical transform on both timesteps => distance preserved",
          abs(d0 - d90) / max(d0, 1e-9) < 0.05, f"d0={d0:.6f} d90={d90:.6f}")

    # ---------------- T10 零初始化门 ----------------
    m = STGeoEvidence(in_ch=3, out_dim=8, mid=4, form="inject")
    out, _ = m(torch.randn(2, 3, 32, 32), torch.randn(2, 3, 32, 32), (8, 8))
    check("T10 gamma zero-init => module output identically 0",
          float(out.detach().abs().max()) == 0.0 and m.param_report()["gamma_init_zero"],
          f"params={m.param_report()['params']}")

    # ---------------- T11 抗伪变化曲线 ----------------
    base = torch.randn(1, 3, 64, 64, generator=gen) * 0.4 + 0.5
    rows = []
    for c in (1.0, 1.1, 1.25, 1.5, 2.0):
        _, pc = spd_geodesic_evidence(base, base * c, sigma_d=1.0)
        rows.append((c, float((base * c - base).abs().mean()),
                     float(pc["d_shape"].max()), float(pc["d_str"].mean())))
    print("       c | mean|dI| | max d_shape | mean d_str")
    for c, raw, dsm, dsc in rows:
        print(f"      {c:4.2f} | {raw:8.5f} | {dsm:11.3e} | {dsc:10.4f}")
    ok11 = all(r[2] == 0.0 for r in rows) and rows[-1][1] > 5 * rows[1][1]
    check("T11 radiometric change: |dI| grows, d_shape stays 0", ok11)

    # ---------------- T12 真实结构变化必须有响应 ----------------
    base2 = torch.randn(1, 3, 64, 64, generator=gen) * 0.4 + 0.5
    changed = base2.clone()
    changed[:, :, 20:28, 20:28] += 0.6
    _, pst = spd_geodesic_evidence(base2, changed, sigma_d=1.0)
    resp = float(pst["d_shape"][0, 0, 18:30, 18:30].max())
    check("T12 8x8 structural change => d_shape responds (>0.1)",
          resp > 0.1, f"max d_shape in region={resp:.4f}")

    # ---------------- T13 尺度通道对结构变化同样有响应且连续 ----------------
    ratio = float(pst["d_str"][0, 0, 18:30, 18:30].max())
    check("T13 scale channel responds to structural change (>0.1)", ratio > 0.1,
          f"max d_str in region={ratio:.4f}")

    # ==================== v2 验收 ====================
    from model.layers.st_geodesic import (  # noqa: E402
        STGEO_FORM_V2, _cell_topk_mean, _pool_evidence,
    )

    # T14 鲁棒池化：单点尖峰 vs 2×2 真实结构
    spike = torch.zeros(1, 1, 4, 4)
    spike[0, 0, 1, 1] = 1.0                       # 单点噪声
    block = torch.zeros(1, 1, 4, 4)
    block[0, 0, 1:3, 1:3] = 1.0                   # 2×2 真实小结构
    mx_s = float(torch.nn.functional.max_pool2d(spike, 4).max())
    tk_s = float(_cell_topk_mean(spike, 1, k=4).max())
    mx_b = float(torch.nn.functional.max_pool2d(block, 4).max())
    tk_b = float(_cell_topk_mean(block, 1, k=4).max())
    check("T14 鲁棒池化：单点尖峰被压到 1/4，而 2x2 结构无损",
          abs(tk_s - 0.25) < 1e-6 and abs(mx_s - 1.0) < 1e-6
          and abs(tk_b - 1.0) < 1e-6 and abs(mx_b - 1.0) < 1e-6,
          f"spike: max={mx_s:.3f} topk={tk_s:.3f} | block: max={mx_b:.3f} topk={tk_b:.3f}")

    # T15 跨尺度门：通道数与零初始化门
    m2 = STGeoEvidence(in_ch=3, out_dim=8, mid=4, form="inject",
                       pool_mode="topk_mean", xscale_gate=True)
    out2, _ = m2(torch.randn(2, 3, 32, 32), torch.randn(2, 3, 32, 32), (8, 8))
    rep2 = m2.param_report()
    check("T15 v2：in_ch=16（3 尺度×5 + 跨尺度门）、form 标记、γ=0 ⇒ 输出恒 0",
          rep2["in_ch_ev"] == 16 and rep2["form"] == STGEO_FORM_V2
          and float(out2.detach().abs().max()) == 0.0,
          f"in_ch={rep2['in_ch_ev']} form={rep2['form']} params={rep2['params']}")

    # T16 v2 证据在纯辐射变化下仍恒为 0（含跨尺度门）
    evs, gates = [], []
    for s_ in (1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        ev_, pr_ = spd_geodesic_evidence(base, base * 1.6, sigma_d=s_)
        pool_ = _pool_evidence(ev_, 16, mode="topk_mean")
        evs.append(pool_)
        gates.append(float(pool_[:, 0:1].abs().max()))
    gate = torch.stack([e[:, 0:1] for e in evs], dim=0).amin(dim=0)
    check("T16 v2（topk_mean + 跨尺度门）在纯对比度变化下 d_shape 与门恒为 0",
          all(g == 0.0 for g in gates) and float(gate.abs().max()) == 0.0,
          f"per-scale max={gates} gate max={float(gate.abs().max()):.1e}")

    print()
    if FAILED:
        print(f"RESULT: FAIL ({len(FAILED)}): {FAILED}")
        return 1
    print("RESULT: ALL OK")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

================================================================================
# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/test_str_reparam_equivalence.py
================================================================================
"""STRFusion Run1 structural re-parameterization equivalence tests (T0/T1/T2).

Ported from STR-RepNet `script/test_reparam_equivalence.py` (standalone, no yacs).

Thresholds (pre-registered, STRFusion design doc §4.3, do NOT change afterwards):
  - T0: pure FP64 kernel/bias algebra, target < 1e-12, hard assert < 1e-10
        (dims 160-384, O(1) magnitudes -> accumulation floor ~1e-14);
  - T1: block-level FP32 train-graph vs deploy-graph, hard assert < 2e-5,
        branches perturbed to TRAINED magnitudes (conv std 0.05, BN gamma/beta
        std 0.5, running stats randomized) so the fold is non-trivial;
  - T2: whole-model FP32 error RECORDED, hard assert < 2e-4, plus binarization
        (0.5 threshold) disagreement == 0 (STR argmax=0 analog for the 1-channel
        probability head), deploy params <= 3.0M, branch-free deploy state_dict.

Usage (server):
    CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1 python test_str_reparam_equivalence.py \
        --pretrained_weight_path pretrained_weight/deit_tiny_patch16_224-a1311bcf.pth
"""
import argparse
import copy
import os
import sys

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

_MODELS_ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if _MODELS_ROOT not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, _MODELS_ROOT)

from model.str_reparam import (RepDW3, RepPW1x1, RepPairFuse1x1, fold_conv_bn)
from model.str_tar import TemporalRep1x1, TARStage, MultiScaleTAR
from model.str_dcr import RepLocalBlock, DCRDecoder
from model.str_fusion import STRFusionNet

FP64_TOL = 1e-10
FP32_TOL = 2e-5
WHOLE_TOL = 2e-4
DEPLOY_BUDGET = 3.0e6


def perturb_conv_bn_module(mod, bn_names, conv_names, diag_names=()):
    """Randomize a rep module to TRAINED magnitudes (non-trivial fold)."""
    with torch.no_grad():
        for n, p in mod.named_parameters():
            if any(k in n for k in bn_names):
                p.normal_(0.0, 0.5)
        for n, p in mod.named_parameters():
            if any(k in n for k in conv_names) and n.endswith("weight"):
                p.normal_(0.0, 0.05)
        for n, p in mod.named_parameters():
            if any(k in n for k in diag_names):
                p.normal_(0.0, 0.05)
        for m in mod.modules():
            if isinstance(m, nn.BatchNorm2d):
                m.running_mean.normal_()
                m.running_var.uniform_(0.5, 2.0)


def check_fold(module, inputs, tol=FP32_TOL, name="module"):
    module.eval()
    with torch.no_grad():
        y0 = module(*inputs)
    m2 = copy.deepcopy(module)
    m2.switch_to_deploy()
    m2.eval()
    with torch.no_grad():
        y1 = m2(*inputs)
    err = (y0 - y1).abs().max().item()
    ok = err < tol
    print(f"[{'OK' if ok else 'FAIL'}] {name}: max_abs_error={err:.3e}")
    return ok


def assert_branch_free(m2, name):
    keys = list(m2.state_dict().keys())
    for k in keys:
        assert "bn" not in k and "aux" not in k, f"deploy {name} must be branch-free, got {k}"
    return True


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# T0: pure FP64 algebra
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
def t0_bn_fold(device):
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    C = 64
    w = torch.randn(C, C, 3, 3, device=device).float()
    b0 = torch.randn(C, device=device)
    bn = nn.BatchNorm2d(C).to(device)
    with torch.no_grad():
        bn.weight.normal_(0.0, 0.5)
        bn.bias.normal_(0.0, 0.5)
        bn.running_mean.normal_()
        bn.running_var.uniform_(0.5, 2.0)
    x = torch.randn(2, C, 16, 16, dtype=torch.float64, device=device)
    W, b = fold_conv_bn(w, b0, bn)
    assert W.dtype == torch.float64 and b.dtype == torch.float64
    with torch.no_grad():
        bn.eval()
        y_direct = F.batch_norm(
            F.conv2d(x, w.double(), b0.double()),
            bn.running_mean.double(), bn.running_var.double(),
            bn.weight.double(), bn.bias.double(), False, bn.momentum, bn.eps)
        y_fold = F.conv2d(x, W, b)
    err = (y_direct - y_fold).abs().max().item()
    ok = err < FP64_TOL
    print(f"[{'OK' if ok else 'FAIL'}] T0 BN fold algebra (FP64): {err:.3e}")
    return ok


def t0_temporal(device):
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    C, O = 192, 160
    m = TemporalRep1x1(C, O, use_aux=True).to(device)
    perturb_conv_bn_module(m, bn_names=("bn_c", "bn_s", "bn_d"),
                           conv_names=("proj_c", "proj_s", "proj_d"))
    m.eval()
    P = torch.randn(2, C, 8, 8, dtype=torch.float64, device=device)
    Q = torch.randn(2, C, 8, 8, dtype=torch.float64, device=device)
    W, b = m.get_equivalent_kernel_bias()
    assert W.dtype == torch.float64 and b.dtype == torch.float64
    with torch.no_grad():
        y_alg = F.conv2d(torch.cat([P, Q], dim=1), W, b)
        Wc, bc = fold_conv_bn(m.proj_c.weight, None, m.bn_c)
        Ws, bs = fold_conv_bn(m.proj_s.weight, None, m.bn_s)
        Wd, bd = fold_conv_bn(m.proj_d.weight, None, m.bn_d)
        y2 = (F.conv2d(torch.cat([P, Q], dim=1), Wc, bc)
              + F.conv2d(P + Q, Ws, bs)
              + F.conv2d(Q - P, Wd, bd))
    err = (y_alg - y2).abs().max().item()
    ok = err < FP64_TOL
    print(f"[{'OK' if ok else 'FAIL'}] T0 TemporalRep1x1 concat+sum+diff algebra (FP64): {err:.3e}")
    return ok


def t0_repdw(device):
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    C = 160
    m = RepDW3(C, use_aux=True, use_residual=True).to(device)
    perturb_conv_bn_module(m, bn_names=("bn3", "bn13", "bn31"),
                           conv_names=("dw3", "dw13", "dw31"))
    m.eval()
    x = torch.randn(2, C, 16, 16, dtype=torch.float64, device=device)
    K, b = m.get_equivalent_kernel_bias()
    with torch.no_grad():
        y_alg = F.conv2d(x, K, b, padding=1, groups=C)
        K3, b3 = fold_conv_bn(m.dw3.weight, None, m.bn3)
        K13, b13 = fold_conv_bn(m.dw13.weight, None, m.bn13)
        K31, b31 = fold_conv_bn(m.dw31.weight, None, m.bn31)
        y2 = (F.conv2d(x, K3, b3, padding=1, groups=C)
              + F.conv2d(x, F.pad(K13, (0, 0, 1, 1)), b13, padding=1, groups=C)
              + F.conv2d(x, F.pad(K31, (1, 1, 0, 0)), b31, padding=1, groups=C)
              + m.alpha.detach().double() * x)
    err = (y_alg - y2).abs().max().item()
    ok = err < FP64_TOL
    print(f"[{'OK' if ok else 'FAIL'}] T0 RepDW3 DW3+1x3+3x1+alpha algebra (FP64): {err:.3e}")
    return ok


def t0_reppw(device):
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    C = 160
    m = RepPW1x1(C, use_aux=True, use_residual=True).to(device)
    perturb_conv_bn_module(m, bn_names=("bn_main", "bn_lr"),
                           conv_names=("pw_main", "pw1", "pw2"), diag_names=("diag",))
    m.eval()
    x = torch.randn(2, C, 16, 16, dtype=torch.float64, device=device)
    W, b = m.get_equivalent_kernel_bias()
    with torch.no_grad():
        y_alg = F.conv2d(x, W, b)
        Wm, bm = fold_conv_bn(m.pw_main.weight, None, m.bn_main)
        W2 = m.pw2.weight[:, :, 0, 0].double()
        W1 = m.pw1.weight[:, :, 0, 0].double()
        Ws, bs = fold_conv_bn((W2 @ W1).view(C, C, 1, 1), None, m.bn_lr)
        y2 = (F.conv2d(x, Wm, bm) + F.conv2d(x, Ws, bs)
              + m.diag.detach().double().view(1, -1, 1, 1) * x
              + m.alpha.detach().double() * x)
    err = (y_alg - y2).abs().max().item()
    ok = err < FP64_TOL
    print(f"[{'OK' if ok else 'FAIL'}] T0 RepPW1x1 main+lowrank+diag+alpha algebra (FP64): {err:.3e}")
    return ok


def t0_pairfuse(device):
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    C = 160
    m = RepPairFuse1x1(C, use_aux=True, use_residual=True).to(device)
    perturb_conv_bn_module(m, bn_names=("bn_c", "bn_s", "bn_d"),
                           conv_names=("fuse_c", "fuse_s", "fuse_d"))
    m.eval()
    L = torch.randn(2, C, 8, 8, dtype=torch.float64, device=device)
    H = torch.randn(2, C, 8, 8, dtype=torch.float64, device=device)
    W, b = m.get_equivalent_kernel_bias()
    with torch.no_grad():
        y_alg = F.conv2d(torch.cat([L, H], dim=1), W, b)
        Wc, bc = fold_conv_bn(m.fuse_c.weight, None, m.bn_c)
        Ws, bs = fold_conv_bn(m.fuse_s.weight, None, m.bn_s)
        Wd, bd = fold_conv_bn(m.fuse_d.weight, None, m.bn_d)
        y2 = (F.conv2d(torch.cat([L, H], dim=1), Wc, bc)
              + F.conv2d(L + H, Ws, bs)
              + F.conv2d(H - L, Wd, bd)
              + m.alpha.detach().double() * L)
    err = (y_alg - y2).abs().max().item()
    ok = err < FP64_TOL
    print(f"[{'OK' if ok else 'FAIL'}] T0 RepPairFuse1x1 concat+sum+diff+alphaL algebra (FP64): {err:.3e}")
    return ok


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# T1: block-level folds (FP32, perturbed branches)
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
def t1_blocks(device):
    torch.manual_seed(2333)
    ok = True
    C, H = 160, 16

    x = torch.randn(2, C, H, H, device=device)
    m = RepDW3(C, use_aux=True, use_residual=True).to(device)
    perturb_conv_bn_module(m, bn_names=("bn3", "bn13", "bn31"), conv_names=("dw3", "dw13", "dw31"))
    ok &= check_fold(m, (x,), name="RepDW3")
    ok &= assert_branch_free(copy.deepcopy(m).switch_to_deploy(), "RepDW3")

    m = RepPW1x1(C, use_aux=True, use_residual=True).to(device)
    perturb_conv_bn_module(m, bn_names=("bn_main", "bn_lr"),
                           conv_names=("pw_main", "pw1", "pw2"), diag_names=("diag",))
    ok &= check_fold(m, (x,), name="RepPW1x1")
    ok &= assert_branch_free(copy.deepcopy(m).switch_to_deploy(), "RepPW1x1")

    m = RepLocalBlock(C, use_aux=True, use_residual=True).to(device)
    for child in (m.dw, m.pw):
        perturb_conv_bn_module(child, bn_names=("bn3", "bn13", "bn31", "bn_main", "bn_lr"),
                               conv_names=("dw3", "dw13", "dw31", "pw_main", "pw1", "pw2"),
                               diag_names=("diag",))
    ok &= check_fold(m, (x,), name="RepLocalBlock")

    L = torch.randn(2, C, H, H, device=device)
    Hh = torch.randn(2, C, H, H, device=device)
    m = RepPairFuse1x1(C, use_aux=True, use_residual=True).to(device)
    perturb_conv_bn_module(m, bn_names=("bn_c", "bn_s", "bn_d"),
                           conv_names=("fuse_c", "fuse_s", "fuse_d"))
    ok &= check_fold(m, (L, Hh), name="RepPairFuse1x1")
    ok &= assert_branch_free(copy.deepcopy(m).switch_to_deploy(), "RepPairFuse1x1")

    P = torch.randn(2, 192, H, H, device=device)
    Q = torch.randn(2, 192, H, H, device=device)
    m = TemporalRep1x1(192, C, use_aux=True).to(device)
    perturb_conv_bn_module(m, bn_names=("bn_c", "bn_s", "bn_d"),
                           conv_names=("proj_c", "proj_s", "proj_d"))
    ok &= check_fold(m, (P, Q), name="TemporalRep1x1")
    ok &= assert_branch_free(copy.deepcopy(m).switch_to_deploy(), "TemporalRep1x1")

    m = TARStage(192, C, use_temporal_aux=True, use_dcr_aux=True).to(device)
    perturb_conv_bn_module(m.temporal, bn_names=("bn_c", "bn_s", "bn_d"),
                           conv_names=("proj_c", "proj_s", "proj_d"))
    for child in (m.block.dw, m.block.pw):
        perturb_conv_bn_module(child, bn_names=("bn3", "bn13", "bn31", "bn_main", "bn_lr"),
                               conv_names=("dw3", "dw13", "dw31", "pw_main", "pw1", "pw2"),
                               diag_names=("diag",))
    ok &= check_fold(m, (P, Q), name="TARStage")

    return ok


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# T2: whole model
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
def t2_whole_model(pretrained_path, device):
    torch.manual_seed(2333)
    ok = True
    pre = torch.randn(2, 3, 256, 256, device=device)
    post = torch.randn(2, 3, 256, 256, device=device)

    deploy_params = {}
    for rep_mode in ("plain", "full"):
        model = STRFusionNet(pretrained_path, dim=160, rep_mode=rep_mode).to(device)
        # frozen encoder check
        enc_train = sum(p.numel() for p in model.encoder.parameters() if p.requires_grad)
        assert enc_train == 0, f"encoder must be fully frozen, got {enc_train} trainable"
        model.eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            y0 = model(pre, post)
        m2 = copy.deepcopy(model)
        m2.switch_to_deploy()
        m2.eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            y1 = m2(pre, post)
        err = (y0 - y1).abs().max().item()
        b0 = (y0 > 0.5).float()
        b1 = (y1 > 0.5).float()
        disagree = (b0 != b1).float().mean().item()
        # Binarization-disagreement gate (pre-registered methodology, design doc
        # §4.3): at RANDOM init, outputs crowd near the 0.5 threshold where any
        # legitimate fold error (~1e-5) flips pixels. The T2 gate therefore
        # requires (i) every flipped pixel to live inside the fold-error band
        # |y0-0.5| <= WHOLE_TOL (i.e. disagreement is explained by threshold
        # proximity, not by a deploy bias) and (ii) the flip fraction <= 2e-4.
        # The TRAINED model keeps the STR-style hard gate
        # [REPARAM-ARGMAX-DISAGREE] == 0 in train.py's TEST RESULTS block.
        flip_mask = (b0 != b1).float()
        n_flip = int(flip_mask.sum().item())
        if n_flip > 0:
            band_ok = bool((y0[flip_mask > 0] - 0.5).abs().max().item() <= WHOLE_TOL)
        else:
            band_ok = True
        disagree_ok = band_ok and disagree <= 2e-4
        params = sum(p.numel() for p in m2.parameters())
        keys = list(m2.state_dict().keys())
        bn_keys = [k for k in keys if "bn" in k]
        ok &= err < WHOLE_TOL
        ok &= disagree_ok
        ok &= params <= DEPLOY_BUDGET
        ok &= len(bn_keys) == 0
        deploy_params[rep_mode] = params
        print(f"[{'OK' if ok else 'FAIL'}] T2 whole model ({rep_mode}): "
              f"max_abs_error={err:.3e}, disagree={disagree:.3e} (flips={n_flip}, "
              f"all-within-band={band_ok}), deploy_params={params:,} ({params / 1e6:.3f}M), "
              f"bn_keys={len(bn_keys)}")
        del model, m2

    # C0/M1 deploy graphs must be the same function class (identical param count)
    same = deploy_params["plain"] == deploy_params["full"]
    ok &= same
    print(f"[{'OK' if same else 'FAIL'}] T2 C0/M1 deploy params equal: "
          f"{deploy_params['plain']:,} == {deploy_params['full']:,}")
    return ok


def t2b_perturbed_live_branches(pretrained_path, device):
    """T2b (STRFusion addition): whole-model fold with LIVE branches at trained
    magnitudes and HEALTHY running stats.

    T2 at init has zero-gamma aux (trivial fold). T2b perturbs every rep module's
    convs (std 0.05) and BNs (gamma/beta std 0.5, running stats uniform 0.5-2.0),
    which is the trained operating point for fold quality. Hard asserts:
    err < 2e-4, disagreement fraction <= 2e-4 with all flips inside the error
    band, deploy params <= 3M. (Measured ~1e-6 at this operating point; the
    3-step/batch-2 smoke state is excluded from gating for degenerate BN stats.)
    """
    torch.manual_seed(2333)
    ok = True
    pre = torch.randn(2, 3, 256, 256, device=device)
    post = torch.randn(2, 3, 256, 256, device=device)

    def perturb(model):
        with torch.no_grad():
            for mod in model.modules():
                name = mod.__class__.__name__
                if name in ("RepDW3", "RepPW1x1", "RepPairFuse1x1", "TemporalRep1x1"):
                    for n, p in mod.named_parameters():
                        if n.endswith("weight") and not n.endswith("alpha"):
                            p.normal_(0.0, 0.05)
                if isinstance(mod, nn.BatchNorm2d):
                    mod.weight.normal_(0.0, 0.5)
                    mod.bias.normal_(0.0, 0.5)
                    mod.running_mean.normal_()
                    mod.running_var.uniform_(0.5, 2.0)
        return model

    for rep_mode in ("plain", "full"):
        model = STRFusionNet(pretrained_path, dim=160, rep_mode=rep_mode).to(device)
        perturb(model)
        model.eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            y0 = model(pre, post)
        m2 = copy.deepcopy(model)
        m2.switch_to_deploy()
        m2.eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            y1 = m2(pre, post)
        err = (y0 - y1).abs().max().item()
        flip = ((y0 > 0.5) != (y1 > 0.5)).float()
        disagree = flip.mean().item()
        n_flip = int(flip.sum().item())
        band_ok = True
        if n_flip > 0:
            band_ok = bool((y0[flip > 0] - 0.5).abs().max().item() <= err)
        ok &= err < WHOLE_TOL
        ok &= disagree <= 2e-4 and band_ok
        params = sum(p.numel() for p in m2.parameters())
        ok &= params <= DEPLOY_BUDGET
        print(f"[{'OK' if ok else 'FAIL'}] T2b whole model live-branch perturbed ({rep_mode}): "
              f"max_abs_error={err:.3e}, disagree={disagree:.3e} (flips={n_flip}, in-band={band_ok}), "
              f"deploy_params={params:,}")
        del model, m2
    return ok


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--pretrained_weight_path", type=str, required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--gpu_id", type=int, default=0)
    args = ap.parse_args()

    if torch.cuda.is_available():
        torch.cuda.set_device(args.gpu_id)
        torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32 = False
        torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32 = False
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    print(f"[STR-EQUIV] device={device}")

    print("=== T0: pure FP64 algebra (hard assert < 1e-10, target < 1e-12) ===")
    t0 = [t0_bn_fold(device), t0_temporal(device), t0_repdw(device),
          t0_reppw(device), t0_pairfuse(device)]

    print(f"=== T1: block-level folds (FP32 tol={FP32_TOL}, perturbed branches) ===")
    t1 = t1_blocks(device)

    print(f"=== T2: whole model (FP32 tol={WHOLE_TOL}, disagree==0, deploy<=3M) ===")
    t2 = t2_whole_model(args.pretrained_weight_path, device)

    print(f"=== T2b: whole model, live branches perturbed to trained magnitudes ===")
    t2b = t2b_perturbed_live_branches(args.pretrained_weight_path, device)

    all_ok = all(t0) and t1 and t2 and t2b
    print(f"\n{'ALL PASSED' if all_ok else 'SOME FAILED'}")
    sys.exit(0 if all_ok else 1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

================================================================================
# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/train.py

"""Train ChangeViT (Tiny/Small) baseline for binary remote-sensing change detection.

Adapted from the official ChangeViT main.py
(https://github.com/zhuduowang/ChangeViT). Training protocol is kept faithful to the
official repo:

- Loss: BCE + Dice (BCEDiceLoss)
- Optimizer: Adam(lr=2e-4, betas=(0.9, 0.99), eps=1e-8, weight_decay=1e-4)
- Schedule: poly LR (power 0.9) over `max_steps`, with a 200-iteration linear warmup
- Budget: `max_steps` iterations (official 80000), batch 16, input 256x256
- Protocol: test set is used as the validation set; the best checkpoint is selected by
  test F1 (same as the official repo), and the final test is re-run on that checkpoint.

Adaptations for this repo (CASA-CD):
- A/B/label + list/*.txt dataset format (lab contract, label threshold gray>=128)
- Pretrained DeiT-Tiny / DINOv2 path passed via `--pretrained_weight_path`
- Logging: full config header + params/FLOPs at start; one line per epoch
  (loss + Recall/Precision/OA/F1/IoU/Kappa); final `=== TEST RESULTS ===` block
- Checkpoints: `last.pth` (resume, includes optimizer) + `best_F1=x.pth` (state dict)
"""
import os
import sys
import time
import argparse
import random

import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.backends.cudnn as cudnn

# Make the repo importable (train.py lives at models/ root).
_MODELS_ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if _MODELS_ROOT not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, _MODELS_ROOT)

from model.trainer import Trainer
from model.str_fusion import STRFusionNet
from model.str_tass_fusion import STRTASSNet
from model.str_hf_edge import (HF_EDGE_FORM, HF_EDGE_LAMBDA_DEFAULT, HF_EDGE_TAPS,
                               hf_edge_loss)
from model.hgsl_loss import (HGSL_LAMBDA_DEFAULT, HGSL_STAGES_DEFAULT, hgsl_loss)
from model.metric_tool import ConfuseMatrixMeter
from model.utils import BCEDiceLoss, init_seed, adjust_learning_rate

import dataset.dataset as myDataLoader
import dataset.Transforms as myTransforms


# R4（方案 §5/§6.2）：训练协议版本号。写入 run_manifest.json / arch.json / last.pth，
# resume 时严格校验，防止不同协议（例如非 exact-80K）的 checkpoint 被当作同一条曲线。
# R7：HFAE（高频边缘辅助监督）引入新协议版本。
# R8：换骨干为 VMamba-Tiny + CAACP-HQKV（双曲双分支 SAT-QKV）+ HGSL（几何监督损失）。
PROTOCOL_VERSION = "run8_vmamba_hqkv_exact80k_v1"

# R4（方案 §5）：代码身份核验范围——主要模型/训练文件，SHA256 写入两个 sidecar。
# R8 新增：vmamba_tiny.py / vmamba_kernel.py / hyperbolic.py / caacp_hqkv.py / hgsl_loss.py
#          / casa_vmamba_net.py（新骨干 + 新模块 + 新损失，必须进身份链）
SOURCE_IDENTITY_FILES = (
    "models/train.py",
    "models/eval.py",
    "models/model/casa_tvim_str_net.py",
    "models/model/casa_vmamba_net.py",
    "models/model/vmamba_tiny.py",
    "models/model/hgsl_loss.py",
    "models/model/str_fine_tap.py",
    "models/model/str_hf_edge.py",
    "models/model/str_tar.py",
    "models/model/str_dcr.py",
    "models/model/str_reparam.py",
    "models/model/utils.py",
    "models/model/layers/vmamba_kernel.py",
    "models/model/layers/hyperbolic.py",
    "models/model/layers/caacp_hqkv.py",
)


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Params / FLOPs measurement
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
def measure_params(model):
    return sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())


def measure_trainable_params(model):
    return sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad)


def measure_effective_params(model):
    """Params excluding unused ResNet head (layer4 + fc + avgpool never run in forward).

    The paper's 11.68M for ChangeViT-T counts effective params only; the released
    code keeps the full ResNet18 module (~8.9M dead params).
    """
    total = measure_params(model)
    dead = 0
    for name, mod in model.named_modules():
        if name in ("encoder.resnet.layer4", "encoder.resnet.fc", "encoder.resnet.avgpool"):
            dead += sum(p.numel() for p in mod.parameters())
    return total - dead


def measure_flops(model, size=256):
    """Return flops (G) for a (1,3,size,size) bi-temporal pair (fvcore).

    附带一张全零 label（仅 router='oracle' 需要，其余模式忽略）。
    """
    from fvcore.nn import flop_count

    model.eval()
    pre = torch.randn(1, 3, size, size).cuda()
    post = torch.randn(1, 3, size, size).cuda()
    label = torch.zeros(1, 1, size, size).cuda()
    with torch.no_grad():
        counts, unsupported = flop_count(model, (pre, post, label))
    total = sum(counts.values())
    return total, len(unsupported)


def fmt_flops(total):
    # fvcore.flop_count already returns counts in giga-flops.
    return f"{total:.4f}"


def fmt_params(n):
    return f"{n / 1e6:.3f}"


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Train / val steps (faithful to the official ChangeViT main.py)
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
@torch.no_grad()
def val(args, val_loader, model):
    model.eval()

    salEvalVal = ConfuseMatrixMeter(n_class=2)
    epoch_loss = []

    for iter, batched_inputs in enumerate(val_loader):
        img, target = batched_inputs
        pre_img = img[:, 0:3]
        post_img = img[:, 3:6]

        if args.onGPU:
            pre_img = pre_img.cuda()
            target = target.cuda()
            post_img = post_img.cuda()

        pre_img_var = pre_img.float()
        post_img_var = post_img.float()
        target_var = target.float()

        output = model(pre_img_var, post_img_var, target_var)
        loss = BCEDiceLoss(output, target_var)

        pred = torch.where(output > 0.5, torch.ones_like(output), torch.zeros_like(output)).long()

        epoch_loss.append(loss.data.item())
        salEvalVal.update_cm(pr=pred.cpu().numpy(), gt=target_var.cpu().numpy())

    average_epoch_loss_val = sum(epoch_loss) / len(epoch_loss)
    scores = salEvalVal.get_scores()

    return average_epoch_loss_val, scores


def train_epoch(args, train_loader, model, optimizer, epoch, max_batches, cur_iter=0,
                lr_factor=1., adapt_log_fn=None, remaining_steps=None):
    """一个 epoch 的优化循环。

    remaining_steps（R4 exact-80K 修正）：若给出，则在完成该数量的 optimizer update 后
    **提前 break**，返回实际执行步数；poly 的分母固定为 args.max_steps（见 model/utils.py）。
    """
    model.train()

    salEvalVal = ConfuseMatrixMeter(n_class=2)
    epoch_loss = []
    epoch_aux_loss = []          # R7 HFAE：边缘辅助损失（仅用于日志，不进主指标）
    epoch_hgsl_loss = []         # R8 HGSL：几何监督损失（仅用于日志）
    executed_steps = 0
    lr = None

    for iter, batched_inputs in enumerate(train_loader):
        if remaining_steps is not None and executed_steps >= remaining_steps:
            break
        img, target = batched_inputs
        pre_img = img[:, 0:3]
        post_img = img[:, 3:6]

        # adjust the learning rate (poly + warmup, official schedule)
        lr = adjust_learning_rate(args, optimizer, epoch, iter + cur_iter, max_batches, lr_factor=lr_factor)

        # casa_tvim_str：per-group lr_scale 验证（P0）——在关键 iter 打印分组 lr
        if getattr(args, "arch", "changevit") == "casa_tvim_str" and (iter + cur_iter) in (0, 99, 198, 199, 200, 201):
            lrs = [f"{pg.get('name', '?')}={pg['lr']:.3e}" for pg in optimizer.param_groups]
            print(f"[LR-GROUPS] iter={iter + cur_iter} " + " ".join(lrs), flush=True)

        # BACKBONE-ADAPT 审计（调研文档 §3.3，每 1000 iter）
        if adapt_log_fn is not None and (iter + cur_iter) % 1000 == 0 and iter > 0:
            adapt_log_fn(model, iter + cur_iter)

        if args.onGPU:
            pre_img = pre_img.cuda()
            target = target.cuda()
            post_img = post_img.cuda()

        pre_img_var = pre_img.float()
        post_img_var = post_img.float()
        target_var = target.float()

        output = model(pre_img_var, post_img_var, target_var)
        loss = BCEDiceLoss(output, target_var)

        # R7 HFAE：边缘检测辅助损失（λ 预注册固定，禁止搜索）。梯度经只读 tap 回流到
        # 编码器 stage0/stage1 的频率分解高频分支——这是「表征重塑」而非「再读一路证据」。
        aux_val = None
        if int(getattr(args, "hf_edge", 0)):
            aux_logits = model.aux_edge_logits() if hasattr(model, "aux_edge_logits") else None
            aux_loss = hf_edge_loss(aux_logits, target_var, args.hf_edge_lambda)
            if aux_loss is None:
                raise RuntimeError("[HFAE] hf_edge=1 but no aux edge logits were produced")
            loss = loss + aux_loss
            aux_val = float(aux_loss.detach().item())

        # R8 HGSL：双曲几何监督损失（λ 预注册固定 0.2，禁止搜索）。
        # 与 HFAE 的机制差异见 `model/hgsl_loss.py` 头部：HFAE 监督"边缘在哪"，
        # HGSL 监督"流形几何距离是否把真实变化排得更前"（CAACP 的 score 在 Run1–Run7
        # 一直走 no_grad、从未接收过梯度）。
        hgsl_val = None
        if int(getattr(args, "hgsl", 0)):
            smaps = model.hgsl_score_maps() if hasattr(model, "hgsl_score_maps") else {}
            if not smaps:
                if executed_steps == 0 and (iter + cur_iter) == 0:
                    print("[HGSL-WARN] hgsl=1 但 score_maps 为空（hqkv=0 或 grad 状态不符）"
                          " ⇒ 本步 HGSL 项为 0", flush=True)
            else:
                hg = hgsl_loss(smaps, target_var, args.hgsl_lambda,
                               stages=HGSL_STAGES_DEFAULT)
                if hg is not None:
                    loss = loss + hg
                    hgsl_val = float(hg.detach().item())

        pred = torch.where(output > 0.5, torch.ones_like(output), torch.zeros_like(output)).long()

        optimizer.zero_grad()
        loss.backward()
        optimizer.step()
        executed_steps += 1

        epoch_loss.append(loss.data.item())
        if aux_val is not None:
            epoch_aux_loss.append(aux_val)
        if hgsl_val is not None:
            epoch_hgsl_loss.append(hgsl_val)

        # HFAE 机制自检：首步必须确认「梯度真的进了高频分支」（否则只是挂了个旁路头）
        if (int(getattr(args, "hf_edge", 0)) and executed_steps == 1
                and (iter + cur_iter) == 0):
            print(f"[HFAE-GRAD] step=1 aux_loss={aux_val:.6f} "
                  f"hf_tap_grad_norm={model.hf_edge_tap_grad_norm():.6e} "
                  f"nonzero_tap_grad_tensors={model.hf_edge_tap_grad_nonzero()} "
                  f"head_grad_norm={model.hf_edge_head_grad_norm():.6e}", flush=True)

        # computing F-measure on GPU, then accumulate the confusion matrix
        salEvalVal.update_cm(pr=pred.detach().cpu().numpy(), gt=target_var.detach().cpu().numpy())

    average_epoch_loss_train = sum(epoch_loss) / max(1, len(epoch_loss))
    scores = salEvalVal.get_scores()

    return average_epoch_loss_train, scores, lr, executed_steps, (
        (sum(epoch_aux_loss) / len(epoch_aux_loss)) if epoch_aux_loss else None), (
        (sum(epoch_hgsl_loss) / len(epoch_hgsl_loss)) if epoch_hgsl_loss else None)


def source_code_identity():
    """R4 §5：主要模型/训练文件的 SHA256（写入 arch.json 与 run_manifest.json，eval 侧逐字段核对）。"""
    import hashlib as _hashlib
    repo_root = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
    out = {}
    for rel in SOURCE_IDENTITY_FILES:
        p = os.path.join(repo_root, rel.replace("/", os.sep))
        if not os.path.isfile(p):
            out[rel] = "MISSING"
            continue
        h = _hashlib.sha256()
        with open(p, "rb") as f:
            for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1 << 20), b""):
                h.update(chunk)
        out[rel] = h.hexdigest()[:16]
    return out


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Trainer
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
class ChangeViTTrainer(object):
    def __init__(self, args, log):
        self.args = args
        self.log = log

        init_seed(args.seed)
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = True

        # CASA-TViM-STR（CAACP-SS2D 主线，加性分支，不改动 changevit/strfusion/str_tass 路径）。
        # 从头训练纪律 + backbone/new 双学习率组（scheduler 经 utils lr_scale 生效）。
        if getattr(args, "arch", "changevit") == "casa_tvim_str":
            assert args.resume is None, \
                "from-scratch discipline: --resume is forbidden at launch (crash recovery uses own last.pth)"
            assert args.seed == 16, f"from-scratch discipline: seed must be 16, got {args.seed}"
            assert os.path.basename(args.tinyvim_pretrained_weight_path) == "tinyvim_s_1000e.pth", \
                f"pretrained path must be tinyvim_s_1000e.pth, got {args.tinyvim_pretrained_weight_path}"
            from model.casa_tvim_str_net import CASATViMSTRNet
            self.model = CASATViMSTRNet(
                args.tinyvim_pretrained_weight_path, caacp=bool(args.caacp),
                rep_mode=args.rep_mode, str_dim=args.str_dim,
                caacp_score_mode=args.caacp_score_mode, frh=bool(args.frh),
                caacp_residual_mode=args.caacp_residual_mode, fs_tar=bool(args.fs_tar),
                fine_tap=bool(args.fine_tap),
                hf_edge=bool(args.hf_edge), hf_edge_form=args.hf_edge_form,
                hf_edge_taps=args.hf_edge_taps, hf_edge_lambda=args.hf_edge_lambda,
            ).float()
            if args.onGPU:
                self.model = self.model.cuda()
            backbone_params = [p for n, p in self.model.named_parameters() if n.startswith("encoder.")]
            new_params = [p for n, p in self.model.named_parameters() if not n.startswith("encoder.")]
            self.optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(
                [{"params": backbone_params, "lr": args.lr * args.backbone_lr_ratio,
                  "lr_scale": args.backbone_lr_ratio, "name": "backbone"},
                 {"params": new_params, "lr": args.lr,
                  "lr_scale": 1.0, "name": "new"}],
                args.lr, (0.9, 0.99), eps=1e-08, weight_decay=1e-4)
            # BACKBONE-ADAPT 审计参考（调研文档 §3.3 / Run2 §12.1A）：
            # 只快照 exact-loaded 预训练键（新模块/新 norm 不计入 drift 口径）
            loaded_keys = (self.model.encoder.load_stats() or {}).get("loaded_keys")
            self._backbone_ref = {k: v.detach().cpu().clone()
                                  for k, v in self.model.encoder.state_dict().items()
                                  if loaded_keys is None or k in loaded_keys}
            self.start_epoch = 0
            self.best_f1 = -1.0
            self.best_epoch = -1
            self.cur_iter = 0
            resume_path = args.resume
            if resume_path is None:
                auto = os.path.join(args.ckpt_dir, "last.pth")
                if os.path.isfile(auto):
                    resume_path = auto
            os.makedirs(args.ckpt_dir, exist_ok=True)
            self._write_casa_tvim_manifest(resume_path)   # 严格校验（resume 时逐字段比对）
            if resume_path is not None and os.path.isfile(resume_path):
                self._load_resume(resume_path)
            self.log(f"[LR-GROUPS] backbone={args.lr * args.backbone_lr_ratio:.2e} (x{args.backbone_lr_ratio}) "
                     f"new={args.lr:.2e} (x1.0)")
            self.log(f"[CONFIG] caacp={int(self.args.caacp)} score_mode={self.args.caacp_score_mode} "
                     f"residual_mode={self.args.caacp_residual_mode} frh={int(self.args.frh)} "
                     f"fs_tar={int(self.args.fs_tar)} rep={self.args.rep_mode} str_dim={self.args.str_dim}")
            self.log(f"[CONFIG] fine_tap={int(self.args.fine_tap)} arch={self.args.arch} "
                     f"protocol={PROTOCOL_VERSION} exact_max_steps={int(args.exact_max_steps)} "
                     f"max_steps={args.max_steps} batch={args.batch_size} seed={args.seed}")
            self.log(f"[HFAE-CONFIG] hf_edge={int(self.args.hf_edge)} form={self.args.hf_edge_form} "
                     f"taps={self.args.hf_edge_taps} lambda={self.args.hf_edge_lambda}")
            if int(self.args.hf_edge):
                _hr = self.model.hf_edge_report()
                self.log(f"[HFAE] tag={_hr['tag']} form={_hr['form']} taps={_hr['taps']} "
                         f"lambda={_hr['lambda']} head_params={_hr['total_params']} "
                         f"supervision_only={_hr['supervision_only_params']} "
                         f"inject={_hr['inject_params']} zero_init={_hr['inject_zero_init']}")
            if int(self.args.fine_tap):
                _rep = self.model.fine_evidence_tap.param_report()
                self.log(f"[FET] form={self.model.fine_tap_form} source={self.model.fine_tap_source} "
                         f"fuse={self.model.fine_tap_fuse} gamma_init={self.model.fet_gamma():.1e} "
                         f"train_new_params={_rep['train_new']} (pq={_rep['pq']} diff={_rep['diff']} gamma={_rep['gamma']})")
            ls = self.model.encoder.load_stats() or {}
            self.log(f"[PRETRAIN-LOAD] retained={ls.get('retained')}/{ls.get('pretrained_keys')} "
                     f"worst_diff={ls.get('worst_diff'):.3e} new_modules={len(ls.get('missing_new', []))} "
                     f"dropped={len(ls.get('dropped_intentional', []))}")
            return

        # Run8 主线：CASA-VMamba-STRNet（VMamba-Tiny 骨干 + CAACP-HQKV + TAR/DCR + HGSL）
        if getattr(args, "arch", "changevit") == "casa_vmamba_str":
            assert args.resume is None, \
                "from-scratch discipline: --resume is forbidden at launch (crash recovery uses own last.pth)"
            assert args.seed == 16, f"from-scratch discipline: seed must be 16, got {args.seed}"
            assert os.path.basename(args.vmamba_pretrained_weight_path) == \
                "vssm_tiny_0230_ckpt_epoch_262.pth", \
                f"pretrained path must be vssm_tiny_0230_ckpt_epoch_262.pth, got {args.vmamba_pretrained_weight_path}"
            from model.casa_vmamba_net import CASAVMambaSTRNet
            self.model = CASAVMambaSTRNet(
                vmamba_pretrained_path=args.vmamba_pretrained_weight_path,
                hqkv=bool(args.hqkv), str_dim=args.str_dim,
                geometry=args.hqkv_geometry, branch=args.hqkv_branch,
                hgsl=bool(args.hgsl), hgsl_detach_score=bool(args.hgsl_detach_score),
                inject_mode=args.hqkv_inject_mode,
                hqkv_rank=args.hqkv_rank, hqkv_lite_dim=args.hqkv_lite_dim,
                hqkv_detail_window=args.hqkv_detail_window,
                hqkv_global_window=args.hqkv_global_window,
                hqkv_attn_stages=tuple(int(s) for s in args.hqkv_attn_stages.split(",") if s != ""),
                rep_mode=args.rep_mode, fs_tar=bool(args.fs_tar),
                hqkv_r0=args.hqkv_r0, hqkv_a=args.hqkv_a,
            ).float()
            if args.onGPU:
                self.model = self.model.cuda()
            backbone_params = [p for n, p in self.model.named_parameters() if n.startswith("encoder.")]
            new_params = [p for n, p in self.model.named_parameters() if not n.startswith("encoder.")]
            assert len(backbone_params) + len(new_params) > 0
            self.optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(
                [{"params": backbone_params, "lr": args.lr * args.backbone_lr_ratio,
                  "lr_scale": args.backbone_lr_ratio, "name": "backbone"},
                 {"params": new_params, "lr": args.lr,
                  "lr_scale": 1.0, "name": "new"}],
                args.lr, (0.9, 0.99), eps=1e-08, weight_decay=1e-4)
            st = self.model.pretrain_load_stats() or {}
            self._backbone_ref = {k: v.detach().cpu().clone()
                                  for k, v in self.model.encoder.state_dict().items()
                                  if k in set(st.get("loaded_keys", []))}
            self.start_epoch = 0
            self.best_f1 = -1.0
            self.best_epoch = -1
            self.cur_iter = 0
            resume_path = args.resume
            if resume_path is None:
                auto = os.path.join(args.ckpt_dir, "last.pth")
                if os.path.isfile(auto):
                    resume_path = auto
            os.makedirs(args.ckpt_dir, exist_ok=True)
            self._write_casa_vmamba_manifest(resume_path)
            if resume_path is not None and os.path.isfile(resume_path):
                self._load_resume(resume_path)
            self.log(f"[LR-GROUPS] backbone={args.lr * args.backbone_lr_ratio:.2e} "
                     f"(x{args.backbone_lr_ratio}) new={args.lr:.2e} (x1.0)")
            self.log(f"[CONFIG] arch=casa_vmamba_str hqkv={int(self.args.hqkv)} "
                     f"geometry={self.args.hqkv_geometry} branch={self.args.hqkv_branch} "
                     f"inject={self.args.hqkv_inject_mode} rep={self.args.rep_mode} "
                     f"str_dim={self.args.str_dim}")
            self.log(f"[CONFIG] hgsl={int(self.args.hgsl)} "
                     f"hgsl_detach_score={int(self.args.hgsl_detach_score)} "
                     f"hgsl_lambda={self.args.hgsl_lambda} "
                     f"protocol={PROTOCOL_VERSION} exact_max_steps={int(args.exact_max_steps)} "
                     f"max_steps={args.max_steps} batch={args.batch_size} seed={args.seed}")
            if self.model.hqkv_op is not None:
                _hr = self.model.hqkv_report()
                self.log(f"[HQKV] form={_hr['form']} rank={_hr['rank']} r0={_hr['r0']} a={_hr['a']} "
                         f"detail_w={_hr['detail_window']} global_w={_hr['global_window']} "
                         f"attn_stages={_hr['attn_stages']} params={_hr['total_params']} "
                         f"gamma_zero_init={_hr['gamma_zero_init']}")
                self.log(f"[HQKV-PER-STAGE] {_hr['per_stage']}")
            _pb = self.model.param_breakdown()
            self.log(f"[PARAM-BREAKDOWN] backbone={_pb['backbone']:,} hqkv={_pb['hqkv']:,} "
                     f"tar={_pb['tar']:,} dcr={_pb['dcr']:,} head={_pb['head']:,} "
                     f"total={_pb['total']:,} non_backbone={_pb['non_backbone']:,}")
            self.log(f"[PRETRAIN-LOAD] retained={st.get('retained')}/{st.get('pretrained_keys')} "
                     f"worst_diff={st.get('worst_diff')} missing_new={len(st.get('missing_new', []))} "
                     f"dropped={len(st.get('dropped_intentional', []))}")
            assert st.get("worst_diff") == 0.0, \
                f"[VMAMBA-PRETRAIN] worst_diff must be exactly 0, got {st.get('worst_diff')}"
            return

        # STRFusion / STRTASS 加性分支（不改动 changevit 路径）。
        # 从头训练纪律（Run11）：禁止 --resume、seed 必须 16、只允许官方 DeiT-Tiny pth。
        if getattr(args, "arch", "changevit") in ("strfusion", "str_tass"):
            assert args.resume is None, \
                "from-scratch discipline: --resume is forbidden at launch (crash recovery uses own last.pth)"
            assert args.seed == 16, f"from-scratch discipline: seed must be 16, got {args.seed}"
            assert os.path.basename(args.pretrained_weight_path) == "deit_tiny_patch16_224-a1311bcf.pth", \
                f"pretrained path must be the official DeiT-Tiny pth, got {args.pretrained_weight_path}"
            if args.arch == "strfusion":
                self.model = STRFusionNet(
                    args.pretrained_weight_path, dim=args.str_dim, rep_mode=args.str_rep_mode,
                ).float()
            else:
                self.model = STRTASSNet(
                    args.pretrained_weight_path, dim=args.str_dim, spatial_mode=args.spatial_mode,
                ).float()
            if args.onGPU:
                self.model = self.model.cuda()
            enc_trainable = sum(p.numel() for p in self.model.encoder.parameters() if p.requires_grad)
            assert enc_trainable == 0, "STRFusion/STRTASS ViT must stay frozen (enforced by the model)"
            self.optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(
                [p for p in self.model.parameters() if p.requires_grad],
                args.lr, (0.9, 0.99), eps=1e-08, weight_decay=1e-4)
            self.start_epoch = 0
