        train_loader.generator.set_state(checkpoint['loader_rng_state'])
        best_f1, best_epoch = checkpoint["best_f1"], checkpoint["best_epoch"]
        manifest = dict(checkpoint["manifest"])
        continuation = dict(start_epoch=start_epoch, start_step=step,
                            remaining_steps=actual_steps-step, source_files=identity,
                            rng_state="restored" if restored_rng else "reseeded_2333",
                            session_started=time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"))
        manifest["continuations"] = [*manifest.get("continuations", []), continuation]
        for parent in [args.output_dir, args.checkpoint_dir]:
            (parent / "continuations.json").write_text(
                json.dumps(manifest["continuations"], indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        print(f"[RESUME] {json.dumps(continuation)}", flush=True)
        del checkpoint
    else:
        for parent in [args.output_dir, args.checkpoint_dir]:
            (parent / "run_manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
            (parent / "arch.json").write_text(json.dumps(dict(arch="casa_moe", module=args.module,
                                                              str_dim=64), indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    evaluation_manifest = dict(protocol=PROTOCOL, deploy_verification=DEPLOY_POLICY,
                               tolerance=2e-4, source_files=identity, continuation=continuation,
                               selection_split="test", independent_test=False,
                               test_backend="training settings restored after fold verification")
    (args.output_dir / "evaluation_manifest.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(evaluation_manifest, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"[PROTOCOL-VERSION] {PROTOCOL}", flush=True)
    print(f"[CONFIG] {json.dumps(manifest)}", flush=True)
    print(f"[TOTAL-PARAMS] {params:.6f} M", flush=True)
    print(f"[MAX-STEPS] requested={args.max_steps} actual={actual_steps} epochs={epochs} train_batches={len(train_loader)}", flush=True)
    session_start_step = step
    started = time.time()
    torch.cuda.reset_peak_memory_stats()
    for epoch in range(start_epoch, epochs):
        model.train()
        epoch_loss, main_sum, guide_sum, tick = 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, time.time()
        for batch_index, (image, target) in enumerate(train_loader):
            image, target = image.to(device).float(), target.to(device).float()
            lr = learning_rate(5e-4, epoch, step, actual_steps)
            update_learning_rate(optimizer, lr)
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            outputs, auxiliary = model(image[:, :3], image[:, 3:], return_aux=True)
            if not all(torch.isfinite(x).all() for x in outputs):
                raise FloatingPointError(f"non-finite prediction at step {step}")
            loss, main_loss, auxiliary_loss = training_losses(outputs, auxiliary, target)
            if not torch.isfinite(loss):
                raise FloatingPointError(f"non-finite loss at step {step}")
            if epoch < 3 and batch_index == 2 and args.module != "none":
                print(f"[TASK-GRAD] {json.dumps(dict(epoch=epoch, step=step, norms=task_gradient_diagnostics(model, main_loss)))}", flush=True)
            loss.backward()
            torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), float("inf"),
                                            error_if_nonfinite=True, foreach=True)
            optimizer.step()
            epoch_loss += float(loss.detach())
            main_sum += float(main_loss.detach())
            guide_sum += float(auxiliary_loss.detach())
            step += 1
            if step % 100 == 0:
                print(f"[TRAIN] step={step}/{actual_steps} loss={float(loss.detach()):.5f} lr={lr:.8f} encoder_lr={lr*args.encoder_lr_mult:.8f}", flush=True)
        print(f"[ACTUAL-OPT-STEPS] epoch={epoch} executed={len(train_loader)} cumulative={step}/{actual_steps}", flush=True)
        print(f"[TRAIN-EPOCH] epoch={epoch} loss={epoch_loss/len(train_loader):.6f} main={main_sum/len(train_loader):.6f} guide={guide_sum/len(train_loader):.6f} lr={lr:.8f} encoder_lr={lr*args.encoder_lr_mult:.8f}", flush=True)
        model.context.collect_diag = True
        eval_loss, score, spatial = evaluate(model, test_loader, device, return_diagnostics=True)
        model.context.collect_diag = False
        print(f"Epoch {epoch}/{epochs} | Loss={eval_loss:.6f} | Recall={score['recall']:.6f} | Precision={score['precision']:.6f} | OA={score['OA']:.6f} | F1={score['F1']:.6f} | IoU={score['IoU']:.6f} | Kappa={score['Kappa']:.6f} | t={time.time()-tick:.1f}s", flush=True)
        if model.context.geometry != "none":
            print(f"[MODULE-DIAG] {json.dumps(model.diagnostics())}", flush=True)
        print(f"[SPATIAL-DIAG] {json.dumps(dict(epoch=epoch, split='test', **spatial))}", flush=True)
        if score["F1"] >= best_f1:
            best_f1, best_epoch = float(score["F1"]), epoch
            torch.save(model.state_dict(), args.checkpoint_dir / "best_model.pth")
        torch.save(dict(epoch=epoch + 1, step=step, state_dict=model.state_dict(),
                        optimizer=optimizer.state_dict(), best_f1=best_f1, best_epoch=best_epoch,
                        manifest=manifest, rng_state=rng_state(),
                        loader_rng_state=train_loader.generator.get_state()), args.checkpoint_dir / "last.pth")
    model.load_state_dict(torch.load(args.checkpoint_dir / "best_model.pth", map_location=device, weights_only=True))
    image, _ = next(iter(test_loader))
    image = image[:1].to(device).float()
    deploy, deployment = verify_deployment(model, image)
    difference, disagreement = deployment["max_diff"], deployment["binary_disagreement"]
    (args.output_dir / "deploy_check.json").write_text(json.dumps(deployment, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    test_loss, score, spatial = evaluate(deploy, test_loader, device, return_diagnostics=True)
    deploy_params = sum(p.numel() for p in deploy.parameters()) / 1e6
    result = dict(protocol=PROTOCOL, module=args.module, metrics=score, best_epoch=best_epoch,
                  selection_split="test", independent_test=False,
                  optimizer_steps=step, test_loss=test_loss, spatial_diagnostics=spatial, total_params_m=params,
                  deploy_params_m=deploy_params, elapsed_seconds=time.time()-started,
                  elapsed_scope="current_session" if args.resume else "complete_run",
                  session_optimizer_steps=step-session_start_step, continuation=continuation,
                  deploy_verification=DEPLOY_POLICY,
                  deploy_max_diff=difference, deploy_binary_disagreement=disagreement)
    (args.output_dir / "result.json").write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print("=== TEST RESULTS ===", flush=True)
    print(f"[MODE] casa_moe_{args.module}", flush=True)
    print(f"[TOTAL-PARAMS] {deploy_params:.6f} M", flush=True)
    print(f"[TRAINABLE-PARAMS] {params:.6f} M", flush=True)
    print(f"Recall={score['recall']:.6f} | Precision={score['precision']:.6f} | OA={score['OA']:.6f} | F1={score['F1']:.6f} | IoU={score['IoU']:.6f} | Kappa={score['Kappa']:.6f}", flush=True)
    print(f"[BEST-EPOCH] {best_epoch}", flush=True)
    print(f"[DEPLOY-CHECK] maxdiff={difference:.3e} binary_disagreement={disagreement}", flush=True)
    print(f"[TIME-SCOPE] {result['elapsed_scope']}", flush=True)
    print(f"[TIME] {time.time()-started:.1f}s [PEAK-MEMORY] {torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated()/1024**2:.0f} MiB", flush=True)
    print("=== END TEST RESULTS ===", flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

================================================================================
# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/train_stgeo.py
================================================================================
"""探索线 Exp1 训练入口（models_v1 专用，**不触碰 models/train.py**）。

设计原则：
  1. **协议与 Run8 逐项一致**（80,000 optimizer.step / batch 32 / 256² / seed 16 /
     Adam(2e-4, 0.9/0.99, wd=1e-4) / poly^0.9 + 200 iter warmup / test-as-val /
     prob > 0.5 / gray ≥ 128），因此已跑完的 `CTRL_R8` 可作为**同期同协议对照**；
  2. **日志格式与 Run8 相同**（`=== TEST RESULTS ===` … `=== END TEST RESULTS ===`，
     内含 `[ACTUAL-OPT-STEPS]`、`[PROTOCOL-VERSION]`），使 tools/archive/ 的现成解析器
     （`extract_metrics_to_excel.py` / `tvim_diag_common.parse_test_block`）可直接使用；
  3. **fail-closed**：run_manifest.json 逐字段校验；`--resume` 在启动时禁止
     （崩溃恢复只允许从自己的 last.pth 自动续训）。
用法（服务器）：
  cd models_v1 && CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1 python train_stgeo.py --dataset SYSU-CD-256 ...
"""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
import random
import sys
import time

import numpy as np
import torch

_MODELS_V1 = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if _MODELS_V1 not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, _MODELS_V1)

from model.casa_vmamba_stgeo_net import CASAVMambaSTGeoNet  # noqa: E402
from model.layers.st_geodesic import STGEO_FORM  # noqa: E402
from model.layers.stgeo_sup import STGEO_SUP_FORM  # noqa: E402
from model.metric_tool import ConfuseMatrixMeter  # noqa: E402
from model.utils import BCEDiceLoss, adjust_learning_rate, init_seed  # noqa: E402

import dataset.Transforms as myTransforms  # noqa: E402
import dataset.dataset as myDataLoader  # noqa: E402

PROTOCOL_VERSION = "exp1_stgeo_exact80k_v1"
MEAN6 = [0.406, 0.456, 0.485, 0.406, 0.456, 0.485]
STD6 = [0.225, 0.224, 0.229, 0.225, 0.224, 0.229]
SOURCE_FILES = (
    "models_v1/train_stgeo.py",
    "models_v1/model/casa_vmamba_stgeo_net.py",
    "models_v1/model/layers/st_geodesic.py",
    "models_v1/model/vmamba_tiny.py",
    "models_v1/model/layers/vmamba_kernel.py",
    "models_v1/model/str_tar.py",
    "models_v1/model/str_dcr.py",
    "models_v1/model/str_reparam.py",
    "models_v1/model/utils.py",
)


def log(msg):
    print(msg, flush=True)


def fmt_params(n):
    return f"{n / 1e6:.6f}"


def measure_params(model):
    return sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())


def source_code_identity():
    repo_root = os.path.dirname(_MODELS_V1)
    out = {}
    for rel in SOURCE_FILES:
        p = os.path.join(repo_root, rel.replace("/", os.sep))
        if not os.path.isfile(p):
            out[rel] = "MISSING"
            continue
        h = hashlib.sha256()
        with open(p, "rb") as f:
            for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1 << 20), b""):
                h.update(chunk)
        out[rel] = h.hexdigest()[:16]
    return out


def sha16(path):
    if not path or not os.path.isfile(path):
        return ""
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1 << 20), b""):
            h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()[:16]


def build_loader(args, list_path, batch_size, shuffle, train):
    if train:
        tf = myTransforms.Compose([
            myTransforms.Normalize(mean=args.mean, std=args.std),
            myTransforms.Scale(args.inWidth, args.inHeight),
            myTransforms.RandomCropResize(int(7. / 224. * args.inWidth)),
            myTransforms.RandomFlip(),
            myTransforms.RandomExchange(),
            myTransforms.ToTensor(),
        ])
    else:
        tf = myTransforms.Compose([
            myTransforms.Normalize(mean=args.mean, std=args.std),
            myTransforms.Scale(args.inWidth, args.inHeight),
            myTransforms.ToTensor(),
        ])
    ds = myDataLoader.Dataset(file_root=args.dataset_root, list_path=list_path,
                              transform=tf)
    return torch.utils.data.DataLoader(ds, batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=shuffle,
                                       num_workers=args.num_workers, pin_memory=True,
                                       drop_last=False)


@torch.no_grad()
def evaluate(args, loader, model):
    model.eval()
    meter = ConfuseMatrixMeter(n_class=2)
    losses = []
    for img, target in loader:
        pre, post = img[:, 0:3], img[:, 3:6]
        if args.onGPU:
            pre, post, target = pre.cuda(), post.cuda(), target.cuda()
        target = target.float()
        out = model(pre.float(), post.float(), target)
        losses.append(float(BCEDiceLoss(out, target).item()))
        pred = (out > 0.5).long()
        meter.update_cm(pr=pred.cpu().numpy(), gt=target.cpu().numpy())
    return sum(losses) / max(1, len(losses)), meter.get_scores()


def train_one_epoch(args, loader, model, optimizer, epoch, max_batches, cur_iter,
                    remaining):
    model.train()
    meter = ConfuseMatrixMeter(n_class=2)
    losses, executed, lr = [], 0, None
    aux_hist = []
    for it, (img, target) in enumerate(loader):
        if remaining is not None and executed >= remaining:
            break
        pre, post = img[:, 0:3], img[:, 3:6]
        lr = adjust_learning_rate(args, optimizer, epoch, it + cur_iter, max_batches)
        if args.onGPU:
            pre, post, target = pre.cuda(), post.cuda(), target.cuda()
        target = target.float()
        out = model(pre.float(), post.float(), target)
        loss = BCEDiceLoss(out, target)
        aux_val = None
        if args.stgeo == "sup":
            logit = model.aux_change_logits()
            if logit is None:
                raise RuntimeError("[STGEO-SUP] stgeo=sup 但没有产生辅助 logits")
            aux = model.stgeo_sup.loss(logit, target)
            loss = loss + aux
            aux_val = float(aux.detach())
        optimizer.zero_grad()
        loss.backward()
        if args.stgeo == "sup" and executed == 0 and (it + cur_iter) == 0:
            # **必须在 backward 之后**读梯度（曾把诊断写在 backward 之前，恒打印 0）
            gn = model.stgeo_sup.proj.weight.grad
            enc_gn = sum(float(p.grad.norm()) for n, p in model.encoder.named_parameters()
                         if p.grad is not None and "layers.0" in n)
            print(f"[STGEO-GRAD] step=1 aux_loss={aux_val:.6f} "
                  f"proj_grad_norm={(float(gn.norm()) if gn is not None else 0.0):.6e} "
                  f"encoder_stage0_grad_norm={enc_gn:.6e}", flush=True)
        optimizer.step()
        executed += 1
        losses.append(float(loss.item()))
        if aux_val is not None:
            aux_hist.append(aux_val)
        with torch.no_grad():
            pred = (out > 0.5).long()
            meter.update_cm(pr=pred.cpu().numpy(), gt=target.cpu().numpy())
    return (sum(losses) / max(1, len(losses)), meter.get_scores(), lr, executed,
            (sum(aux_hist) / len(aux_hist)) if aux_hist else None)


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser("Exp1 ST-GEO trainer (models_v1)")
    ap.add_argument("--dataset", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--dataset_root", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--train_list", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--test_list", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--ckpt_dir", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--vmamba_pretrained_weight_path", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--pretrained_weight_path", default=None,
                    help="历史占位（本架构不用 DeiT）")
    ap.add_argument("--inWidth", type=int, default=256)
    ap.add_argument("--inHeight", type=int, default=256)
    ap.add_argument("--max_steps", type=int, default=80000)
    ap.add_argument("--exact_max_steps", type=int, default=1, choices=[0, 1])
    ap.add_argument("--batch_size", type=int, default=32)
    ap.add_argument("--test_batch_size", type=int, default=16)
    ap.add_argument("--num_workers", type=int, default=4)
    ap.add_argument("--lr", type=float, default=2e-4)
    ap.add_argument("--lr_mode", default="poly")
    ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=16)
    ap.add_argument("--gpu_id", type=int, default=0)
    ap.add_argument("--resume", default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--arch", default="casa_vmamba_stgeo")
    ap.add_argument("--stgeo", default="inject")
    ap.add_argument("--stgeo_sigmas", default="1.0,2.0,4.0")
    ap.add_argument("--stgeo_mid", type=int, default=32)
    ap.add_argument("--stgeo_sup_small", type=int, default=16)
    ap.add_argument("--stgeo_geom", default="hyperbolic",
                    choices=["hyperbolic", "euclidean"])
    ap.add_argument("--stgeo_detail_only", type=int, default=1, choices=[0, 1])
    ap.add_argument("--stgeo_sup_lambda", type=float, default=0.2,
                    help="辅助损失权重（预注册固定 0.2，禁止搜索）")
    ap.add_argument("--stgeo_dim", type=int, default=192)
    ap.add_argument("--stgeo_pool", default="max_shape",
                    choices=["max_shape", "topk_mean"])
    ap.add_argument("--stgeo_xscale", type=int, default=0, choices=[0, 1])
    ap.add_argument("--rep_mode", default="full", choices=["plain", "full"])
    ap.add_argument("--fs_tar", type=int, default=0, choices=[0, 1])
    ap.add_argument("--str_dim", type=int, default=192)
    ap.add_argument("--backbone_lr_ratio", type=float, default=0.1)
    ap.add_argument("--mean", type=float, nargs=6, default=MEAN6)
    ap.add_argument("--std", type=float, nargs=6, default=STD6)
    ap.add_argument("--onGPU", default=True, type=lambda x: str(x).lower() == "true")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    args.max_epochs = 0

    assert args.resume is None, "from-scratch: --resume 禁止在启动时使用"
    assert args.seed == 16, f"from-scratch: seed 必须为 16，得到 {args.seed}"
    assert os.path.basename(args.vmamba_pretrained_weight_path) == \
        "vssm_tiny_0230_ckpt_epoch_262.pth", "预训练必须是官方 vssm_tiny_0230 权重"

    if args.onGPU:
        torch.cuda.set_device(args.gpu_id)
    init_seed(args.seed)
    os.makedirs(args.ckpt_dir, exist_ok=True)

    model = CASAVMambaSTGeoNet(
        vmamba_pretrained_path=args.vmamba_pretrained_weight_path,
        str_dim=args.str_dim, stgeo=args.stgeo,
        stgeo_sigmas=tuple(float(s) for s in args.stgeo_sigmas.split(",") if s),
        stgeo_mid=args.stgeo_mid, stgeo_dim=args.stgeo_dim,
        stgeo_pool=args.stgeo_pool, stgeo_xscale=bool(args.stgeo_xscale),
        stgeo_sup_small=args.stgeo_sup_small, stgeo_sup_lambda=args.stgeo_sup_lambda,
        stgeo_geom=args.stgeo_geom, stgeo_detail_only=bool(args.stgeo_detail_only),
        rep_mode=args.rep_mode, fs_tar=bool(args.fs_tar)).float()
    if args.onGPU:
        model = model.cuda()

    bb = [p for n, p in model.named_parameters() if n.startswith("encoder.")]
    new = [p for n, p in model.named_parameters() if not n.startswith("encoder.")]
    optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(
        [{"params": bb, "lr": args.lr * args.backbone_lr_ratio,
          "lr_scale": args.backbone_lr_ratio, "name": "backbone"},
         {"params": new, "lr": args.lr, "lr_scale": 1.0, "name": "new"}],
        args.lr, (0.9, 0.99), eps=1e-08, weight_decay=1e-4)

    st = model.pretrain_load_stats() or {}
    pb = model.param_breakdown()
    rep = model.stgeo_report()

    # ---------------- 配置头
    log("=" * 72)
    log(f"[MODEL] CASA-VMamba-STGeo (VMamba-Tiny + SPD(2)/H2 structure-tensor geodesic)")
    log(f"[ARCH] {args.arch}  [STGEO] {args.stgeo}  [REP-MODE] {args.rep_mode}")
    log(f"[CONFIG] protocol={PROTOCOL_VERSION} exact_max_steps={args.exact_max_steps} "
        f"max_steps={args.max_steps} batch={args.batch_size} seed={args.seed} "
        f"lr={args.lr} backbone_lr_ratio={args.backbone_lr_ratio}")
    if args.stgeo == "sup":
        log(f"[STGEO] form={rep['form']} in_ch={rep['in_ch']} small={rep['small']} "
            f"lambda={rep['lambda']} params={rep['params']} "
            f"deploy_dropped={rep['deploy_dropped']}")
    else:
        log(f"[STGEO] form={rep['form']} sigmas={rep['sigmas']} mid={rep['mid']} "
            f"out_dim={rep['out_dim']} pool={rep.get('pool_mode')} "
            f"xscale={rep.get('xscale_gate')} geom={rep.get('geom')} "
            f"detail_only={rep.get('detail_only')} in_ch={rep.get('in_ch_ev')} "
            f"params={rep['params']} gamma_init_zero={rep.get('gamma_init_zero')}")
    log(f"[PRETRAIN-LOAD] retained={st.get('retained')}/{st.get('pretrained_keys')} "
        f"worst_diff={st.get('worst_diff')} missing_new={len(st.get('missing_new', []))} "
        f"dropped={len(st.get('dropped_intentional', []))}")
    log(f"[PRETRAIN-SHA256] {sha16(args.vmamba_pretrained_weight_path)}")
    log(f"[PARAM-BREAKDOWN] backbone={pb['backbone']:,} stgeo={pb['stgeo']:,} "
        f"tar={pb['tar']:,} dcr={pb['dcr']:,} head={pb['head']:,} "
        f"total={pb['total']:,} non_backbone={pb['non_backbone']:,}")
    log(f"[TOTAL-PARAMS] {fmt_params(measure_params(model))} M")
    log(f"[DATA-CONTRACT] legacy_6ch_reverse_v1  [THRESHOLD] prob>0.5  [LABEL] gray>=128")
    log("=" * 72)
    assert st.get("worst_diff") == 0.0, f"[VMAMBA-PRETRAIN] worst_diff 必须为 0，得到 {st.get('worst_diff')}"

    # ---------------- run_manifest（fail-closed）
    manifest = {
        "arch": args.arch, "protocol_version": PROTOCOL_VERSION,
        "backbone": "vmamba_tiny_v2",
        "backbone_weight": os.path.basename(args.vmamba_pretrained_weight_path),
        "backbone_weight_sha256": sha16(args.vmamba_pretrained_weight_path),
        "stgeo": args.stgeo, "stgeo_sigmas": args.stgeo_sigmas,
        "stgeo_mid": args.stgeo_mid, "stgeo_dim": args.stgeo_dim,
        "stgeo_pool": args.stgeo_pool, "stgeo_xscale": int(args.stgeo_xscale),
        "stgeo_sup_small": (int(args.stgeo_sup_small) if args.stgeo == "sup" else None),
        "stgeo_sup_lambda": (float(args.stgeo_sup_lambda) if args.stgeo == "sup" else 0.0),
        "stgeo_sup_form": (STGEO_SUP_FORM if args.stgeo == "sup" else None),
        "stgeo_geom": (args.stgeo_geom if args.stgeo == "rel" else None),
        "stgeo_detail_only": (int(args.stgeo_detail_only) if args.stgeo == "rel" else None),
        "stgeo_form_tag": (STGEO_FORM if args.stgeo != "none" else None),
        "rep_mode": args.rep_mode, "fs_tar": int(args.fs_tar), "str_dim": args.str_dim,
        "backbone_lr_ratio": args.backbone_lr_ratio,
        "data_contract": "legacy_6ch_reverse_v1", "seed": args.seed,
        "max_steps": args.max_steps, "batch_size": args.batch_size,
        "dataset": args.dataset, "exact_max_steps": int(args.exact_max_steps),
        "source_code_sha256": source_code_identity(),
        "resume_source": None,
    }
    mpath = os.path.join(args.ckpt_dir, "run_manifest.json")
    if os.path.isfile(mpath):
        old = json.load(open(mpath, encoding="utf-8"))
        for k in [k for k in manifest if k not in ("source_code_sha256", "resume_source")]:
            if old.get(k) != manifest[k]:
                raise SystemExit(f"[MANIFEST-MISMATCH] {k}: old={old.get(k)} "
                                 f"new={manifest[k]}; aborting")
        log(f"[MANIFEST] validated existing run_manifest.json")
    else:
        json.dump(manifest, open(mpath, "w", encoding="utf-8"), indent=2)
        log(f"[MANIFEST] run_manifest.json written")

    def save_arch():
        arch = dict(manifest)
        arch.pop("resume_source", None)
        if "gamma" in rep:
            arch["gamma"] = rep["gamma"]
        arch["stgeo_params"] = rep.get("params")
        json.dump(arch, open(os.path.join(args.ckpt_dir, "arch.json"), "w",
                             encoding="utf-8"), indent=2)

    # ---------------- resume（只允许自己的 last.pth）
    start_epoch, best_f1, best_epoch, cur_iter = 0, -1.0, -1, 0
    auto = os.path.join(args.ckpt_dir, "last.pth")
    if os.path.isfile(auto):
        ck = torch.load(auto, map_location="cpu", weights_only=False)
        model.load_state_dict(ck["state_dict"])
        if ck.get("optimizer") is not None:
            optimizer.load_state_dict(ck["optimizer"])
        start_epoch = ck.get("epoch", 0)
        best_f1 = ck.get("best_f1", -1.0)
        best_epoch = ck.get("best_epoch", -1)
        cur_iter = int(ck.get("actual_steps", 0))
        if ck.get("protocol_version") not in (None, PROTOCOL_VERSION):
            raise SystemExit(f"[PROTOCOL-MISMATCH] ckpt={ck.get('protocol_version')}")
        rng = ck.get("rng_state")
        if rng is not None:
            torch.set_rng_state(rng["torch"])
            np.random.set_state(rng["numpy"])
            random.setstate(rng["python"])
            if rng.get("cuda") is not None and torch.cuda.is_available():
                torch.cuda.set_rng_state_all(rng["cuda"])
        log(f"[RESUME] last.pth actual_steps={cur_iter} epoch={start_epoch} "
            f"best_f1={best_f1:.4f}")

    train_loader = build_loader(args, args.train_list, args.batch_size, True, True)
    test_loader = build_loader(args, args.test_list, args.test_batch_size, False, False)
    max_batches = len(train_loader)
    args.max_epochs = int(np.ceil(args.max_steps / max_batches))
    log(f"[MAX-STEPS] {args.max_steps} [iters/epoch] {max_batches} [max_epochs] {args.max_epochs}")
    log("EPOCH | Loss | Recall | Precision | OA | F1 | IoU | Kappa | t(s)")
    save_arch()

    def save_last(epoch):
        torch.save({"state_dict": model.state_dict(), "optimizer": optimizer.state_dict(),
                    "epoch": epoch, "best_f1": best_f1, "best_epoch": best_epoch,
                    "actual_steps": cur_iter, "protocol_version": PROTOCOL_VERSION,
                    "arch": args.arch, "stgeo": args.stgeo,
                    "rng_state": {"torch": torch.get_rng_state(),
                                  "numpy": np.random.get_state(),
                                  "python": random.getstate(),
                                  "cuda": (torch.cuda.get_rng_state_all()
                                           if torch.cuda.is_available() else None)}},
                   os.path.join(args.ckpt_dir, "last.pth"))

    exact = bool(args.exact_max_steps)
    for epoch in range(start_epoch, args.max_epochs):
        t0 = time.time()
        remaining = (args.max_steps - cur_iter) if exact else None
        if remaining is not None and remaining <= 0:
            log(f"[EXACT-STEPS] budget reached ({cur_iter})")
            break
        loss_tr, score_tr, lr, executed, aux_tr = train_one_epoch(
            args, train_loader, model, optimizer, epoch, max_batches, cur_iter, remaining)
        cur_iter += executed if exact else max_batches
        if aux_tr is not None:
            log(f"[STGEO-LOSS] epoch={epoch} aux_loss={aux_tr:.6f} "
                f"lambda={args.stgeo_sup_lambda}")
        if exact:
            log(f"[ACTUAL-OPT-STEPS] epoch={epoch} executed={executed} "
                f"cumulative={cur_iter}/{args.max_steps}")
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()
        if epoch == 0:
            save_last(epoch)
            if exact and cur_iter >= args.max_steps:
                break
            continue
        loss_val, sv = evaluate(args, test_loader, model)
        log(f"Epoch {epoch}/{args.max_epochs} | Loss={loss_val:.4f} | "
            f"Recall={sv['recall']:.4f} | Precision={sv['precision']:.4f} | "
            f"OA={sv['OA']:.4f} | F1={sv['F1']:.4f} | IoU={sv['IoU']:.4f} | "
            f"Kappa={sv['Kappa']:.4f} | t={time.time() - t0:.0f}s")
        if sv["F1"] > best_f1:
            prev = os.path.join(args.ckpt_dir, f"best_F1={best_f1:.4f}.pth")
            if best_f1 >= 0 and os.path.isfile(prev):
                os.remove(prev)
            best_f1, best_epoch = sv["F1"], epoch
            torch.save(model.state_dict(),
                       os.path.join(args.ckpt_dir, f"best_F1={best_f1:.4f}.pth"))
        save_last(epoch)
        save_arch()
        if exact and cur_iter >= args.max_steps:
            log(f"[EXACT-STEPS] reached {cur_iter}/{args.max_steps}, stopping")
            break
    log(f"[BEST] F1={best_f1:.4f} at epoch {best_epoch}")

    # ---------------- 正式测试（deploy 折叠图）
    best_path = os.path.join(args.ckpt_dir, f"best_F1={best_f1:.4f}.pth")
    if not os.path.isfile(best_path):
        log("[TEST] best checkpoint not found, skipping final test")
        return
    model.load_state_dict(torch.load(best_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=False))
    model = model.cuda()
    model.eval()
    prev_tf32 = torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32
    prev_det = torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic
    torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32 = False
    torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32 = False
    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
    torch.manual_seed(16)
    pre_fix = torch.randn(8, 3, args.inWidth, args.inHeight).cuda()
    post_fix = torch.randn(8, 3, args.inWidth, args.inHeight).cuda()
    real_loader = build_loader(args, args.test_list, 16, False, False)
    img_r, _ = next(iter(real_loader))
    pre_r, post_r = img_r[:, 0:3].cuda(), img_r[:, 3:6].cuda()
    with torch.no_grad():
        y_tr = model(pre_fix, post_fix)
        y_tr_r = model(pre_r.float(), post_r.float())
        gamma_final = (float(model.stgeo_op.gamma.detach())
                       if model.stgeo_op is not None else 0.0)
    model.switch_to_deploy()
    model.eval()
    with torch.no_grad():
        y_dp = model(pre_fix, post_fix)
        y_dp_r = model(pre_r.float(), post_r.float())
    fold_err = float((y_tr - y_dp).abs().max())
    dis = float(((y_tr > 0.5) != (y_dp > 0.5)).float().mean())
    fold_err_r = float((y_tr_r - y_dp_r).abs().max())
    dis_r = float(((y_tr_r > 0.5) != (y_dp_r > 0.5)).float().mean())
    flips = ((y_tr_r > 0.5) != (y_dp_r > 0.5))
    n_flip = int(flips.sum())
    margin = float((y_tr_r - 0.5).abs()[flips].max()) if n_flip else 0.0
    knife = bool(n_flip > 0 and margin < fold_err_r)
    deploy_params = measure_params(model)

    _, sv = evaluate(args, test_loader, model)
    log("=== TEST RESULTS ===")
    log("[MODEL] CASA-VMamba-STGeo (VMamba-Tiny + SPD(2)/H2 geodesic evidence + TAR/DCR)")
    log(f"[ARCH] {args.arch}")
    log(f"[STGEO] {args.stgeo} form={rep.get('form')} "
        f"sigmas={args.stgeo_sigmas} mid={args.stgeo_mid} dim={args.stgeo_dim} "
        f"pool={args.stgeo_pool} xscale={int(args.stgeo_xscale)} "
        f"sup_small={args.stgeo_sup_small} sup_lambda={args.stgeo_sup_lambda} "
        f"geom={args.stgeo_geom} detail_only={int(args.stgeo_detail_only)}")
    if args.stgeo == "sup":
        log(f"[STGEO-SUP] lambda={args.stgeo_sup_lambda} small={args.stgeo_sup_small} "
            f"form={STGEO_SUP_FORM} dropped_at_deploy=1")
    else:
        log(f"[STGEO-GAMMA] {gamma_final:.6e} (zero-init gate; value on best ckpt)")
    log(f"[STGEO-DIAG] {json.dumps(model.stgeo_diag(), default=str)}")
    log(f"[REP-MODE] {args.rep_mode}")
    log(f"[BACKBONE-LR-RATIO] {args.backbone_lr_ratio}")
    log("[DATA-CONTRACT] legacy_6ch_reverse_v1")
    log("[DEPLOY-NUMERICS] tf32=off deterministic=on (STR T2 protocol)")
    log(f"[PRETRAIN-SHA256] {sha16(args.vmamba_pretrained_weight_path)}")
    log(f"[PRETRAIN-LOAD] retained={st.get('retained')}/{st.get('pretrained_keys')} "
        f"worst_diff={st.get('worst_diff'):.3e}")
    log(f"[PARAM-BREAKDOWN] backbone={pb['backbone']} stgeo={pb['stgeo']} tar={pb['tar']} "
        f"dcr={pb['dcr']} head={pb['head']} total={pb['total']} "
        f"non_backbone={pb['non_backbone']}")
    log(f"[REPARAM-MAX-ABS-ERROR] {fold_err:.3e} (train vs deploy, fixed random batch)")
    log(f"[REPARAM-ARGMAX-DISAGREE] {dis:.3e} (0.5-binarization; hard gate: must be 0)")
    log(f"[REPARAM-REAL-MAX-ABS-ERROR] {fold_err_r:.3e} (real batch 16)")
    log(f"[REPARAM-REAL-ARGMAX-DISAGREE] {dis_r:.3e} (operational gate: must be 0)")
    log(f"[FOLD-KNIFE-EDGE] n_flip={n_flip} flipped_margin_max={margin:.3e} "
        f"max_abs_diff={fold_err_r:.3e} verdict="
        f"{'NO_FLIP' if n_flip == 0 else ('KNIFE_EDGE_ARTIFACT' if knife else 'NEEDS_REVIEW')}")
    log(f"[DEPLOY-PARAMS] total={fmt_params(deploy_params)} M")
    log(f"Recall={sv['recall']:.4f} | Precision={sv['precision']:.4f} | OA={sv['OA']:.4f} | "
        f"F1={sv['F1']:.4f} | IoU={sv['IoU']:.4f} | Kappa={sv['Kappa']:.4f}")
    log(f"[BEST-F1] {best_f1:.4f} (epoch {best_epoch})")
    log(f"[ACTUAL-OPT-STEPS] {int(cur_iter)}")
    log(f"[PROTOCOL-VERSION] {PROTOCOL_VERSION}")
    log("=== END TEST RESULTS ===")
    torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32 = prev_tf32
    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = prev_det


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/dataset\Transforms.py

import numpy
import numpy as np
import torch
import random
import cv2


class Scale(object):
    """
    Resize the given image to a fixed scale
    """

    def __init__(self, wi, he):
        '''
        :param wi: width after resizing
        :param he: height after reszing
        '''
        self.w = wi
        self.h = he

    # modified from torchvision to add support for max size

    def __call__(self, img, label):
        '''
        :param img: RGB image
        :param label: semantic label image
        :return: resized images
        '''
        # bilinear interpolation for RGB image
        img = cv2.resize(img, (self.w, self.h))
        # nearest neighbour interpolation for label image
        label = cv2.resize(label, (self.w, self.h), interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST)
        return [img, label]


class Resize(object):
    def __init__(self, min_size, max_size, strict=False):
        if not isinstance(min_size, (list, tuple)):
            min_size = (min_size,)
        self.min_size = min_size
        self.max_size = max_size
        self.strict = strict

    # modified from torchvision to add support for max size
    def get_size(self, image_size):
        w, h = image_size
        if not self.strict:
            size = random.choice(self.min_size)
            max_size = self.max_size
            if max_size is not None:
                min_original_size = float(min((w, h)))
                max_original_size = float(max((w, h)))
                if max_original_size / min_original_size * size > max_size:
                    size = int(round(max_size * min_original_size / max_original_size))

            if (w <= h and w == size) or (h <= w and h == size):
                return (h, w)

            if w < h:
                ow = size
                oh = int(size * h / w)
            else:
                oh = size
                ow = int(size * w / h)

            return (oh, ow)
        else:
            if w < h:
                return (self.max_size, self.min_size[0])
            else:
                return (self.min_size[0], self.max_size)

    def __call__(self, image, label):
        size = self.get_size(image.shape[:2])
        image = cv2.resize(image, size)
        # I confirm that the output size is right, not reversed
        label = cv2.resize(label, size, interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST)
        return (image, label)


class RandomCropResize(object):
    """
    Randomly crop and resize the given image with a probability of 0.5
    """

    def __init__(self, crop_area):
        '''
        :param crop_area: area to be cropped (this is the max value and we select between 0 and crop area
        '''
        self.cw = crop_area
        self.ch = crop_area

    def __call__(self, img, label):
        if random.random() < 0.5:
            h, w = img.shape[:2]
            x1 = random.randint(0, self.ch)
            y1 = random.randint(0, self.cw)

            img_crop = img[y1:h - y1, x1:w - x1]
            label_crop = label[y1:h - y1, x1:w - x1]

            img_crop = cv2.resize(img_crop, (w, h))
            label_crop = cv2.resize(label_crop, (w, h), interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST)

            return img_crop, label_crop
        else:
            return [img, label]


class RandomFlip(object):
    """
    Randomly flip the given Image with a probability of 0.5
    """

    def __call__(self, image, label):
        if random.random() < 0.5:
                image = cv2.flip(image, 0)  # horizontal flip
                label = cv2.flip(label, 0)  # horizontal flip
        if random.random() < 0.5:
                image = cv2.flip(image, 1)  # veritcal flip
                label = cv2.flip(label, 1)  # veritcal flip
        return [image, label]


class RandomExchange(object):
    """
    Randomly flip the given Image with a probability of 0.5
    """

    def __call__(self, image, label):
        if random.random() < 0.5:
            pre_img = image[:, :, 0:3]
            post_img = image[:, :, 3:6]
            image = numpy.concatenate((post_img, pre_img), axis=2)
        return [image, label]


class Normalize(object):
    """
    Given mean: (B, G, R) and std: (B, G, R),
    will normalize each channel of the torch.*Tensor, i.e.
    channel = (channel - mean) / std
    """

    def __init__(self, mean, std):
        '''
        :param mean: global mean computed from dataset
        :param std: global std computed from dataset
        '''
        self.mean = mean
        self.std = std
        self.depth_mean = [0.5]
        self.depth_std = [0.5]

    def __call__(self, image, label):
        image = image.astype(np.float32)
        image = image / 255
        # lab contract: binarize labels with threshold gray >= 128
        # (CDD labels are JPEG with intermediate gray values)
        label = (label >= 128).astype(np.float32)
        for i in range(6):
            image[:, :, i] -= self.mean[i]
        for i in range(6):
            image[:, :, i] /= self.std[i]

        return [image, label]


class GaussianNoise(object):
    def __init__(self, std=0.05):
        '''
        :param mean: global mean computed from dataset
        :param std: global std computed from dataset
        '''
        self.std = std

    def __call__(self, image, label):
        noise = np.random.normal(loc=0, scale=self.std, size=image.shape)
        image = image + noise.astype(np.float32)
        return [image, label]


class ToTensor(object):
    '''
    This class converts the data to tensor so that it can be processed by PyTorch
    '''

    def __init__(self, scale=1):
        '''
        :param scale: set this parameter according to the output scale
        '''
        self.scale = scale

    def __call__(self, image, label):
        if self.scale != 1:
            h, w = label.shape[:2]
            image = cv2.resize(image, (int(w), int(h)))
            label = cv2.resize(label, (int(w / self.scale), int(h / self.scale)), \
                               interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST)
        image = image[:, :, ::-1].copy()  # .copy() is to solve "torch does not support negative index"
        image = image.transpose((2, 0, 1))
        image_tensor = torch.from_numpy(image)
        label_tensor = torch.LongTensor(np.array(label, dtype=np.int64)).unsqueeze(dim=0)

        return [image_tensor, label_tensor]


class Compose(object):
    """
    Composes several transforms together.
    """

    def __init__(self, transforms):
        self.transforms = transforms

    def __call__(self, *args):
        for t in self.transforms:
            args = t(*args)
        return args

# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/dataset\casa_transforms.py
================================================================================
import numpy as np
import torch
import random
import cv2
import numpy


class Scale(object):
    """Resize image and label to a fixed size"""

    def __init__(self, wi, he):
        self.w = wi
        self.h = he

    def __call__(self, img, label):
        img = cv2.resize(img, (self.w, self.h))
        label = cv2.resize(label, (self.w, self.h), interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST)
        return [img, label]


class RandomCropResize(object):
    """Random crop followed by resize back to original size"""

    def __init__(self, crop_area):
        self.cw = crop_area
        self.ch = crop_area

    def __call__(self, img, label):
        if random.random() < 0.5:
            h, w = img.shape[:2]
            top, bottom = random.randint(0, self.ch), random.randint(0, self.ch)
            left, right = random.randint(0, self.cw), random.randint(0, self.cw)

            img_crop = img[top:h - bottom, left:w - right]
            label_crop = label[top:h - bottom, left:w - right]

            img_crop = cv2.resize(img_crop, (w, h))
            label_crop = cv2.resize(label_crop, (w, h), interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST)
            return img_crop, label_crop

        return [img, label]


class RandomFlip(object):
    """Random horizontal and vertical flip"""

    def __call__(self, image, label):
        if random.random() < 0.5:
            image = cv2.flip(image, 0)
            label = cv2.flip(label, 0)
        if random.random() < 0.5:
            image = cv2.flip(image, 1)
            label = cv2.flip(label, 1)
        return [image, label]


class RandomExchange(object):
    """Randomly swap pre- and post-event images (for bi-temporal input)"""

    def __call__(self, image, label):
        if random.random() < 0.5:
            pre_img = image[:, :, 0:3]
            post_img = image[:, :, 3:6]
            image = numpy.concatenate((post_img, pre_img), axis=2)
        return [image, label]


class RandomRotate90(object):
    """Apply one shared quarter-turn rotation to both dates and the mask."""

    def __call__(self, image, label):
        k = random.randrange(4)
        return [np.rot90(image, k).copy(), np.rot90(label, k).copy()]


class Normalize(object):
    """Normalize both RGB dates and binarize the 0/255 change mask."""

    def __init__(self, mean, std):
        self.mean = mean
        self.std = std

    def __call__(self, image, label):
        image = image.astype(np.float32) / 255.0
        label = (label >= 128).astype(np.float32)

        for i in range(6):
            image[:, :, i] = (image[:, :, i] - self.mean[i]) / self.std[i]

        return [image, label]


class ToTensor(object):
    """Convert numpy arrays to PyTorch tensors"""

    def __init__(self, scale=1):
        self.scale = scale

    def __call__(self, image, label):
        if self.scale != 1:
            h, w = label.shape[:2]
            image = cv2.resize(image, (w, h))
            label = cv2.resize(
                label,
                (int(w / self.scale), int(h / self.scale)),
                interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST
            )

        image = np.ascontiguousarray(image.transpose((2, 0, 1)))

        image_tensor = torch.from_numpy(image)
        label_tensor = torch.LongTensor(
            np.array(label, dtype=np.int_)
        ).unsqueeze(0)

        return [image_tensor, label_tensor]


class Compose(object):
    """Compose multiple transforms"""

    def __init__(self, transforms):
        self.transforms = transforms

    def __call__(self, *args):
        for t in self.transforms:
            args = t(*args)
        return args

# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/dataset\dataset.py
================================================================================
import cv2
from os.path import join as osp
import numpy
import torch.utils.data


def read_list(list_path):
    """Read a list/*.txt file: one image filename per line.

    The server-side dataset layout is `<root>/{A,B,label}/` + `<root>/list/*.txt`.
    """
    with open(list_path, "r") as f:
        names = [line.strip() for line in f if line.strip()]
    return names


class Dataset(torch.utils.data.Dataset):
    """Bi-temporal change detection dataset in A/B/label + list format.

    Args:
        file_root: dataset root containing A/ B/ label/ dirs
        list_path: path to list/*.txt with image filenames
        transform: Compose of Transforms
    """
    def __init__(self, file_root, list_path, transform=None):
        self.file_list = read_list(list_path)

        self.pre_images = [osp(file_root, 'A', x) for x in self.file_list]
        self.post_images = [osp(file_root, 'B', x) for x in self.file_list]
        self.gts = [osp(file_root, 'label', x) for x in self.file_list]

        self.transform = transform

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.pre_images)

    def __getitem__(self, idx):
        pre_image_name = self.pre_images[idx]
        label_name = self.gts[idx]
        post_image_name = self.post_images[idx]

        pre_image = cv2.cvtColor(cv2.imread(pre_image_name), cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
        label = cv2.imread(label_name, 0)
        post_image = cv2.cvtColor(cv2.imread(post_image_name), cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)

        img = numpy.concatenate((pre_image, post_image), axis=2)

        if self.transform:
            [img, label] = self.transform(img, label)

        return img, label

# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/model\b4_spe_head.py
================================================================================
"""Run8 B4-SPE head：B4 Semantic Pair Evidence + Sub-patch Expansion。

设计（CASA-CD_Run8_B4-SPE最终路线与预注册.md §5）：
  输入 fx=[Vx]、fy=[Vy]，V = frozen ViT4 final-LN B4 token（B×256×192）。
  单一 Symmetric Pair Descriptor（严格时间交换对称）：

      D = [ |T_A − T_B| , (T_A + T_B)/2 ]     # 384
      Z = Linear(384 → 96, bias)              # B×256×96 → 96×16×16

  然后 Sub-patch Expansion（复用 Run7 已 smoke 的四级拓扑，无 attention/gate/残差）：

      96×16×16 → Conv1×1 96→128 + BN + ReLU → PixelShuffle×2 → 32×32×32 → DW3×3+BN+ReLU
              → Conv1×1 32→64  → PS×2 → 64×64×16 → DW3×3
              → Conv1×1 16→64  → PS×2 → 128×128×16 → DW3×3
              → Conv1×1 16→16  → PS×2 → 256×256×4 → DW3×3 → Conv1×1 4→1 → sigmoid

参数：pair 36,960 + expansion 16,912 = **53,872**（head 全部 trainable）。
整体 = ViT4 1,976,832 + head 53,872 = **2,030,704**。
"""
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F


class B4SPEHead(nn.Module):
    """B4-SPE：Change-Sensitive B4 Semantic Source + Symmetric Pair Descriptor + Sub-patch Expansion。"""

    def __init__(self, token_dim=192, proj_dim=96):
        super().__init__()
        self.pair = nn.Linear(token_dim * 2, proj_dim, bias=True)

        self.exp1 = nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(96, 128, 1, bias=False), nn.BatchNorm2d(128), nn.ReLU(inplace=True))
        self.dw1 = nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(32, 32, 3, 1, padding=1, groups=32, bias=False),
                                 nn.BatchNorm2d(32), nn.ReLU(inplace=True))
        self.exp2 = nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(32, 64, 1, bias=False), nn.BatchNorm2d(64), nn.ReLU(inplace=True))
        self.dw2 = nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(16, 16, 3, 1, padding=1, groups=16, bias=False),
                                 nn.BatchNorm2d(16), nn.ReLU(inplace=True))
        self.exp3 = nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(16, 64, 1, bias=False), nn.BatchNorm2d(64), nn.ReLU(inplace=True))
        self.dw3 = nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(16, 16, 3, 1, padding=1, groups=16, bias=False),
                                 nn.BatchNorm2d(16), nn.ReLU(inplace=True))
        self.exp4 = nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(16, 16, 1, bias=False), nn.BatchNorm2d(16), nn.ReLU(inplace=True))
        self.dw4 = nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(4, 4, 3, 1, padding=1, groups=4, bias=False),
                                 nn.BatchNorm2d(4), nn.ReLU(inplace=True))
        self.out = nn.Conv2d(4, 1, 1, bias=False)

    def forward(self, fx, fy):
        vx = fx[0]                                    # (B,192,16,16)
        vy = fy[0]
        B, C, H, W = vx.shape
        tx = vx.flatten(2).transpose(1, 2)            # (B,256,192)
        ty = vy.flatten(2).transpose(1, 2)
        d = torch.cat([torch.abs(tx - ty), (tx + ty) / 2.0], dim=-1)   # (B,256,384)
        z = self.pair(d)                              # (B,256,96)
        x = z.transpose(1, 2).reshape(B, 96, 16, 16)

        x = self.dw1(F.pixel_shuffle(self.exp1(x), 2))      # 32×32×32
        x = self.dw2(F.pixel_shuffle(self.exp2(x), 2))      # 64×64×16
        x = self.dw3(F.pixel_shuffle(self.exp3(x), 2))      # 128×128×16
        x = self.dw4(F.pixel_shuffle(self.exp4(x), 2))      # 256×256×4
        return torch.sigmoid(self.out(x))
