# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/model\vmamba_tiny.py
================================================================================
"""VMamba-Tiny 自包含主干（Run8 换骨干主线）。

## 来源与许可边界（**必须如实标注**）

结构逐项对齐 **官方 VMamba v2**（*VMamba: Visual State Space Model*, NeurIPS 2024；
官方仓库 `MzeroMiko/VMamba`，`classification/models/vmamba.py`）。
本文件是**自包含移植**（去掉 `einops` / `timm` / `fvcore` / `csm_triton` 依赖），
目的是让 `casa_vmamba_net.py` 与 Run1–Run7 的 TinyViM 主线共用同一套工程约束。
**这是复用既有公开架构，不是本项目原创**，论文中不得计为贡献。

## 为什么不用 `mamba_ssm` 的那套

RSML-3 实测：`casacd` 里那个 10-03 编译的 `selective_scan_cuda.so` ABI 已坏
（`undefined symbol: _ZN3c104cuda29c10_cuda_check_implementationE...`）。
而 `selective_scan_cuda_oflex` 是 **Run1–Run7 生产同款**、已验证的内核。
因此本文件默认走 oflex，`mamba_ssm.selective_scan_fn` 只作兜底（见 `vmamba_kernel.py`）。

## 与本项目 TinyViM 主线的三点结构差异（都会影响"换骨干"这一唯一变量，须在论文披露）

| 项 | TinyViM-S-Slim（Run1–Run7） | VMamba-Tiny（Run8） |
|---|---|---|
| 频率解耦 | 有（Laplace mixer：低频进 scan、高频走 `local_conv` RepDW） | **无**（全部通道进 scan，无 dense 高频旁路） |
| drop_path | 0（slim 构造里未启用） | **0.1**（官方默认） |
| 归一化位置 | `Conv2d_BN` 为主 | **pre-norm**（`x + op(norm(x))`） |

⇒ Run8 的 Stage A **不能**用 Run7 的数字当同期对照。

## 官方 v2 权重对齐（`vssm_tiny_0230_ckpt_epoch_262.pth`）

实测 key 形状（2026-10-15）：
`patch_embed.{0,2}` = 48 ch（`Conv2d(3,48,k4,s4)` + `LayerNorm2d(48)`），
`patch_embed.{5,7}` = 96 ch，`layers.{i}.blocks.{j}.{norm,norm2,mlp,op.*}`，
`layers.{i}.downsample.{1,3}`，`classifier.{norm,head}`。
⇒ `patch_size=4` / `downsample_version="v2"` / `norm_layer="ln2d"` / `dims=[96,192,384,768]`
   / `depths=[2,2,9,2]` 与权重逐 key 对齐。
"""
import math
from collections import OrderedDict
from functools import partial
from typing import Any

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

from model.layers.vmamba_kernel import (
    load_mamba_selective_scan_fn,
    load_selective_scan_oflex,
)

VMAMBA_TINY = dict(
    patch_size=4,
    in_chans=3,
    depths=(2, 2, 5, 2),          # ⚠️ 实测：vssm_tiny_0230 是 5 不是 9（总 11 个 block）
    dims=(96, 192, 384, 768),
    ssm_d_state=1,                # ⚠️ 实测：A_logs=(768,1)、x_proj dim1=8=6+2*1
    ssm_ratio=2.0,                # d_inner = 2*d_model
    ssm_disable_z=True,           # ⚠️ 实测：in_proj.weight=(192,96) ⇒ 无 z 门控
    ssm_dt_rank="auto",
    ssm_conv=3,
    ssm_conv_bias=False,          # ⚠️ 实测：ckpt 无 op.conv2d.bias
    ssm_bias=False,               # ⚠️ 实测：ckpt 无 in_proj.bias / out_proj.bias
    drop_path_rate=0.1,           # 官方 tiny 常用 0.2；不影响加载
    patch_norm=True,
    downsample_version="v3",      # ⚠️ 实测：downsample.1.weight = (192,96,3,3) ⇒ k3s2
    # ⚠️ 官方 0230 权重用的是 **v2 patch embed**（两个 stride-2 的 3×3 conv，GELU 分隔），
    # 不是 v1 的单个 stride-4 conv。逐 key 由权重反推确认（2026-10-15 服务器实测）：
    #   patch_embed.0.weight (48, 3, 3, 3)   ← Conv2d(3, 48, k3, s2, p1)
    #   patch_embed.2.weight (48,)           ← LayerNorm2d(48)
    #   patch_embed.5.weight (96, 48, 3, 3)  ← Conv2d(48, 96, k3, s2, p1)
    #   patch_embed.7.weight (96,)           ← LayerNorm2d(96)
    # ⇒ 1/4 分辨率、dims[0]=96。
    # 首版曾误用 v1（stride-4 单 conv），smoke 报
    #   "shape mismatch patch_embed.0.weight: ckpt (48,3,3,3) vs model (96,3,4,4)" ⇒ 已修正。
    patchembed_version="v2",
)

# 权重实测（2026-10-15，服务器 `_r8_ckpt_audit.sh`）：
#   vssm_tiny_0230_ckpt_epoch_262.pth  sha256 09739f6d95638e5caf0d33fcbca85b7cff62b8ca16ec2926d781109939c6b201
#   len(ck["model"]) = 222 keys；sum(numel) = 30,705,832（含 1000 类分类头 770,536）
#   ⇒ 纯骨干 = 29,935,296（29.94 M）
#   ⇒ 项目文档里"官方 VMamba-T ≈30M"对这枚权重**成立**，但**必须连同配置一起标注**：
#      depths=[2,2,5,2] / dims=[96,192,384,768] / ssm_ratio=2.0 / d_state=1 / noz /
#      patch_embed v2 / downsample v3 / ln2d / mlp_ratio=4.0 / ImageNet-1k 1000 类。
#   ⚠️ 不得再引用 `others/VMamba/vmamba.py::VSSM.__init__` 的**类默认值**
#      （那里是 [2,2,9,2]/d_state16/v1/v2 ⇒ 42.95M，是错误口径的来源）。

__all__ = ["VMambaBackbone", "build_vmamba_tiny", "VSSM_STAGE_DIMS", "STAGE_RES"]

VSSM_STAGE_DIMS = (96, 192, 384, 768)
STAGE_RES = ("1/4", "1/8", "1/16", "1/32")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 基础算子（与官方逐行一致；只去掉外部依赖）
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _trunc_normal_(tensor, mean=0.0, std=1.0, a=-2.0, b=2.0):
    """timm.trunc_normal_ 的无 scipy 实现（与 `layers/ss2d.py` 同口径）。"""
    with torch.no_grad():
        l = (1.0 + math.erf((a - mean) / std / math.sqrt(2.0))) / 2.0
        u = (1.0 + math.erf((b - mean) / std / math.sqrt(2.0))) / 2.0
        tensor.uniform_(2 * l - 1, 2 * u - 1)
        tensor.erfinv_()
        tensor.mul_(std * math.sqrt(2.0)).add_(mean)
        tensor.clamp_(min=a, max=b)
    return tensor


class DropPath(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, drop_prob=0.0):
        super().__init__()
        self.drop_prob = float(drop_prob)

    def forward(self, x):
        if self.drop_prob == 0.0 or not self.training:
            return x
        keep = 1.0 - self.drop_prob
        shape = (x.shape[0],) + (1,) * (x.ndim - 1)
        return x * x.new_empty(shape).bernoulli_(keep).div_(keep)


class LayerNorm2d(nn.LayerNorm):
    """官方 `LayerNorm2d`：在 (B,H,W,C) 布局上做 LayerNorm（channel_first 模式下用）。"""

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor):
        x = x.permute(0, 2, 3, 1)
        x = super().forward(x)
        return x.permute(0, 3, 1, 2)


class Linear2d(nn.Linear):
    """官方 `Linear2d`：把 nn.Linear 当作 1×1 卷积用（channel-first 模式下必需）。

    ⚠️ 为什么必须用它（2026-10-15 smoke 抓到的真实 bug）：
    channel-first 模式下 `LayerNorm2d` 的输出是 **(B,C,H,W)**，张量**最后一维是 W 不是 C**。
    若这里用普通 `nn.Linear`，`F.linear` 会把 W 当成特征维 ⇒
    `mat1 and mat2 shapes cannot be multiplied (12288x64 and 96x192)`。

    同时**权重形状必须保持 (out, in)**（官方 ckpt 就是 (192,96)），所以不能改写成
    `nn.Conv2d(96,192,1)`（那是 (192,96,1,1)，会与侧车/审计的形状对拍冲突）。
    `Linear2d` 用 `weight[:, :, None, None]` 在 forward 里即时视图成 conv2d 的 4D 权重，
    权重本身仍是 2D ⇒ 两边都满足。`_load_from_state_dict` 里那次 `view` 是为了兼容
    "checkpoint 用 Conv2d 存的 4D 权重" 的历史情况。
    """

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor):
        return F.conv2d(x, self.weight[:, :, None, None], self.bias)

    def _load_from_state_dict(self, state_dict, prefix, local_metadata, strict,
                              missing_keys, unexpected_keys, error_msgs):
        super()._load_from_state_dict(state_dict, prefix, local_metadata, strict,
                                      missing_keys, unexpected_keys, error_msgs)
        state_dict[prefix + "weight"] = state_dict[prefix + "weight"].view(self.weight.shape)


class Permute(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, *args):
        super().__init__()
        self.args = args

    def forward(self, x):
        return x.permute(*self.args)


class Mlp(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, in_features, hidden_features=None, out_features=None,
                 act_layer=nn.GELU, drop=0.0, channels_first=False):
        super().__init__()
        out_features = out_features or in_features
        hidden_features = hidden_features or in_features
        # channel-first ⇒ 输入是 (B,C,H,W)，最后一维是 W 不是 C ⇒ 必须用 Linear2d
        Linear = Linear2d if channels_first else nn.Linear
        self.fc1 = Linear(in_features, hidden_features)
        self.act = act_layer()
        self.fc2 = Linear(hidden_features, out_features)
        self.drop = nn.Dropout(drop) if drop > 0.0 else nn.Identity()

    def forward(self, x):
        x = self.fc1(x)
        x = self.act(x)
        x = self.drop(x)
        x = self.fc2(x)
        x = self.drop(x)
        return x


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 四向交叉扫描
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
class CrossScan(torch.autograd.Function):
    @staticmethod
    def forward(ctx, x: torch.Tensor):
        B, C, H, W = x.shape
        ctx.shape = (B, C, H, W)
        xs = x.new_empty((B, 4, C, H * W))
        xs[:, 0] = x.flatten(2, 3)
        xs[:, 1] = x.transpose(dim0=2, dim1=3).flatten(2, 3)
        xs[:, 2:4] = torch.flip(xs[:, 0:2], dims=[-1])
        return xs

    @staticmethod
    def backward(ctx, ys: torch.Tensor):
        B, C, H, W = ctx.shape
        L = H * W
        ys = ys[:, 0:2] + ys[:, 2:4].flip(dims=[-1]).view(B, 2, -1, L)
        y = ys[:, 0] + ys[:, 1].view(B, -1, W, H).transpose(dim0=2, dim1=3).contiguous().view(B, -1, L)
        return y.view(B, -1, H, W)


class CrossMerge(torch.autograd.Function):
    @staticmethod
    def forward(ctx, ys: torch.Tensor):
        B, K, D, H, W = ys.shape
        ctx.shape = (H, W)
        ys = ys.view(B, K, D, -1)
        ys = ys[:, 0:2] + ys[:, 2:4].flip(dims=[-1]).view(B, 2, D, -1)
        y = ys[:, 0] + ys[:, 1].view(B, -1, W, H).transpose(dim0=2, dim1=3).contiguous().view(B, D, -1)
        return y

    @staticmethod
    def backward(ctx, x: torch.Tensor):
        H, W = ctx.shape
        B, C, L = x.shape
        xs = x.new_empty((B, 4, C, L))
        xs[:, 0] = x
        xs[:, 1] = x.view(B, C, H, W).transpose(dim0=2, dim1=3).flatten(2, 3)
        xs[:, 2:4] = torch.flip(xs[:, 0:2], dims=[-1])
        return xs.view(B, 4, C, H, W)


def _scan_oflex(u, delta, A, B, C, D, delta_bias, delta_softplus, nrows=1):
    """oflex 内核前向（与 `layers/ss2d.py::SelectiveScan` 完全同调用口径）。"""
    oflex = load_selective_scan_oflex()
    out, x, *rest = oflex.fwd(u, delta, A, B, C, D, delta_bias, delta_softplus, nrows, True)
    return out, x


def _scan_mamba_ssm(u, delta, A, B, C, D, delta_bias, delta_softplus):
    """兜底：mamba_ssm 官方 `selective_scan_fn`，按 K 维循环调用。"""
    fn = load_mamba_selective_scan_fn()
    K = B.shape[1]
    Cdim = u.shape[1] // K
    outs = []
    for k in range(K):
        uk = u[:, k * Cdim:(k + 1) * Cdim].permute(0, 2, 1).contiguous()
        dk = delta[:, k * Cdim:(k + 1) * Cdim].permute(0, 2, 1).contiguous()
        ak = A[k * Cdim:(k + 1) * Cdim].contiguous()
        bk = B[:, k].contiguous()
        ck = C[:, k].contiguous()
        dk_vec = D[k * Cdim:(k + 1) * Cdim].contiguous() if D is not None else None
        db = delta_bias[k * Cdim:(k + 1) * Cdim].contiguous() if delta_bias is not None else None
        yk = fn(uk, dk, ak, bk, ck, dk_vec, z=None, delta_bias=db, delta_softplus=delta_softplus)
        outs.append(yk.permute(0, 2, 1).contiguous())
    return torch.cat(outs, dim=1), None


class SelectiveScan(torch.autograd.Function):
    """可切换后端的 selective scan（oflex 优先，mamba_ssm 兜底）。

    入参/返回与官方 `selective_scan_cuda.fwd` 完全一致：
    `u (B, K*C, L)`、`delta (B, K*C, L)`、`A (K*C, N)`、`B/C (B, K, N, L)`、
    `D (K*C,)`、`delta_bias (K*C,)`；输出 `(B, K*C, L)`。
    """

    @staticmethod
    def forward(ctx, u, delta, A, B, C, D=None, delta_bias=None,
                delta_softplus=False, nrows=1):
        for t in (u, delta, B, C):
            if t.stride(-1) != 1:
                t = t.contiguous()
        if B.dim() == 3:
            B = B.unsqueeze(1)
            ctx.squeeze_B = True
        else:
            ctx.squeeze_B = False
        if C.dim() == 3:
            C = C.unsqueeze(1)
            ctx.squeeze_C = True
        else:
            ctx.squeeze_C = False
        ctx.delta_softplus = delta_softplus
        try:
            out, x = _scan_oflex(u, delta, A, B, C, D, delta_bias, delta_softplus, nrows)
            ctx.backend = "oflex"
            ctx.save_for_backward(u, delta, A, B, C, D, delta_bias, x)
            return out
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001  —— 兜底到 mamba_ssm
            out, _x = _scan_mamba_ssm(u, delta, A, B, C, D, delta_bias, delta_softplus)
            ctx.backend = "mamba_ssm"
            return out

    @staticmethod
    def backward(ctx, dout, *args):
        if getattr(ctx, "backend", "") != "oflex":
            raise RuntimeError(
                "[SCAN-BACKEND] mamba_ssm 兜底路径的 autograd 由 selective_scan_fn 自己接管，"
                "不应走到这里；请检查是否误用了本类的 apply。")
        oflex = load_selective_scan_oflex()
        u, delta, A, B, C, D, delta_bias, x = ctx.saved_tensors
        if dout.stride(-1) != 1:
            dout = dout.contiguous()
        du, ddelta, dA, dB, dC, dD, ddelta_bias, *rest = oflex.bwd(
            u, delta, A, B, C, D, delta_bias, dout, x, ctx.delta_softplus, 1)
        dB = dB.squeeze(1) if getattr(ctx, "squeeze_B", False) else dB
        dC = dC.squeeze(1) if getattr(ctx, "squeeze_C", False) else dC
        return (du, ddelta, dA, dB, dC, dD, ddelta_bias, None, None)


def cross_selective_scan(
    x=None, x_proj_weight=None, x_proj_bias=None,
    dt_projs_weight=None, dt_projs_bias=None,
    A_logs=None, Ds=None, delta_softplus=True,
    out_norm=None, out_norm_shape="v0", channel_first=False,
    to_dtype=True, force_fp32=False, nrows=-1, backnrows=-1, ssoflex=True,
    SelectiveScan=SelectiveScan, CrossScan=CrossScan, CrossMerge=CrossMerge,
    no_einsum=False, dt_low_rank=True, **kwargs,
):
    """与官方 `cross_selective_scan` 同语义；`channel_first=True` 时返回 (B,C,H,W)。"""
    B, D, H, W = x.shape
    D, N = A_logs.shape
    K, D, R = dt_projs_weight.shape
    L = H * W
    if nrows == 0:
        nrows = 4 if D % 4 == 0 else (3 if D % 3 == 0 else (2 if D % 2 == 0 else 1))
    if backnrows == 0:
        backnrows = 4 if D % 4 == 0 else (3 if D % 3 == 0 else (2 if D % 2 == 0 else 1))

    def _scan(u, delta, A, B_, C_, Dd=None, delta_bias=None, delta_softplus=True):
        return SelectiveScan.apply(u, delta, A, B_, C_, Dd, delta_bias,
                                   delta_softplus, nrows)

    if no_einsum:
        xs = CrossScan.apply(x)
        x_dbl = F.conv1d(xs.view(B, -1, L), x_proj_weight.view(-1, D, 1),
                         bias=(x_proj_bias.view(-1) if x_proj_bias is not None else None), groups=K)
        dts, Bs, Cs = torch.split(x_dbl.view(B, K, -1, L), [R, N, N], dim=2)
        dts = F.conv1d(dts.contiguous().view(B, -1, L), dt_projs_weight.view(K * D, -1, 1), groups=K)
    else:
        xs = CrossScan.apply(x)
        x_dbl = torch.einsum("b k d l, k c d -> b k c l", xs, x_proj_weight)
        if x_proj_bias is not None:
            x_dbl = x_dbl + x_proj_bias.view(1, K, -1, 1)
        dts, Bs, Cs = torch.split(x_dbl, [R, N, N], dim=2)
        dts = torch.einsum("b k r l, k d r -> b k d l", dts, dt_projs_weight)

    xs = xs.view(B, -1, L)
    dts = dts.contiguous().view(B, -1, L)
    As = -torch.exp(A_logs.to(torch.float))
    Bs = Bs.contiguous().view(B, K, N, L)
    Cs = Cs.contiguous().view(B, K, N, L)
    Ds = Ds.to(torch.float)
    delta_bias = dt_projs_bias.view(-1).to(torch.float)

    if force_fp32:
        xs = xs.to(torch.float)
        dts = dts.to(torch.float)
        Bs = Bs.to(torch.float)
        Cs = Cs.to(torch.float)

    ys = _scan(xs, dts, As, Bs, Cs, Ds, delta_bias, delta_softplus).view(B, K, -1, H, W)
    y = CrossMerge.apply(ys)

    if channel_first:
        y = y.view(B, -1, H, W)
        if out_norm_shape in ("v1",):
            y = out_norm(y)
        else:
            y = out_norm(y.permute(0, 2, 3, 1)).permute(0, 3, 1, 2)
        return (y.to(x.dtype) if to_dtype else y)

    if out_norm_shape in ("v1",):
        y = out_norm(y.view(B, -1, H, W)).permute(0, 2, 3, 1)
    else:
        y = y.transpose(dim0=1, dim1=2).contiguous()
        y = out_norm(y).view(B, H, W, -1)
    return (y.to(x.dtype) if to_dtype else y)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# SS2D（官方 v2 口径：无频率解耦，全部通道进 scan）
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
class SS2D(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, d_model=96, d_state=16, ssm_ratio=2.0, dt_rank="auto",
                 act_layer=nn.SiLU, d_conv=3, conv_bias=True, dropout=0.0, bias=False,
                 dt_min=0.001, dt_max=0.1, dt_init="random", dt_scale=1.0,
                 dt_init_floor=1e-4, initialize="v0", forward_type="v2",
                 disable_z=False, channel_first=False, **kwargs):
        super().__init__()
        factory_kwargs = {"device": None, "dtype": None}
        d_inner = int(ssm_ratio * d_model)
        dt_rank = math.ceil(d_model / 16) if dt_rank == "auto" else dt_rank
        self.d_model = d_model
        self.d_inner = d_inner
        self.d_state = d_state
        self.dt_rank = dt_rank
        self.d_conv = d_conv
        self.channel_first = channel_first
        self.forward_type = forward_type
        self.out_norm_shape = "v1" if channel_first else "v0"
        # `disable_z`：官方 v2 的 `forward_type="v2noz"` 语义 —— in_proj 只出 d_inner
        # （无 z 门控）。**vssm_tiny_0230 权重实测就是这种**（in_proj.weight=(192,96)）。
        self.disable_z = bool(disable_z)

        self.out_norm = LayerNorm2d(d_inner) if channel_first else nn.LayerNorm(d_inner)
        # channel-first ⇒ 必须用 Linear2d（见该类注释：LayerNorm2d 的输出最后一维是 W）
        Linear = Linear2d if channel_first else nn.Linear

        k_group = 4
        d_proj = d_inner if self.disable_z else (d_inner * 2)
        self.in_proj = Linear(d_model, d_proj, bias=bias, **factory_kwargs)
        self.act = act_layer()
        if d_conv > 1:
            self.conv2d = nn.Conv2d(d_inner, d_inner, kernel_size=d_conv,
                                    padding=(d_conv - 1) // 2, groups=d_inner,
                                    bias=conv_bias, **factory_kwargs)

        x_proj = [nn.Linear(d_inner, dt_rank + d_state * 2, bias=False, **factory_kwargs)
                  for _ in range(k_group)]
        self.x_proj_weight = nn.Parameter(torch.stack([t.weight for t in x_proj], dim=0))
        del x_proj

        dt_projs = [self.dt_init(dt_rank, d_inner, dt_scale, dt_init, dt_min, dt_max,
                                 dt_init_floor, **factory_kwargs) for _ in range(k_group)]
        self.dt_projs_weight = nn.Parameter(torch.stack([t.weight for t in dt_projs], dim=0))
        self.dt_projs_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.stack([t.bias for t in dt_projs], dim=0))
        del dt_projs

        self.A_logs = self.A_log_init(d_state, d_inner, copies=k_group, merge=True)
        self.Ds = self.D_init(d_inner, copies=k_group, merge=True)

        self.out_proj = Linear(d_inner, d_model, bias=bias, **factory_kwargs)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout) if dropout > 0.0 else nn.Identity()

    @staticmethod
    def dt_init(dt_rank, d_inner, dt_scale=1.0, dt_init="random", dt_min=0.001,
                dt_max=0.1, dt_init_floor=1e-4, **factory_kwargs):
        dt_proj = nn.Linear(dt_rank, d_inner, bias=True, **factory_kwargs)
        dt_init_std = dt_rank ** -0.5 * dt_scale
        if dt_init == "constant":
            nn.init.constant_(dt_proj.weight, dt_init_std)
        elif dt_init == "random":
            nn.init.uniform_(dt_proj.weight, -dt_init_std, dt_init_std)
        else:
            raise NotImplementedError(dt_init)
        dt = torch.exp(torch.rand(d_inner, **factory_kwargs)
                       * (math.log(dt_max) - math.log(dt_min)) + math.log(dt_min)).clamp(min=dt_init_floor)
        inv_dt = dt + torch.log(-torch.expm1(-dt))
        with torch.no_grad():
            dt_proj.bias.copy_(inv_dt)
        return dt_proj

    @staticmethod
    def A_log_init(d_state, d_inner, copies=-1, device=None, merge=True):
        A = torch.arange(1, d_state + 1, dtype=torch.float32, device=device)
        A = A.unsqueeze(0).repeat(d_inner, 1).contiguous()
        A_log = torch.log(A)
        if copies > 0:
            A_log = A_log.unsqueeze(0).repeat(copies, 1, 1)
            if merge:
                A_log = A_log.flatten(0, 1)
        A_log = nn.Parameter(A_log)
        A_log._no_weight_decay = True
        return A_log

    @staticmethod
    def D_init(d_inner, copies=-1, device=None, merge=True):
        D = torch.ones(d_inner, device=device)
        if copies > 0:
            D = D.unsqueeze(0).repeat(copies, 1)
            if merge:
                D = D.flatten(0, 1)
        D = nn.Parameter(D)
        D._no_weight_decay = True
        return D

    def forward_core(self, x: torch.Tensor, **kwargs):
        return cross_selective_scan(
            x, self.x_proj_weight, None, self.dt_projs_weight, self.dt_projs_bias,
            self.A_logs, self.Ds, delta_softplus=True,
            out_norm=self.out_norm, channel_first=self.channel_first,
            out_norm_shape=self.out_norm_shape, force_fp32=True, **kwargs)

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor, **kwargs):
        """x: channel_first 时 (B,C,H,W)，否则 (B,H,W,C)。"""
        x = self.in_proj(x)
        if not self.disable_z:
            x, z = x.chunk(2, dim=(1 if self.channel_first else -1))
            z = self.act(z)
        if not self.channel_first:
            x = x.permute(0, 3, 1, 2).contiguous()
        if self.d_conv > 1:
            x = self.conv2d(x)
        x = self.act(x)
        y = self.forward_core(x)
        if not self.disable_z:
            y = y * z
        return self.dropout(self.out_proj(y))


class VSSBlock(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, hidden_dim=0, drop_path=0.0, norm_layer=nn.LayerNorm,
                 channel_first=False, ssm_d_state=16, ssm_ratio=2.0, ssm_dt_rank="auto",
                 ssm_act_layer=nn.SiLU, ssm_conv=3, ssm_conv_bias=True, ssm_drop_rate=0.0,
                 ssm_init="v0", forward_type="v2", ssm_disable_z=False, ssm_bias=False,
                 mlp_ratio=4.0, mlp_act_layer=nn.GELU,
                 mlp_drop_rate=0.0, gmlp=False, use_checkpoint=False, **kwargs):
        super().__init__()
        self.ssm_branch = ssm_ratio > 0
        self.mlp_branch = mlp_ratio > 0
        self.use_checkpoint = use_checkpoint
        if self.ssm_branch:
            self.norm = norm_layer(hidden_dim)
            self.op = SS2D(d_model=hidden_dim, d_state=ssm_d_state, ssm_ratio=ssm_ratio,
                           dt_rank=ssm_dt_rank, act_layer=ssm_act_layer, d_conv=ssm_conv,
                           conv_bias=ssm_conv_bias, dropout=ssm_drop_rate, bias=ssm_bias,
                           initialize=ssm_init, forward_type=forward_type,
                           disable_z=ssm_disable_z, channel_first=channel_first)
        self.drop_path = DropPath(drop_path)
        if self.mlp_branch:
            self.norm2 = norm_layer(hidden_dim)
            self.mlp = Mlp(in_features=hidden_dim, hidden_features=int(hidden_dim * mlp_ratio),
                           act_layer=mlp_act_layer, drop=mlp_drop_rate,
                           channels_first=channel_first)

    def _forward(self, inp):
        x = inp
        if self.ssm_branch:
            x = x + self.drop_path(self.op(self.norm(x)))
        if self.mlp_branch:
            x = x + self.drop_path(self.mlp(self.norm2(x)))
        return x

    def forward(self, inp):
        return self._forward(inp)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 主干
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
class VMambaBackbone(nn.Module):
    """VMamba-Tiny 特征提取（输出四个 stage 的 (B,C,H,W)，channel-first）。"""

    def __init__(self, pretrained_path=None, cfg=None, out_indices=(0, 1, 2, 3),
                 norm_layer="ln2d"):
        super().__init__()
        c = dict(VMAMBA_TINY)
        if cfg:
            c.update(cfg)
        self.cfg = c
        self.dims = list(c["dims"])
        self.depths = list(c["depths"])
        self.out_indices = tuple(out_indices)
        assert norm_layer.lower() == "ln2d", "Run8 只用 channel-first (ln2d) 口径"
        self.channel_first = True
        nl = LayerNorm2d

        # ---- patch embed：官方 v2 stem（两个 3×3/s2 卷积 + GELU + LN2d）
        # 与权重 key 逐项对齐：.0=Conv(3→48) .2=LN2d(48) .5=Conv(48→96) .7=LN2d(96)
        d0 = self.dims[0]
        self.patch_embed = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(c["in_chans"], d0 // 2, kernel_size=3, stride=2, padding=1),
            nl(d0 // 2),
            nn.GELU(),
            nn.Conv2d(d0 // 2, d0, kernel_size=3, stride=2, padding=1),
            nl(d0),
        )

        dpr = [x.item() for x in torch.linspace(0, c["drop_path_rate"], sum(self.depths))]
        ch_first = self.channel_first
        downsample_version = str(c.get("downsample_version", "v3")).lower()
        self.layers = nn.ModuleList()
        for i in range(len(self.depths)):
            if i >= len(self.depths) - 1:
                downsample = nn.Identity()
            elif downsample_version == "v3":
                # 官方 `_make_downsample_v3`：Conv k3 s2 p1（实测权重 k=3）
                downsample = nn.Sequential(
                    nn.Identity() if ch_first else Permute(0, 3, 1, 2),
                    nn.Conv2d(self.dims[i], self.dims[i + 1], kernel_size=3, stride=2, padding=1),
                    nn.Identity() if ch_first else Permute(0, 2, 3, 1),
                    nl(self.dims[i + 1]),
                )
            else:
                # 官方 `_make_downsample`：Conv k2 s2
                downsample = nn.Sequential(
                    nn.Identity() if ch_first else Permute(0, 3, 1, 2),
                    nn.Conv2d(self.dims[i], self.dims[i + 1], kernel_size=2, stride=2),
                    nn.Identity() if ch_first else Permute(0, 2, 3, 1),
                    nl(self.dims[i + 1]),
                )
            blocks = [VSSBlock(
                hidden_dim=self.dims[i], drop_path=dpr[sum(self.depths[:i]) + d],
                norm_layer=nl, channel_first=ch_first,
                ssm_d_state=c["ssm_d_state"], ssm_ratio=c["ssm_ratio"],
                ssm_dt_rank=c["ssm_dt_rank"], ssm_act_layer=nn.SiLU,
                ssm_conv=c["ssm_conv"], ssm_conv_bias=c["ssm_conv_bias"],
                ssm_bias=c["ssm_bias"], ssm_disable_z=c["ssm_disable_z"],
                ssm_drop_rate=0.0, ssm_init="v0", forward_type="v2",
                mlp_ratio=4.0, mlp_act_layer=nn.GELU,
            ) for d in range(self.depths[i])]
            self.layers.append(nn.Sequential(OrderedDict(
                blocks=nn.Sequential(*blocks), downsample=downsample)))

        for i in self.out_indices:
            self.add_module(f"outnorm{i}", nl(self.dims[i]))

        self.apply(self._init_weights)
        self._load_stats = None
        # 官方 v2 对 SS2D 内部参数做一次专门的初始化（必须在 apply 之后）
        for m in self.modules():
            if isinstance(m, SS2D):
                self._init_ss2d(m)
        if pretrained_path is not None:
            self.load_pretrained(pretrained_path)

    @staticmethod
    def _init_weights(m):
        if isinstance(m, nn.Linear):
            _trunc_normal_(m.weight, std=0.02)
            if m.bias is not None:
                nn.init.constant_(m.bias, 0)
        elif isinstance(m, nn.LayerNorm):
            nn.init.constant_(m.bias, 0)
            nn.init.constant_(m.weight, 1.0)
        elif isinstance(m, nn.Conv2d):
            _trunc_normal_(m.weight, std=0.02)
            if m.bias is not None:
                nn.init.constant_(m.bias, 0)

    @staticmethod
    def _init_ss2d(m):
        """官方 v2 在 `_init_weights` 之后对 SS2D 的 dt/A/D 重做初始化。

        注意：**本函数只在"无预训练"时有意义**；有预训练时全部 key 会被 load_pretrained
        覆盖，因此这里与权重无关。保留是为了"无预训练 smoke"与官方构建逐位一致。
        """
        nn.init.uniform_(m.dt_projs_weight, -0.1, 0.1)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def load_pretrained(self, path):
        """加载官方 `vssm_tiny_0230_ckpt_epoch_262.pth`（top-level key = `model`）。

        返回 load stats（含 `worst_diff`），供 smoke 断言"预训练语义继承"。
        """
        ck = torch.load(path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=False)
        src = ck.get("model", ck)
        model_sd = self.state_dict()
        kept, dropped = {}, []
        for k, v in src.items():
            if k.startswith("classifier."):
                dropped.append(k)
                continue
            if k in model_sd:
                assert model_sd[k].shape == v.shape, \
                    f"[VMAMBA-PRETRAIN] shape mismatch {k}: ckpt {tuple(v.shape)} vs model {tuple(model_sd[k].shape)}"
                kept[k] = v
            else:
                dropped.append(k)
        missing = [k for k in model_sd if k not in kept]
        self.load_state_dict(kept, strict=False)
        worst = 0.0
        for k, v in kept.items():
            worst = max(worst, (model_sd[k].float() - v.float()).abs().max().item())
        self._load_stats = {
            "pretrained_keys": len(src),
            "retained": len(kept),
            "worst_diff": worst,
            "missing_new": missing,
            "dropped_intentional": dropped,
            "loaded_keys": list(kept.keys()),
        }
        return self._load_stats

    def load_stats(self):
        return self._load_stats

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward_features(self, x):
        """返回 `(f1, f2, f3, f4)`，各自 (B, dim_i, H/4, W/4 ... )，channel-first。"""
        x = self.patch_embed(x)                     # (B, 96, 64, 64)
        outs = []
        for i, layer in enumerate(self.layers):
            o = layer.blocks(x)                     # 该 stage 最后一个 block 的输出
            x = layer.downsample(o)
            if i in self.out_indices:
                outs.append(getattr(self, f"outnorm{i}")(o))
        return outs

    def forward(self, x):
        return self.forward_features(x)

    def param_count(self):
        return sum(p.numel() for p in self.parameters())

    def trainable_param_count(self):
        return sum(p.numel() for p in self.parameters() if p.requires_grad)


def build_vmamba_tiny(pretrained_path=None, out_indices=(0, 1, 2, 3), cfg=None):
    return VMambaBackbone(pretrained_path=pretrained_path, cfg=cfg, out_indices=out_indices)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    m = VMambaBackbone()
    print("vssm_tiny params:", f"{m.param_count():,}", f"({m.param_count()/1e6:.3f} M)")
    outs = m(torch.randn(1, 3, 256, 256))
    print("taps:", [tuple(t.shape) for t in outs])

"""Change-aware compressed attention and Poincare geometry."""

================================================================================
# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/model\layers\attention.py
================================================================================
# Copyright (c) Meta Platforms, Inc. and affiliates.
#
# This source code is licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0
# found in the LICENSE file in the root directory of this source tree.

# References:
#   https://github.com/facebookresearch/dino/blob/master/vision_transformer.py
#   https://github.com/rwightman/pytorch-image-models/tree/master/timm/models/vision_transformer.py

import logging
import os
import warnings

from torch import Tensor
from torch import nn


logger = logging.getLogger("dinov2")


XFORMERS_ENABLED = os.environ.get("XFORMERS_DISABLED") is None
try:
    if XFORMERS_ENABLED:
        from xformers.ops import memory_efficient_attention, unbind

        XFORMERS_AVAILABLE = True
        warnings.warn("xFormers is available (Attention)")
    else:
        warnings.warn("xFormers is disabled (Attention)")
        raise ImportError
except ImportError:
    XFORMERS_AVAILABLE = False
    warnings.warn("xFormers is not available (Attention)")


class Attention(nn.Module):
    def __init__(
        self,
        dim: int,
        num_heads: int = 8,
        qkv_bias: bool = False,
        proj_bias: bool = True,
        attn_drop: float = 0.0,
        proj_drop: float = 0.0,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.num_heads = num_heads
        head_dim = dim // num_heads
        self.scale = head_dim**-0.5

        self.qkv = nn.Linear(dim, dim * 3, bias=qkv_bias)
        self.attn_drop = nn.Dropout(attn_drop)
        self.proj = nn.Linear(dim, dim, bias=proj_bias)
        self.proj_drop = nn.Dropout(proj_drop)

    def forward(self, x: Tensor) -> Tensor:
        B, N, C = x.shape
        qkv = self.qkv(x).reshape(B, N, 3, self.num_heads, C // self.num_heads).permute(2, 0, 3, 1, 4)

        q, k, v = qkv[0] * self.scale, qkv[1], qkv[2]
        attn = q @ k.transpose(-2, -1)

        attn = attn.softmax(dim=-1)
        attn = self.attn_drop(attn)

        x = (attn @ v).transpose(1, 2).reshape(B, N, C)
        x = self.proj(x)
        x = self.proj_drop(x)
        return x


class MemEffAttention(Attention):
    def forward(self, x: Tensor, attn_bias=None) -> Tensor:
        if not XFORMERS_AVAILABLE:
            if attn_bias is not None:
                raise AssertionError("xFormers is required for using nested tensors")
            return super().forward(x)

        B, N, C = x.shape
        qkv = self.qkv(x).reshape(B, N, 3, self.num_heads, C // self.num_heads)

        q, k, v = unbind(qkv, 2)

        x = memory_efficient_attention(q, k, v, attn_bias=attn_bias)
        x = x.reshape([B, N, C])

        x = self.proj(x)
        x = self.proj_drop(x)
        return x

================================================================================
# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/model\layers\block.py
================================================================================
# Copyright (c) Meta Platforms, Inc. and affiliates.
#
# This source code is licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0
# found in the LICENSE file in the root directory of this source tree.

# References:
#   https://github.com/facebookresearch/dino/blob/master/vision_transformer.py
#   https://github.com/rwightman/pytorch-image-models/tree/master/timm/layers/patch_embed.py

import logging
import os
from typing import Callable, List, Any, Tuple, Dict
import warnings

import torch
from torch import nn, Tensor

from .attention import Attention, MemEffAttention
from .drop_path import DropPath
from .layer_scale import LayerScale
from .mlp import Mlp


logger = logging.getLogger("dinov2")


XFORMERS_ENABLED = os.environ.get("XFORMERS_DISABLED") is None
try:
    if XFORMERS_ENABLED:
        from xformers.ops import fmha, scaled_index_add, index_select_cat

        XFORMERS_AVAILABLE = True
        warnings.warn("xFormers is available (Block)")
    else:
        warnings.warn("xFormers is disabled (Block)")
        raise ImportError
except ImportError:
    XFORMERS_AVAILABLE = False

    warnings.warn("xFormers is not available (Block)")


class Block(nn.Module):
    def __init__(
        self,
        dim: int,
        num_heads: int,
        mlp_ratio: float = 4.0,
        qkv_bias: bool = False,
        proj_bias: bool = True,
        ffn_bias: bool = True,
        drop: float = 0.0,
        attn_drop: float = 0.0,
        init_values=None,
        drop_path: float = 0.0,
        act_layer: Callable[..., nn.Module] = nn.GELU,
        norm_layer: Callable[..., nn.Module] = nn.LayerNorm,
        attn_class: Callable[..., nn.Module] = Attention,
        ffn_layer: Callable[..., nn.Module] = Mlp,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        # print(f"biases: qkv: {qkv_bias}, proj: {proj_bias}, ffn: {ffn_bias}")
        self.norm1 = norm_layer(dim)
        self.attn = attn_class(
            dim,
            num_heads=num_heads,
            qkv_bias=qkv_bias,
            proj_bias=proj_bias,
            attn_drop=attn_drop,
            proj_drop=drop,
        )
        self.ls1 = LayerScale(dim, init_values=init_values) if init_values else nn.Identity()
        self.drop_path1 = DropPath(drop_path) if drop_path > 0.0 else nn.Identity()

        self.norm2 = norm_layer(dim)
        mlp_hidden_dim = int(dim * mlp_ratio)
        self.mlp = ffn_layer(
            in_features=dim,
            hidden_features=mlp_hidden_dim,
            act_layer=act_layer,
            drop=drop,
            bias=ffn_bias,
        )
        self.ls2 = LayerScale(dim, init_values=init_values) if init_values else nn.Identity()
        self.drop_path2 = DropPath(drop_path) if drop_path > 0.0 else nn.Identity()

        self.sample_drop_ratio = drop_path

    def forward_pair(self, x1: Tensor, x2: Tensor, score_hint=None) -> Tuple[Tensor, Tensor]:
        """双时相 paired forward（CASAA）：注意力共享变化感知路由，MLP 各自独立。

        仅当 self.attn 提供 forward_pair（CASAAAttention）时使用；
        norm1/norm2/mlp/ls/drop_path 全部沿用本 block 原有参数。
        score_hint 仅 router='oracle'（GT 诊断）使用，其余模式为 None。
        """
        x1n = self.norm1(x1)
        x2n = self.norm1(x2)
        y1, y2 = self.attn.forward_pair(x1n, x2n, score_hint=score_hint)
        x1 = x1 + self.drop_path1(self.ls1(y1))
        x2 = x2 + self.drop_path1(self.ls1(y2))

        x1 = x1 + self.drop_path2(self.ls2(self.mlp(self.norm2(x1))))
        x2 = x2 + self.drop_path2(self.ls2(self.mlp(self.norm2(x2))))
        return x1, x2

    def forward(self, x: Tensor) -> Tensor:
        def attn_residual_func(x: Tensor) -> Tensor:
            return self.ls1(self.attn(self.norm1(x)))

        def ffn_residual_func(x: Tensor) -> Tensor:
            return self.ls2(self.mlp(self.norm2(x)))

        if self.training and self.sample_drop_ratio > 0.1:
            # the overhead is compensated only for a drop path rate larger than 0.1
            x = drop_add_residual_stochastic_depth(
                x,
                residual_func=attn_residual_func,
                sample_drop_ratio=self.sample_drop_ratio,
            )
            x = drop_add_residual_stochastic_depth(
                x,
                residual_func=ffn_residual_func,
                sample_drop_ratio=self.sample_drop_ratio,
            )
        elif self.training and self.sample_drop_ratio > 0.0:
            x = x + self.drop_path1(attn_residual_func(x))
            x = x + self.drop_path1(ffn_residual_func(x))  # FIXME: drop_path2
        else:
            x = x + attn_residual_func(x)
            x = x + ffn_residual_func(x)
        return x


def drop_add_residual_stochastic_depth(
    x: Tensor,
    residual_func: Callable[[Tensor], Tensor],
    sample_drop_ratio: float = 0.0,
) -> Tensor:
    # 1) extract subset using permutation
    b, n, d = x.shape
    sample_subset_size = max(int(b * (1 - sample_drop_ratio)), 1)
    brange = (torch.randperm(b, device=x.device))[:sample_subset_size]
    x_subset = x[brange]

    # 2) apply residual_func to get residual
    residual = residual_func(x_subset)

    x_flat = x.flatten(1)
    residual = residual.flatten(1)

    residual_scale_factor = b / sample_subset_size

    # 3) add the residual
    x_plus_residual = torch.index_add(x_flat, 0, brange, residual.to(dtype=x.dtype), alpha=residual_scale_factor)
    return x_plus_residual.view_as(x)


def get_branges_scales(x, sample_drop_ratio=0.0):
    b, n, d = x.shape
    sample_subset_size = max(int(b * (1 - sample_drop_ratio)), 1)
    brange = (torch.randperm(b, device=x.device))[:sample_subset_size]
    residual_scale_factor = b / sample_subset_size
    return brange, residual_scale_factor


def add_residual(x, brange, residual, residual_scale_factor, scaling_vector=None):
    if scaling_vector is None:
        x_flat = x.flatten(1)
        residual = residual.flatten(1)
        x_plus_residual = torch.index_add(x_flat, 0, brange, residual.to(dtype=x.dtype), alpha=residual_scale_factor)
    else:
        x_plus_residual = scaled_index_add(
            x, brange, residual.to(dtype=x.dtype), scaling=scaling_vector, alpha=residual_scale_factor
        )
    return x_plus_residual


attn_bias_cache: Dict[Tuple, Any] = {}


def get_attn_bias_and_cat(x_list, branges=None):
    """
    this will perform the index select, cat the tensors, and provide the attn_bias from cache
    """
    batch_sizes = [b.shape[0] for b in branges] if branges is not None else [x.shape[0] for x in x_list]
    all_shapes = tuple((b, x.shape[1]) for b, x in zip(batch_sizes, x_list))
    if all_shapes not in attn_bias_cache.keys():
        seqlens = []
        for b, x in zip(batch_sizes, x_list):
            for _ in range(b):
                seqlens.append(x.shape[1])
        attn_bias = fmha.BlockDiagonalMask.from_seqlens(seqlens)
        attn_bias._batch_sizes = batch_sizes
        attn_bias_cache[all_shapes] = attn_bias

    if branges is not None:
        cat_tensors = index_select_cat([x.flatten(1) for x in x_list], branges).view(1, -1, x_list[0].shape[-1])
    else:
        tensors_bs1 = tuple(x.reshape([1, -1, *x.shape[2:]]) for x in x_list)
        cat_tensors = torch.cat(tensors_bs1, dim=1)

    return attn_bias_cache[all_shapes], cat_tensors


def drop_add_residual_stochastic_depth_list(
    x_list: List[Tensor],
    residual_func: Callable[[Tensor, Any], Tensor],
    sample_drop_ratio: float = 0.0,
    scaling_vector=None,
) -> Tensor:
    # 1) generate random set of indices for dropping samples in the batch
    branges_scales = [get_branges_scales(x, sample_drop_ratio=sample_drop_ratio) for x in x_list]
    branges = [s[0] for s in branges_scales]
    residual_scale_factors = [s[1] for s in branges_scales]

    # 2) get attention bias and index+concat the tensors
    attn_bias, x_cat = get_attn_bias_and_cat(x_list, branges)

    # 3) apply residual_func to get residual, and split the result
    residual_list = attn_bias.split(residual_func(x_cat, attn_bias=attn_bias))  # type: ignore

    outputs = []
    for x, brange, residual, residual_scale_factor in zip(x_list, branges, residual_list, residual_scale_factors):
        outputs.append(add_residual(x, brange, residual, residual_scale_factor, scaling_vector).view_as(x))
    return outputs


class NestedTensorBlock(Block):
    def forward_nested(self, x_list: List[Tensor]) -> List[Tensor]:
        """
        x_list contains a list of tensors to nest together and run
        """
        assert isinstance(self.attn, MemEffAttention)

        if self.training and self.sample_drop_ratio > 0.0:

            def attn_residual_func(x: Tensor, attn_bias=None) -> Tensor:
                return self.attn(self.norm1(x), attn_bias=attn_bias)

            def ffn_residual_func(x: Tensor, attn_bias=None) -> Tensor:
                return self.mlp(self.norm2(x))

            x_list = drop_add_residual_stochastic_depth_list(
                x_list,
                residual_func=attn_residual_func,
                sample_drop_ratio=self.sample_drop_ratio,
                scaling_vector=self.ls1.gamma if isinstance(self.ls1, LayerScale) else None,
            )
            x_list = drop_add_residual_stochastic_depth_list(
                x_list,
                residual_func=ffn_residual_func,
                sample_drop_ratio=self.sample_drop_ratio,
                scaling_vector=self.ls2.gamma if isinstance(self.ls1, LayerScale) else None,
            )
            return x_list
        else:

            def attn_residual_func(x: Tensor, attn_bias=None) -> Tensor:
                return self.ls1(self.attn(self.norm1(x), attn_bias=attn_bias))

            def ffn_residual_func(x: Tensor, attn_bias=None) -> Tensor:
                return self.ls2(self.mlp(self.norm2(x)))

            attn_bias, x = get_attn_bias_and_cat(x_list)
            x = x + attn_residual_func(x, attn_bias=attn_bias)
            x = x + ffn_residual_func(x)
            return attn_bias.split(x)

    def forward(self, x_or_x_list):
        if isinstance(x_or_x_list, Tensor):
            return super().forward(x_or_x_list)
        elif isinstance(x_or_x_list, list):
            if not XFORMERS_AVAILABLE:
                raise AssertionError("xFormers is required for using nested tensors")
            return self.forward_nested(x_or_x_list)
        else:
            raise AssertionError

# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/model\layers\caacp_hqkv.py
================================================================================
"""Run8 核心创新：CAACP-HQKV（Hyperbolic dual-branch SAT-style QKV change aggregation）。

对应本轮用户要求的 R2–R8：
每层输出 → 映射到双曲流形 → 分成「细节/局部/纹理/边缘」与「全局语义」两个分支 →
各自做特化 QKV → 注入解码器。K/V 按 **SAT 思想做 token 压缩（`N → k·N`）**，**Q 保持 `N`**。

## 与 Run1–Run3 的 CASAA（已终止）的**实质差异**（P0-3 陷阱①的交代）

| 维度 | CASAA（Run1/Run3，已终止） | CAACP-HQKV（Run8） |
|---|---|---|
| 空间结构 | ViT **变长 token 序列** + 密度峰值聚类（`cluster_and_merge` 含 `randperm`/`rand_like` 随机采样） | **规则二维网格**（固定 window 池化），无随机采样 ⇒ epoch-0 与 RNG 账本可控 |
| 为什么压缩 | 「省算力」（但实测 FLOPs 只降 **0.25%**） | 压缩是**几何分解的载体**：两个分支用**两种半径**分别定义 K/V |
| 打分来源 | GT patch occupancy / 单时相 ViT 特征 rank | **双时相 Lorentz 测地距离 + 局部离散度半径**（参数自由） |
| 分支数 | 1 | **2（细节/全局），各自独立 QKV 权重** |
| 注入位置 | 编码器内部第 8–11 层 | 编码器→解码器的**跳层连接**（可选消融：只注入 1/4 / 1/16） |
| 终止原因 | 「deployable routing ranking 提升 ≠ F1 提升」 | ⚠️ 本方案**尚未证伪该风险**，因此 Stage 设计把「注入」与「几何」拆开做单变量 |

## 为什么**不用** `rank_normalize_2d`（P0-3 陷阱③的交代）

`rank(·)` 只保留序、丢弃一切幅度；任何**保序**变换（包括固定半径双曲距离）在其后都
产生**逐位相同**的权重。Run8 因此让 Q/K/V 直接消费**数值分数**（`1+Q` 形式，非保序）：
非恒定半径的双曲距离会改变 `Q` 的**数值**，从而改变 softmax 权重，不会退化。

## 数据流（真实，不是概念图）

```
stage i 输出  fA, fB            (B, C_i, H_i, W_i)   ← 同一时相各自的 encoder 输出
   │
   ├─ detail 分支:  r = r0 + a·q_local(3×3 离散度)  ─┐
   └─ global  分支:  r = r0 + a·q_global(全图偏离)  ─┤
                                                    ↓
                        s_b = 1 − exp(−d_Lorentz)      (B,1,H_i,W_i)  数值分数，无 rank
                                                    ↓
        K/V 压缩:  window 内 w ∝ 1 + s_b  →  kv_b  (B, C_i, H_i/w_b, W_i/w_b)    [token: N → k_b·N]
        Q 保持:   q  = Linear(f_b)                     (B, r, H_i, W_i)          [token: N 不变]
                                                    ↓
        attn_b = softmax(qᵀk_b/√r) · v_b             每分支**独立**的 q/k/v 投影
                                                    ↓
        out = γ_detail·Proj(attn_detail) + γ_global·Proj(attn_global)   γ 零初始化
                                                    ↓
                        返回到 1/4 尺度 → 加入 DCR 最细尺度输入
```

## 零初始化与预训练纪律

- 两个门 `γ_detail` / `γ_global` **零初始化** ⇒ **关掉开关时模型前向与"无该模块"逐位一致**；
- 模块**最后构造**且用 `fork_rng` 固定局部种子 ⇒ 不推进全局随机流，既有模块初始化不受影响；
- 因此 `hf_edge=0/1` × `hqkv=0/1` 的对照臂可以共用同一套预训练加载（`worst_diff` 必须为 0）。
"""
import math

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

from model.layers.hyperbolic import (
    A_DEFAULT,
    R0_DEFAULT,
    euclidean_change_score,
    lorentz_change_score,
)
from model.layers.vmamba_kernel import backend_report  # noqa: F401  (日志/诊断用)

HQKV_FORM = "hqkv_v1"
HQKV_SEED = 17          # 局部 RNG 种子（与 HFAE 的 16 / FET 的 16 同惯例，但取不同值避免同流）
ATTN_STAGES_DEFAULT = (1, 2, 3)     # 在 1/8、1/16、1/32 上做注意力；1/4 用廉价融合


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 分数 → cell 权重（**非保序**：直接消费数值分数，不经 rank）
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def change_weighted_pool(x, score, window=2, eps=1e-6):
    """规则 window×window 网格内按变化分数加权聚合。

    `x`     (B,C,H,W)
    `score` (B,1,H,W) 数值分数（**不是 rank**）
    返回    (B,C,H/window,W/window)；对 `x` 保梯度，权重路径无梯度。

    权重 `w_i = (1 + s_i) / Σ_j (1 + s_j)`：
    - `s=0`（完全无变化）⇒ 全部 `1/4` ⇒ 精确退化为均匀池化；
    - `s` 越大越主导该 cell 的低频上下文。
    """
    B, C, H, W = x.shape
    assert H % window == 0 and W % window == 0, f"{H}×{W} 不能被 window={window} 整除"
    ho, wo = H // window, W // window
    s = score.view(B, 1, ho, window, wo, window)
    w = 1.0 + s
    w = w / w.sum(dim=(3, 5), keepdim=True).clamp_min(eps)
    xr = x.view(B, C, ho, window, wo, window)
    return (xr * w).sum(dim=(3, 5))


def global_deviation_weights(x, window=4, eps=1e-6):
    """全局分支的压缩：按「相对全图均值的偏离」加权池化（同样规则网格）。

    只用该时相自己的全局均值 ⇒ 单时相统计量、A/B 严格对称。
    """
    B, C, H, W = x.shape
    mu = x.mean(dim=(2, 3), keepdim=True)
    dev = (x - mu).pow(2).sum(dim=1, keepdim=True).clamp_min(0.0).sqrt()
    w = 1.0 + dev
    return F.avg_pool2d(x * w, kernel_size=window, stride=window) / \
        F.avg_pool2d(w, kernel_size=window, stride=window).clamp_min(eps)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 单分支的 SAT 式非对称注意力（Q 全分辨率，K/V 压缩）
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
class BranchSATAttention(nn.Module):
    """一个分支的特化 QKV + 非对称注意力（Q: N，K/V: k·N）。

    `out_dim` 让**所有 stage 的注意力输出统一到 `lite_dim`**，这样 4 个 stage 的增量
    可以直接相加后再上采样（否则 stage1 输出 `rank`=32、stage0 输出 `lite_dim`=192 会 shape 冲突）。
    多出来的 `rank → out_dim` 每 stage 只有 `rank*out_dim` 个参数（32×192 = 6,144）。
    """

    def __init__(self, dim, rank=32, window=2, global_branch=False,
                 out_dim=None, init_seed=HQKV_SEED):
        super().__init__()
        self.dim = int(dim)
        self.rank = int(rank)
        self.window = int(window)
        self.global_branch = bool(global_branch)
        self.out_dim = int(out_dim if out_dim is not None else dim)
        with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=[]):
            torch.manual_seed(init_seed)
            self.q = nn.Conv2d(self.dim, self.rank, 1, bias=False)
            self.k = nn.Conv2d(self.dim, self.rank, 1, bias=False)
            self.v_split = nn.Conv2d(self.dim, self.rank, 1, groups=self.rank, bias=False)
            self.proj = nn.Conv2d(self.rank, self.out_dim, 1, bias=False)
            self.apply(self._init_weights)
        self.scale = self.rank ** -0.5

    def _init_weights(self, m):
        if isinstance(m, nn.Conv2d):
            nn.init.trunc_normal_(m.weight, std=0.02)
            if m.bias is not None:
                nn.init.constant_(m.bias, 0)

    def param_report(self):
        return {"dim": self.dim, "rank": self.rank, "window": self.window,
                "out_dim": self.out_dim, "global_branch": self.global_branch,
                "params": sum(p.numel() for p in self.parameters())}

    def forward(self, x_q, x_kv, score):
        """`x_q` (B,C,H,W)：Query 来源（保持全分辨率，**不压缩**）。
        `x_kv` (B,C,H,W)：K/V 来源（同 stage，将按 window 压缩）。
        `score` (B,1,H,W)：该分支的双曲变化分数（数值，非 rank）。

        参数省法：`V` 用 **per-channel depthwise 1×1**（`groups=rank`，每通道一个标量），
        把 value 通道降到 `rank` 后在注意力输出处再用 `proj: rank→dim` 升回。
        这使单分支参数从 `2·dim²` 降到 `dim·rank + dim·rank + rank·dim`，
        在 `dim=768, rank=32` 时从 1.18M 降到 74K（**降 16×**）。
        """
        if self.global_branch:
            kv_src = global_deviation_weights(x_kv, window=self.window)
        else:
            kv_src = change_weighted_pool(x_kv, score, window=self.window)
        k = self.k(kv_src)
        v = self.v_split(kv_src)
        q = self.q(x_q)

        B, r, H, W = q.shape
        _, _, hk, wk = k.shape
        qf = q.flatten(2).transpose(1, 2)                 # (B, N, r)
        kf = k.flatten(2)                                 # (B, r, n)
        vf = v.flatten(2).transpose(1, 2)                 # (B, n, r)
        attn = torch.softmax(qf @ kf * self.scale, dim=-1)     # (B, N, n)
        out = (attn @ vf).transpose(1, 2).reshape(B, r, H, W)
        return self.proj(out)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4 层 × 双分支的完整模块
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
class CAACPHQKV(nn.Module):
    """跨 4 个 stage 的双曲双分支 SAT-QKV 变化聚合。

    - 4 个 stage 各自独立参数（满足 R2「每层」）；
    - 每个 stage 两个分支（detail / global）各自独立 QKV（满足 R7「特化」）；
    - K/V token 压缩到 `k·N`，Q 保持 `N`（满足 R8）；
    - 输出在 1/4 尺度上按 stage 大小上采样后相加，再乘零初始化门（满足 R4 的"跳层连接"注入）。
    """

    def __init__(self, stage_dims=(96, 192, 384, 768), rank=32,
                 detail_window=2, global_window=4,
                 attn_stages=ATTN_STAGES_DEFAULT,
                 r0=R0_DEFAULT, a=A_DEFAULT, local_ksize=3,
                 lite_dim=96, init_seed=HQKV_SEED,
                 score_stages=(0, 1, 2, 3)):
        super().__init__()
        self.stage_dims = tuple(int(d) for d in stage_dims)
        self.rank = int(rank)
        self.detail_window = int(detail_window)
        self.global_window = int(global_window)
        self.attn_stages = tuple(int(i) for i in attn_stages)
        self.r0 = float(r0)
        self.a = float(a)
        self.local_ksize = int(local_ksize)
        self.lite_dim = int(lite_dim)
        # 哪些 stage 的双曲分数参与 HGSL 监督（= 需要保留计算图的那些）
        self.score_stages = tuple(int(i) for i in score_stages)
        self._scores = {}

        # 每 stage 的双分支注意力（**最后构造** + fork_rng 固定局部种子）
        self.detail_attn = nn.ModuleList()
        self.global_attn = nn.ModuleList()
        self.lite_fuse = nn.ModuleList()
        for i, d in enumerate(self.stage_dims):
            if i in self.attn_stages:
                with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=[]):
                    torch.manual_seed(init_seed + i)
                    self.detail_attn.append(BranchSATAttention(
                        d, rank=rank, window=detail_window, global_branch=False,
                        out_dim=lite_dim, init_seed=init_seed + i))
                    self.global_attn.append(BranchSATAttention(
                        d, rank=rank, window=global_window, global_branch=True,
                        out_dim=lite_dim, init_seed=init_seed + 8 + i))
                    self.lite_fuse.append(None)
            else:
                self.detail_attn.append(None)
                self.global_attn.append(None)
                with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=[]):
                    torch.manual_seed(init_seed + 16 + i)
                    fuse = nn.Sequential(
                        nn.Conv2d(3 * d, self.lite_dim, 1, bias=False),
                        nn.BatchNorm2d(self.lite_dim),
                        nn.SiLU(),
                        nn.Conv2d(self.lite_dim, self.lite_dim, 1, bias=False),
                    )
                    fuse.apply(self._init_weights)
                self.lite_fuse.append(fuse)

        # 零初始化门（**在 apply 之后**，否则会被初始化覆盖——HFAE 首版踩过这个坑）
        self.gamma_detail = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(1))
        self.gamma_global = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(1))
        self.gamma_lite = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(1))

        # 诊断量（只读，供日志/裁决）
        self._diag = {}

    @staticmethod
    def _init_weights(m):
        if isinstance(m, nn.Conv2d):
            nn.init.trunc_normal_(m.weight, std=0.02)
            if m.bias is not None:
                nn.init.constant_(m.bias, 0)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def branch_scores(self, xa, xb, stage, geometry="hyperbolic"):
        """返回 `(s_detail, s_global)`，各 (B,1,H,W)。

        **本方法不自带 `no_grad`**：梯度是否流回几何量由调用方决定
        （`score_stages` 里包含 `stage` ⇒ 保留计算图；否则调用方在 `no_grad` 下调用）。
        这一点必须显式，否则 HGSL 的 `hgsl_detach_score=False` 臂会静默失效。
        """
        score_fn = lorentz_change_score if geometry == "hyperbolic" else euclidean_change_score
        s_d = score_fn(xa, xb, branch="detail", r0=self.r0, a=self.a, ksize=self.local_ksize)
        s_g = score_fn(xa, xb, branch="global", r0=self.r0, a=self.a, ksize=self.local_ksize)
        return s_d, s_g

    def param_report(self):
        out = {"form": HQKV_FORM, "rank": self.rank,
               "detail_window": self.detail_window, "global_window": self.global_window,
               "attn_stages": list(self.attn_stages),
               "r0": self.r0, "a": self.a,
               "gammas": {"detail": float(self.gamma_detail.detach()),
                          "global": float(self.gamma_global.detach()),
                          "lite": float(self.gamma_lite.detach())},
               "per_stage": {}}
        total = 0
        for i, d in enumerate(self.stage_dims):
            ent = {"dim": d}
            for name, mod in (("detail", self.detail_attn[i]), ("global", self.global_attn[i]),
                              ("lite", self.lite_fuse[i])):
                if mod is not None:
                    np_ = sum(p.numel() for p in mod.parameters())
                    ent[name] = np_
                    total += np_
            out["per_stage"][f"stage{i}"] = ent
        out["total_params"] = total + 3          # 3 个 gamma
        out["gamma_zero_init"] = all(float(g.detach().abs().sum()) == 0.0 for g in
                                     (self.gamma_detail, self.gamma_global, self.gamma_lite))
        return out

    def diag(self):
        return dict(self._diag)

    def score_maps(self):
        """上一次 forward 缓存的每 stage 双曲变化分数（供 HGSL 使用）。

        返回 `{stage_i: (B,1,H_i,W_i)}`，**B 尺寸**（每对样品一张），已 `.detach()` 与否
        取决于构造时的 `score_requires_grad`。空的 dict 表示本步没有分数（`caacp=0`）。
        """
        return dict(self._scores)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(self, feats_a, feats_b, geometry="hyperbolic", inject_indices=None):
        """`feats_a/feats_b`: 每时相 4 个 stage 的 (B, C_i, H_i, W_i)。

        返回 `(out, diag)`：
        - `out` (B, lite_dim, 64, 64)：待注入 DCR 最细尺度的增量（**已乘 γ_detail**）；
        - `diag`：各 stage 的分支门值、分数统计、分支间排序分歧（非退化性证据）。
        """
        if inject_indices is None:
            inject_indices = (0, 1, 2, 3)
        assert len(feats_a) == len(feats_b) == len(self.stage_dims)
        ref_hw = feats_a[0].shape[-2:]
        acc_d, acc_g, acc_l = None, None, None
        diag = {"stages": {}}
        self._scores = {}

        for i in range(len(self.stage_dims)):
            if i in self.score_stages:
                xa = feats_a[i].float()
                xb = feats_b[i].float()
                self._scores[i] = self.branch_scores(xa, xb, i, geometry=geometry)[0]
            else:
                xa = feats_a[i].detach().float()
                xb = feats_b[i].detach().float()
            with torch.no_grad():
                if i in self.score_stages:
                    s_d = self._scores[i].detach()
                    s_g = self.branch_scores(xa, xb, i, geometry=geometry)[1].detach()
                else:
                    s_d, s_g = self.branch_scores(xa, xb, i, geometry=geometry)
                    s_d, s_g = s_d.detach(), s_g.detach()
                diag["stages"][f"stage{i}"] = {
                    "score_detail_mean": float(s_d.mean()),
                    "score_detail_max": float(s_d.amax()),
                    "score_global_mean": float(s_g.mean()),
                    "score_global_max": float(s_g.amax()),
                }
                from model.layers.hyperbolic import branch_rank_disagreement
                dg = branch_rank_disagreement(
                    s_d.reshape(s_d.shape[0], -1), s_g.reshape(s_g.shape[0], -1))
                diag["stages"][f"stage{i}"].update(
                    {f"deg_d_vs_g_{k}": v for k, v in dg.items()})

            if i not in inject_indices:
                continue
            if i in self.attn_stages:
                a = self.detail_attn[i](feats_a[i], feats_a[i], s_d)
                b = self.global_attn[i](feats_b[i], feats_b[i], s_g)
                o = a + b
                with torch.no_grad():
                    diag["stages"][f"stage{i}"]["branch_out_ratio_db"] = float(
                        a.detach().norm() / b.detach().norm().clamp_min(1e-12))
            else:
                o = self.lite_fuse[i](
                    torch.cat([feats_a[i], feats_b[i], (xa - xb).abs()], dim=1))
            if o.shape[-2:] != ref_hw:
                o = F.interpolate(o, size=ref_hw, mode="bilinear", align_corners=False)
            acc_d = o if acc_d is None else acc_d + o
            with torch.no_grad():
                diag["stages"][f"stage{i}"]["contrib_norm"] = float(o.detach().norm())

        assert acc_d is not None, "至少需要一个 stage 参与注入"
        out = self.gamma_detail * acc_d
        self._diag = diag
        self._diag["gammas"] = {"detail": float(self.gamma_detail.detach()),
                                "global": float(self.gamma_global.detach()),
                                "lite": float(self.gamma_lite.detach())}
        return out, diag


def build_caacp_hqkv(stage_dims=(96, 192, 384, 768), **kwargs):
    return CAACPHQKV(stage_dims=stage_dims, **kwargs)

================================================================================
# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/model\layers\caacp_ss2d.py
================================================================================
"""CAACP-SS2D：Change-Aware Asymmetric Context Pooling for SS2D（创新一）。

在 TinyViM-S 的 Stage3（0-based index=2）最终 TViMBlock 的 SS2D 上，把官方
「均匀 AvgPool 低频下采样」替换为「双时相变化感知的 2×2 cell 加权聚合」：

    c_avg = AvgPool2x2(x_low)                       # 官方均匀池化
    c_ca  = change_weighted_pool2x2(x_low, score)   # 变化感知加权聚合（A/B 共享权重）
    c     = c_avg + beta * (c_ca - c_avg)           # β=0 初始化 → 精确恢复官方预训练

设计纪律（调研文档 §8 / §13.3）：
- 压缩发生在**规则二维网格**上（2×2 cell → 规则 8×8 lattice），再进行四向
  CrossScan——不做 TopK、不做 token 重排、不按方向分别压缩（STORM/官方结构前提）；
- 完整 dense local 路径（高频 RepDW 分支 + 16×16 residual）始终保留；
- score 由 1/16 双时相特征 cosine 计算（参数自由、no_grad、T1/T2 对称）；
- A/B 共享同一组 cell 权重（同一 spatial assignment）；
- 只使用一个零初始化机制：β=0 external gate（SS2D 全部预训练参数正常继承）。
"""
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

from model.layers.ss2d import SS2D, cross_selective_scan


def change_score_cosine_2d(x1, x2):
    """双时相特征的变化分数：s = 1 - cos(x1, x2)，逐像素，参数自由，T1/T2 对称。

    x1/x2: (B, C, H, W) -> (B, H, W) in [0, 2]。
    """
    p1 = F.normalize(x1, dim=1, eps=1e-8)
    p2 = F.normalize(x2, dim=1, eps=1e-8)
    return (1.0 - (p1 * p2).sum(dim=1)).clamp(min=0.0)


def rank_normalize_2d(s):
    """逐图 rank 归一化到 [0, 1]（与 CASAA 的 rank_normalize_per_image 同口径）。"""
    B, H, W = s.shape
    flat = s.flatten(1)                                   # (B, N)
    ranks = flat.argsort(dim=1).argsort(dim=1).float()
    return (ranks / max(flat.shape[1] - 1, 1)).view(B, H, W)


def change_weighted_pool2x2(x, score, eps=1e-6):
    """结构保持的变化感知 2×2 cell 加权聚合（规则网格，非 TopK）。

    x: (B, C, H, W)；score: (B, H, W) 已 rank 归一化（调用方 no_grad）。
    每个 2×2 cell 内：w_i = (eps + s_i) / sum_j(eps + s_j)；A/B 共享权重。
    返回 (B, C, H/2, W/2)。对 x 保留梯度（权重路径无梯度）。
    """
    B, C, H, W = x.shape
    assert H % 2 == 0 and W % 2 == 0
    s = score.view(B, 1, H // 2, 2, W // 2, 2)
    w = (s + eps)
    w = w / w.sum(dim=(3, 5), keepdim=True)               # cell 内归一化
    xr = x.view(B, C, H // 2, 2, W // 2, 2)
    return (xr * w).sum(dim=(3, 5))                       # (B, C, H/2, W/2)


def confidence_preserving_pool2x2(x, abs_score, rank_score, eps=1e-6):
    """CP-CAACP（Run2 首选一）：置信度保留的 2×2 cell 加权聚合。

    动机：rank-only 权重在「零变化图」上仍强制 cell 内不均匀（rank 存在 min/max），
    使 c_ca 偏离 c_avg → 训练早期向低频特征注入噪声 → 弱化 dense 路径、拖低 Precision。
    CP 公式：q_i = s_i * r_i（abs 余弦变化 × rank 归一化），
            w_i = (1 + q_i) / Σ_j(1 + q_j)。
    - s_i = 0（该像素双时相特征完全一致）→ q_i = 0 → 与所有零变化邻居等权：
      全零变化 cell 严格退化为均匀池化（c_ca == c_avg，修正项精确为 0）；
    - s_i 高且 rank 高 → 权重显著抬升（变化区域主导聚合）；
    - 参数自由、A/B 共享权重、规则网格不变。

    abs_score: (B, H, W) in [0, 2]（clamp 后的 1-cos）；rank_score: (B, H, W) in [0, 1]。
    """
    B, C, H, W = x.shape
    assert H % 2 == 0 and W % 2 == 0
    q = abs_score * rank_score                            # (B, H, W)，无变化 → 0
    q = q.view(B, 1, H // 2, 2, W // 2, 2)
    w = (1.0 + q)
    w = w / w.sum(dim=(3, 5), keepdim=True)
    xr = x.view(B, C, H // 2, 2, W // 2, 2)
    return (xr * w).sum(dim=(3, 5))


class CAACPSS2D(SS2D):
    """SS2D + CAACP（只用于 index=2 / Stage3 最终 TViM）。

    参数结构 = 官方 SS2D + 唯一新增 scalar beta（zero-init）。预训练加载时
    beta 不在 checkpoint 中 → 保持 0，epoch-0 输出与官方逐位一致。

    score_mode:
      "rank" — Run1 公式 w ∝ eps + rank（默认，向后兼容）；
      "cp"   — Run2 CP 公式 w ∝ 1 + s·r（confidence-preserving）。
    residual_mode（Run3 E4 RA-CAACP）:
      "current"    — res = x - Up(c)（官方结构；change-aware c 同时进 SS2D 与 residual 扣除）
      "avg_anchor" — res = x - Up(c_avg)（RA：residual 永远锚定官方均匀 context，
                     保护 dense 高频残差不被 change-aware pooling 减掉；β=0 两者等价）
    """

    def __init__(self, eps=1e-6, score_mode="rank", residual_mode="current", **kwargs):
        assert kwargs.get("index", -1) == 2, "CAACP only applies to Stage3 (index=2) final TViM"
        assert score_mode in ("rank", "cp")
        assert residual_mode in ("current", "avg_anchor")
        super().__init__(**kwargs)
        self.score_mode = score_mode
        self.residual_mode = residual_mode
        self.beta = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(1))
        self._pair_score = None
        self._pair_abs = None
        self._score_delta = 0.0    # |c_ca - c_avg| 均值（诊断记录）
        self._abs_mean = None      # cp 模式 abs score 均值（诊断记录）
        self._weight_entropy = None  # 2×2 cell 权重熵均值（诊断记录；均匀=ln4）

    def set_pair_score(self, score, abs_score=None):
        """注入共享变化分数（2B, H, W；A/B 两半相同）。调用方负责 no_grad。

        score: rank 归一化分数；abs_score: clamp 后的 [0,2] 余弦分数（cp 模式必填）。
        """
        self._pair_score = score
        self._pair_abs = abs_score

    @staticmethod
    def _cp_weights(abs_score, rank_score):
        """cp 模式 cell 权重 (B,1,H//2,2,W//2,2)，与 confidence_preserving_pool2x2 同口径（仅诊断）。"""
        B, H, W = abs_score.shape
        q = (abs_score * rank_score).view(B, 1, H // 2, 2, W // 2, 2)
        w = 1.0 + q
        return w / w.sum(dim=(3, 5), keepdim=True)

    @staticmethod
    def _rank_weights(score):
        """rank 模式 cell 权重 (B,1,H//2,2,W//2,2)，与 change_weighted_pool2x2 同口径（仅诊断）。"""
        B, H, W = score.shape
        s = score.view(B, 1, H // 2, 2, W // 2, 2)
        w = s + 1e-6
        return w / w.sum(dim=(3, 5), keepdim=True)

    def weight_entropy(self):
        return self._weight_entropy

    def abs_mean(self):
        return self._abs_mean

    def forward_core(self, x, nrows=-1, channel_first=False):
        nrows = 1
        if not channel_first:
            x = x.permute(0, 3, 1, 2).contiguous()
        if self.ssm_low_rank:
            x = self.in_rank(x)

        x_low, x_high = torch.split(x, self.split, dim=1)
        B, C, H, W = x.shape
        x_high = self.local_conv(x_high)
        if self.index < 3:
            x0 = x_low
            c_avg = self.pool(x_low)
            if self._pair_score is not None:
                if self.score_mode == "cp":
                    assert self._pair_abs is not None, "cp mode requires abs_score in set_pair_score"
                    c_ca = confidence_preserving_pool2x2(x_low, self._pair_abs, self._pair_score)
                    w = self._cp_weights(self._pair_abs, self._pair_score)
                else:
                    c_ca = change_weighted_pool2x2(x_low, self._pair_score)
                    w = self._rank_weights(self._pair_score)
                with torch.no_grad():
                    self._score_delta = (c_ca - c_avg).abs().mean().item()
                    # Run2 §12.1B 诊断：cell 权重熵（均匀=ln4≈1.386）+ abs score 均值
                    ent = -(w * (w + 1e-12).log()).sum(dim=(3, 5)).mean().item()
                    self._weight_entropy = ent
                    self._abs_mean = (float(self._pair_abs.mean().item())
                                      if self._pair_abs is not None else None)
                c = c_avg + self.beta * (c_ca - c_avg)
            else:
                c = c_avg
            # RA-CAACP（Run3 E4）：residual 锚定 c_avg（官方均匀 context），
            # 使 change-aware c 只进 SS2D、不从 dense residual 中扣除高频证据；
            # β=0 时两种模式与官方 TinyViM 逐位一致。
            if self.residual_mode == "avg_anchor":
                res = x0 - F.interpolate(c_avg, (H, W), mode='nearest')
            else:
                res = x0 - F.interpolate(c, (H, W), mode='nearest')
            x_low = c

        x_low = cross_selective_scan(
            x_low, self.x_proj_weight, None, self.dt_projs_weight, self.dt_projs_bias,
            self.A_logs, self.Ds, getattr(self, "out_norm", None),
            nrows=nrows, delta_softplus=True, force_fp32=self.training,
        )
        x_low = x_low.permute(0, 3, 1, 2)

        if self.index < 3:
            x_low = F.interpolate(x_low, scale_factor=2 ** (3 - self.index), mode='bilinear') + res
        x = torch.cat((x_low, x_high), dim=1)
        if self.ssm_low_rank:
            x = self.out_rank(x)
        return x

# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/model\layers\candidate_context.py

"""Matched H/E retrieval, temporal Haar evidence and decoder-conditioned routing."""
import math
import torch
from torch import nn
from torch.nn import functional as F
from model.layers.lorentz_ops import acosh1p_squared
from model.layers.pyramid_context import channel_norm, feature_regions, pool_features, rms
from model.layers.moe_context import haar_split, haar_merge

MODEL_VARIANTS = ('none', 'static', 'moe')
EXPERTS = ('hyperbolic', 'qkv', 'frequency', 'tar')


class TemporalStem(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.project = nn.ModuleList(nn.Conv2d(c, 64, 1, bias=False) for c in (24, 32, 96, 320))
        self.fuse = nn.ModuleList(nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(128, 64, 1, bias=False),
                                               nn.BatchNorm2d(64), nn.SiLU()) for _ in range(4))

    def forward(self, a, b):
        pa, pb = [p(x) for p, x in zip(self.project, a)], [p(x) for p, x in zip(self.project, b)]
        x = [f(torch.cat(((v-u).abs(), (v+u)/2), 1)) for f, u, v in zip(self.fuse, pa, pb)]
        return x, pa, pb

    def switch_to_deploy(self):
        from torch.nn.utils.fusion import fuse_conv_bn_eval
        for f in self.fuse:
            f[0] = fuse_conv_bn_eval(f[0], f[1])
            f[1] = nn.Identity()


class TemporalWavelet(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.high = nn.ModuleList(nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(192, 192, 1, bias=False), nn.SiLU(),
            nn.Conv2d(192, 192, 3, padding=1, groups=192, bias=False)) for _ in range(2))
        self.out = nn.ModuleList(nn.Conv2d(64, 64, 1, bias=False) for _ in range(2))
        self.low = nn.ModuleList(nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(64, 64, 1, bias=False), nn.SiLU(),
            nn.Conv2d(64, 64, 3, padding=1, groups=64, bias=False)) for _ in range(2))
        self.eta = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(4, 64))

    def forward(self, pa, pb):
        # Linearity gives exactly the signed difference of the two DWTs.
        bands = [haar_split(b-a) for a, b in zip(pa[:3], pb[:3])]
        outputs = []
        for s in range(2):
            low, high = bands[s]
            packed = high.transpose(1, 2).flatten(1, 2)
            update = packed + self.high[s](packed)
            high = update.reshape(update.shape[0], 3, 64, *low.shape[-2:]).transpose(1, 2)
            outputs.append(self.out[s](haar_merge(torch.zeros_like(low), high)))
        outputs += [layer(bands[s+1][0].abs()/2) for s, layer in enumerate(self.low)]
        return [x * self.eta[s][None, :, None, None] for s, x in enumerate(outputs)]


class PairedRetrieval(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.qk = nn.Conv2d(64, 32, 1, bias=False)
        self.v = nn.Conv2d(64, 32, 1, bias=False)
        self.out = nn.Conv2d(32, 64, 1, bias=False)
        self.scale_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(4, 4))
        self.raw_temperature = nn.Parameter(torch.full((2,), math.log(math.expm1(.5))))
        self.gamma = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(4, 64))
        self.counts = (128, 32, 32, 32)

    def prepare(self, prototype, support):
        return (F.linear(prototype, self.qk.weight[:, :, 0, 0])/math.sqrt(32),
                F.linear(prototype, self.v.weight[:, :, 0, 0]), support)

    def forward(self, x, bank, scale, diagnose=False):
        key, value, support = bank
        query = self.qk(x).flatten(2).transpose(1, 2)/math.sqrt(32)
        q2, k2 = query.square().sum(-1, keepdim=True), key.square().sum(-1).unsqueeze(1)
        gram = query @ key.transpose(1, 2)
        euclidean = (q2+k2-2*gram).clamp_min(0)
        time_diff = (1+q2).sqrt() - (1+k2).sqrt()
        hyperbolic = acosh1p_squared((.5*(euclidean-time_diff.square())).clamp_min(0))
        bias = torch.cat([self.scale_bias[scale, s].expand(n)-math.log(n)
                          for s, n in enumerate(self.counts)])
        temp = F.softplus(self.raw_temperature)
        residuals, attentions, stats = [], [], {}
        for i, distance in enumerate((hyperbolic, euclidean)):
            scaled = distance/(2*temp[i].square())
            attention = (-scaled + support.log()[:, None] + bias).softmax(-1)
            retrieved = (attention @ value).transpose(1, 2).reshape(x.shape[0], 32, *x.shape[-2:])
            residuals.append(self.gamma[scale][None, :, None, None] * self.out(retrieved-self.v(x)))
            attentions.append(attention)
            if diagnose:
                with torch.no_grad():
                    stats[EXPERTS[i]] = dict(temperature=float(temp[i]), scaled_distance_mean=float(scaled.mean()),
                        scaled_distance_std=float(scaled.std(unbiased=False)),
                        scale_mass=[float(a.sum(-1).mean()) for a in attention.split(self.counts, -1)],
                        entropy=float(-(attention*attention.clamp_min(1e-9).log()).sum(-1).mean()/math.log(224)),
                        retrieved_spatial_std=float(retrieved.flatten(2).std(-1, unbiased=False).mean()))
        if diagnose:
            with torch.no_grad():
                radius = query.norm(dim=-1).asinh()
                stats.update(radius_mean=float(radius.mean()), radius_cv=float(radius.std(unbiased=False)/radius.mean().clamp_min(1e-8)),
                             attention_he_tv=float((attentions[0]-attentions[1]).abs().sum(-1).mean()/2),
                             residual_he_cosine=float(F.cosine_similarity(residuals[0].flatten(1), residuals[1].flatten(1)).mean()))
        return residuals, stats


class CandidateContext(nn.Module):
    geometry = 'paired_lorentz_euclidean'
    route_names = EXPERTS
    grid_sizes = (8, 4, 4, 4)

    def __init__(self, variant):
        super().__init__()
        self.variant = variant
        self.stem = TemporalStem()
        self.norm = nn.LayerNorm(64)
        self.guide = nn.Conv2d(64, 1, 1)
        if variant != 'none':
            self.retrieval = PairedRetrieval()
            self.wavelet = TemporalWavelet()
        if variant == 'static':
            self.static_logits = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(4, 4))
        if variant == 'moe':
            self.router = nn.ModuleList(nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(64 if s == 3 else 128, 16, 1), nn.SiLU(),
                nn.Conv2d(16, 16, 3, padding=1, groups=16), nn.SiLU(), nn.Conv2d(16, 4, 1)) for s in range(4))
            for layer in self.router:
                nn.init.zeros_(layer[-1].weight)
                nn.init.zeros_(layer[-1].bias)
        self.collect_diag = False
        self.capture = False
        self.last_diag = {}
        self.captured = {}

    def prepare(self, a, b):
        x, pa, pb = self.stem(a, b)
        normalized = [channel_norm(self.norm, f) for f in x]
        guide = [self.guide(f) for f in normalized]
        bank, waves = None, None
        if self.variant != 'none':
            prototypes, supports = [], []
            for f, g, grid in zip(normalized, guide, self.grid_sizes):
                p = feature_regions(g.sigmoid(), grid).squeeze(-1)
                membership = torch.stack((p, 1-p), 2)
                weights = (membership+1e-6).square()
                weights = weights/weights.sum(-1, keepdim=True)
                prototypes.append(pool_features(f, weights, grid))
                supports.append((weights*membership).sum(-1).flatten(1).clamp_min(1e-6))
            bank = self.retrieval.prepare(torch.cat(prototypes, 1), torch.cat(supports, 1))
            waves = self.wavelet(pa, pb)
        if self.capture:
            self.captured = {}
        return x, normalized, guide, bank, waves

    def weights(self, scale, x, coarse):
        if self.variant == 'none':
            g = x.new_zeros(x.shape[0], 4, *x.shape[-2:]); g[:, 3] = 1
            return g
        if self.variant == 'static':
            return self.static_logits[scale].softmax(0)[None, :, None, None].expand(x.shape[0], -1, *x.shape[-2:])
        norm = lambda f: F.layer_norm(f.permute(0, 2, 3, 1), (64,)).permute(0, 3, 1, 2)
        inputs = norm(x) if coarse is None else torch.cat((norm(x), norm(coarse)), 1)
        return self.router[scale](inputs).softmax(1)

    def mix(self, scale, x, normalized, tar, coarse, bank, waves):
        if self.variant == 'none':
            return tar
        g = self.weights(scale, x, coarse)
        (rh, re), geometry = self.retrieval(normalized, bank, scale, self.collect_diag)
        rw = waves[scale]
        h, e, w, t = g.split(1, 1)
        z = (h+e+w)*x + t*tar + h*rh + e*re + w*rw
        if self.capture:
            self.captured[scale] = dict(routes=g, residuals=(rh, re, rw), tar=tar, x=x)
        if self.collect_diag:
            with torch.no_grad():
                deltas = (rh, re, rw, tar-x)
                base = rms(x).clamp_min(1e-8)
                self.last_diag[f'stage{scale+1}'] = dict(finite=bool(torch.isfinite(z).all()), contexts=224,
                    route_mass=dict(zip(EXPERTS, g.mean((0, 2, 3)).tolist())),
                    route_variance=dict(zip(EXPERTS, g.flatten(2).var(-1, unbiased=False).mean(0).tolist())),
                    route_entropy=float(-(g*g.clamp_min(1e-9).log()).sum(1).mean()/math.log(4)),
                    expert_rms={n: float(rms(d)) for n, d in zip(EXPERTS, deltas)},
                    effective_rms={n: float(rms(g[:, i:i+1]*d)) for i, (n, d) in enumerate(zip(EXPERTS, deltas))},
                    residual_to_feature_rms=float(rms(z-x)/base), feature_rms=float(base), tar_rms=float(rms(tar)),
                    wavelet_ll_rms=float(rms(haar_split(rw)[0])) if scale < 2 else None,
                    routed_wavelet_ll_rms=float(rms(haar_split(w*rw)[0])) if scale < 2 else None,
                    geometry=geometry)
        return z

    def metadata(self):
        return dict(variant=self.variant, active_experts=list(EXPERTS) if self.variant != 'none' else ['tar'],
            routing_mode={'none': 'tar_only', 'static': 'learned_per_scale_constant', 'moe': 'coarse_to_fine_spatial'}[self.variant],
            execution='dense', context_grid_sizes=list(self.grid_sizes), context_prototypes_per_scale=[128,32,32,32],
            joint_contexts=224, heads=1, qkv_dim=32, value_geometry='euclidean',
            key_mode='shared_pooled_content', hyperbolic_radius='asinh(projection_norm)',
            shared_he=['qk','v','out','gamma','scale_bias','guide','norm','prototypes'],
            frequency_transform='signed_temporal_haar_H1_H2_L2_L3', guide_input='common_X',
            context_gamma_shape=[4,64], wavelet_gamma_shape=[4,64], initializer_seed=2333)
