# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/model\layers\casaa.py
================================================================================
"""CASAA (Change-Aware Selective Aggregation Attention) — CASA-CD 创新主线一。

「完整 Query + 变化感知压缩 K/V」的非对称注意力：

    Y_t = Softmax(Q_t @ K_t^T / sqrt(d)) @ V_t,  t in {1, 2}
    Q_t: (B, N, C)     —— N 个 query 完整保留（逐位置判别能力不变）
    K_t/V_t: (B, K, C) —— K = keep_ratio * N 个压缩上下文 token，K << N
    注意力交互量：O(N^2) -> O(NK)（routing 之外）

与 SAT SAA（others/SAT/saa.py）的关系——复用其思想，不照搬参数化：
  - 复用：density-peak 选心、token-to-center 分配、簇内均值、Full Q / 压缩 K/V
    的注意力结构、norm preservation。
  - 改动：deterministic stratified sampling（eval routing 与 RNG 无关）；
    双时相共享路由；疑似变化 token 原位保留 + 稳定背景 token 强聚合；
    原 DeiT-Tiny fused qkv 权重切片（qkv/proj 参数名与形状不变，零新增参数）。

router 模式：
  - 'change'  (CASAA, A2)：Kc=change_share*K 个 change-score TopK token 原位保留，
    Kb=K-Kc 个稳定背景 token 按共享内容聚类；C_t = [X_t[Ic]; mean_cluster(X_t_bg)]。
    change score = 1 - cos(x1_i, x2_i)（参数自由、T1/T2 对称）。
  - 'content' (SAA-style 对照, A1)：Kc=0，全部 token 按共享内容聚类成 K 个原型。
  - 'oracle'  (A3, 仅诊断)：change score 直接用 GT patch occupancy
    （Encoder 从 label 计算后以 score_hint 传入）。DIAGNOSTIC-ONLY，不可部署。
  - 'detail'  (A4-D)：change score 直接用 rank 归一化的 detail 1/8 cosine
    （Encoder 计算后经 score_hint 传入；可部署，零新增参数）。
  - 'detail_fused' (A4, CASAA-v2)：s = 0.5*rank(s_v) + 0.5*rank(s_d)，
    s_v=ViT 双时相 cosine，s_d=已有 detail branch 1/8（layer3, 32×32→pool→16×16）
    的双时相 cosine（Encoder 计算并 rank 归一化后经 score_hint 传入）。
    零新增参数、零新 loss。

设计要点：
  - 切片 qkv 投影：对 full X 只算 Q，对压缩上下文只算 K/V（权重仍为原 fused
    qkv 的行切片，数学与原「整段 qkv 后切片」等价，不白算 full K/V 与 compressed Q）。
  - routing 索引（TopK / assignment）在 torch.no_grad() 下计算；聚合/收集仍使用
    带梯度的原 feature tensor，梯度可回传到背景 token。
  - _routing 记录上一次 forward_pair 的诊断统计（score / 索引 / 聚类大小 /
    bank 范数 / 注意力质量），供 tools/archive/casaa_router_diagnostic.py 做 Router Audit。
"""
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

from .attention import Attention


def change_score_cosine(x1, x2):
    """参数自由的双时相变化显著性：s_i = 1 - cos(x1_i, x2_i)。

    Args:
        x1, x2: (B, N, C)
    Returns:
        s: (B, N)，越大越可能是变化区域。
    """
    x1_n = F.normalize(x1, dim=-1)
    x2_n = F.normalize(x2, dim=-1)
    return 1.0 - (x1_n * x2_n).sum(dim=-1)


@torch.no_grad()
def rank_normalize_per_image(s):
    """每张图内的 ordinal rank 归一化（stable sort）：最低 score → 0，最高 → 1。

    用于把不同 feature space 的 change score（ViT cosine / detail cosine）
    拉到同一尺度后做无参数融合。只用于 routing，禁止进入梯度图。
    """
    B, N = s.shape
    order = torch.argsort(s, dim=-1, stable=True)   # 升序
    rank = torch.empty_like(order, dtype=s.dtype)
    base = torch.arange(N, device=s.device, dtype=s.dtype)
    base = base.unsqueeze(0).expand(B, -1)
    rank.scatter_(1, order, base)
    return rank / max(N - 1, 1)


def deterministic_stratified_subindex(M, K, S, device):
    """从 M 个 token 里确定性分层取 S 个子采样点（每区均匀分布，与 RNG 无关）。

    只使用整数运算（对 fvcore tracer 友好）。
    """
    samples_per_region = S // K
    sub = []
    for i in range(K):
        start = i * (M // K)
        end = (i + 1) * (M // K) if i < K - 1 else M
        region_size = end - start
        if region_size > 0:
            n = min(samples_per_region, region_size)
            if n == 1:
                pos = torch.zeros(1, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
            else:
                j = torch.arange(n, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
                pos = j * (region_size - 1) // (n - 1)   # 整数除法，均匀分布
            sub.append(start + pos)
    sub_idx = torch.cat(sub) if sub else torch.zeros(0, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
    if len(sub_idx) < S:
        all_idx = torch.arange(M, device=device)
        mask = torch.ones(M, dtype=torch.bool, device=device)
        mask[sub_idx] = False
        sub_idx = torch.cat([sub_idx, all_idx[mask][: S - len(sub_idx)]])
    return sub_idx


def deterministic_density_assign(z, K):
    """在 M 个（已 L2 归一化的）token 上做确定性 density-peak 聚类。

    算法结构沿用 SAT 的 cluster_and_merge，但子采样是确定性的（分层均匀采样），
    且只在 background 集合上执行。

    Args:
        z: (B, M, C) 归一化 token（Run1 为共享背景描述 Norm((x1+x2)/2)）。
        K: 聚类数。
    Returns:
        assign_idx: (B, M) long，每个 token 所属 cluster id ∈ [0, K)。
    """
    B, M, C = z.shape
    # 显式转 int：JIT 追踪下 shape 是符号量，Python 侧整数运算（range/min//）需要真 int
    B, M, C = int(B), int(M), int(C)
    device = z.device
    assert M >= K, f"density assign needs M({M}) >= K({K})"

    S = min(M, max(2 * K, 4 * K))
    sub_idx = deterministic_stratified_subindex(M, K, S, device)

    z_sub = z[:, sub_idx]                      # (B, S, C)
    sim_sub = z_sub @ z_sub.transpose(1, 2)    # 余弦相似度 (B, S, S)
    torch.diagonal(sim_sub, dim1=1, dim2=2).fill_(-1)

    k = min(K, S - 1)
    sim_topk_sub, _ = torch.topk(sim_sub, k=k, dim=-1)
    density_sub = sim_topk_sub.mean(dim=-1)    # (B, S) 局部密度 rho
    # 确定性 tie-break（SAT 用 rand*1e-6 随机扰动；这里用与索引相关的固定扰动）
    density_sub = density_sub + torch.arange(S, device=device, dtype=z.dtype) * 1e-6

    mask_higher = (density_sub[:, None, :] > density_sub[:, :, None]).float()
    masked_sim = sim_sub * mask_higher - 1e9 * (1.0 - mask_higher)
    max_sim_to_higher, _ = masked_sim.max(dim=-1)

    delta_sub = 1.0 - max_sim_to_higher          # 距离 delta
    max_density_mask = (mask_higher.sum(dim=-1) == 0)
    min_sim_global = sim_sub.min(dim=-1)[0]
    delta_sub[max_density_mask] = (1.0 - min_sim_global)[max_density_mask]
    delta_sub = delta_sub.clamp(min=0.0)

    score_sub = density_sub * delta_sub          # gamma = rho * delta
    _, center_idx_in_sub = torch.topk(score_sub, k=K, dim=-1)
    center_idx = sub_idx[center_idx_in_sub]      # (B, K)

    centers = z.gather(1, center_idx[..., None].expand(B, K, C))
    sim_token_center = z @ centers.transpose(1, 2)   # (B, M, K)
    assign_idx = sim_token_center.argmax(dim=-1)     # (B, M)
    return assign_idx


def aggregate_with_shared_assignment(x1, x2, assign_idx, K):
    """按共享 assignment 对两个时相分别做簇内均值（特征不混时相）。

    Args:
        x1, x2: (B, M, C) 两个时相的 background token。
        assign_idx: (B, M) 共享 cluster id。
        K: 聚类数。
    Returns:
        agg1, agg2: (B, K, C)
    """
    B, M, C = x1.shape
    one_hot = F.one_hot(assign_idx, num_classes=K).type_as(x1)   # (B, M, K)
    counts = one_hot.sum(dim=1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-6)    # (B, 1, K)
    agg1 = torch.einsum("bmc,bmk->bkc", x1, one_hot) / counts.transpose(1, 2)
    agg2 = torch.einsum("bmc,bmk->bkc", x2, one_hot) / counts.transpose(1, 2)
    return agg1, agg2


def norm_preserve(agg, ref):
    """把聚合原型范数拉回原 token 的最大范数（沿用 SAT 的 norm preservation）。

    聚合平均会压低原型范数，用原 token 最大范数对其重标定。
    """
    norms = torch.norm(ref, dim=-1)                                  # (B, M)
    max_norm = norms.max(dim=-1, keepdim=True)[0].unsqueeze(-1)      # (B, 1, 1)
    avg_norm = torch.norm(agg, dim=-1, keepdim=True)                 # (B, K, 1)
    eps = 1e-6
    scaled = (agg / (avg_norm + eps)) * max_norm
    mask = avg_norm > eps
    return torch.where(mask, scaled, agg)


class CASAAAttention(Attention):
    """变化感知非对称注意力：完整 Q、压缩 K/V；qkv/proj 与 DeiT 完全同名同形。

    继承 Attention 以保留 fused `qkv`（C -> 3C）与 `proj` 的参数名和形状，
    DeiT-Tiny 预训练 Q/K/V 权重可原位加载；单时相 `forward(x)` 仍是原
    self-attention（用于回归/对照）。

    前向只做切片投影：对 full X 只算 Q、对压缩上下文只算 K/V（权重是 fused qkv
    的行切片，数学与整段 qkv 后切片等价，不白算 full K/V 与 compressed Q）。

    Args:
        dim, num_heads, qkv_bias, proj_bias, attn_drop, proj_drop: 与 Attention 一致。
        keep_ratio: K/N，K/V 压缩 token 总数比例（Run1/2: 0.25）。
        change_share: K 中「疑似变化 token 直保留」占比（router='change'/'oracle' 时）。
        router: 'change'（cosine score）/ 'content'（A1 对照）/ 'oracle'（GT 诊断）。
        norm_preserve: 是否对聚合原型做范数保持。
    """

    def __init__(self, dim, num_heads=8, qkv_bias=False, proj_bias=True,
                 attn_drop=0.0, proj_drop=0.0,
                 keep_ratio=0.25, change_share=0.5, router='change',
                 norm_preserve=True):
        super().__init__(dim, num_heads=num_heads, qkv_bias=qkv_bias,
                         proj_bias=proj_bias, attn_drop=attn_drop, proj_drop=proj_drop)
        assert router in ('change', 'content', 'oracle', 'detail', 'detail_fused'), \
            f"unknown router {router}"
        self.dim = dim
        self.keep_ratio = keep_ratio
        self.change_share = change_share
        self.router = router
        self.norm_preserve = norm_preserve
        self._routing = None   # 上一次 forward_pair 的 routing 统计（供 smoke/诊断）

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 切片 qkv 投影（Run2 等价优化：只算需要的 Q / K / V）
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _proj_q(self, x):
        """只算 full X 的 Q：qkv.weight[0:C] / qkv.bias[0:C]。"""
        w = self.qkv.weight[: self.dim]
        b = self.qkv.bias[: self.dim] if self.qkv.bias is not None else None
        return F.linear(x, w, b)

    def _proj_kv(self, c):
        """只算压缩上下文的 K/V：qkv.weight[C:3C] / qkv.bias[C:3C]。

        Returns:
            kv: (B, K, 2*C)，[..., :C] 为 K，[..., C:] 为 V。
        """
        w = self.qkv.weight[self.dim:]
        b = self.qkv.bias[self.dim:] if self.qkv.bias is not None else None
        return F.linear(c, w, b)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 变化 score 接口
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _change_score(self, x1, x2, score_hint):
        """返回 (s, extras)。extras 为诊断用分量（仅 detail_fused 非空）。"""
        if self.router == 'oracle':
            # DIAGNOSTIC-ONLY：score 来自 GT patch occupancy（Encoder 传入）
            assert score_hint is not None, "oracle router requires the GT score_hint"
            return score_hint, {}
        if self.router == 'detail':
            # A4-D：score = rank 归一化的 detail 1/8 cosine（可部署）
            assert score_hint is not None, "detail router requires the detail score_hint"
            return score_hint, {}
        if self.router == 'detail_fused':
            # A4：0.5*rank(ViT cosine) + 0.5*rank(detail 1/8 cosine)
            # score_hint 已由 Encoder 计算为 rank-normalized detail score
            assert score_hint is not None, "detail_fused router requires the detail score_hint"
            s_v = change_score_cosine(x1, x2)
            r_v = rank_normalize_per_image(s_v)
            s = 0.5 * r_v + 0.5 * score_hint
            return s, {"s_v_raw": s_v, "s_v_rank": r_v, "s_d_rank": score_hint}
        return change_score_cosine(x1, x2), {}

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 路由与上下文构造
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def compute_contexts(self, x1, x2, score_hint=None):
        """计算两个时相的压缩上下文 (C1, C2) 与 routing 统计。

        routing（change score / TopK / assignment）在 no_grad 下计算；
        聚合与收集仍用带梯度的原 feature，梯度可回传。
        """
        B, N, C = x1.shape
        # 显式转 int：JIT 追踪下 shape 是符号量，Python 侧整数运算需要真 int
        B, N, C = int(B), int(N), int(C)
        K = max(int(round(self.keep_ratio * N)), 1)
        if self.router in ('change', 'oracle', 'detail', 'detail_fused'):
            Kc = min(int(round(self.change_share * K)), K)
        else:
            Kc = 0
        Kb = K - Kc

        with torch.no_grad():
            s, extras = self._change_score(x1, x2, score_hint)   # (B, N)
            if Kc > 0:
                Ic = torch.topk(s, Kc, dim=-1).indices   # (B, Kc) 疑似变化位置
                # int scatter（fvcore tracer 不支持 bool scalar 的 scatter_.value）
                mask = torch.ones(B, N, dtype=torch.long, device=x1.device)
                mask.scatter_(1, Ic, 0)
                mask = mask.bool()
            else:
                Ic = None
                mask = torch.ones(B, N, dtype=torch.bool, device=x1.device)

            assign = None
            if Kb > 0:
                # 共享背景描述：只在背景 token 上聚类（仅用于算 assignment）
                x1_bg_n = x1[mask]                       # (B*Nbg, C)
                x2_bg_n = x2[mask]
                z_bg = F.normalize((x1_bg_n + x2_bg_n) / 2.0, dim=-1).view(B, N - Kc, C)
                assign = deterministic_density_assign(z_bg, Kb)   # (B, Nbg)

        # 聚合/收集：使用带梯度的原 feature tensor
        if Ic is not None:
            c1_change = x1.gather(1, Ic[..., None].expand(B, Kc, C))
            c2_change = x2.gather(1, Ic[..., None].expand(B, Kc, C))
        if Kb > 0:
            x1_bg = x1[mask].view(B, N - Kc, C)
            x2_bg = x2[mask].view(B, N - Kc, C)
            agg1, agg2 = aggregate_with_shared_assignment(x1_bg, x2_bg, assign, Kb)
            if self.norm_preserve:
                agg1 = norm_preserve(agg1, x1)
                agg2 = norm_preserve(agg2, x2)
            if Ic is not None:
                C1 = torch.cat([c1_change, agg1], dim=1)   # (B, K, C)
                C2 = torch.cat([c2_change, agg2], dim=1)
            else:
                C1, C2 = agg1, agg2
        else:
            C1, C2 = c1_change, c2_change

        # 诊断统计（Router Audit 用；no_grad 下已是常量）
        cluster_size = None
        if assign is not None:
            cluster_size = F.one_hot(assign, num_classes=Kb).sum(dim=1).float()   # (B, Kb)
        c_norm_mean = torch.norm(C1[:, :Kc], dim=-1).mean().item() if Kc > 0 else float("nan")
        bg_norm_mean = torch.norm(C1[:, Kc:], dim=-1).mean().item() if Kb > 0 else float("nan")

        self._routing = {"N": N, "K": K, "Kc": Kc, "Kb": Kb,
                         "s": s, "Ic": Ic, "assign": assign,
                         "cluster_size": cluster_size,
                         "c_norm_mean": c_norm_mean, "bg_norm_mean": bg_norm_mean,
                         "attn_mass_change": None, "attn_mass_bg": None,
                         **{k: v for k, v in extras.items()}}
        return C1, C2

    def forward_pair(self, x1, x2, score_hint=None):
        """双时相 paired attention：Q 完整，K/V 来自压缩上下文，输出 N 不变。

        Args:
            x1, x2: (B, N, C)
            score_hint: (B, N) 外部变化 score（仅 router='oracle' 使用）。
        Returns:
            y1, y2: (B, N, C)
        """
        B, N, C = x1.shape
        C1, C2 = self.compute_contexts(x1, x2, score_hint=score_hint)
        K = C1.shape[1]
        H = self.num_heads
        hd = C // H
        Kc = self._routing["Kc"]

        # 切片投影：full X -> Q only；压缩上下文 -> K/V only
        q1 = self._proj_q(x1).reshape(B, N, H, hd).permute(0, 2, 1, 3) * self.scale
        q2 = self._proj_q(x2).reshape(B, N, H, hd).permute(0, 2, 1, 3) * self.scale
        kv1 = self._proj_kv(C1).reshape(B, K, 2, H, hd).permute(2, 0, 3, 1, 4)
        kv2 = self._proj_kv(C2).reshape(B, K, 2, H, hd).permute(2, 0, 3, 1, 4)
        k1, v1 = kv1[0], kv1[1]
        k2, v2 = kv2[0], kv2[1]

        a1 = (q1 @ k1.transpose(-2, -1))
        a1_s = a1.softmax(dim=-1)
        a1 = self.attn_drop(a1_s)
        y1 = (a1 @ v1).transpose(1, 2).reshape(B, N, C)
        a2 = (q2 @ k2.transpose(-2, -1))
        a2_s = a2.softmax(dim=-1)
        a2 = self.attn_drop(a2_s)
        y2 = (a2 @ v2).transpose(1, 2).reshape(B, N, C)

        y1 = self.proj_drop(self.proj(y1))
        y2 = self.proj_drop(self.proj(y2))

        # 注意力质量统计：query 对 change bank / bg bank 的平均 attention mass
        m_change = (a1_s[..., :Kc].mean() + a2_s[..., :Kc].mean()) / 2.0 if Kc > 0 else None
        m_bg = (a1_s[..., Kc:].mean() + a2_s[..., Kc:].mean()) / 2.0 if K > Kc else None
        self._routing["attn_mass_change"] = float(m_change.item()) if m_change is not None else float("nan")
        self._routing["attn_mass_bg"] = float(m_bg.item()) if m_bg is not None else float("nan")
        return y1, y2


if __name__ == "__main__":
    # CPU 单 block 机制冒烟（无需 GPU / 预训练权重）：
    #   cd <repo_root> && python -m models.model.layers.casaa
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    B, N, C, H = 2, 256, 192, 6
    x1 = torch.randn(B, N, C, requires_grad=True)
    x2 = torch.randn(B, N, C, requires_grad=True)

    print("== change score ==")
    s12 = change_score_cosine(x1, x2)
    s21 = change_score_cosine(x2, x1)
    assert torch.allclose(s12, s21), "change score must be T1/T2 symmetric"
    print(f"  symmetric OK, shape {tuple(s12.shape)}")

    print("== qkv sliced projection equivalence ==")
    attn = CASAAAttention(dim=C, num_heads=H, qkv_bias=True)
    with torch.no_grad():
        # 参考实现：整段 qkv 后切片（旧逻辑）
        def ref_forward_pair(a, xa, xb):
            Ca, Cb = a.compute_contexts(xa, xb)
            Kref = Ca.shape[1]
            qkv1 = a.qkv(xa).reshape(B, N, 3, H, C // H).permute(2, 0, 3, 1, 4)
            qkv2 = a.qkv(xb).reshape(B, N, 3, H, C // H).permute(2, 0, 3, 1, 4)
            qkv1c = a.qkv(Ca).reshape(B, Kref, 3, H, C // H).permute(2, 0, 3, 1, 4)
            qkv2c = a.qkv(Cb).reshape(B, Kref, 3, H, C // H).permute(2, 0, 3, 1, 4)
            q1, k1, v1 = qkv1[0] * a.scale, qkv1[1], qkv1[2]
            q2, k2, v2 = qkv2[0] * a.scale, qkv2[1], qkv2[2]
            _, k1c, v1c = qkv1c
            _, k2c, v2c = qkv2c
            y1 = ((q1 @ k1c.transpose(-2, -1)).softmax(-1) @ v1c).transpose(1, 2).reshape(B, N, C)
            y2 = ((q2 @ k2c.transpose(-2, -1)).softmax(-1) @ v2c).transpose(1, 2).reshape(B, N, C)
            return a.proj_drop(a.proj(y1)), a.proj_drop(a.proj(y2))

        for r in ("change", "content"):
            aa = CASAAAttention(dim=C, num_heads=H, qkv_bias=True, router=r)
            aa.load_state_dict(attn.state_dict())
            y1, y2 = aa.forward_pair(x1, x2)
            yr1, yr2 = ref_forward_pair(aa, x1, x2)
            d = max((y1 - yr1).abs().max().item(), (y2 - yr2).abs().max().item())
            assert d < 1e-6, f"router={r} sliced vs full qkv mismatch: {d}"
            print(f"  router={r}: sliced == full-qkv, max_abs_err = {d:.2e}")

    print("== CASAA (router=change) ==")
    y1, y2 = attn.forward_pair(x1, x2)
    assert y1.shape == y2.shape == (B, N, C), (y1.shape, y2.shape)
    r = attn._routing
    print(f"  N={r['N']} K={r['K']} Kc={r['Kc']} Kb={r['Kb']}  output {tuple(y1.shape)}")
    assert (r["N"], r["K"], r["Kc"], r["Kb"]) == (256, 64, 32, 32)
    assert r["s"].shape == (B, N) and r["cluster_size"].shape == (B, 32)
    assert r["attn_mass_change"] is not None and r["attn_mass_bg"] is not None
    assert torch.isfinite(y1).all() and torch.isfinite(y2).all()
    # 确定性：同一输入两次 routing 一致
    _ = attn.forward_pair(x1, x2)
    r2 = attn._routing
    assert torch.equal(r["Ic"], r2["Ic"]) and torch.equal(r["assign"], r2["assign"])
    # T1/T2 交换对称：routing 一致
    _ = attn.forward_pair(x2, x1)
    r3 = attn._routing
    assert torch.equal(r["Ic"], r3["Ic"]) and torch.equal(r["assign"], r3["assign"])
    print("  deterministic + swap-symmetric routing OK")
    # 梯度回传（qkv / proj / 上游 token）
    loss = (y1 * torch.randn_like(y1)).sum() + (y2 * torch.randn_like(y2)).sum()
    loss.backward()
    assert attn.qkv.weight.grad is not None and attn.qkv.weight.grad.norm() > 0
    assert attn.proj.weight.grad is not None and attn.proj.weight.grad.norm() > 0
    assert torch.isfinite(attn.qkv.weight.grad).all() and torch.isfinite(attn.proj.weight.grad).all()
    assert x1.grad is not None and x1.grad.norm() > 0 and torch.isfinite(x1.grad).all()
    print("  gradients OK (qkv/proj/upstream tokens finite & nonzero)")

    print("== SAA-style control (router=content) ==")
    attn_c = CASAAAttention(dim=C, num_heads=H, qkv_bias=True, router='content')
    with torch.no_grad():
        _, _ = attn_c.forward_pair(x1, x2)
    rc = attn_c._routing
    print(f"  N={rc['N']} K={rc['K']} Kc={rc['Kc']} Kb={rc['Kb']}")
    assert (rc["K"], rc["Kc"], rc["Kb"]) == (64, 0, 64)
    assert rc["Ic"] is None

    print("== Oracle router (DIAGNOSTIC-ONLY) ==")
    attn_o = CASAAAttention(dim=C, num_heads=H, qkv_bias=True, router='oracle')
    hint = torch.rand(B, N)
    with torch.no_grad():
        _, _ = attn_o.forward_pair(x1, x2, score_hint=hint)
        ro1 = attn_o._routing
        _, _ = attn_o.forward_pair(x2, x1, score_hint=hint)
        ro2 = attn_o._routing
    assert (ro1["K"], ro1["Kc"], ro1["Kb"]) == (64, 32, 32)
    assert torch.equal(ro1["s"], hint), "oracle score must equal the GT hint"
    assert torch.equal(ro1["Ic"], torch.topk(hint, 32, dim=-1).indices)
    assert torch.equal(ro1["Ic"], ro2["Ic"]) and torch.equal(ro1["assign"], ro2["assign"])
    try:
        attn_o.forward_pair(x1, x2)   # 无 hint 必须报错
        raise AssertionError("oracle without hint should raise")
    except AssertionError:
        pass
    print("  oracle score == hint, TopK correct, swap-invariant, requires hint")

    print("== Detail-fused router (A4) ==")
    attn_d = CASAAAttention(dim=C, num_heads=H, qkv_bias=True, router='detail_fused')
    hint = rank_normalize_per_image(torch.rand(B, N))
    with torch.no_grad():
        _, _ = attn_d.forward_pair(x1, x2, score_hint=hint)
    rd1 = attn_d._routing
    sv_ref = change_score_cosine(x1.detach(), x2.detach())
    rv_ref = rank_normalize_per_image(sv_ref)
    assert torch.allclose(rd1["s"], 0.5 * rv_ref + 0.5 * hint), "fused score wrong"
    assert torch.equal(rd1["s_v_raw"], sv_ref) and torch.equal(rd1["s_d_rank"], hint)
    assert (rd1["K"], rd1["Kc"], rd1["Kb"]) == (64, 32, 32)
    s_rand = torch.rand(4, 256)
    r_rand = rank_normalize_per_image(s_rand)
    assert r_rand.shape == (4, 256)
    assert r_rand.min().item() == 0.0 and r_rand.max().item() == 1.0
    assert torch.equal(r_rand, rank_normalize_per_image(s_rand)), "rank must be deterministic"
    # 单调性：升序位置的 rank 也升序
    assert (torch.diff(r_rand[0][torch.argsort(s_rand[0])]) >= 0).all()
    try:
        attn_d.forward_pair(x1, x2)   # 无 hint 必须报错
        raise AssertionError("detail_fused without hint should raise")
    except AssertionError:
        pass
    print("  fused=0.5R(sv)+0.5R(sd) OK; rank norm [0,1] deterministic & monotonic")

    print("== single-phase forward (pretrained-path regression) ==")
    y = attn(x1)
    assert y.shape == (B, N, C)
    print("  OK")
    print("CASAA mechanics smoke ALL OK")

# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/model\layers\dino_head.py

# Copyright (c) Meta Platforms, Inc. and affiliates.
#
# This source code is licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0
# found in the LICENSE file in the root directory of this source tree.

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from torch.nn.init import trunc_normal_
from torch.nn.utils import weight_norm


class DINOHead(nn.Module):
    def __init__(
        self,
        in_dim,
        out_dim,
        use_bn=False,
        nlayers=3,
        hidden_dim=2048,
        bottleneck_dim=256,
        mlp_bias=True,
    ):
        super().__init__()
        nlayers = max(nlayers, 1)
        self.mlp = _build_mlp(nlayers, in_dim, bottleneck_dim, hidden_dim=hidden_dim, use_bn=use_bn, bias=mlp_bias)
        self.apply(self._init_weights)
        self.last_layer = weight_norm(nn.Linear(bottleneck_dim, out_dim, bias=False))
        self.last_layer.weight_g.data.fill_(1)

    def _init_weights(self, m):
        if isinstance(m, nn.Linear):
            trunc_normal_(m.weight, std=0.02)
            if isinstance(m, nn.Linear) and m.bias is not None:
                nn.init.constant_(m.bias, 0)

    def forward(self, x):
        x = self.mlp(x)
        eps = 1e-6 if x.dtype == torch.float16 else 1e-12
        x = nn.functional.normalize(x, dim=-1, p=2, eps=eps)
        x = self.last_layer(x)
        return x


def _build_mlp(nlayers, in_dim, bottleneck_dim, hidden_dim=None, use_bn=False, bias=True):
    if nlayers == 1:
        return nn.Linear(in_dim, bottleneck_dim, bias=bias)
    else:
        layers = [nn.Linear(in_dim, hidden_dim, bias=bias)]
        if use_bn:
            layers.append(nn.BatchNorm1d(hidden_dim))
        layers.append(nn.GELU())
        for _ in range(nlayers - 2):
            layers.append(nn.Linear(hidden_dim, hidden_dim, bias=bias))
            if use_bn:
                layers.append(nn.BatchNorm1d(hidden_dim))
            layers.append(nn.GELU())
        layers.append(nn.Linear(hidden_dim, bottleneck_dim, bias=bias))
        return nn.Sequential(*layers)

================================================================================
# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/model\layers\drop_path.py
================================================================================
# Copyright (c) Meta Platforms, Inc. and affiliates.
#
# This source code is licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0
# found in the LICENSE file in the root directory of this source tree.

# References:
#   https://github.com/facebookresearch/dino/blob/master/vision_transformer.py
#   https://github.com/rwightman/pytorch-image-models/tree/master/timm/layers/drop.py


from torch import nn


def drop_path(x, drop_prob: float = 0.0, training: bool = False):
    if drop_prob == 0.0 or not training:
        return x
    keep_prob = 1 - drop_prob
    shape = (x.shape[0],) + (1,) * (x.ndim - 1)  # work with diff dim tensors, not just 2D ConvNets
    random_tensor = x.new_empty(shape).bernoulli_(keep_prob)
    if keep_prob > 0.0:
        random_tensor.div_(keep_prob)
    output = x * random_tensor
    return output


class DropPath(nn.Module):
    """Drop paths (Stochastic Depth) per sample (when applied in main path of residual blocks)."""

    def __init__(self, drop_prob=None):
        super(DropPath, self).__init__()
        self.drop_prob = drop_prob

    def forward(self, x):
        return drop_path(x, self.drop_prob, self.training)

# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/model\layers\euclidean_context.py

"""Euclidean retrieval with selective prototypes and a dense self reference."""
import math

from model.layers.pyramid_context import ResidualPyramidContext, project_tokens, split_heads


class EuclideanContext(ResidualPyramidContext):
    geometry = "euclidean"

    def project_queries(self, feature):
        return split_heads(self.qk(feature), self.heads) / math.sqrt(feature.shape[1] // self.heads)

    def pool_keys(self, query, prototype, weights, grid):
        # LN precedes compression; the shared linear map commutes with weighted pooling.
        return project_tokens(self.qk, prototype, self.heads) / math.sqrt(prototype.shape[-1] // self.heads)

    @staticmethod
    def squared_distance(query, key):
        return (query.square().sum(-1, keepdim=True) + key.square().sum(-1).unsqueeze(-2)
                - 2 * (query @ key.transpose(-1, -2))).clamp_min(0)

    @staticmethod
    def geometry_diagnostics(query, key):
        return dict(query_norm_mean=float(query.norm(dim=-1).mean()),
                    query_norm_max=float(query.norm(dim=-1).max()))

================================================================================
# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/model\layers\hyperbolic.py
================================================================================
"""Run8：双曲流形空间的变化证据算子（Lorentz 模型，参数自由）。

## 为什么用 Lorentz 而不是 Poincaré 球

Lorentz（双叶双曲面）模型的距离只需要「半径 + 方向点积」，**不必显式构造 C+1 通道的
双曲张量**，对 C=96…768 的稠密特征图更省算力，数值也更稳（没有球边界 `atanh` 爆炸）。
两份调研（2026-10-13 §2.2 / 2026-10-14 §5.1）都给出了这两个模型并注明该优点。

## ⚠️ 必须避开的退化定理（调研 2026-10-13 §2.3 / 2026-10-14 §5.2）

若把单位化特征以**同一个固定半径** `r` 映射到双曲面，则

    cosh d_H = cosh²r − sinh²r·cosθ = 1 + sinh²r·(1 − cosθ)

`acosh` 严格递增 ⇒ `d_H` 与 `1−cosθ` **逐像素同序** ⇒ 走逐图 rank 之后权重逐位相同。
**「把 1−cos 换成双曲距离」因此不构成任何新增信息。**

**本模块的非退化机制（关键）**：半径**不是固定的**，而是由**同一时相的特征范数**决定，
并且两个分支用**两种不同的范数**：

| 分支 | 半径来源 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|
| `detail` | **局部离散度** `q = 1 − cos(x_i, mean_{3×3}(x_i))` → `r = r0 + a·q` | 局部/纹理/边缘结构强度 |
| `global` | **全局半径**：`x` 逐图归一化后相对全图均值的偏离 → `r = r0 + a·q_global` | 相对全图背景的整体语义角色 |

于是对**相同夹角 θ** 的两个位置，只要它们的范数不同，`cosh d_H` 就不同 ⇒ 排序改变。
这正是调研要求的「非退化性」：**在固定方向相似度下改变重要性排序**。

## 数学定义（实现逐项对应）

设时相 `t∈{A,B}`、位置 `i`，特征 `x_{t,i}∈R^C`：

1. `u_{t,i} = x_{t,i} / max(‖x_{t,i}‖, ε)`（单位方向）
2. `q^detail_{t,i} = clip( (1 − u_{t,i}·m_{t,i}) / 2, 0, 1 )`，
   其中 `m_{t,i} = Normalize( mean_{3×3}(u_{t,·}) )`（局部中心方向）
3. `q^global_{t,i} = clip( ‖x_{t,i} − mean_{HW}(x_t)‖ / (‖x_{t,i}‖ + ε), 0, 1 )`
   （对该时相自己的全局均值；**只用自己的时相，不含跨时相信息**）
4. `r^b_{t,i} = r0 + a · q^b_{t,i}`，`b ∈ {detail, global}`；`r ∈ [r0, r0+a]`
5. Lorentz 测地距离（`κ=1`，点 `h=(cosh r, sinh r·u)`）：
   `cosh d = cosh r_A cosh r_B − sinh r_A sinh r_B (u_A·u_B)`，`d = acosh(max(1, cosh d))`
6. 有界变化强度 `s = 1 − exp(−d) ∈ [0, 1)`

`r0`/`a` **在预注册里冻结，禁止在 test 上搜索**（调研 §6.2 的纪律）。

## 数值纪律

- 全部在 **FP32** 下算，且只读（调用方 `torch.no_grad()`）；
- `acosh` 自变量先 `clamp_min(1.0)`；`cosh/sinh` 的半径为有界小量（默认 ≤2.0），不会溢出；
- `ε=1e-8`；全零输入时 `q=0` ⇒ `r=r0` ⇒ 退化为固定半径（此时按上面的定理与余弦同序，
  这是**正确的退化行为**，不是 bug）。
"""
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F

__all__ = [
    "R0_DEFAULT", "A_DEFAULT",
    "unit_direction", "local_center_direction", "detail_scatter",
    "global_radius_scatter", "branch_radii", "lorentz_change_score",
    "euclidean_change_score", "cosine_change_score", "fixed_radius_change_score",
    "rank_normalize_2d", "branch_rank_disagreement",
]

R0_DEFAULT = 0.5      # 半径下界（远离双曲数值边界）
A_DEFAULT = 0.5       # 半径跨度 ⇒ r ∈ [0.5, 1.0]


def unit_direction(x, eps=1e-8):
    """`u = x / max(‖x‖, ε)`；(B,C,H,W) → (B,C,H,W)，FP32。"""
    x = x.float()
    n = x.pow(2).sum(dim=1, keepdim=True).clamp_min(eps).sqrt()
    return x / n


def local_center_direction(u, ksize=3):
    """`m = Normalize(mean_{ksize×ksize}(u))`；(B,C,H,W) → (B,C,H,W)。"""
    pad = ksize // 2
    m = F.avg_pool2d(u, kernel_size=ksize, stride=1, padding=pad,
                     count_include_pad=False)
    n = m.pow(2).sum(dim=1, keepdim=True).clamp_min(1e-8).sqrt()
    return m / n


def detail_scatter(u, ksize=3):
    """局部离散度 `q = clip((1 − u·m)/2, 0, 1)`；(B,C,H,W) → (B,1,H,W)。"""
    m = local_center_direction(u, ksize=ksize)
    cos = (u * m).sum(dim=1, keepdim=True).clamp(-1.0, 1.0)
    return ((1.0 - cos) * 0.5).clamp(0.0, 1.0)


def global_radius_scatter(x, eps=1e-8):
    """全局语义半径代理 `q = clip(‖x − mean_HW(x)‖ / (‖x‖ + ε), 0, 1)`。

    只用**该时相自己**的全局均值 ⇒ 单时相统计量，A/B 严格对称，不含跨时相泄漏。
    """
    x = x.float()
    mu = x.mean(dim=(2, 3), keepdim=True)
    dev = (x - mu).pow(2).sum(dim=1, keepdim=True).clamp_min(0.0).sqrt()
    nrm = x.pow(2).sum(dim=1, keepdim=True).clamp_min(eps).sqrt()
    return (dev / (nrm + eps)).clamp(0.0, 1.0)


def branch_radii(xa, xb, branch="detail", r0=R0_DEFAULT, a=A_DEFAULT, ksize=3):
    """返回 `(r_A, r_B, u_A, u_B)`，全部 (B,C,H,W) / 半径 (B,1,H,W)。"""
    assert branch in ("detail", "global"), branch
    ua = unit_direction(xa)
    ub = unit_direction(xb)
    if branch == "detail":
        qa = detail_scatter(ua, ksize=ksize)
        qb = detail_scatter(ub, ksize=ksize)
    else:
        qa = global_radius_scatter(xa)
        qb = global_radius_scatter(xb)
    ra = (r0 + a * qa)
    rb = (r0 + a * qb)
    return ra, rb, ua, ub


def lorentz_change_score(xa, xb, branch="detail", r0=R0_DEFAULT, a=A_DEFAULT, ksize=3):
    """Lorentz 测地距离 → 有界变化强度 `s = 1 − exp(−d)`；(B,C,H,W) → (B,1,H,W) ∈ [0,1)。"""
    ra, rb, ua, ub = branch_radii(xa, xb, branch=branch, r0=r0, a=a, ksize=ksize)
    cos_ab = (ua * ub).sum(dim=1, keepdim=True).clamp(-1.0, 1.0)
    z = (torch.cosh(ra) * torch.cosh(rb)
         - torch.sinh(ra) * torch.sinh(rb) * cos_ab)
    d = torch.acosh(z.clamp_min(1.0))
    return (1.0 - torch.exp(-d)).clamp(0.0, 1.0)


def euclidean_change_score(xa, xb, branch="detail", r0=R0_DEFAULT, a=A_DEFAULT, ksize=3):
    """**同式欧氏对照**：用完全相同的 `r_A, r_B, u_A, u_B`，只把测地距离换成切向欧氏距离。

    调研（2026-10-13 §6.3 / 2026-10-14 §11.2 G4）要求必须有这个对照，否则无法区分
    「局部径向信息有用」与「负曲率有用」。
    """
    ra, rb, ua, ub = branch_radii(xa, xb, branch=branch, r0=r0, a=a, ksize=ksize)
    cos_ab = (ua * ub).sum(dim=1, keepdim=True).clamp(-1.0, 1.0)
    d2 = (ra * ra + rb * rb - 2.0 * ra * rb * cos_ab).clamp_min(0.0)
    d = d2.sqrt()
    return (1.0 - torch.exp(-d)).clamp(0.0, 1.0)


def cosine_change_score(xa, xb):
    """现状口径对照：`1 − cos(x_A, x_B)`。

    ⚠️ 与生产实现 `caacp_ss2d.change_score_cosine_2d` **逐位同式**：只 `clamp(min=0.0)`，
    **不做上界夹紧**。这一点是 §2.3 退化定理能成立的前提：

    - `1−cos` 的天然上界是 2（当 `cos` 可为 −1 时），若写成 `.clamp(0,1)`，任何
      `1−cos > 1`（即夹角大于 90°）的位置会被**压平到同一个值 1** ⇒ 产生大量并列
      ⇒ **rank 不再一一对应**，从而**伪造出"排序分歧"**。本文件首版正是踩了这个坑
      （C2 退化检验 FAIL，`frac_rank_diff=0.482`）；改成只夹下界后 rank 分歧 = 0。
    - 同理，双曲路径内部必须先 `clamp(-1,1)` 才能喂 `acosh`；`1−cos` 路径内部
      `cos` 天然在 [−1,1]（unit 向量点积的浮点越界 ≤1e-7，不影响 rank）。
    """
    ua = unit_direction(xa)
    ub = unit_direction(xb)
    cos_ab = (ua * ub).sum(dim=1, keepdim=True)
    return (1.0 - cos_ab).clamp(min=0.0)


def fixed_radius_change_score(xa, xb, r=R0_DEFAULT + 0.5):
    """**退化负对照**：固定半径的双曲距离。按 §2.3 定理，它应与 `cosine` 逐像素同序。

    这个函数存在的唯一目的就是**在真实数据上复现退化定理**（smoke T3）。
    """
    ua = unit_direction(xa)
    ub = unit_direction(xb)
    cos_ab = (ua * ub).sum(dim=1, keepdim=True).clamp(-1.0, 1.0)
    z = (torch.cosh(torch.as_tensor(r, dtype=torch.float32, device=ua.device)) ** 2
         - torch.sinh(torch.as_tensor(r, dtype=torch.float32, device=ua.device)) ** 2 * cos_ab)
    d = torch.acosh(z.clamp_min(1.0))
    return (1.0 - torch.exp(-d)).clamp(0.0, 1.0)


def rank_normalize_2d(s):
    """逐图 rank 归一化到 [0,1]（与 `caacp_ss2d.rank_normalize_2d` **逐位同口径**）。

    注意：这是**退化的来源**——`rank(·)` 只保留序、丢弃一切幅度信息，因此任何保序变换
    在此之后都不可能改变权重。Run8 的 SAT-QKV 因此**不用 rank**，改为在 Q/K/V 里
    直接消费数值分数（见 `layers/caacp_hqkv.py`）。本函数只保留给对照/诊断。
    """
    B, C, H, W = s.shape
    flat = s.flatten(2)                                   # (B,1,N)
    ranks = flat.argsort(dim=2).argsort(dim=2).float()
    return (ranks / max(flat.shape[2] - 1, 1)).view(B, C, H, W)


@torch.no_grad()
def branch_rank_disagreement(s_a, s_b):
    """两个分数图的排序分歧（Kendall τ 的 1−τ 近似 + 逐位置 rank 不同比例）。

    返回 `dict(frac_rank_diff=..., kendall_tau=...)`。用于 smoke 的**非退化性**判据：
    `frac_rank_diff` 必须显著 > 0，否则新几何只是余弦换皮。
    """
    a = s_a.flatten(1)
    b = s_b.flatten(1)
    ra = a.argsort(dim=1).argsort(dim=1).float()
    rb = b.argsort(dim=1).argsort(dim=1).float()
    frac = (ra != rb).float().mean().item()
    # Kendall τ（用中心化后的 Pearson 相关在秩上计算，等价且向量化）
    ra = ra - ra.mean(dim=1, keepdim=True)
    rb = rb - rb.mean(dim=1, keepdim=True)
    num = (ra * rb).sum(dim=1)
    den = ra.pow(2).sum(dim=1).sqrt() * rb.pow(2).sum(dim=1).sqrt()
    tau = (num / den.clamp_min(1e-12)).mean().item()
    return {"frac_rank_diff": frac, "kendall_tau": tau}

# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/model\layers\hyperbolic_context.py
================================================================================
"""Task-calibrated Lorentz retrieval with aligned content and learned correction."""
import math

import torch
from torch import nn
from torch.nn import functional as F

from model.layers.lorentz_ops import lift, pairwise_squared_distance
from model.layers.pyramid_context import (
    attention_diagnostics, channel_norm, feature_regions, highpass, merge_heads,
    pool_features, project_tokens, regions, rms, source_bias, split_heads,
)


def subtype_assignments(probability, appearance, grid):
    """Two appearance representatives per class; posterior denotes area occupancy."""
    positive = feature_regions(probability, grid).squeeze(-1)
    subtype = feature_regions(appearance, grid).squeeze(-1)
    classes = torch.stack((positive, 1 - positive), dim=2)
    modes = torch.stack((subtype, 1 - subtype), dim=2)
    weights = (classes.unsqueeze(3) + 1e-6).square() * (modes.unsqueeze(2) + 1e-6)
    weights = weights / weights.sum(-1, keepdim=True)
    support = (weights * classes.unsqueeze(3)).sum(-1)
    return weights.flatten(2, 3), support.flatten(1).clamp_min(1e-6)


class HyperbolicContext(nn.Module):
    """Pooled K/V content, supervised occupancy radius, independently gated readout."""
    geometry = "hyperbolic"
    value_geometry = "euclidean"
    compression_power = 2
    support_power = 1
    self_reference = False
    radius_mode = "asinh(1 + supervised_change_occupancy)"
    key_mode = "project_same_pooled_content_as_values_then_lift"
    readout_mode = "learned_gate_times_conditional_context_minus_query_value"
    squared_distance = staticmethod(pairwise_squared_distance)

    def __init__(self, channels=64, heads=1, head_dim=32, grid_sizes=(8, 4, 4, 4)):
        super().__init__()
        self.heads = heads
        self.head_dim = head_dim
        self.grid_sizes = tuple(grid_sizes)
        self.tokens_per_scale = tuple(4 * grid ** 2 for grid in grid_sizes)
        width = heads * head_dim
        self.norm = nn.LayerNorm(channels)
        self.guide = nn.Conv2d(channels, 1, 1)
        self.qk = nn.Conv2d(channels, width, 1, bias=False)
        self.v = nn.Conv2d(channels, width, 1, bias=False)
        self.out = nn.Conv2d(width, channels, 1, bias=False)
        self.appearance = nn.Conv2d(channels, 1, 1)
        self.gate = nn.Conv2d(2 * width + 2, heads, 1)
        nn.init.zeros_(self.gate.bias)
        self.local_dw = nn.Conv2d(channels, channels, 3, padding=1,
                                  padding_mode="replicate", groups=channels, bias=False)
        self.local_out = nn.Conv2d(channels, channels, 1, bias=False)
        self.scale_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(heads, 4, 4))
        self.raw_temperature = nn.Parameter(torch.full((heads,), math.log(math.expm1(.5))))
        self.gamma_context = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(4, channels))
        self.gamma_detail = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(2, channels))
        self.collect_diag = False
        self.last_diag = {}

    @staticmethod
    def geometric_point(projected, occupancy):
        # A unit spatial baseline preserves directions for background prototypes.
        # Zero projected content has no direction and maps to the Lorentz origin.
        direction = F.normalize(projected, dim=-1, eps=1e-6)
        return lift((1 + occupancy) * direction)

    def project_queries(self, feature, occupancy):
        direction = split_heads(self.qk(feature), self.heads)
        occupancy = occupancy.permute(0, 2, 3, 1).unsqueeze(1)
        return self.geometric_point(direction, occupancy)

    def project_keys(self, prototype):
        direction = project_tokens(self.qk, prototype, self.heads)
        occupancy = F.linear(prototype, self.guide.weight[:, :, 0, 0], self.guide.bias).sigmoid()
        return self.geometric_point(direction, occupancy[:, None]), occupancy

    def attention(self, query, key, support, query_scale):
        temperature = F.softplus(self.raw_temperature).view(1, self.heads, 1, 1)
        logits = -self.squared_distance(query, key) / (2 * temperature.square())
        logits = logits + support.log()[:, None, None] + source_bias(
            self.scale_bias, query_scale, self.tokens_per_scale)
        return logits.softmax(-1)

    def readout(self, feature, probability, values, occupancy, prototype_occupancy):
        height, width = feature.shape[-2:]
        retrieved = merge_heads(probability @ values, height, width)
        own_value = self.v(feature)
        discrepancy = retrieved - own_value
        retrieved_occupancy = (probability @ prototype_occupancy[:, None]).mean(1)
        retrieved_occupancy = retrieved_occupancy.transpose(1, 2).reshape(-1, 1, height, width)
        gate = self.gate(torch.cat((own_value, discrepancy, occupancy, retrieved_occupancy), 1)).sigmoid()
        gate_map = gate[:, :, None].expand(-1, -1, self.head_dim, -1, -1).flatten(1, 2)
        gated = gate_map * discrepancy
        return self.out(gated), gate, retrieved, own_value, discrepancy, gated

    def forward(self, tar):
        normalized = [channel_norm(self.norm, x) for x in tar]
        guide = [self.guide(x) for x in normalized]
        occupancy = [g.sigmoid() for g in guide]
        compression = [subtype_assignments(p, self.appearance(x).sigmoid(), grid)
                       for x, p, grid in zip(normalized, occupancy, self.grid_sizes)]
        prototypes = [pool_features(x, weights, grid)
                      for x, (weights, _), grid in zip(normalized, compression, self.grid_sizes)]
        prototype = torch.cat(prototypes, dim=1)
        key, prototype_occupancy = self.project_keys(prototype)
        values = project_tokens(self.v, prototype, self.heads)
        support = torch.cat([item[1] for item in compression], dim=1)
        enhanced, diagnostics = [], {}
        for index, (original, x, p) in enumerate(zip(tar, normalized, occupancy)):
            query = self.project_queries(x, p)
            probability = self.attention(query.flatten(2, 3), key, support, index)
            update, gate, retrieved, own_value, discrepancy, gated = self.readout(
                x, probability, values, p, prototype_occupancy)
            context_residual = self.gamma_context[index][None, :, None, None] * update
            local_residual = torch.zeros_like(original)
            local = None
            if index < 2:
                local = self.local_out(self.local_dw(highpass(x)))
                local_residual = self.gamma_detail[index][None, :, None, None] * local
            result = original + context_residual + local_residual
            enhanced.append(result)
            if self.collect_diag:
                with torch.no_grad():
                    base = rms(original).clamp_min(1e-8)
                    radius = torch.asinh(query[..., 1:].norm(dim=-1))
                    key_radius = torch.asinh(key[..., 1:].norm(dim=-1))
                    q_flat = query.flatten(2, 3)
                    direction_q = F.normalize(q_flat[..., 1:], dim=-1, eps=1e-6)
                    direction_k = F.normalize(key[..., 1:], dim=-1, eps=1e-6)
                    angular = (1 - direction_q @ direction_k.transpose(-1, -2)).clamp_min(0)
                    radial = (radius.flatten(2)[..., None] - key_radius[:, :, None]).abs()
                    weights, class_support = compression[index]
                    local_direction = regions(direction_q.reshape(*query.shape[:-1], -1), self.grid_sizes[index])
                    mean_direction = torch.einsum("brtp,bhrpd->bhrtd", weights, local_direction)
                    variance = (1 - mean_direction.square().sum(-1)).clamp_min(0)
                    pairs = prototypes[index].reshape(x.shape[0], -1, 2, 2, x.shape[1])
                    separation = (pairs[..., 0, :] - pairs[..., 1, :]).square().mean(-1).sqrt()
                    diag = dict(
                        queries=x.shape[-2] * x.shape[-1], contexts=key.shape[2],
                        gamma_context=float(self.gamma_context[index].mean()),
                        gamma_context_rms=float(rms(self.gamma_context[index])),
                        gamma_detail=float(self.gamma_detail[index].mean()) if index < 2 else 0.,
                        gamma_detail_rms=float(rms(self.gamma_detail[index])) if index < 2 else 0.,
                        feature_rms=float(base), context_rms=float(rms(update)), branch_rms=float(rms(update)),
                        local_rms=float(rms(local)) if local is not None else 0.,
                        relative_update_rms=float(rms(discrepancy)),
                        gated_difference_rms=float(rms(gated)),
                        ungated_projection_rms=float(rms(F.conv2d(discrepancy, self.out.weight))),
                        residual_rms=float(rms(context_residual + local_residual)),
                        residual_to_feature_rms=float(rms(context_residual + local_residual) / base),
                        context_residual_to_feature_rms=float(rms(context_residual) / base),
                        local_residual_to_feature_rms=float(rms(local_residual) / base),
                        guide_mean=float(p.mean()), class_support_mean=float(class_support.mean()),
                        class_support_min=float(class_support.min()),
                        retrieved_rms=float(rms(retrieved)), self_value_rms=float(rms(own_value)),
                        retrieved_spatial_rms=float(rms(retrieved - retrieved.mean((-2, -1), keepdim=True))),
                        retrieved_self_cosine=float(F.cosine_similarity(retrieved.flatten(1), own_value.flatten(1)).mean()),
                        qk_weight_rms=float(rms(self.qk.weight)), v_weight_rms=float(rms(self.v.weight)),
                        out_weight_rms=float(rms(self.out.weight)),
                        temperature=F.softplus(self.raw_temperature).tolist(),
                        radius_mean=float(radius.mean()), radius_max=float(radius.max()),
                        radius_cv=float(radius.std(unbiased=False) / radius.mean().clamp_min(1e-8)),
                        key_radius_mean=float(key_radius.mean()),
                        radial_gap_attention_mean=float((probability * radial).sum(-1).mean()),
                        angular_gap_attention_mean=float((probability * angular).sum(-1).mean()),
                        prototype_direction_variance_mean=float(variance.mean()),
                        subtype_separation_rms=float(separation.mean()),
                        subtype_duplicate_fraction=float((separation < 1e-3).float().mean()),
                        finite=bool(torch.isfinite(result).all()))
                    diag.update(attention_diagnostics(probability, self.tokens_per_scale))
                    diag.pop("self_mass_mean")
                    diag.update(context_mass_mean=float(gate.mean()), context_mass_min=float(gate.min()),
                                context_mass_max=float(gate.max()), context_mass_std=float(gate.std(unbiased=False)),
                                adoption_source="learned_value_correction_gate")
                    diagnostics[f"stage{index+1}"] = diag
        if self.collect_diag:
            self.last_diag = diagnostics
        return enhanced, dict(guide_logits=tuple(guide))

# Modified from: https://github.com/huggingface/pytorch-image-models/blob/main/timm/models/vision_transformer.py#L103-L110

from typing import Union

import torch
from torch import Tensor
from torch import nn


class LayerScale(nn.Module):
    def __init__(
        self,
        dim: int,
        init_values: Union[float, Tensor] = 1e-5,
        inplace: bool = False,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.inplace = inplace
        self.gamma = nn.Parameter(init_values * torch.ones(dim))

    def forward(self, x: Tensor) -> Tensor:
        return x.mul_(self.gamma) if self.inplace else x * self.gamma

# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/model\layers\lorentz_ops.py

"""Curvature -1 geometry using unconstrained spatial Lorentz coordinates."""
import torch


def lift(space):
    """The intrinsic radius is asinh(||space||); there is no radial clipping."""
    return torch.cat(((1 + space.square().sum(-1, keepdim=True)).sqrt(), space), dim=-1)


def normalize_sum(point):
    """Lorentz centroid of a positive weighted mean of unit hyperboloid points."""
    norm2 = point[..., :1].square() - point[..., 1:].square().sum(-1, keepdim=True)
    # Convex weights imply norm2 >= 1. Rebuild time to preserve the unit manifold.
    return lift(point[..., 1:] / norm2.clamp_min(1).sqrt())


def acosh1p_squared(z):
    """Squared geodesic distance with its finite analytic derivative at z=0."""
    regular_z = z.clamp_min(1e-3)
    regular = torch.log1p(regular_z + (regular_z * (regular_z + 2)).sqrt()).square()
    series = 2 * z - z.square() / 3 + 4 * z.pow(3) / 45 - z.pow(4) / 35
    return torch.where(z <= 1e-3, series, regular)


def pairwise_squared_distance(query, key):
    space_q, space_k = query[..., 1:], key[..., 1:]
    squared_chord = (space_q.square().sum(-1, keepdim=True)
                     + space_k.square().sum(-1).unsqueeze(-2)
                     - 2 * (space_q @ space_k.transpose(-1, -2)))
    time_difference = query[..., :1] - key[..., :1].transpose(-1, -2)
    z = (0.5 * (squared_chord - time_difference.square())).clamp_min(0)
    return acosh1p_squared(z)

# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/model\layers\mamba_scan_triton.py

"""Triton selective scan kernel（vendored from mamba-ssm 2.3.2，Apache-2.0）。

Copyright (c) 2024, Tri Dao, Albert Gu.
来源：mamba_ssm/ops/triton/selective_state_update.py + mamba_ssm/ops/triton/softplus.py
（https://github.com/state-spaces/mamba）

用途：RSML-3（torch 2.14 + CUDA 13.2 + RTX 5090 sm_120）上官方 selective_scan_cuda
CUDA kernel 无可用 wheel/ABI 不兼容；Triton 内核 JIT 适配当前架构，语义与官方
selective scan（S6 递推）完全一致。只依赖 torch + triton + einops。
"""

import triton
import triton.language as tl
from packaging import version

TRITON3 = version.parse(triton.__version__) >= version.parse("3.0.0")


if TRITON3:
    @triton.jit
    def softplus(dt):
        return tl.math.log(tl.math.exp(dt) + 1)
else:
    @triton.jit
    def softplus(dt):
        return tl.math.log1p(tl.exp(dt))

# Copyright (c) 2024, Tri Dao, Albert Gu.

"""We want triton==2.1.0 or triton==2.2.0 or triton==2.3.0 for this
"""

import math
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F

import triton
import triton.language as tl

from einops import rearrange, repeat


@triton.heuristics({"HAS_DT_BIAS": lambda args: args["dt_bias_ptr"] is not None})
@triton.heuristics({"HAS_D": lambda args: args["D_ptr"] is not None})
@triton.heuristics({"HAS_Z": lambda args: args["z_ptr"] is not None})
@triton.heuristics({"HAS_STATE_BATCH_INDICES": lambda args: args["state_batch_indices_ptr"] is not None})
@triton.heuristics({"BLOCK_SIZE_DSTATE": lambda args: triton.next_power_of_2(args["dstate"])})
@triton.jit
def _selective_scan_update_kernel(
    # Pointers to matrices
    state_ptr, x_ptr, dt_ptr, dt_bias_ptr, A_ptr, B_ptr, C_ptr, D_ptr, z_ptr, out_ptr, state_batch_indices_ptr,
    # Matrix dimensions
    batch, nheads, dim, dstate, nheads_ngroups_ratio,
    # Strides
    stride_state_batch, stride_state_head, stride_state_dim, stride_state_dstate,
    stride_x_batch, stride_x_head, stride_x_dim,
    stride_dt_batch, stride_dt_head, stride_dt_dim,
    stride_dt_bias_head, stride_dt_bias_dim,
    stride_A_head, stride_A_dim, stride_A_dstate,
    stride_B_batch, stride_B_group, stride_B_dstate,
    stride_C_batch, stride_C_group, stride_C_dstate,
    stride_D_head, stride_D_dim,
    stride_z_batch, stride_z_head, stride_z_dim,
    stride_out_batch, stride_out_head, stride_out_dim,
    # Meta-parameters
    DT_SOFTPLUS: tl.constexpr,
    TIE_HDIM: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_SIZE_M: tl.constexpr,
    HAS_DT_BIAS: tl.constexpr,
    HAS_D: tl.constexpr,
    HAS_Z: tl.constexpr,
    HAS_STATE_BATCH_INDICES: tl.constexpr,
    BLOCK_SIZE_DSTATE: tl.constexpr,
):
    pid_m = tl.program_id(axis=0)
    pid_b = tl.program_id(axis=1)
    pid_h = tl.program_id(axis=2)

    offs_m = pid_m * BLOCK_SIZE_M + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_SIZE_M)
    out_ptr += pid_b * stride_out_batch + pid_h * stride_out_head
    out_ptrs = out_ptr + offs_m * stride_out_dim

    if HAS_STATE_BATCH_INDICES:
        state_batch_indices_ptr += pid_b
        state_batch_idx = tl.load(state_batch_indices_ptr)
        # Skip padding tokens
        if state_batch_idx < 0:
            tl.store(out_ptrs, 0.0, mask=offs_m < dim)
            return
        state_ptr += state_batch_idx * stride_state_batch + pid_h * stride_state_head
    else:
        state_ptr += pid_b * stride_state_batch + pid_h * stride_state_head

    x_ptr += pid_b * stride_x_batch + pid_h * stride_x_head
    dt_ptr += pid_b * stride_dt_batch + pid_h * stride_dt_head
    if HAS_DT_BIAS:
        dt_bias_ptr += pid_h * stride_dt_bias_head
    A_ptr += pid_h * stride_A_head
    B_ptr += pid_b * stride_B_batch + (pid_h // nheads_ngroups_ratio) * stride_B_group
    C_ptr += pid_b * stride_C_batch + (pid_h // nheads_ngroups_ratio) * stride_C_group
    if HAS_Z:
        z_ptr += pid_b * stride_z_batch + pid_h * stride_z_head

    offs_n = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_SIZE_DSTATE)
    state_ptrs = state_ptr + (offs_m[:, None] * stride_state_dim + offs_n[None, :] * stride_state_dstate)
    x_ptrs = x_ptr + offs_m * stride_x_dim
    dt_ptrs = dt_ptr + offs_m * stride_dt_dim
    if HAS_DT_BIAS:
        dt_bias_ptrs = dt_bias_ptr + offs_m * stride_dt_bias_dim
    if HAS_D:
        D_ptr += pid_h * stride_D_head
    A_ptrs = A_ptr + (offs_m[:, None] * stride_A_dim + offs_n[None, :] * stride_A_dstate)
    B_ptrs = B_ptr + offs_n * stride_B_dstate
    C_ptrs = C_ptr + offs_n * stride_C_dstate
    if HAS_D:
        D_ptrs = D_ptr + offs_m * stride_D_dim
    if HAS_Z:
        z_ptrs = z_ptr + offs_m * stride_z_dim

    state = tl.load(state_ptrs, mask=(offs_m[:, None] < dim) & (offs_n[None, :] < dstate), other=0.0)
    x = tl.load(x_ptrs, mask=offs_m < dim, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    if not TIE_HDIM:
        dt = tl.load(dt_ptrs, mask=offs_m < dim, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
        if HAS_DT_BIAS:
            dt += tl.load(dt_bias_ptrs, mask=offs_m < dim, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
        if DT_SOFTPLUS:
            dt = tl.where(dt <= 20.0, softplus(dt), dt)
        A = tl.load(A_ptrs, mask=(offs_m[:, None] < dim) & (offs_n[None, :] < dstate), other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
        dA = tl.exp(A * dt[:, None])
    else:
        dt = tl.load(dt_ptr).to(tl.float32)
        if HAS_DT_BIAS:
            dt += tl.load(dt_bias_ptr).to(tl.float32)
        if DT_SOFTPLUS:
            dt = tl.where(dt <= 20.0, softplus(dt), dt)
        A = tl.load(A_ptr).to(tl.float32)
        dA = tl.exp(A * dt)  # scalar, not a matrix

    B = tl.load(B_ptrs, mask=offs_n < dstate, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    C = tl.load(C_ptrs, mask=offs_n < dstate, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    if HAS_D:
        D = tl.load(D_ptrs, mask=offs_m < dim, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    if HAS_Z:
        z = tl.load(z_ptrs, mask=offs_m < dim, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)

    if not TIE_HDIM:
        dB = B[None, :] * dt[:, None]
    else:
        dB = B * dt  # vector of size (dstate,)
    state = state * dA + dB * x[:, None]
    tl.store(state_ptrs, state, mask=(offs_m[:, None] < dim) & (offs_n[None, :] < dstate))
    out = tl.sum(state * C[None, :], axis=1)
    if HAS_D:
        out += x * D
    if HAS_Z:
        out *= z * tl.sigmoid(z)
    tl.store(out_ptrs, out, mask=offs_m < dim)


def selective_state_update(state, x, dt, A, B, C, D=None, z=None, dt_bias=None, dt_softplus=False,
                           state_batch_indices=None):
    """
    Argument:
        state: (batch, dim, dstate) or (batch, nheads, dim, dstate)
        x: (batch, dim) or (batch, nheads, dim)
        dt: (batch, dim) or (batch, nheads, dim)
        A: (dim, dstate) or (nheads, dim, dstate)
        B: (batch, dstate) or (batch, ngroups, dstate)
        C: (batch, dstate) or (batch, ngroups, dstate)
        D: (dim,) or (nheads, dim)
        z: (batch, dim) or (batch, nheads, dim)
        dt_bias: (dim,) or (nheads, dim)
    Return:
        out: (batch, dim) or (batch, nheads, dim)
    """
    has_heads = state.dim() > 3
    if state.dim() == 3:
        state = state.unsqueeze(1)
    if x.dim() == 2:
        x = x.unsqueeze(1)
    if dt.dim() == 2:
        dt = dt.unsqueeze(1)
    if A.dim() == 2:
        A = A.unsqueeze(0)
    if B.dim() == 2:
        B = B.unsqueeze(1)
    if C.dim() == 2:
        C = C.unsqueeze(1)
    if D is not None and D.dim() == 1:
        D = D.unsqueeze(0)
    if z is not None and z.dim() == 2:
        z = z.unsqueeze(1)
    if dt_bias is not None and dt_bias.dim() == 1:
        dt_bias = dt_bias.unsqueeze(0)
    _, nheads, dim, dstate = state.shape
    batch = x.shape[0]
    if x.shape != (batch, nheads, dim):
        print(f"{state.shape} {x.shape} {batch} {nheads} {dim}")
    assert x.shape == (batch, nheads, dim)
    assert dt.shape == x.shape
    assert A.shape == (nheads, dim, dstate)
    ngroups = B.shape[1]
    assert nheads % ngroups == 0, "nheads must be divisible by ngroups"
    assert B.shape == (batch, ngroups, dstate)
    assert C.shape == B.shape
    if D is not None:
        assert D.shape == (nheads, dim)
    if z is not None:
        assert z.shape == x.shape
    if dt_bias is not None:
        assert dt_bias.shape == (nheads, dim)
    if state_batch_indices is not None:
        assert state_batch_indices.shape == (batch,)
    out = torch.empty_like(x)
    grid = lambda META: (triton.cdiv(dim, META['BLOCK_SIZE_M']), batch, nheads)
    z_strides = ((z.stride(0), z.stride(1), z.stride(2)) if z is not None else (0, 0, 0))
    # We don't want autotune since it will overwrite the state
    # We instead tune by hand.
    BLOCK_SIZE_M, num_warps = ((32, 4) if dstate <= 16
                               else ((16, 4) if dstate <= 32 else
                                     ((8, 4) if dstate <= 64 else
                                      ((4, 4) if dstate <= 128 else
                                       ((4, 8))))))
    tie_hdim = A.stride(-1) == 0 and A.stride(-2) == 0 and dt.stride(-1) == 0 and dt_bias.stride(-1) == 0
    with torch.cuda.device(x.device.index):
        _selective_scan_update_kernel[grid](
            state, x, dt, dt_bias, A, B, C, D, z, out, state_batch_indices,
            batch, nheads, dim, dstate, nheads // ngroups,
            state.stride(0), state.stride(1), state.stride(2), state.stride(3),
            x.stride(0), x.stride(1), x.stride(2),
            dt.stride(0), dt.stride(1), dt.stride(2),
            *(dt_bias.stride(0), dt_bias.stride(1)) if dt_bias is not None else 0,
            A.stride(0), A.stride(1), A.stride(2),
            B.stride(0), B.stride(1), B.stride(2),
            C.stride(0), C.stride(1), C.stride(2),
            *(D.stride(0), D.stride(1)) if D is not None else 0,
            z_strides[0], z_strides[1], z_strides[2],
            out.stride(0), out.stride(1), out.stride(2),
            dt_softplus,
            tie_hdim,
            BLOCK_SIZE_M,
            num_warps=num_warps,
        )
    if not has_heads:
        out = out.squeeze(1)
    return out


def selective_state_update_ref(state, x, dt, A, B, C, D=None, z=None, dt_bias=None, dt_softplus=False):
    """
    Argument:
        state: (batch, dim, dstate) or (batch, nheads, dim, dstate)
        x: (batch, dim) or (batch, nheads, dim)
        dt: (batch, dim) or (batch, nheads, dim)
        A: (dim, dstate) or (nheads, dim, dstate)
        B: (batch, dstate) or (batch, ngroups, dstate)
        C: (batch, dstate) or (batch, ngroups, dstate)
        D: (dim,) or (nheads, dim)
        z: (batch, dim) or (batch, nheads, dim)
        dt_bias: (dim,) or (nheads, dim)
    Return:
        out: (batch, dim) or (batch, nheads, dim)
    """
    has_heads = state.dim() > 3
    if state.dim() == 3:
        state = state.unsqueeze(1)
    if x.dim() == 2:
        x = x.unsqueeze(1)
    if dt.dim() == 2:
        dt = dt.unsqueeze(1)
    if A.dim() == 2:
        A = A.unsqueeze(0)
    if B.dim() == 2:
        B = B.unsqueeze(1)
    if C.dim() == 2:
        C = C.unsqueeze(1)
    if D is not None and D.dim() == 1:
        D = D.unsqueeze(0)
    if z is not None and z.dim() == 2:
        z = z.unsqueeze(1)
    if dt_bias is not None and dt_bias.dim() == 1:
        dt_bias = dt_bias.unsqueeze(0)
    batch, nheads, dim, dstate = state.shape
    assert x.shape == (batch, nheads, dim)
    assert dt.shape == x.shape
    assert A.shape == (nheads, dim, dstate)
    ngroups = B.shape[1]
    assert nheads % ngroups == 0, "nheads must be divisible by ngroups"
    assert B.shape == (batch, ngroups, dstate)
    assert C.shape == B.shape
    if D is not None:
        assert D.shape == (nheads, dim)
    if z is not None:
        assert z.shape == x.shape
    if dt_bias is not None:
        assert dt_bias.shape == (nheads, dim)
        dt = dt + dt_bias
    dt = F.softplus(dt) if dt_softplus else dt
    dA = torch.exp(rearrange(dt, "b h d -> b h d 1") * A)  # (batch, nheads, dim, dstate)
    B = repeat(B, "b g n -> b (g h) n", h=nheads // ngroups)  # (batch, nheads, dstate)
    C = repeat(C, "b g n -> b (g h) n", h=nheads // ngroups)  # (batch, nheads, dstate)
    dB = rearrange(dt, "b h d -> b h d 1") * rearrange(B, "b h n -> b h 1 n")  # (batch, nheads, dim, dstate)
    state.copy_(state * dA + dB * rearrange(x, "b h d -> b h d 1"))  # (batch, dim, dstate
    out = torch.einsum("bhdn,bhn->bhd", state.to(C.dtype), C)
    if D is not None:
        out += (x * D).to(out.dtype)
    out = (out if z is None else out * F.silu(z)).to(x.dtype)
    if not has_heads:
        out = out.squeeze(1)
    return out

# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/model\layers\mlp.py

# Copyright (c) Meta Platforms, Inc. and affiliates.
#
# This source code is licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0
# found in the LICENSE file in the root directory of this source tree.

# References:
#   https://github.com/facebookresearch/dino/blob/master/vision_transformer.py
#   https://github.com/rwightman/pytorch-image-models/tree/master/timm/layers/mlp.py


from typing import Callable, Optional

from torch import Tensor, nn


class Mlp(nn.Module):
    def __init__(
        self,
        in_features: int,
        hidden_features: Optional[int] = None,
        out_features: Optional[int] = None,
        act_layer: Callable[..., nn.Module] = nn.GELU,
        drop: float = 0.0,
        bias: bool = True,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        out_features = out_features or in_features
        hidden_features = hidden_features or in_features
        self.fc1 = nn.Linear(in_features, hidden_features, bias=bias)
        self.act = act_layer()
        self.fc2 = nn.Linear(hidden_features, out_features, bias=bias)
        self.drop = nn.Dropout(drop)

    def forward(self, x: Tensor) -> Tensor:
        x = self.fc1(x)
        x = self.act(x)
        x = self.drop(x)
        x = self.fc2(x)
        x = self.drop(x)
        return x

# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/model\layers\moe_context.py

"""Shared change prototypes and dense routing of temporal, geometric and frequency evidence."""
import math

import torch
from torch import nn
from torch.nn import functional as F

from model.layers.lorentz_ops import lift, pairwise_squared_distance
from model.layers.pyramid_context import (
    attention_diagnostics, channel_norm, feature_regions, merge_heads, pool_features,
    project_tokens, rms, source_bias, split_heads,
)


MODEL_VARIANTS = (
    "none", "hyperbolic", "qkv", "frequency", "equal", "moe",
    "no_hyperbolic", "no_qkv", "no_frequency",
)
EXPERTS = ("hyperbolic", "qkv", "frequency")


def subtype_assignments(probability, appearance, grid):
    """Two appearance representatives for each change/background class in a region."""
    positive = feature_regions(probability, grid).squeeze(-1)
    subtype = feature_regions(appearance, grid).squeeze(-1)
    classes = torch.stack((positive, 1 - positive), dim=2)
    modes = torch.stack((subtype, 1 - subtype), dim=2)
    weights = (classes.unsqueeze(3) + 1e-6).square() * (modes.unsqueeze(2) + 1e-6)
    weights = weights / weights.sum(-1, keepdim=True)
    support = (weights * classes.unsqueeze(3)).sum(-1)
    return weights.flatten(2, 3), support.flatten(1).clamp_min(1e-6)


def haar_split(x):
    """Orthonormal Haar analysis of even spatial sizes; retain signed LH/HL/HH."""
    if x.shape[-2] % 2 or x.shape[-1] % 2:
        raise ValueError("Haar analysis requires even height and width")
    a, b = x[..., 0::2, 0::2], x[..., 0::2, 1::2]
    c, d = x[..., 1::2, 0::2], x[..., 1::2, 1::2]
    low = (a + b + c + d) / 2
    high = torch.stack(((a - b + c - d) / 2, (a + b - c - d) / 2,
                        (a - b - c + d) / 2), dim=2)
    return low, high


def haar_merge(low, high):
    """Exact inverse of haar_split before learned subband transformations."""
    lh, hl, hh = high.unbind(2)
    top = torch.stack(((low + lh + hl + hh) / 2,
                       (low - lh + hl - hh) / 2), -1).flatten(-2)
    bottom = torch.stack(((low + lh - hl - hh) / 2,
                          (low - lh - hl + hh) / 2), -1).flatten(-2)
    return torch.stack((top, bottom), -2).flatten(-3, -2)


class RetrievalExpert(nn.Module):
    """Full-resolution queries read a shared compressed content pool."""
    def __init__(self, geometry, channels=64, width=32):
        super().__init__()
        self.geometry = geometry
        self.heads = 1
        self.qk = nn.Conv2d(channels, width, 1, bias=False)
        self.v = nn.Conv2d(channels, width, 1, bias=False)
        self.out = nn.Conv2d(width, channels, 1, bias=False)
        self.scale_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(1, 4, 4))
        self.raw_temperature = nn.Parameter(torch.full((1,), math.log(math.expm1(.5))))

    def point(self, projection, occupancy):
        direction = F.normalize(projection, dim=-1, eps=1e-6)
        if self.geometry == "hyperbolic":
            return lift((1 + occupancy) * direction)
        return direction

    def prepare(self, prototype, prototype_occupancy):
        key = self.point(project_tokens(self.qk, prototype, 1), prototype_occupancy[:, None])
        return key, project_tokens(self.v, prototype, 1)

    def forward(self, feature, occupancy, key, values, support, scale, counts):
        height, width = feature.shape[-2:]
        query = self.point(split_heads(self.qk(feature), 1),
                           occupancy.permute(0, 2, 3, 1).unsqueeze(1)).flatten(2, 3)
        if self.geometry == "hyperbolic":
            distance = pairwise_squared_distance(query, key)
        else:
            distance = (query.square().sum(-1, keepdim=True)
                        + key.square().sum(-1).unsqueeze(-2)
                        - 2 * query @ key.transpose(-1, -2)).clamp_min(0)
        temperature = F.softplus(self.raw_temperature).view(1, 1, 1, 1)
        logits = -distance / (2 * temperature.square())
        logits = logits + support.log()[:, None, None] + source_bias(self.scale_bias, scale, counts)
        attention = logits.softmax(-1)
        retrieved = merge_heads(attention @ values, height, width)
        correction = self.out(retrieved - self.v(feature))
        return correction, attention, query


class FrequencyExpert(nn.Module):
    """Low-frequency structure conditions signed directional high-frequency correction."""
    def __init__(self, channels=64, width=16):
        super().__init__()
        self.width = width
        self.input = nn.Conv2d(channels, width, 1, bias=False)
        self.low_dw = nn.Conv2d(width, width, 3, padding=1, padding_mode="replicate",
                                groups=width, bias=False)
        self.low_out = nn.Conv2d(width, width, 1, bias=False)
        self.high_dw = nn.Conv2d(3 * width, 3 * width, 3, padding=1,
                                 padding_mode="replicate", groups=3 * width, bias=False)
        self.high_out = nn.Conv2d(3 * width, 3 * width, 1, groups=3, bias=False)
        self.high_gate = nn.Conv2d(width, 3 * width, 1)
        self.out = nn.Conv2d(width, channels, 1, bias=False)

    def forward(self, feature):
        low, high = haar_split(self.input(feature))
        # Group each directional band across channels; keep its signed coefficients.
        directional = high.transpose(1, 2).flatten(1, 2)
        low_update = self.low_out(F.silu(self.low_dw(low)))
        high_update = self.high_out(self.high_dw(directional)) * self.high_gate(low).sigmoid()
        high_update = high_update.reshape(feature.shape[0], 3, self.width,
                                          *low.shape[-2:]).transpose(1, 2)
        return self.out(haar_merge(low_update, high_update))


class MoEContext(nn.Module):
    """TAR identity and three correction experts with one residual amplitude."""
    geometry = "heterogeneous"
    value_geometry = "euclidean"
    compression_power = 2
    support_power = 1
    self_reference = False
    radius_mode = "hyperbolic: asinh(1 + supervised_change_occupancy)"
    key_mode = "shared_pooled_content_projected_by_each_expert"
    readout_mode = "single_gamma_times_router_weighted_relative_corrections"

    def __init__(self, variant="moe", channels=64, grid_sizes=(8, 4, 4, 4)):
        super().__init__()
        if variant not in MODEL_VARIANTS[1:]:
            raise ValueError(f"Unknown MoE correction variant: {variant}")
        self.variant = variant
        self.channels = channels
        self.grid_sizes = tuple(grid_sizes)
        self.heads = 1
        self.head_dim = 32
        self.norm = nn.LayerNorm(channels)
        self.guide = nn.Conv2d(channels, 1, 1)
        self.enabled_experts = ((variant,) if variant in EXPERTS else
                                tuple(name for name in EXPERTS if variant != f"no_{name}"))
        self.routing_mode = "single" if variant in EXPERTS else "equal" if variant == "equal" else "learned"
        self.route_names = ("tar",) + self.enabled_experts
        self.uses_context = any(name in self.enabled_experts for name in ("hyperbolic", "qkv"))
        self.tokens_per_scale = tuple(4 * grid ** 2 if self.uses_context else 0 for grid in grid_sizes)
        self.experts = nn.ModuleDict()
        # Composition-independent seeds match expert initialization across ablations.
        for name, seed in zip(EXPERTS, (3401, 3402, 3403)):
            if name in self.enabled_experts:
                with torch.random.fork_rng():
                    torch.manual_seed(seed)
                    self.experts[name] = (FrequencyExpert(channels) if name == "frequency"
                                          else RetrievalExpert(name, channels))
        if self.uses_context:
            with torch.random.fork_rng():
                torch.manual_seed(3404)
                self.appearance = nn.Conv2d(channels, 1, 1)
        if self.routing_mode == "learned":
            with torch.random.fork_rng():
                torch.manual_seed(3405)
                self.router = nn.Sequential(nn.Conv2d(channels, 16, 1), nn.SiLU(),
                                             nn.Conv2d(16, 4, 1))
                nn.init.normal_(self.router[2].weight, std=.01)
                nn.init.zeros_(self.router[2].bias)
        self.gamma_context = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(4, channels))
        self.collect_diag = False
        self.last_diag = {}

    def route_weights(self, feature):
        shape = (feature.shape[0], len(self.route_names), *feature.shape[-2:])
        if self.routing_mode == "learned":
            indices = [0] + [1 + EXPERTS.index(name) for name in self.enabled_experts]
            return self.router(feature)[:, indices].softmax(1)
        weights = feature.new_full(shape, 1 / len(self.route_names))
        if self.routing_mode == "single":
            weights[:, 0] = 0
            weights[:, 1] = 1
        return weights

    def metadata(self):
        return dict(
            variant=self.variant, active_experts=list(self.route_names), routing_mode=self.routing_mode,
            execution="dense_all_enabled_experts", context_grid_sizes=list(self.grid_sizes),
            context_prototypes_per_scale=list(self.tokens_per_scale), joint_contexts=sum(self.tokens_per_scale),
            qk_geometry={name: "lorentz_occupancy_radius" if name == "hyperbolic" else "euclidean_unit_direction"
                         for name in self.enabled_experts if name != "frequency"},
            value_geometry="euclidean", heads=1 if self.uses_context else 0,
            query_key_head_dim=32 if self.uses_context else 0, value_head_dim=32 if self.uses_context else 0,
            qkv_dim=32 if self.uses_context else 0, compression_power=2 if self.uses_context else 0,
            support_power=1 if self.uses_context else 0, self_reference=False,
            hyperbolic_radius=self.radius_mode if "hyperbolic" in self.enabled_experts else None,
            key_mode=self.key_mode, readout_mode=self.readout_mode, context_gamma_shape=[4, self.channels],
            local_gamma_shape=[], frequency_width=16 if "frequency" in self.enabled_experts else 0,
            frequency_transform="single_level_signed_haar" if "frequency" in self.enabled_experts else None,
            router_hidden_channels=16 if self.routing_mode == "learned" else 0,
            initialization_seeds={"hyperbolic": 3401, "qkv": 3402, "frequency": 3403,
                                  "appearance": 3404, "router": 3405},
        )

    def forward(self, tar):
        normalized = [channel_norm(self.norm, x) for x in tar]
        guide = [self.guide(x) for x in normalized]
        occupancies = [g.sigmoid() for g in guide]
        prepared, support = {}, None
        if self.uses_context:
            compression = [subtype_assignments(p, self.appearance(x).sigmoid(), grid)
                           for x, p, grid in zip(normalized, occupancies, self.grid_sizes)]
            prototype = torch.cat([pool_features(x, weights, grid)
                                   for x, (weights, _), grid in zip(normalized, compression, self.grid_sizes)], 1)
            prototype_occupancy = F.linear(prototype, self.guide.weight[:, :, 0, 0], self.guide.bias).sigmoid()
            support = torch.cat([item[1] for item in compression], 1)
            for name in self.enabled_experts:
                if name != "frequency":
                    prepared[name] = self.experts[name].prepare(prototype, prototype_occupancy)
        enhanced, diagnostics = [], {}
        for scale, (original, feature, occupancy) in enumerate(zip(tar, normalized, occupancies)):
            routes = self.route_weights(feature)
            updates, attention_diags = {}, {}
            for name in self.enabled_experts:
                expert = self.experts[name]
                if name == "frequency":
                    updates[name] = expert(feature)
                else:
                    key, values = prepared[name]
                    update, attention, query = expert(feature, occupancy, key, values, support,
                                                       scale, self.tokens_per_scale)
                    updates[name] = update
                    if self.collect_diag:
                        with torch.no_grad():
                            stats = attention_diagnostics(attention, self.tokens_per_scale)
                            stats["temperature"] = F.softplus(expert.raw_temperature).tolist()
                            if name == "hyperbolic":
                                radius = torch.asinh(query[..., 1:].norm(dim=-1))
                                key_radius = torch.asinh(key[..., 1:].norm(dim=-1))
                                stats.update(radius_mean=float(radius.mean()),
                                             radius_cv=float(radius.std(unbiased=False) / radius.mean().clamp_min(1e-8)),
                                             key_radius_mean=float(key_radius.mean()))
                            attention_diags[name] = stats
            weighted = {name: routes[:, index:index + 1] * updates[name]
                        for index, name in enumerate(self.route_names) if name != "tar"}
            gamma = self.gamma_context[scale][None, :, None, None]
            residual = gamma * sum(weighted.values())
            result = original + residual
            enhanced.append(result)
            if self.collect_diag:
                with torch.no_grad():
                    base = rms(original).clamp_min(1e-8)
                    route_mean = routes.mean((0, 2, 3))
                    foreground = (routes * occupancy).sum((0, 2, 3)) / occupancy.sum().clamp_min(1e-8)
                    background = (routes * (1 - occupancy)).sum((0, 2, 3)) / (1 - occupancy).sum().clamp_min(1e-8)
                    cosines = {f"{left}/{right}": float(F.cosine_similarity(updates[left].flatten(1),
                                                                            updates[right].flatten(1)).mean())
                               for i, left in enumerate(self.enabled_experts)
                               for right in self.enabled_experts[i + 1:]}
                    diagnostics[f"stage{scale + 1}"] = dict(
                        queries=feature.shape[-2] * feature.shape[-1], contexts=sum(self.tokens_per_scale),
                        gamma_context=float(self.gamma_context[scale].mean()),
                        gamma_context_rms=float(rms(self.gamma_context[scale])), feature_rms=float(base),
                        residual_to_feature_rms=float(rms(residual) / base),
                        branch_rms=float(rms(sum(weighted.values()))),
                        route_mass=dict(zip(self.route_names, route_mean.tolist())),
                        route_change_mass=dict(zip(self.route_names, foreground.tolist())),
                        route_background_mass=dict(zip(self.route_names, background.tolist())),
                        route_grouping="predicted_change_occupancy_weighted",
                        route_entropy=float(-(routes * routes.clamp_min(1e-9).log()).sum(1).mean()
                                            / math.log(len(self.route_names))),
                        expert_rms={name: float(rms(update)) for name, update in updates.items()},
                        expert_residual_to_feature_rms={name: float(rms(gamma * value) / base)
                                                       for name, value in weighted.items()},
                        expert_cosine=cosines, expert_attention=attention_diags,
                        guide_mean=float(occupancy.mean()), finite=bool(torch.isfinite(result).all()))
        if self.collect_diag:
            self.last_diag = diagnostics
        return enhanced, dict(guide_logits=tuple(guide))

# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/model\layers\patch_embed.py
