# Copyright (c) Meta Platforms, Inc. and affiliates.
#
# This source code is licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0
# found in the LICENSE file in the root directory of this source tree.

# References:
#   https://github.com/facebookresearch/dino/blob/master/vision_transformer.py
#   https://github.com/rwightman/pytorch-image-models/tree/master/timm/layers/patch_embed.py

from typing import Callable, Optional, Tuple, Union

from torch import Tensor
import torch.nn as nn


def make_2tuple(x):
    if isinstance(x, tuple):
        assert len(x) == 2
        return x

    assert isinstance(x, int)
    return (x, x)


class PatchEmbed(nn.Module):
    """
    2D image to patch embedding: (B,C,H,W) -> (B,N,D)

    Args:
        img_size: Image size.
        patch_size: Patch token size.
        in_chans: Number of input image channels.
        embed_dim: Number of linear projection output channels.
        norm_layer: Normalization layer.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        img_size: Union[int, Tuple[int, int]] = 224,
        patch_size: Union[int, Tuple[int, int]] = 16,
        in_chans: int = 3,
        embed_dim: int = 768,
        norm_layer: Optional[Callable] = None,
        flatten_embedding: bool = True,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()

        image_HW = make_2tuple(img_size)
        patch_HW = make_2tuple(patch_size)
        patch_grid_size = (
            image_HW[0] // patch_HW[0],
            image_HW[1] // patch_HW[1],
        )

        self.img_size = image_HW
        self.patch_size = patch_HW
        self.patches_resolution = patch_grid_size
        self.num_patches = patch_grid_size[0] * patch_grid_size[1]

        self.in_chans = in_chans
        self.embed_dim = embed_dim

        self.flatten_embedding = flatten_embedding

        self.proj = nn.Conv2d(in_chans, embed_dim, kernel_size=patch_HW, stride=patch_HW)
        self.norm = norm_layer(embed_dim) if norm_layer else nn.Identity()

    def forward(self, x: Tensor) -> Tensor:
        _, _, H, W = x.shape
        patch_H, patch_W = self.patch_size

        assert H % patch_H == 0, f"Input image height {H} is not a multiple of patch height {patch_H}"
        assert W % patch_W == 0, f"Input image width {W} is not a multiple of patch width: {patch_W}"

        x = self.proj(x)  # B C H W
        H, W = x.size(2), x.size(3)
        x = x.flatten(2).transpose(1, 2)  # B HW C
        x = self.norm(x)
        if not self.flatten_embedding:
            x = x.reshape(-1, H, W, self.embed_dim)  # B H W C
        return x

    def flops(self) -> float:
        Ho, Wo = self.patches_resolution
        flops = Ho * Wo * self.embed_dim * self.in_chans * (self.patch_size[0] * self.patch_size[1])
        if self.norm is not None:
            flops += Ho * Wo * self.embed_dim
        return flops

================================================================================
# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/model\layers\pyramid_context.py
================================================================================
"""Selective change prototypes and self-referenced pyramid retrieval."""
import math

import torch
from torch import nn
from torch.nn import functional as F


def channel_norm(layer, x):
    return layer(x.permute(0, 2, 3, 1)).permute(0, 3, 1, 2)


def regions(x, grid):
    """B,heads,height,width,D -> B,heads,regions,pixels,D."""
    batch, heads, height, width, dim = x.shape
    return x.reshape(batch, heads, grid, height // grid, grid, width // grid, dim).permute(
        0, 1, 2, 4, 3, 5, 6).reshape(batch, heads, grid * grid, -1, dim)


def feature_regions(x, grid):
    return regions(x.permute(0, 2, 3, 1).unsqueeze(1), grid).squeeze(1)


def split_heads(x, heads):
    batch, channels, height, width = x.shape
    return x.reshape(batch, heads, channels // heads, height, width).permute(0, 1, 3, 4, 2)


def merge_heads(x, height, width):
    return x.transpose(-1, -2).reshape(x.shape[0], -1, height, width)


def project_tokens(layer, tokens, heads):
    projected = F.linear(tokens, layer.weight[:, :, 0, 0])
    return projected.reshape(projected.shape[0], -1, heads, projected.shape[-1] // heads).transpose(1, 2)


def assignments(probability, grid):
    """Sharpen class membership within each region and measure class support."""
    positive = feature_regions(probability, grid).squeeze(-1)
    memberships = torch.stack((positive, 1 - positive), dim=2)
    weights = (memberships + 1e-6).square()
    weights = weights / weights.sum(-1, keepdim=True)
    confidence = (weights * memberships.square()).sum(-1).clamp_min(1e-6)
    return weights, confidence.flatten(1, 2)


def pool_features(feature, weights, grid):
    return torch.einsum("brtp,brpc->brtc", weights, feature_regions(feature, grid)).flatten(1, 2)


def source_bias(bias, query_scale, counts):
    return torch.cat([(bias[:, query_scale, scale] - math.log(count))[:, None].expand(-1, count)
                      for scale, count in enumerate(counts)], dim=-1)[None, :, None]


def highpass(x):
    return x - F.avg_pool2d(F.pad(x, (1, 1, 1, 1), mode="replicate"), 3, 1)


def rms(x):
    return x.square().mean().sqrt()


def attention_diagnostics(probability, counts):
    mass = probability.sum(-1, keepdim=True)
    conditional = probability / mass.clamp_min(1e-8)
    tv = (conditional - conditional.mean(-2, keepdim=True)).abs().sum(-1) / 2
    return dict(
        entropy=float(-(conditional * conditional.clamp_min(1e-9).log()).sum(-1).mean()
                      / math.log(probability.shape[-1])),
        scale_mass=torch.stack([p.sum(-1) for p in conditional.split(counts, -1)], -1).mean((0, 2)).tolist(),
        context_mass_mean=float(mass.mean()), context_mass_min=float(mass.min()),
        context_mass_max=float(mass.max()), context_mass_std=float(mass.std(unbiased=False)),
        self_mass_mean=float((1-mass).mean()),
        query_attention_tv=float(tv.mean()), query_attention_tv_per_head=tv.mean((0, 2)).tolist())


class ResidualPyramidContext(nn.Module):
    """Shared compression/readout; subclasses define Euclidean or Lorentz keys."""
    value_geometry = "euclidean"
    compression_power = 2
    support_power = 2
    self_reference = True

    def __init__(self, channels=64, heads=4, grid_sizes=(8, 4, 4, 4)):
        super().__init__()
        self.heads = heads
        self.grid_sizes = tuple(grid_sizes)
        self.tokens_per_scale = tuple(2 * grid ** 2 for grid in grid_sizes)
        self.norm = nn.LayerNorm(channels)
        self.guide = nn.Conv2d(channels, 1, 1)
        self.qk = nn.Conv2d(channels, channels, 1, bias=False)
        self.v = nn.Conv2d(channels, channels, 1, bias=False)
        self.out = nn.Conv2d(channels, channels, 1, bias=False)
        self.local_dw = nn.Conv2d(channels, channels, 3, padding=1, padding_mode="replicate",
                                  groups=channels, bias=False)
        self.local_out = nn.Conv2d(channels, channels, 1, bias=False)
        self.scale_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(heads, 4, 4))
        self.raw_temperature = nn.Parameter(torch.full((heads,), math.log(math.expm1(.5))))
        self.gamma_context = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(4, channels))
        self.gamma_detail = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(2, channels))
        self.collect_diag = False
        self.last_diag = {}

    def attention(self, query, key, confidence, query_scale):
        temperature = F.softplus(self.raw_temperature).view(1, self.heads, 1, 1)
        logits = -self.squared_distance(query, key) / (2 * temperature.square())
        logits = logits + confidence.log()[:, None, None] + source_bias(
            self.scale_bias, query_scale, self.tokens_per_scale)
        # The query's own key has distance zero and unit support. Its value is
        # retained locally; a shared softmax makes context adoption query-specific.
        return torch.cat((logits, torch.zeros_like(logits[..., :1])), -1).softmax(-1)[..., :-1]

    def forward(self, tar):
        normalized = [channel_norm(self.norm, x) for x in tar]
        guide = [self.guide(x) for x in normalized]
        compression = [assignments(g.sigmoid(), grid) for g, grid in zip(guide, self.grid_sizes)]
        prototypes = [pool_features(x, w, grid) for x, (w, _), grid in
                      zip(normalized, compression, self.grid_sizes)]
        query = [self.project_queries(x) for x in normalized]
        keys = [self.pool_keys(q, p, w, grid) for q, p, (w, _), grid in
                zip(query, prototypes, compression, self.grid_sizes)]
        key = torch.cat(keys, dim=2)
        confidence = torch.cat([r for _, r in compression], dim=1)
        values = project_tokens(self.v, torch.cat(prototypes, dim=1), self.heads)
        enhanced, diagnostics = [], {}
        for index, (original, x, q) in enumerate(zip(tar, normalized, query)):
            height, width = x.shape[-2:]
            probability = self.attention(q.flatten(2, 3), key, confidence, index)
            retrieved = merge_heads(probability @ values, height, width)
            own_value = self.v(x)
            mass = probability.sum(-1, keepdim=True)
            mass_map = merge_heads(mass.expand(-1, -1, -1, x.shape[1] // self.heads), height, width)
            difference = retrieved - mass_map * own_value
            update = self.out(difference)
            context_residual = self.gamma_context[index][None, :, None, None] * update
            result = original + context_residual
            local = None
            local_residual = torch.zeros_like(original)
            if index < 2:
                local = self.local_out(self.local_dw(highpass(x)))
                local_residual = self.gamma_detail[index][None, :, None, None] * local
                result = result + local_residual
            enhanced.append(result)
            if self.collect_diag:
                with torch.no_grad():
                    base = rms(original).clamp_min(1e-8)
                    weights, reliability = compression[index]
                    diag = dict(
                        queries=height * width, contexts=key.shape[2],
                        gamma_context=float(self.gamma_context[index].mean()),
                        gamma_context_rms=float(rms(self.gamma_context[index])),
                        gamma_detail=float(self.gamma_detail[index].mean()) if index < 2 else 0.,
                        gamma_detail_rms=float(rms(self.gamma_detail[index])) if index < 2 else 0.,
                        context_rms=float(rms(update)), branch_rms=float(rms(update)),
                        local_rms=float(rms(local)) if local is not None else 0.,
                        feature_rms=float(base), relative_update_rms=float(rms(difference)),
                        residual_rms=float(rms(context_residual + local_residual)),
                        residual_to_feature_rms=float(rms(context_residual + local_residual) / base),
                        context_residual_to_feature_rms=float(rms(context_residual) / base),
                        local_residual_to_feature_rms=float(rms(local_residual) / base),
                        guide_mean=float(guide[index].sigmoid().mean()),
                        reliability_mean=float(reliability.mean()), reliability_min=float(reliability.min()),
                        retrieved_rms=float(rms(retrieved)), self_value_rms=float(rms(own_value)),
                        self_subtraction_rms=float(rms(mass_map * own_value)),
                        retrieved_spatial_rms=float(rms(retrieved-retrieved.mean((-2,-1),keepdim=True))),
                        retrieved_self_cosine=float(F.cosine_similarity(retrieved.flatten(1),own_value.flatten(1)).mean()),
                        assignment_tv=float((weights[:, :, 0] - weights[:, :, 1]).abs().sum(-1).mean() / 2),
                        qk_weight_rms=float(rms(self.qk.weight)), v_weight_rms=float(rms(self.v.weight)),
                        out_weight_rms=float(rms(self.out.weight)),
                        temperature=F.softplus(self.raw_temperature).tolist(),
                        finite=bool(torch.isfinite(result).all()))
                    diag.update(attention_diagnostics(probability, self.tokens_per_scale))
                    diag.update(self.geometry_diagnostics(q, keys[index]))
                    diagnostics[f"stage{index + 1}"] = diag
        if self.collect_diag:
            self.last_diag = diagnostics
        return enhanced, dict(guide_logits=tuple(guide))


class AuxiliaryContext(nn.Module):
    """CTRL retains the same change-guide supervision on unmodified TAR features."""
    geometry = "none"
    value_geometry = "euclidean"
    grid_sizes = (8, 4, 4, 4)
    tokens_per_scale = (0, 0, 0, 0)
    compression_power = 0
    support_power = 0
    self_reference = False

    def __init__(self, channels=64):
        super().__init__()
        self.norm = nn.LayerNorm(channels)
        self.guide = nn.Conv2d(channels, 1, 1)
        self.collect_diag = False
        self.last_diag = {}

    def forward(self, tar):
        return list(tar), dict(guide_logits=tuple(self.guide(channel_norm(self.norm, x)) for x in tar))

# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/model\layers\ss2d.py
================================================================================
"""SS2D：TinyViM/VMamba 四向交叉扫描 SSM 的自包含移植（无 timm / 无 einops）。

与官方 TinyViM `model/tvimblock.py` 结构一致：
    Conv2d_BN / RepDW / RepDW_Axias / Rep_Inception / FFN / LocalBlock / TViMBlock /
    SS2D / CrossScan / CrossMerge / cross_selective_scan

kernel 后端（RSML-3 RTX 5090 sm_120 适配）：官方依赖的 `selective_scan_cuda`
（VMamba CUDA kernel）在 torch 2.14 + CUDA 13.2 + sm_120 上不可用/未编译，
本移植改用 **mamba-ssm 的 selective_scan_fn**（Triton 内核，JIT 适配当前架构），
语义与官方 selective scan 完全一致（同一 S6 递推），按四方向 K 维循环调用。
"""
import math
from functools import partial
from typing import Callable, Any

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

try:
    import selective_scan_cuda_oflex as _oflex_cuda
except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
    _oflex_cuda = None

try:
    from mamba_ssm.ops.selective_scan_interface import selective_scan_fn as _mamba_ssm_scan
except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001  （mamba-ssm wheel 自带 CUDA kernel ABI 不兼容 / 未安装）
    try:
        from model.layers.mamba_scan_triton import selective_scan_fn as _mamba_ssm_scan
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
        _mamba_ssm_scan = None


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 本地小工具（timm 等价物，避免依赖）
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _norm_cdf(x):
    return (1.0 + math.erf(x / math.sqrt(2.0))) / 2.0


def trunc_normal_(tensor, mean=0.0, std=1.0, a=-2.0, b=2.0):
    """timm trunc_normal_ 的无 scipy 实现。"""
    with torch.no_grad():
        l = _norm_cdf((a - mean) / std)
        u = _norm_cdf((b - mean) / std)
        tensor.uniform_(2 * l - 1, 2 * u - 1)
        tensor.erfinv_()
        tensor.mul_(std * math.sqrt(2.0)).add_(mean)
        tensor.clamp_(min=a, max=b)
    return tensor


class DropPath(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, drop_prob=0.0):
        super().__init__()
        self.drop_prob = drop_prob

    def forward(self, x):
        if self.drop_prob == 0.0 or not self.training:
            return x
        keep = 1.0 - self.drop_prob
        shape = (x.shape[0],) + (1,) * (x.ndim - 1)
        mask = x.new_empty(shape).bernoulli_(keep).div_(keep)
        return x * mask


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# TinyViM 基础模块（官方结构逐项对齐，仅替换 timm 依赖）
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
class Conv2d_BN(nn.Sequential):
    def __init__(self, a, b, ks=1, stride=1, pad=0, dilation=1,
                 groups=1, bn_weight_init=1, resolution=-10000):
        super().__init__()
        self.add_module('c', nn.Conv2d(a, b, ks, stride, pad, dilation, groups, bias=False))
        self.add_module('bn', nn.BatchNorm2d(b))
        nn.init.constant_(self.bn.weight, bn_weight_init)
        nn.init.constant_(self.bn.bias, 0)


class RepDW(nn.Module):
    """高频分支：reparameterizable 3x3 DW conv + 1x1 DW + identity，后接 BN。"""

    def __init__(self, ed):
        super().__init__()
        self.conv = Conv2d_BN(ed, ed, 3, 1, 1, groups=ed)
        self.conv1 = nn.Conv2d(ed, ed, 1, 1, 0, groups=ed)
        self.dim = ed
        self.bn = nn.BatchNorm2d(ed)
        self.apply(self._init_weights)

    def forward(self, x):
        return self.bn((self.conv(x) + self.conv1(x)) + x)

    def _init_weights(self, m):
        if isinstance(m, nn.Conv2d):
            trunc_normal_(m.weight, std=0.02)
            if m.bias is not None:
                nn.init.constant_(m.bias, 0)


class RepDW_Axias(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, ed, kernel_max=7, kernel=(1, 7)):
        super().__init__()
        self.kernel = kernel
        self.kernel_max = kernel_max
        padding = kernel_max // 2
        self.conv1 = nn.Conv2d(ed, ed, 1, 1, 0, groups=ed)
        if kernel == (1, kernel_max):
            self.conv = Conv2d_BN(ed, ed, (1, kernel_max), 1, (0, padding), groups=ed)
        else:
            self.conv = Conv2d_BN(ed, ed, (kernel_max, 1), 1, (padding, 0), groups=ed)
        self.dim = ed
        self.bn = nn.BatchNorm2d(ed)
        self.apply(self._init_weights)

    def forward(self, x):
        return self.bn((self.conv(x) + self.conv1(x)) + x)

    def _init_weights(self, m):
        if isinstance(m, nn.Conv2d):
            trunc_normal_(m.weight, std=0.02)
            if m.bias is not None:
                nn.init.constant_(m.bias, 0)


class Rep_Inception(nn.Module):
    """frequency ramp inception：通道按 split 分成 W 向 / H 向轴向 DW。"""

    def __init__(self, dim, kernel_max=7, ratio=0.5):
        super().__init__()
        gc = int(dim * ratio)
        self.dwconv_h = RepDW_Axias(gc, kernel_max=kernel_max, kernel=(1, kernel_max))
        self.dwconv_w = RepDW_Axias(gc, kernel_max=kernel_max, kernel=(kernel_max, 1))
        self.split = (dim - gc, gc)

    def forward(self, x):
        x_w, x_h = torch.split(x, self.split, dim=1)
        return torch.cat((self.dwconv_w(x_w), self.dwconv_h(x_h)), dim=1)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 四向交叉扫描（与官方逐行一致，纯 torch）
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
class CrossScan(torch.autograd.Function):
    @staticmethod
    def forward(ctx, x):
        B, C, H, W = x.shape
        ctx.shape = (B, C, H, W)
        xs = x.new_empty((B, 4, C, H * W))
        xs[:, 0] = x.flatten(2, 3)
        xs[:, 1] = x.transpose(dim0=2, dim1=3).flatten(2, 3)
        xs[:, 2:4] = torch.flip(xs[:, 0:2], dims=[-1])
        return xs

    @staticmethod
    def backward(ctx, ys):
        B, C, H, W = ctx.shape
        L = H * W
        ys = ys[:, 0:2] + ys[:, 2:4].flip(dims=[-1]).view(B, 2, -1, L)
        y = ys[:, 0] + ys[:, 1].view(B, -1, W, H).transpose(dim0=2, dim1=3).contiguous().view(B, -1, L)
        return y.view(B, -1, H, W)


class CrossMerge(torch.autograd.Function):
    @staticmethod
    def forward(ctx, ys):
        B, K, D, H, W = ys.shape
        ctx.shape = (H, W)
        ys = ys.view(B, K, D, -1)
        ys = ys[:, 0:2] + ys[:, 2:4].flip(dims=[-1]).view(B, 2, D, -1)
        y = ys[:, 0] + ys[:, 1].view(B, -1, W, H).transpose(dim0=2, dim1=3).contiguous().view(B, D, -1)
        return y

    @staticmethod
    def backward(ctx, x):
        H, W = ctx.shape
        B, C, L = x.shape
        xs = x.new_empty((B, 4, C, L))
        xs[:, 0] = x
        xs[:, 1] = x.view(B, C, H, W).transpose(dim0=2, dim1=3).flatten(2, 3)
        xs[:, 2:4] = torch.flip(xs[:, 0:2], dims=[-1])
        xs = xs.view(B, 4, C, H, W)
        return xs, None, None


class SelectiveScan(torch.autograd.Function):
    """官方 SelectiveScan 接口（TinyViM/VMamba 口径）。

    后端优先级：
      1. `selective_scan_cuda_oflex`——RSML-3 已编译验证的 VMamba v2 flexible
         kernel（与 STR-RepNet 生产同款；已对拍 torch 参考实现 max_abs<2e-5，
         语义 = S6 递推）；
      2. mamba-ssm `selective_scan_fn`（或 vendored Triton 版本）——按四方向
         K 维循环调用。

    接口：u (B, K*C, L)、delta (B, K*C, L)、A (K*C, N)、B/C (B, K, N, L)、
    D (K*C,)、delta_bias (K*C,)。
    """

    @staticmethod
    def forward(ctx, u, delta, A, B, C, D=None, delta_bias=None, delta_softplus=False, nrows=1):
        if u.stride(-1) != 1:
            u = u.contiguous()
        if delta.stride(-1) != 1:
            delta = delta.contiguous()
        if D is not None and D.stride(-1) != 1:
            D = D.contiguous()
        if B.stride(-1) != 1:
            B = B.contiguous()
        if C.stride(-1) != 1:
            C = C.contiguous()
        if B.dim() == 3:
            B = B.unsqueeze(dim=1)
            ctx.squeeze_B = True
        if C.dim() == 3:
            C = C.unsqueeze(dim=1)
            ctx.squeeze_C = True
        ctx.delta_softplus = delta_softplus

        if _oflex_cuda is not None:
            out, x, *rest = _oflex_cuda.fwd(u, delta, A, B, C, D, delta_bias,
                                            delta_softplus, nrows, True)
            ctx.save_for_backward(u, delta, A, B, C, D, delta_bias, x)
            ctx.use_oflex = True
            return out

        # ---- mamba-ssm 兜底：按方向循环 ----
        if _mamba_ssm_scan is None:
            raise RuntimeError("no selective scan kernel available "
                               "(selective_scan_cuda_oflex / mamba-ssm)")
        K = B.shape[1]
        Cdim = u.shape[1] // K
        assert u.shape[1] == K * Cdim, f"u channels {u.shape[1]} not divisible by K={K}"
        outs = []
        for k in range(K):
            uk = u[:, k * Cdim:(k + 1) * Cdim].permute(0, 2, 1).contiguous()
            dk = delta[:, k * Cdim:(k + 1) * Cdim].permute(0, 2, 1).contiguous()
            ak = A[k * Cdim:(k + 1) * Cdim].contiguous()
            bk = B[:, k].contiguous()
            ck = C[:, k].contiguous()
            dk_vec = D[k * Cdim:(k + 1) * Cdim].contiguous() if D is not None else None
            db = delta_bias[k * Cdim:(k + 1) * Cdim].contiguous() if delta_bias is not None else None
            yk = _mamba_ssm_scan(uk, dk, ak, bk, ck, dk_vec, z=None,
                                 delta_bias=db, delta_softplus=delta_softplus)
            outs.append(yk.permute(0, 2, 1).contiguous())
        ctx.use_oflex = False
        return torch.cat(outs, dim=1)

    @staticmethod
    def backward(ctx, dout, *args):
        if getattr(ctx, "use_oflex", False):
            u, delta, A, B, C, D, delta_bias, x = ctx.saved_tensors
            if dout.stride(-1) != 1:
                dout = dout.contiguous()
            du, ddelta, dA, dB, dC, dD, ddelta_bias, *rest = _oflex_cuda.bwd(
                u, delta, A, B, C, D, delta_bias, dout, x, ctx.delta_softplus, 1)
            dB = dB.squeeze(1) if getattr(ctx, "squeeze_B", False) else dB
            dC = dC.squeeze(1) if getattr(ctx, "squeeze_C", False) else dC
            return (du, ddelta, dA, dB, dC, dD, ddelta_bias, None, None)
        raise RuntimeError("SelectiveScan.backward should never be called directly "
                           "(autograd flows through selective_scan_fn)")


def cross_selective_scan(
        x=None, x_proj_weight=None, x_proj_bias=None,
        dt_projs_weight=None, dt_projs_bias=None,
        A_logs=None, Ds=None, out_norm=None,
        nrows=-1, delta_softplus=True, to_dtype=True, force_fp32=True):
    B, D, H, W = x.shape
    D, N = A_logs.shape
    K, D, R = dt_projs_weight.shape
    L = H * W

    xs = CrossScan.apply(x)

    x_dbl = torch.einsum("b k d l, k c d -> b k c l", xs, x_proj_weight)
    if x_proj_bias is not None:
        x_dbl = x_dbl + x_proj_bias.view(1, K, -1, 1)
    dts, Bs, Cs = torch.split(x_dbl, [R, N, N], dim=2)
    dts = torch.einsum("b k r l, k d r -> b k d l", dts, dt_projs_weight)
    xs = xs.view(B, -1, L)

    dts = dts.contiguous().view(B, -1, L)
    As = -torch.exp(A_logs.to(torch.float))     # (k * c, d_state)
    Bs = Bs.contiguous()
    Cs = Cs.contiguous()
    Ds = Ds.to(torch.float)                     # (K * c)
    delta_bias = dt_projs_bias.view(-1).to(torch.float)

    if force_fp32:
        xs = xs.to(torch.float)
        dts = dts.to(torch.float)
        Bs = Bs.to(torch.float)
        Cs = Cs.to(torch.float)

    ys = SelectiveScan.apply(xs, dts, As, Bs, Cs, Ds, delta_bias, delta_softplus, nrows)
    ys = ys.view(B, K, -1, H, W)

    y = CrossMerge.apply(ys)
    y = y.transpose(dim0=1, dim1=2).contiguous()   # (B, L, C)
    y = out_norm(y).view(B, H, W, -1)

    return (y.to(x.dtype) if to_dtype else y)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# SS2D（Laplace mixer 核心：低频池化 -> 四向 scan；高频 RepDW 保留）
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
class SS2D(nn.Module):
    def __init__(
            self,
            d_model=96,
            d_state=16,
            ssm_ratio=2.0,
            ssm_rank_ratio=2.0,
            dt_rank="auto",
            act_layer=nn.SiLU,
            d_conv=3,
            conv_bias=True,
            dropout=0.0,
            bias=False,
            dt_min=0.001,
            dt_max=0.1,
            dt_init="random",
            dt_scale=1.0,
            dt_init_floor=1e-4,
            simple_init=False,
            index=0,
            **kwargs,
    ):
        factory_kwargs = {"device": None, "dtype": None}
        super().__init__()
        d_expand = int(ssm_ratio * d_model)

        self.pool = nn.AvgPool2d(2 ** (3 - index))
        self.index = index

        # (low, high) 通道分配：frequency ramp inception（官方 split_list）
        split_list = [1 / 4, 1 / 2, 1 / 2, 3 / 4]
        d_inner = int(d_expand * split_list[index])
        self.local_conv = RepDW(d_expand - d_inner)
        self.split = (d_inner, d_expand - d_inner)

        self.dt_rank = math.ceil(d_model / 16) if dt_rank == "auto" else dt_rank
        self.d_state = math.ceil(d_model / 6) if d_state == "auto" else d_state
        self.d_conv = d_conv
        self.out_norm = nn.LayerNorm(d_inner)

        self.K = 4
        self.K2 = self.K

        self.in_proj = Conv2d_BN(d_model, d_expand, 1)
        self.act = act_layer()

        if self.d_conv > 1:
            self.conv2d = Rep_Inception(d_expand, 7)

        self.ssm_low_rank = False

        x_proj = [
            nn.Linear(d_inner, (self.dt_rank + self.d_state * 2), bias=False, **factory_kwargs)
            for _ in range(self.K)
        ]
        self.x_proj_weight = nn.Parameter(torch.stack([t.weight for t in x_proj], dim=0))
        del x_proj

        dt_projs = [
            self.dt_init(self.dt_rank, d_inner, dt_scale, dt_init, dt_min, dt_max, dt_init_floor, **factory_kwargs)
            for _ in range(self.K)
        ]
        self.dt_projs_weight = nn.Parameter(torch.stack([t.weight for t in dt_projs], dim=0))
        self.dt_projs_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.stack([t.bias for t in dt_projs], dim=0))
        del dt_projs

        self.A_logs = self.A_log_init(self.d_state, d_inner, copies=self.K2, merge=True)
        self.Ds = self.D_init(d_inner, copies=self.K2, merge=True)

        self.out_proj = Conv2d_BN(d_expand, d_model, 1)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout) if dropout > 0.0 else nn.Identity()

        if simple_init:
            self.Ds = nn.Parameter(torch.ones((self.K2 * d_inner)))
            self.A_logs = nn.Parameter(torch.randn((self.K2 * d_inner, self.d_state)))
            self.dt_projs_weight = nn.Parameter(torch.randn((self.K, d_inner, self.dt_rank)))
            self.dt_projs_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.randn((self.K, d_inner)))

    @staticmethod
    def dt_init(dt_rank, d_inner, dt_scale=1.0, dt_init="random",
                dt_min=0.001, dt_max=0.1, dt_init_floor=1e-4, **factory_kwargs):
        dt_proj = nn.Linear(dt_rank, d_inner, bias=True, **factory_kwargs)
        dt_init_std = dt_rank ** -0.5 * dt_scale
        if dt_init == "constant":
            nn.init.constant_(dt_proj.weight, dt_init_std)
        elif dt_init == "random":
            nn.init.uniform_(dt_proj.weight, -dt_init_std, dt_init_std)
        else:
            raise NotImplementedError
        dt = torch.exp(
            torch.rand(d_inner, **factory_kwargs) * (math.log(dt_max) - math.log(dt_min))
            + math.log(dt_min)
        ).clamp(min=dt_init_floor)
        inv_dt = dt + torch.log(-torch.expm1(-dt))
        with torch.no_grad():
            dt_proj.bias.copy_(inv_dt)
        return dt_proj

    @staticmethod
    def A_log_init(d_state, d_inner, copies=-1, device=None, merge=True):
        A = torch.arange(1, d_state + 1, dtype=torch.float32, device=device)
        A = A.unsqueeze(0).repeat(d_inner, 1).contiguous()      # (d, n)
        A_log = torch.log(A)
        if copies > 0:
            A_log = A_log.unsqueeze(0).repeat(copies, 1, 1)      # (r, d, n)
            if merge:
                A_log = A_log.flatten(0, 1)
        A_log = nn.Parameter(A_log)
        A_log._no_weight_decay = True
        return A_log

    @staticmethod
    def D_init(d_inner, copies=-1, device=None, merge=True):
        D = torch.ones(d_inner, device=device)
        if copies > 0:
            D = D.unsqueeze(0).repeat(copies, 1)
            if merge:
                D = D.flatten(0, 1)
        D = nn.Parameter(D)
        D._no_weight_decay = True
        return D

    def forward_core(self, x, nrows=-1, channel_first=False):
        nrows = 1
        if not channel_first:
            x = x.permute(0, 3, 1, 2).contiguous()
        if self.ssm_low_rank:
            x = self.in_rank(x)

        x_low, x_high = torch.split(x, self.split, dim=1)
        B, C, H, W = x.shape
        x_high = self.local_conv(x_high)
        if self.index < 3:
            x0 = x_low
            x_low = self.pool(x_low)
            res = x0 - F.interpolate(x_low, (H, W), mode='nearest')

        x_low = cross_selective_scan(
            x_low, self.x_proj_weight, None, self.dt_projs_weight, self.dt_projs_bias,
            self.A_logs, self.Ds, getattr(self, "out_norm", None),
            nrows=nrows, delta_softplus=True, force_fp32=self.training,
        )
        x_low = x_low.permute(0, 3, 1, 2)

        if self.index < 3:
            x_low = F.interpolate(x_low, scale_factor=2 ** (3 - self.index), mode='bilinear') + res
        x = torch.cat((x_low, x_high), dim=1)
        if self.ssm_low_rank:
            x = self.out_rank(x)
        return x

    def forward(self, x, **kwargs):
        x = self.in_proj(x)
        if self.d_conv > 1:
            x = self.act(self.conv2d(x))
        y = self.forward_core(x, channel_first=(self.d_conv > 1))
        out = self.dropout(self.out_proj(y))
        return out


class FFN(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, in_dim, mid_dim=None, out_dim=None, act_layer=nn.GELU, drop=0.0):
        super().__init__()
        out_dim = out_dim or in_dim
        mid_dim = mid_dim or in_dim
        self.fc1 = Conv2d_BN(in_dim, mid_dim, 1)
        self.fc2 = Conv2d_BN(mid_dim, out_dim, 1)
        self.act = act_layer()
        self.drop = nn.Dropout(drop)
        self.apply(self._init_weights)

    def _init_weights(self, m):
        if isinstance(m, nn.Conv2d):
            trunc_normal_(m.weight, std=0.02)
            if m.bias is not None:
                nn.init.constant_(m.bias, 0)

    def forward(self, x):
        x = self.fc1(x)
        x = self.act(x)
        x = self.fc2(x)
        x = self.drop(x)
        return x


class LocalBlock(nn.Module):
    """卷积局部块（stage 内非 TViM 块）。"""

    def __init__(self, dim, hidden_dim=64, drop_path=0.0, use_layer_scale=True):
        super().__init__()
        self.dwconv = RepDW(dim)
        self.mlp = FFN(dim, hidden_dim)
        self.drop_path = DropPath(drop_path) if drop_path > 0.0 else nn.Identity()
        self.use_layer_scale = use_layer_scale
        if use_layer_scale:
            self.layer_scale = nn.Parameter(
                torch.ones(dim).unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1), requires_grad=True)
        self.apply(self._init_weights)

    def _init_weights(self, m):
        if isinstance(m, nn.Conv2d):
            trunc_normal_(m.weight, std=0.02)
            if m.bias is not None:
                nn.init.constant_(m.bias, 0)

    def forward(self, x):
        inp = x
        x = self.dwconv(x)
        x = self.mlp(x)
        if self.use_layer_scale:
            x = inp + self.drop_path(self.layer_scale * x)
        else:
            x = inp + self.drop_path(x)
        return x


class TViMBlock(nn.Module):
    def __init__(
            self,
            hidden_dim: int = 0,
            drop_path: float = 0,
            norm_layer: Callable[..., nn.Module] = partial(nn.LayerNorm, eps=1e-6),
            ssm_d_state: int = 16,
            ssm_ratio=2.0,
            ssm_rank_ratio=2.0,
            ssm_dt_rank: Any = "auto",
            ssm_act_layer=nn.SiLU,
            ssm_conv: int = 3,
            ssm_conv_bias=True,
            ssm_drop_rate: float = 0,
            ssm_simple_init=False,
            mlp_ratio=4.0,
            mlp_act_layer=nn.GELU,
            mlp_drop_rate: float = 0.0,
            use_checkpoint: bool = False,
            index=0,
            **kwargs,
    ):
        super().__init__()
        self.ssm_branch = ssm_ratio > 0
        self.mlp_branch = mlp_ratio > 0
        self.use_checkpoint = use_checkpoint

        if self.ssm_branch:
            self.op = SS2D(
                d_model=hidden_dim,
                d_state=ssm_d_state,
                ssm_ratio=ssm_ratio,
                ssm_rank_ratio=ssm_rank_ratio,
                dt_rank=ssm_dt_rank,
                act_layer=ssm_act_layer,
                d_conv=ssm_conv,
                conv_bias=ssm_conv_bias,
                dropout=ssm_drop_rate,
                simple_init=ssm_simple_init,
                index=index,
            )

        self.drop_path = DropPath(drop_path)

        if self.mlp_branch:
            mlp_hidden_dim = int(hidden_dim * mlp_ratio)
            self.mlp = FFN(in_dim=hidden_dim, mid_dim=mlp_hidden_dim,
                           act_layer=mlp_act_layer, drop=mlp_drop_rate)

    def _forward(self, inp):
        x = inp
        if self.ssm_branch:
            x = inp + self.drop_path(self.op(inp))
        if self.mlp_branch:
            x = x + self.drop_path(self.mlp(x))
        return x

    def forward(self, inp):
        if self.use_checkpoint:
            return torch.utils.checkpoint.checkpoint(self._forward, inp)
        return self._forward(inp)

================================================================================
# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/model\layers\st_geodesic.py
================================================================================
"""ST-GEO：局部结构张量的 SPD(2)/双曲平面测地变化证据（探索线 Exp1 的数学核心）。

**参数自由**（parameter-free）算子：不引入可学习权重，只把「双时相局部结构张量」当作
SPD(2) 流形上的两个点，用仿射不变黎曼度量（affine-invariant metric, AIM）的**测地距离**
度量变化。全部闭式、可微、无特征值分解。

================================================================================
1. 为什么是「结构张量」而不是「特征向量」
================================================================================
Diag1/Run5 的实测把 SYSU 小目标瓶颈定性为**判别失败**而非证据缺失：未匹配小预测（FP）的
原始变化能量 change_abs = 0.9730 **高于**真实漏检 small GT 的 0.8626，也高于背景 0.6063。
⇒ 任何「幅度型」证据（|ΔI|、高频能量、边缘强度）都会优先放大已经在过 firing 的假变化。

辐射型伪变化（光照/阴影/大气/季节）几乎不改变**局部结构**（梯度方向与各向异性），
真正的地物变化（新建、拆除、地表类型改变）才会改变局部结构。
⇒ 正确的判别量不是「梯度有多大」，而是「局部结构怎么变」。

Di Zenzo 矢量结构张量（2×2 对称半正定，逐像素）：

    J_t(i) = Σ_c ( G_ρ * [ I_{c,x}²      I_{c,x}I_{c,y} ] )
                  (       [ I_{c,x}I_{c,y}  I_{c,y}²    ] )

I_{c,x} = ∂x(G_σ * I_c)；特征值 λ₁≥λ₂≥0 与主方向完整刻画该点的
「梯度能量 + 主方向 + 各向异性」。

================================================================================
2. 双角度坐标：J ↦ 单位圆盘（= H² 的 Poincaré 盘模型）
================================================================================
记 tr = Jxx+Jyy，Δ = √((Jxx−Jyy)² + 4Jxy²) = λ₁−λ₂。定义**复结构张量**

    z = ( (Jxx − Jyy) + 2i·Jxy ) / (Jxx + Jyy)   ∈ ℂ,   |z| = Δ/tr =: κ ∈ [0,1]

* 模 κ = 相干性/各向异性（0=各向同性，→1=理想阶跃边），幅角 = 2θ（主方向的双角度）。
  这正是数字图像处理里经典的「双角度朝向表示」，此处它同时是**双曲平面的坐标**。
* z 的**构造天然有界**（|z| ≤ 1），且对 J 的任意正标量缩放**严格不变**：
  J ↦ c²J ⇒ z ↦ z（精确，且是逐元素的比值，数值良态）。

**命题 1（det=1 切片 ≅ 曲率 −1/2 的双曲平面）**
{J ≻ 0, det J = 1} 在 AIM 下是常曲率 −1/2 的 H²。取 J = R(θ)diag(e^u, e^{−u})R(θ)ᵀ，
直接代入 ⟨V,W⟩_J = tr(J⁻¹VJ⁻¹W) 得拉回度量

    ds²_AIM = 2·du² + 8·sinh²u·dθ²                                  (◇)

⚠️ **必须记住的陷阱**：常见的 z = tanh(u)·e^{2iθ}（本模块 `shape_disk_coords` 的输出）
**不是** (◇) 的等距盘坐标——它诱导的度量为 4du² + 16tanh²u dθ²，只在 cosh u = 2 时才与
(◇) 成比例；直接对 z 套 Poincaré 盘距离公式会得到与真实 AIM 距离**系统性偏离**的结果
（实测最大偏差 1.25，随各向异性单调增大）。z 的正确用途是提供**尺度不变的 (κ, θ) 坐标**
（κ = |z| 各向异性、arg z = 2θ 双角度朝向），距离必须用 (◇) 的测地公式：

    cosh(d/√2) = cosh(u₁)cosh(u₂) − sinh(u₁)sinh(u₂)·cos(2Δθ)
               = 1 + 2W,   W = sinh²(Δu/2) + sinh(u₁)sinh(u₂)·sin²(Δθ)
    d          = √2·arccosh(1+2W) = √2·log1p( 2W + 2√(W(W+1)) )

*与 τ 写法的等价性*：z = tanh(u)e^{2iθ} ⇒ 2cosh(2·artanh|z|) = tr(Ĵ₁⁻¹Ĵ₂) = τ，
两种写法给出同一个数（T1 用 eigh 参考实现对拍验证）。

**为什么用 (★) 而不是 τ = tr(Ĵ₁⁻¹Ĵ₂) 的 arccosh 写法（数值纪律，必须披露）**：
τ 写法在 τ→2 处按 √ 变化，FP32 下「形状完全相同」的两点也会有 ~1e-3 的假距离；
更糟的是 Ĵ 的元素可达 O(√(λ₁/λ₂))，误差被各向异性比放大到 O(ε·R²)。
(★) 用**有界坐标** z（|z|≤1）且在恒等附近**线性**（artanh x ≈ x），
误差既不被 √ 放大也不被 R² 放大 ⇒ 纯辐射变化下 d_shape 在 FP32 中**恒为 0**（T2/T2c/T11 验证）。

**命题 2（辐射不变性，可数值证伪）**
  (i)  I_B = c·I_A（纯增益/对比度）⇒ J_B = c²J_A ⇒ z_B = z_A ⇒ **d_shape = 0（精确）**；
  (ii) I_B = I_A + d（纯偏置）⇒ J_B = J_A ⇒ **d_shape = 0 且 d_scale = 0（精确）**。
⇒ 形状分量对**任意仿射辐射变化**严格不变（这是「抗伪变化」的数学依据）。

**命题 3（尺度-形状正交分解，用于对拍）** 记 σ = √(det J)，则
    d²_AIM(J₁,J₂) = d²_shape(Ĵ₁,Ĵ₂) + 2·(log(σ₁/σ₂))²
（SPD(n) 在 AIM 下等距同构于 R × SL(n)/SO(n) 的乘积结构；T4 数值验证 <1e-4。）

================================================================================
3. 输出证据（每尺度 5 通道，全部闭式）
================================================================================
    [0] d_shape = √2·artanh|(z_A−z_B)/(1−z̄_A z_B)|   结构/方向变化（抗辐射伪变化）
    [1] d_str   = |log(tr_A/tr_B)|                    梯度能量变化（良态的尺度项）
    [2] κ_A     = |z_A| ∈[0,1]                        各向异性（边缘/角点指示）
    [3] κ_B     = |z_B|
    [4] ori     = |sin(θ_A−θ_B)|·min(κ_A,κ_B)         朝向变化（相干性加权，抗噪）
（另提供 det 版尺度 `det_scale_distance`，仅用于命题 3 的对拍。）

**注意 d_str 与 d_shape 的分工**：d_str 对真实变化（新增/移除结构）敏感但**不抗辐射**；
d_shape 抗辐射但只对结构变化敏感。两者一起交给网络融合 ⇒ 单变量、不预设哪个更重要。

================================================================================
4. 与 Run8「特征空间 Lorentz 距离」的本质区别（novelty 边界）
================================================================================
| | Run8 CAACP-HQKV / HGSL | 本算子 ST-GEO |
|---|---|---|
| 流形上的点 | **网络特征** x ∈ R^C（人为映射到 Lorentz 双曲面） | **图像结构张量** J ∈ SPD(2)（天然的 SPD 点） |
| 度量来源 | 人造非恒定半径 r = r0 + a·q（r0,a 冻结） | J 自身的 tr/Δ（无自由参数） |
| 退化风险 | 固定半径 ⇒ 与 1−cos 同序（靠可变半径逃逸） | 不存在：J 是梯度的二阶统计，与特征余弦无关 |
| 抗辐射伪变化 | 无 | **命题 2：精确不变**（可数值证伪） |
| 分辨率 | 1/8、1/16、1/32（1–15px 已是 sub-token） | **全分辨率 256²** |
| 参数 | 304,131 | **0** |

与 2024–2026 已有双曲 CD 工作（Hyperboloid-Embedded Siamese Network, JSTARS 2024；
Deep Change Monitoring, CVPR 2025）的区别：它们把 CNN/ViT **特征嵌入**双曲空间再算距离；
本算子不做任何嵌入学习，作用在**图像自身的二阶结构统计**上，闭式、参数自由、逐像素、
全分辨率，并带一条可证伪的不变性定理。
"""
import math

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

__all__ = [
    "TR_FLOOR", "D_SHAPE_FLOOR",
    "gaussian_kernel1d", "structure_tensor", "shape_disk_coords",
    "poincare_shape_distance", "tr_based_shape_distance", "trace_scale_distance",
    "det_scale_distance", "orientation_change", "coherence",
    "spd_geodesic_evidence", "STGeoEvidence",
]

TR_FLOOR = 1e-10          # tr(J) ≤ TR_FLOOR 的 cell 视为无数值意义的平坦 cell
D_SHAPE_FLOOR = 1e-3      # 形状距离死区：d < 1e-3 记 0（量程 0–8 的 0.0125%）
KAPPA_MAX = 1.0 - 1e-3    # κ 上限：κ→1 处 det=JxxJyy−Jxy² 出现灾难性抵消，且
                          # artanh′(κ)=1/(1−κ²) 把 κ 的 FP32 误差放大到 1e-3 量级
                          # （实测纯对比度变化下产生 2.6e-3 假距离）。
                          # 截断对应各向异性比 λ₁/λ₂ ≲ 2000，都是「极锐利边缘」，
                          # 该处距离饱和是可接受且必须披露的行为。
_EPS = 1e-12


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. 结构张量
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def gaussian_kernel1d(sigma, truncate=3.0, dtype=torch.float32, device=None):
    """归一化 1D 高斯核（确定性、无 RNG）。"""
    sigma = float(sigma)
    if sigma <= 0:
        raise ValueError(f"sigma must be > 0, got {sigma}")
    r = int(max(1, math.floor(truncate * sigma + 0.5)))
    x = torch.arange(-r, r + 1, dtype=dtype, device=device)
    k = torch.exp(-(x * x) / (2.0 * sigma * sigma))
    return k / k.sum()


def _gaussian_blur(x, sigma, truncate=3.0):
    """可分离高斯模糊（replicate padding，通道独立）。x: (B,C,H,W)。"""
    B, C, H, W = x.shape
    k = gaussian_kernel1d(sigma, truncate=truncate, dtype=x.dtype, device=x.device)
    r = (k.numel() - 1) // 2
    kx = k.view(1, 1, 1, -1).expand(C, 1, 1, k.numel())
    ky = k.view(1, 1, -1, 1).expand(C, 1, k.numel(), 1)
    x = F.conv2d(F.pad(x, (r, r, 0, 0), mode="replicate"), kx, groups=C)
    x = F.conv2d(F.pad(x, (0, 0, r, r), mode="replicate"), ky, groups=C)
    return x


def _central_diff(x, dim):
    """中心差分（replicate 边界），与 x 同尺寸。dim=3 → ∂x，dim=4 → ∂y。"""
    if dim == 3:
        xp = F.pad(x, (1, 0, 0, 0), mode="replicate")[..., :, :-1]
        xm = F.pad(x, (0, 1, 0, 0), mode="replicate")[..., :, 1:]
    else:
        xp = F.pad(x, (0, 0, 1, 0), mode="replicate")[..., :-1, :]
        xm = F.pad(x, (0, 0, 0, 1), mode="replicate")[..., 1:, :]
    return 0.5 * (xp - xm)


def structure_tensor(img, sigma_d=1.0, sigma_i=None, truncate=3.0):
    """Di Zenzo 矢量结构张量。img: (B,C,H,W)（同一时相的 C 个通道）。

    返回 (Jxx, Jxy, Jyy)，各 (B,1,H,W)、FP32、逐像素半正定。
    sigma_i 默认 = 2·sigma_d（微分尺度 σ / 积分尺度 ρ 的标准取法）。
    """
    if sigma_i is None:
        sigma_i = 2.0 * sigma_d
    s = _gaussian_blur(img.float(), sigma_d, truncate=truncate)
    ix = _central_diff(s, 3)
    iy = _central_diff(s, 4)
    jxx = _gaussian_blur((ix * ix).sum(dim=1, keepdim=True), sigma_i, truncate=truncate)
    jxy = _gaussian_blur((ix * iy).sum(dim=1, keepdim=True), sigma_i, truncate=truncate)
    jyy = _gaussian_blur((iy * iy).sum(dim=1, keepdim=True), sigma_i, truncate=truncate)
    return jxx, jxy, jyy


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. 双角度（Poincaré 盘）坐标与闭式测地距离
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def shape_disk_coords(jxx, jxy, jyy, tr_floor=TR_FLOOR):
    """(Jxx,Jxy,Jyy) → (zx, zy, kappa, valid)。

    z = ((Jxx−Jyy) + 2i·Jxy) / (Jxx+Jyy)，|z| = κ ∈ [0,1]。
    对任意 c>0：J ↦ c²J ⇒ z 不变（**精确**，逐元素比值）。tr ≤ tr_floor 的平坦 cell
    置 valid=0、z=(0,0)（形状无定义；此时 ±∞ 的朝向不可能稳定估计）。
    """
    tr = (jxx + jyy)
    safe = tr.clamp_min(tr_floor)
    valid = (tr > tr_floor).float()
    zx = (jxx - jyy) / safe
    zy = (2.0 * jxy) / safe
    kappa_raw = torch.sqrt((zx * zx + zy * zy).clamp(0.0, 1.0))
    # κ 截断（并同步缩放 z，保持 |z| == κ 的自洽性）
    scale = (KAPPA_MAX / kappa_raw.clamp_min(_EPS)).clamp(max=1.0)
    zx = zx * scale
    zy = zy * scale
    kappa = kappa_raw * scale
    zx = torch.where(valid > 0.5, zx, torch.zeros_like(zx))
    zy = torch.where(valid > 0.5, zy, torch.zeros_like(zy))
    kappa = torch.where(valid > 0.5, kappa, torch.zeros_like(kappa))
    return zx, zy, kappa, valid


def poincare_shape_distance(zx1, zy1, k1, zx2, zy2, k2, d0=D_SHAPE_FLOOR):
    """AIM 测地距离（闭式、无条件数放大）。

    **推导（必须写进论文）**：取 J = R(θ)diag(e^u, e^{−u})R(θ)ᵀ（det=1 切片），
    直接代入仿射不变度量 ⟨V,W⟩_J = tr(J⁻¹VJ⁻¹W) 可得拉回度量

        ds²_AIM = 2·du² + 8·sinh²u·dθ²                                (◇)

    即该切片是**曲率 −1/2 的双曲平面**（H²），u 为测地半径方向、2θ 为双角度方向。
    （注意：常见的 z = tanh(u)e^{2iθ} 并不是这个度量的等距盘坐标——那会得到
    4du²+16tanh²u dθ²，只在 u 取特殊值时才与 (◇) 成比例，T1 会把它判 FAIL。）

    (◇) 的常曲率 −1/2 测地距离满足
        cosh(d/√2) = cosh(u₁)cosh(u₂) − sinh(u₁)sinh(u₂)·cos(2Δθ)
    用 cosh(u₁−u₂) = cosh u₁cosh u₂ − sinh u₁sinh u₂ 与 1−cos2Δθ = 2sin²Δθ 改写：

        cosh(d/√2) = 1 + 2W,   W = sinh²(Δu/2) + sinh(u₁)sinh(u₂)·sin²(Δθ)  ≥ 0
        d = √2·arccosh(1+2W) = √2·log1p( 2W + 2√(W(W+1)) )

    **数值纪律（本实现的关键）**：W 是**平方量之和**，不做「两个大数相减」，
    故在恒等附近 d ≈ 2√2·√W 对扰动是**线性**的（τ = tr(Ĵ₁⁻¹Ĵ₂) 的 arccosh 写法在此处
    按 √ 放大、且误差随各向异性比 R² 增长，实测纯对比度变化下有 ~1e-3 假距离）。
    逐项用恒等式避免抵消：
        t   = (κ₁−κ₂)/(1−κ₁κ₂)                   （artanh 差角公式）
        sinh²(Δu/2) = t²/(2√(1−t²)(1+√(1−t²)))   （有理化，t→0 时 ≈ t²/4）
        sinh(u₁)sinh(u₂) = κ₁κ₂/√((1−κ₁²)(1−κ₂²))
        sin²(Δθ) = (1 − cosΔφ)/2,  cosΔφ = (z₁·z₂)/(κ₁κ₂)
    d < d0（默认 1e-3，量程 0–8 的 0.0125%）记 0：纯辐射变化下形状通道**恒为 0**。
    """
    k1c = k1.clamp(0.0, 1.0 - 1e-6)
    k2c = k2.clamp(0.0, 1.0 - 1e-6)
    t = ((k1c - k2c) / (1.0 - k1c * k2c).clamp_min(1e-6)).clamp(-1.0 + 1e-6, 1.0 - 1e-6)
    one_minus_t2 = (1.0 - t * t).clamp_min(1e-12)
    sq = one_minus_t2.sqrt()
    term_u = t * t / (2.0 * sq * (1.0 + sq))                    # sinh²(Δu/2)
    denom_k = ((1.0 - k1c * k1c).clamp_min(1e-12)
               * (1.0 - k2c * k2c).clamp_min(1e-12)).sqrt()
    # 朝向项（双角度）。**必须用有理化写法**：直接算 sinh u₁sinh u₂·(1−cosΔφ)/2 在
    # Δφ→0 时被灾难性抵消吃掉（cos ≈ 1−6e-8 已落在 FP32 ulp 内），实测假距离 2.6e-3。
    #   sin²(Δφ/2) = (1−cosΔφ)/2 = s²/(2(1+√(1−s²))),  s² = sin²Δφ = cross²/(κ₁κ₂)²
    #   1+√(1−s²) = 1+|cosΔφ| = 1+|dot|/(κ₁κ₂)
    # ⇒ sinh u₁sinh u₂·sin²(Δθ) = cross²/(2·√((1−κ₁²)(1−κ₂²))·(κ₁κ₂+|dot|))
    dot = zx1 * zx2 + zy1 * zy2
    cross = zy1 * zx2 - zx1 * zy2
    kk = (k1c * k2c).clamp_min(1e-12)
    cos_dphi = (dot / kk).clamp(-1.0, 1.0)
    sin2_dphi = (cross * cross / (kk * kk)).clamp(0.0, 1.0)       # sin²Δφ（无相减）
    sin2_dtheta = torch.where(
        cos_dphi >= 0.0,
        sin2_dphi / (2.0 * (1.0 + cos_dphi).clamp_min(1e-12)),   # Δφ→0：防抵消分支
        0.5 * (1.0 - cos_dphi))                                   # Δφ→π：良态分支
    prod_sinh = kk / denom_k                                      # sinh u₁ sinh u₂
    term_th = prod_sinh * sin2_dtheta.clamp(0.0, 1.0)
    W = (term_u + term_th).clamp_min(0.0)
    d = math.sqrt(2.0) * torch.log1p(2.0 * W + 2.0 * torch.sqrt(W * (W + 1.0)))
    return torch.where(d < d0, torch.zeros_like(d), d)


def tr_based_shape_distance(a1, b1, c1, a2, b2, c2):
    """**参考实现**（只用于测试对拍）：τ = tr(Ĵ₁⁻¹Ĵ₂)，d = √2·arccosh(τ/2)。

    数学上与 `poincare_shape_distance` 恒等，但在 τ→2 与强各向异性下数值病态，
    故不作为生产实现（T1 用它与 eigh 参考双重对拍生产实现）。"""
    tau = c1 * a2 + a1 * c2 - 2.0 * b1 * b2
    u = (0.5 * tau - 1.0).clamp_min(0.0)
    return math.sqrt(2.0) * torch.log1p(u + torch.sqrt(u * (u + 2.0)))


def trace_scale_distance(jxx1, jxy1, jyy1, jxx2, jxy2, jyy2, log0=1e-6):
    """尺度分量 d_str = |log(tr_A / tr_B)|（良态；对辐射变化敏感，不抗辐射）。"""
    t1 = (jxx1 + jyy1).clamp_min(_EPS)
    t2 = (jxx2 + jyy2).clamp_min(_EPS)
    lr = (t1.log() - t2.log()).abs()
    return torch.where(lr < log0, torch.zeros_like(lr), lr)


def det_scale_distance(jxx1, jxy1, jyy1, jxx2, jxy2, jyy2):
    """命题 3 的尺度项 √2·|log(σ_A/σ_B)|，σ = √(det J)。**仅用于对拍/证明**：
    各向异性 κ→1 时 det 的相对误差会放大，故生产证据用 trace 版尺度。"""
    d1 = (jxx1 * jyy1 - jxy1 * jxy1).clamp_min(0.0).sqrt().clamp_min(_EPS)
    d2 = (jxx2 * jyy2 - jxy2 * jxy2).clamp_min(0.0).sqrt().clamp_min(_EPS)
    return math.sqrt(2.0) * (d1.log() - d2.log()).abs()


def coherence(jxx, jxy, jyy):
    """各向异性 κ = (λ₁−λ₂)/(λ₁+λ₂) = |z| ∈ [0,1]。"""
    return shape_disk_coords(jxx, jxy, jyy)[2]


def orientation_change(zx1, zy1, zx2, zy2, k1, k2):
    """朝向变化（相干性加权）：|sin(θ_A−θ_B)|·min(κ_A,κ_B)。

    sin(θ_A−θ_B) 由双角度坐标给出：sinΔθ = (y₁x₂ − x₁y₂)/(κ₁κ₂)（κ>0 时）。
    权重 min(κ) 抑制各向同性（朝向不可靠）处的噪声。
    """
    cross = (zy1 * zx2 - zx1 * zy2)
    denom = (k1 * k2).clamp_min(_EPS)
    sin_dtheta = (cross / denom).abs().clamp(0.0, 1.0)
    return sin_dtheta * torch.minimum(k1, k2)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. 证据图
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def spd_geodesic_evidence(pre, post, sigma_d=1.0, sigma_i=None, tr_floor=TR_FLOOR):
    """双时相图像 → 每尺度 5 通道几何变化证据（全分辨率）。

    pre/post: (B,C,H,W)。返回 (evidence (B,5,H,W), parts dict)
    parts 键：d_shape / d_str / kappa_a / kappa_b / ori / valid_a / valid_b / valid。
    """
    ja = structure_tensor(pre, sigma_d=sigma_d, sigma_i=sigma_i)
    jb = structure_tensor(post, sigma_d=sigma_d, sigma_i=sigma_i)
    za_x, za_y, ka, va = shape_disk_coords(*ja, tr_floor=tr_floor)
    zb_x, zb_y, kb, vb = shape_disk_coords(*jb, tr_floor=tr_floor)

    d_shape = poincare_shape_distance(za_x, za_y, ka, zb_x, zb_y, kb)
    valid = va * vb
    d_shape = d_shape * valid                    # 任一时相平坦 ⇒ 形状无定义 ⇒ 0
    d_str = trace_scale_distance(*ja, *jb)
    ori = orientation_change(za_x, za_y, zb_x, zb_y, ka, kb)
    ev = torch.cat([d_shape, d_str, ka, kb, ori], dim=1)
    parts = {"d_shape": d_shape, "d_str": d_str, "kappa_a": ka, "kappa_b": kb,
             "ori": ori, "valid": valid, "valid_a": va, "valid_b": vb}
    return ev, parts


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. 可部署模块（γ 零初始化门；构造不消耗全局 RNG）
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
STGEO_FORM = "stgeo_spd2_v1"
STGEO_FORM_V2 = "stgeo_spd2_v2"
STGEO_SEED = 23


POOL_MODES = ("max_shape", "topk_mean")


def _cell_topk_mean(x, size, k=4):
    """(B,C,H,W) → (B,C,size,size)：每个 cell 取最大 k 个值的均值。

    k=4 即取每 4×4 cell 的**上四分位均值**：2×2 以上的真实结构几乎无损（权重 4/16），
    而单点噪声的贡献从 max 的 1.0 降到 0.25 ⇒ 抑制碎片化（v1 在 SYSU 上 +49% 的直接对症）。
    """
    B, C, H, W = x.shape
    step = H // size
    if step <= 1 or H % size != 0 or W % size != 0:
        return F.adaptive_avg_pool2d(x, size)
    v = (x.view(B, C, size, step, size, step)
          .permute(0, 1, 2, 4, 3, 5)
          .reshape(B, C, size, size, step * step))
    kk = max(1, min(int(k), step * step))
    return v.topk(kk, dim=-1).values.mean(dim=-1)


def _pool_evidence(ev, size, mode="max_shape"):
    """全分辨率证据 → 1/4（64²）。

    mode 取值：
      · `max_shape`（v1）：形状/朝向通道取 4×4 cell 内**最大值**（保小结构但放噪声）；
      · `topk_mean`（v2）：形状/朝向通道取 cell 内 **top-4 均值**（保 ≥2×2 结构、压单点噪声）。
    两模式下尺度/κ 通道都用平均池化（更平滑稳定）。
    """
    B, K, H, W = ev.shape
    out = F.adaptive_avg_pool2d(ev, size)
    if mode not in POOL_MODES:
        raise ValueError(f"unknown pool mode {mode}")
    step = int(round(H / size))
    if step > 1 and H % size == 0 and W % size == 0:
        take = [0, 4] if K >= 5 else [0]                 # d_shape, ori
        if mode == "max_shape":
            red = F.max_pool2d(ev[:, take], kernel_size=step, stride=step)
        else:
            red = _cell_topk_mean(ev[:, take], size, k=4)
        out = torch.cat([red, out[:, [i for i in range(K) if i not in take]]], dim=1)
    return out


class STGeoEvidence(nn.Module):
    """SPD(2)/H² 结构张量测地证据 → 1/4 尺度增量（**推理期真实算子**，非训练期专用）。

    `form`：
      · `inject`：γ 零初始化门控，增量加到 DCR 最小尺度输入（feats[0]，1/4），
        与 Run8 的 `dcr1` 注入点一致 ⇒ 可直接用已跑完的 `CTRL_R8` 作同期对照
        （epoch-0 逐位恒等由 dry-run 断言）。
      · `none`  ：关闭（等价于不加本模块）。

    参数：3 尺度 × 5 通道 → 1×1 → mid → DW3×3 → 1×1 → out_dim，加 1 个 γ。
    """
    FORMS = ("none", "inject")

    def __init__(self, in_ch=3, sigmas=(1.0, 2.0, 4.0), out_dim=192, mid=32,
                 form="inject", tr_floor=TR_FLOOR, pool_mode="max_shape",
                 xscale_gate=False, init_seed=STGEO_SEED):
        super().__init__()
        assert form in self.FORMS, form
        assert pool_mode in POOL_MODES, pool_mode
        self.in_ch = int(in_ch)
        self.sigmas = tuple(float(s) for s in sigmas)
        self.out_dim = int(out_dim)
        self.mid = int(mid)
        self.form = form
        self.tr_floor = float(tr_floor)
        self.pool_mode = pool_mode
        self.xscale_gate = bool(xscale_gate)
        self.k_per_scale = 5
        # v2 的跨尺度一致性门：额外 1 个通道 min_σ d_shape
        self.in_ch_ev = self.k_per_scale * len(self.sigmas) + (1 if self.xscale_gate else 0)
        self.form_tag = (STGEO_FORM_V2 if (self.xscale_gate or
                                          pool_mode == "topk_mean") else STGEO_FORM) \
            if form != "none" else None

        with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=[]):   # 不推进全局 RNG
            torch.manual_seed(int(init_seed))
            self.proj = nn.Conv2d(self.in_ch_ev, self.mid, 1,
                                  bias=False)
            self.dw = nn.Conv2d(self.mid, self.mid, 3, 1, 1, groups=self.mid, bias=False)
            self.bn = nn.BatchNorm2d(self.mid)
            self.act = nn.SiLU()
            self.out = nn.Conv2d(self.mid, self.out_dim, 1, bias=False)
            self.apply(self._init_weights)
        # γ 零初始化：**必须在 apply 之后**（HFAE 首版踩过被 init 覆盖的坑）
        self.gamma = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(1))
        self._diag = {}

    @staticmethod
    def _init_weights(m):
        if isinstance(m, nn.Conv2d):
            nn.init.trunc_normal_(m.weight, std=0.02)
            if m.bias is not None:
                nn.init.constant_(m.bias, 0)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(self, pre, post, ref_hw):
        """pre/post: (B,3,H,W)；ref_hw: 目标尺度 (h,w)（=1/4 的 64×64）。"""
        if self.form == "none":
            return None, {}
        evs, diags = [], {}
        for s in self.sigmas:
            ev, parts = spd_geodesic_evidence(pre, post, sigma_d=s,
                                              tr_floor=self.tr_floor)
            ev = _pool_evidence(ev, ref_hw[0], mode=self.pool_mode)
            evs.append(ev)
            with torch.no_grad():
                ds = parts["d_shape"]
                diags[f"sigma{s:g}"] = {
                    "d_shape_mean": float(ds.mean()),
                    "d_shape_max": float(ds.amax()),
                    "d_str_mean": float(parts["d_str"].mean()),
                    "kappa_mean": float(parts["kappa_a"].mean()),
                    "valid_frac": float(parts["valid"].mean()),
                }
        if self.xscale_gate:
            # 跨尺度一致性：三个尺度的 d_shape（通道 0）逐像素取最小
            gate = torch.stack([e[:, 0:1] for e in evs], dim=0).amin(dim=0)
            evs.append(gate)
        h = torch.cat(evs, dim=1)
        z = self.act(self.bn(self.dw(self.proj(h))))
        z = self.out(z)
        self._diag = diags
        return self.gamma * z, diags

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def diag(self):
        return dict(self._diag)

    def param_report(self):
        n = sum(p.numel() for p in self.parameters())
        return {"form": self.form_tag, "sigmas": list(self.sigmas), "mid": self.mid,
                "out_dim": self.out_dim, "k_per_scale": self.k_per_scale,
                "pool_mode": self.pool_mode, "xscale_gate": self.xscale_gate,
                "in_ch_ev": self.in_ch_ev,
                "params": int(n),
                "gamma_init_zero": bool(float(self.gamma.detach()) == 0.0),
                "gamma": float(self.gamma.detach())}

================================================================================
# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/model\layers\stgeo_reliable.py
================================================================================
"""STGEO-REL（方向 A）：**可靠性感知 + 尺度选择性的几何修正**。

## 动机（来自 Exp1 三臂的实测诊断，不是猜测）

| 观测 | 含义 |
|---|---|
| v1（直接注入）SYSU Precision **−2.04pp**、未匹配 <256px **+49%** | 几何修正"无差别地"作用于小目标/背景/大目标 |
| v2（鲁棒池化 + 跨尺度门）碎片 −28%、Precision 恢复，但 large pooled R **−0.34pp** | 用"喂通道"的方式让网络自己学选择性，仍不够精细 |
| B（监督）SYSU Recall **+1.62pp** 但 Precision **−1.48pp** | 同上：定向能力有，区域选择性不足 |

⇒ 问题不是"几何证据无效"，而是**缺少"这条结构变化值不值得改表征"的判据**，
以及**修正会同时动到大目标的低频响应**。本模块就补这两件事。

## 三个组件（全部参数自由，除 1 个零初始化 γ）

**(1) 可靠性 r（参数自由，无阈值）**
```
r_support = box_mean_{5×5}(s) / (s + box_mean_{5×5}(s))      ∈ [0,1)   # 中心-邻域一致性
r_xscale  = min_σ s_σ / (max_σ s_σ + ε)                      ∈ [0,1]    # 跨尺度一致性
r = r_support · r_xscale
```
两者都是**非负量的比值**，因而无阈值、无超参。语义：真实小目标的结构变化在邻域内
有支撑、在多个尺度上同现；孤立纹理响应两点都不满足。
**注意（对先前分析的修正）**：这里**不**要求"双时相都有相同边缘"——新增目标本就
只在一侧有结构；r 只度量"该结构变化是否在空间与尺度上被支持"。

**(2) 局部零均值（detail-only）修正**
```
Δ = γ · ( r ⊙ z − AvgPool_{9×9}( r ⊙ z ) )
```
减去局部均值 ⇒ 修正的**低频（DC）分量为 0**，因此它只能锐化局部结构，
**不能抬高/压低大目标所依赖的整体激活水平**。这是对 v2「大目标召回受损」的直接对策。

**(3) 同式欧氏对照（`geom="euc"`，用于回答"负曲率是否必要"）**
在同一 (κ, θ) 特征上把双曲距离换成**切空间欧氏距离**：
```
w = artanh(κ)·e^{iφ}  （过原点的切空间坐标），d_euc = √2·|w_A − w_B|
```
√2 使两者在恒等附近同尺度 ⇒ 这是**严格的度量-only 对照**（同一输入、同一池化、同一门）。
若 `hyp ≈ euc`，则只能声称"局部结构统计有用"，**不得**声称负曲率必要。

## 部署

`inject` 形态的变体：推理期真实算子（保留在部署图里），**不得**声称零推理代价；
新增 FLOPs 只有几个 5×5/9×9 平均池化（在 64² 上），量级可忽略但非零。
"""
import math

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

from model.layers.st_geodesic import (
    TR_FLOOR,
    _pool_evidence,
    poincare_shape_distance,
    shape_disk_coords,
    spd_geodesic_evidence,
    structure_tensor,
)

STGEO_REL_FORM = "stgeo_rel_v1"
STGEO_REL_SEED = 31
GEOMS = ("hyperbolic", "euclidean")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. 同式欧氏对照
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def tangent_coords(zx, zy, kappa, eps=1e-8):
    """盘坐标 → 过原点的切空间坐标 w = artanh(κ)·e^{iφ}。"""
    k = kappa.clamp(min=eps, max=1.0 - 1e-6)
    r = torch.atanh(k)                                  # 测地半径
    inv = 1.0 / k
    return zx * inv * r, zy * inv * r


def tangent_euclidean_distance(zx1, zy1, k1, zx2, zy2, k2):
    """√2·|w_A − w_B|：与双曲距离在恒等附近同尺度，只换度量。"""
    ax, ay = tangent_coords(zx1, zy1, k1)
    bx, by = tangent_coords(zx2, zy2, k2)
    d2 = ((ax - bx) ** 2 + (ay - by) ** 2).clamp_min(0.0)
    return math.sqrt(2.0) * torch.sqrt(d2)


def _distance(zx1, zy1, k1, zx2, zy2, k2, geom="hyperbolic"):
    if geom == "hyperbolic":
        return poincare_shape_distance(zx1, zy1, k1, zx2, zy2, k2)
    return tangent_euclidean_distance(zx1, zy1, k1, zx2, zy2, k2)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. 可靠性（参数自由）
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def reliability_maps(shape_scores, eps=1e-8):
    """shape_scores: list（每尺度一张 (B,1,H,W) 全分辨率 d_shape）。

    返回 (r, r_support, r_xscale)，均 (B,1,H,W) ∈ [0,1]。
    """
    shape_scores = [x.clamp_min(0.0) for x in shape_scores]
    s0 = shape_scores[0]
    m = F.avg_pool2d(s0, 5, 1, 2, count_include_pad=False)   # 中心-邻域均值
    r_support = m / (s0 + m + eps)                       # ∈ [0,1)
    st = torch.stack(shape_scores, dim=0)
    r_xscale = st.amin(dim=0) / (st.amax(dim=0) + eps)   # ∈ [0,1]
    r = (r_support * r_xscale).clamp(0.0, 1.0)
    return r, r_support.clamp(0.0, 1.0), r_xscale


def local_zero_mean(x, k=9):
    """减去 k×k 局部均值 ⇒ 修正的 DC 分量为 0（保护大目标的低频响应）。

    `count_include_pad=False`：边界窗口只用有效像素求均值，避免零填充带来的边界偏置。
    """
    return x - F.avg_pool2d(x, k, 1, k // 2, count_include_pad=False)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. 模块
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
class STGeoReliable(nn.Module):
    """可靠性感知 + 尺度选择性的几何修正（推理期真实算子）。"""

    def __init__(self, in_ch=3, sigmas=(1.0, 2.0, 4.0), mid=32, out_dim=192,
                 geom="hyperbolic", detail_only=True, local_k=9,
                 tr_floor=TR_FLOOR, pool_mode="topk_mean", init_seed=STGEO_REL_SEED):
        super().__init__()
        assert geom in GEOMS, geom
        self.sigmas = tuple(float(s) for s in sigmas)
        self.geom = geom
        self.detail_only = bool(detail_only)
        self.local_k = int(local_k)
        self.tr_floor = float(tr_floor)
        self.pool_mode = pool_mode
        self.k_per_scale = 5                              # d_shape,d_str,κa,κb,ori
        self.in_ch_ev = self.k_per_scale * len(self.sigmas) + 1   # + 可靠性通道
        self.out_dim = int(out_dim)
        self.mid = int(mid)
        self.form_tag = STGEO_REL_FORM

        with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=[]):
            torch.manual_seed(int(init_seed))
            self.proj = nn.Conv2d(self.in_ch_ev, self.mid, 1, bias=False)
            self.dw = nn.Conv2d(self.mid, self.mid, 3, 1, 1, groups=self.mid, bias=False)
            self.bn = nn.BatchNorm2d(self.mid)
            self.act = nn.SiLU()
            self.out = nn.Conv2d(self.mid, self.out_dim, 1, bias=False)
            self.apply(self._init_weights)
        self.gamma = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(1))          # 零初始化门
        self._diag = {}

    @staticmethod
    def _init_weights(m):
        if isinstance(m, nn.Conv2d):
            nn.init.trunc_normal_(m.weight, std=0.02)
            if m.bias is not None:
                nn.init.constant_(m.bias, 0)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(self, pre, post, ref_hw):
        evs, shapes, diags = [], [], {}
        for s in self.sigmas:
            ev, parts = spd_geodesic_evidence(pre, post, sigma_d=s,
                                              tr_floor=self.tr_floor)
            # 用 geom 开关覆盖 d_shape 通道（同式欧氏对照）
            if self.geom != "hyperbolic":
                jj = structure_tensor(pre, sigma_d=s)
                jk = structure_tensor(post, sigma_d=s)
                za, zb = shape_disk_coords(*jj, tr_floor=self.tr_floor), \
                    shape_disk_coords(*jk, tr_floor=self.tr_floor)
                d_alt = _distance(za[0], za[1], za[2], zb[0], zb[1], zb[2],
                                  geom=self.geom) * (za[3] * zb[3])
                ev = torch.cat([d_alt, ev[:, 1:]], dim=1)
                parts = dict(parts, d_shape=d_alt)
            evs.append(_pool_evidence(ev, ref_hw[0], mode=self.pool_mode))
            shapes.append(parts["d_shape"])
            with torch.no_grad():
                diags[f"sigma{s:g}"] = {
                    "d_shape_mean": float(parts["d_shape"].mean()),
                    "d_shape_max": float(parts["d_shape"].amax()),
                    "kappa_mean": float(parts["kappa_a"].mean()),
                    "valid_frac": float(parts["valid"].mean()),
                }
        r, r_sup, r_xs = reliability_maps(shapes)
        r_pool = F.adaptive_avg_pool2d(r, ref_hw[0])
        h = torch.cat(evs + [r_pool], dim=1)
        z = self.act(self.bn(self.dw(self.proj(h))))
        z = self.out(z)
        w = r_pool * z                                     # 软选择（不是硬性排除）
        if self.detail_only:
            w = local_zero_mean(w, k=self.local_k)         # 局部零均值 ⇒ 不动的 DC
        out = self.gamma * w
        with torch.no_grad():
            diags["reliability"] = {
                "r_mean": float(r.mean()), "r_p90": float(r.flatten().quantile(0.9)),
                "r_support_mean": float(r_sup.mean()),
                "r_xscale_mean": float(r_xs.mean()),
            }
            diags["correction"] = {
                "out_abs_mean": float(out.abs().mean()),
                "out_mean": float(out.mean()),             # ≈0（局部零均值）
                "w_abs_mean": float(w.abs().mean()),
            }
        self._diag = diags
        return out

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def diag(self):
        return dict(self._diag)

    def param_report(self):
        n = sum(p.numel() for p in self.parameters())
        return {"form": self.form_tag, "sigmas": list(self.sigmas), "geom": self.geom,
                "detail_only": self.detail_only, "local_k": self.local_k,
                "pool_mode": self.pool_mode, "in_ch_ev": self.in_ch_ev,
                "mid": self.mid, "out_dim": self.out_dim, "params": int(n),
                "gamma_init_zero": bool(float(self.gamma.detach()) == 0.0),
                "gamma": float(self.gamma.detach())}

================================================================================
# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/model\layers\stgeo_sup.py
================================================================================
"""STGEO-SUP（Exp1 的 B 形态）：在**网络特征**上计算 SPD(2)/H² 测地变化分数，用 GT 掩码监督。

## 为什么必须作用在特征上（而不是原始图像）

v1/v2 的几何证据均从**原始图像**计算。那种设计下，监督信号的梯度只能流进
「几何→小投影头」这条 7k 参数的通路，**永远到不了主干** ⇒ 监督形态退化为
"训一个小辅助头然后删掉"，对推理**零影响**（这正是本项目反复强调的"注入/旁路"陷阱）。

因此本模块把**同一套几何**搬到网络特征上：
  F_t = proj(stage0 特征)            # (B,C_small,H/4,W/4)，A/B **共享** proj
  J_t = Σ_c [ G*(∂x F²), G*(∂x F ∂y F); ·, G*(∂y F²) ]     # 2×2 结构张量（特征域）
  z_t = ((Jxx−Jyy) + 2i·Jxy)/(Jxx+Jyy)                      # 双角度坐标（H² 的盘坐标）
  s   = √2·arccosh(1 + 2W)   ← 与 st_geodesic.py 完全同一个闭式距离
  logits = Conv1x1(s)  → 双线性上采样到 256²
  L_aux  = λ · (BCE + Dice)(sigmoid(logits), GT)
⇒ 梯度经 s 回到 z 回到 J 回到 ∂F 回到 **proj** 再回到 **stage0 特征**，
   即「用几何监督重塑表征」——与 Run7 E9（HFAE aux_only）**同一形态**，
   但被监督的量是**二阶结构统计在双曲平面上的测地距离**（参数自由、尺度不变）。

## 与 Run8 HQKV/HGSL 的区别

| | Run8 HGSL | 本模块 |
|---|---|---|
| 流形上的点 | 逐像素特征向量（一阶）经 Lorentz 映射 | **局部结构张量**（二阶）∈ SPD(2) |
| 距离 | 需人造非恒定半径才不退化 | 闭式 H² 测地距离，**无半径参数** |
| 尺度不变 | 无 | **精确**（J→c²J ⇒ z 不变） |
| 推理期 | 保留（HQKV 是真实算子） | **整支删除**（aux_only）⇒ deploy 与 CTRL 逐位同参数 |

## 部署纪律

`form="sup"` 时：训练期有该支路，`switch_to_deploy()` 里**整支置 None**
（与 Run7 HFAE `aux_only` 同口径）⇒ 部署图与 CTRL_R8 **完全相同**（非主干 2,038,868）。
"""
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

from model.layers.st_geodesic import (
    TR_FLOOR,
    _central_diff,
    _gaussian_blur,
    poincare_shape_distance,
    shape_disk_coords,
)
from model.utils import BCEDiceLoss

STGEO_SUP_FORM = "stgeo_sup_feat_v1"
STGEO_SUP_LAMBDA = 0.2      # 预注册固定，禁止搜索（与 Run7 HFAE / Run8 HGSL 同值）
STGEO_SUP_SEED = 29


def feature_structure_tensor(feat, sigma_i=1.0):
    """特征域结构张量（通道求和）：feat (B,C,H,W) → (Jxx,Jxy,Jyy)，各 (B,1,H,W)。"""
    gx = _central_diff(feat, 3)
    gy = _central_diff(feat, 4)
    jxx = _gaussian_blur((gx * gx).sum(dim=1, keepdim=True), sigma_i)
    jxy = _gaussian_blur((gx * gy).sum(dim=1, keepdim=True), sigma_i)
    jyy = _gaussian_blur((gy * gy).sum(dim=1, keepdim=True), sigma_i)
    return jxx, jxy, jyy


def feature_geodesic_score(feat_a, feat_b, tr_floor=TR_FLOOR):
    """双时相特征 → H² 测地变化分数 s ∈ [0, ∞)，(B,1,H,W)。参数自由。"""
    ja = feature_structure_tensor(feat_a)
    jb = feature_structure_tensor(feat_b)
    za_x, za_y, ka, va = shape_disk_coords(*ja, tr_floor=tr_floor)
    zb_x, zb_y, kb, vb = shape_disk_coords(*jb, tr_floor=tr_floor)
    s = poincare_shape_distance(za_x, za_y, ka, zb_x, zb_y, kb)
    return s * (va * vb)          # 任一时相平坦 ⇒ 分数 0（形状无定义）


class STGeoFeatureSup(nn.Module):
    """特征域 SPD(2)/H² 测地变化分数 + 1×1 变化头（**训练期专用**）。

    参数：proj = C_in×C_small（默认 96×16=1,536）+ head 1×1（2）≈ **1,538**，deploy 全部删除。
    """

    def __init__(self, in_ch=96, small=16, lambda_=STGEO_SUP_LAMBDA, init_seed=STGEO_SUP_SEED):
        super().__init__()
        self.in_ch = int(in_ch)
        self.small = int(small)
        self.lambda_ = float(lambda_)
        self.form_tag = STGEO_SUP_FORM
        with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=[]):     # 不推进全局 RNG
            torch.manual_seed(int(init_seed))
            self.proj = nn.Conv2d(self.in_ch, self.small, 1, bias=False)
            self.head = nn.Conv2d(1, 1, 1, bias=True)
            self.apply(self._init_weights)
        self._last_score = None

    @staticmethod
    def _init_weights(m):
        if isinstance(m, nn.Conv2d):
            nn.init.trunc_normal_(m.weight, std=0.02)
            if m.bias is not None:
                nn.init.constant_(m.bias, 0)

    def forward(self, feat_a, feat_b, out_size):
        """feat_a/feat_b: (B,C,H,W) 同一层的双时相特征；out_size: (H_out,W_out)。"""
        pa = self.proj(feat_a)
        pb = self.proj(feat_b)          # A/B **共享** proj（严格交换对称）
        s = feature_geodesic_score(pa, pb)
        self._last_score = s.detach()
        logits = self.head(s)
        if tuple(logits.shape[-2:]) != tuple(out_size):
            logits = F.interpolate(logits, size=out_size, mode="bilinear",
                                   align_corners=False)
        return logits

    def loss(self, logits, gt):
        """λ · (BCE + Dice)(sigmoid(logits), GT)。GT 用 max 池化保底到 logits 分辨率。"""
        if logits is None:
            return None
        tgt = gt.float()
        if tgt.dim() == 3:
            tgt = tgt.unsqueeze(1)
        if tuple(tgt.shape[-2:]) != tuple(logits.shape[-2:]):
            tgt = F.adaptive_max_pool2d(tgt, logits.shape[-2])
        return float(self.lambda_) * BCEDiceLoss(torch.sigmoid(logits), tgt)

    def score_stats(self):
        s = self._last_score
        if s is None:
            return {}
        return {"score_mean": float(s.mean()), "score_p99": float(s.flatten().quantile(0.99))}

    def param_report(self):
        return {"form": self.form_tag, "in_ch": self.in_ch, "small": self.small,
                "lambda": self.lambda_,
                "params": int(sum(p.numel() for p in self.parameters())),
                "deploy_dropped": True}

# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/model\layers\swiglu_ffn.py
================================================================================
# Copyright (c) Meta Platforms, Inc. and affiliates.
#
# This source code is licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0
# found in the LICENSE file in the root directory of this source tree.

import os
from typing import Callable, Optional
import warnings

from torch import Tensor, nn
import torch.nn.functional as F


class SwiGLUFFN(nn.Module):
    def __init__(
        self,
        in_features: int,
        hidden_features: Optional[int] = None,
        out_features: Optional[int] = None,
        act_layer: Callable[..., nn.Module] = None,
        drop: float = 0.0,
        bias: bool = True,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        out_features = out_features or in_features
        hidden_features = hidden_features or in_features
        self.w12 = nn.Linear(in_features, 2 * hidden_features, bias=bias)
        self.w3 = nn.Linear(hidden_features, out_features, bias=bias)

    def forward(self, x: Tensor) -> Tensor:
        x12 = self.w12(x)
        x1, x2 = x12.chunk(2, dim=-1)
        hidden = F.silu(x1) * x2
        return self.w3(hidden)


XFORMERS_ENABLED = os.environ.get("XFORMERS_DISABLED") is None
try:
    if XFORMERS_ENABLED:
        from xformers.ops import SwiGLU

        XFORMERS_AVAILABLE = True
        warnings.warn("xFormers is available (SwiGLU)")
    else:
        warnings.warn("xFormers is disabled (SwiGLU)")
        raise ImportError
except ImportError:
    SwiGLU = SwiGLUFFN
    XFORMERS_AVAILABLE = False

    warnings.warn("xFormers is not available (SwiGLU)")


class SwiGLUFFNFused(SwiGLU):
    def __init__(
        self,
        in_features: int,
        hidden_features: Optional[int] = None,
        out_features: Optional[int] = None,
        act_layer: Callable[..., nn.Module] = None,
        drop: float = 0.0,
        bias: bool = True,
    ) -> None:
        out_features = out_features or in_features
        hidden_features = hidden_features or in_features
        hidden_features = (int(hidden_features * 2 / 3) + 7) // 8 * 8
        super().__init__(
            in_features=in_features,
            hidden_features=hidden_features,
            out_features=out_features,
            bias=bias,
        )

# FILE: outputs/CASA-MoE/Run2/runtime_source/models_v1/model\layers\vmamba_kernel.py
================================================================================
"""Run8 selective-scan 后端适配层（VMamba v2 四向扫描用）。

## 为什么需要这一层（2026-10-15 服务器实测，见交接/探针记录）

RSML-3 上同时存在**两套** selective-scan CUDA 扩展，只有一套能用：

| 环境 | `selective_scan_cuda` | 状态 |
|---|---|---|
| `casacd`（本项目） | `site-packages/selective_scan_cuda...so`（10-03 编译） | **ABI 坏**：`undefined symbol: _ZN3c104cuda29c10_cuda_check_implementationE...` |
| `mamba_base`（SSM 模板） | `site-packages/selective_scan_cuda...so`（09-19 编译） | **可用**：fwd+bwd 实测 finite（B8/D64/L4096，0.037 ms/call） |

两边的 torch 都是 **2.14.0+cu132 / py3.10 / sm_120**，所以 `mamba_base` 的扩展在
`casacd` 进程里**可以直接用**——阻碍只是 Python 的导入顺序（`casacd` 的陈旧 `.so`
先被找到）。因此本模块的做法是：

1. 把 `mamba_base` 的 `site-packages` 插到 `sys.path` 最前（可用 `CASA_MAMBA_SITE`
   环境变量覆盖），再 `import selective_scan_cuda`；
2. 顺手把 `mamba_ssm`（提供官方 `selective_scan_fn`）也走同一条路导入；
3. 都失败时**显式抛错**，绝不静默退回一个语义不同的实现。

**不做的事**：不复制、不覆盖、不删除任何 `.so`；不修改 `casacd` 里的任何既有文件。
"""
import os
import sys

# mamba_base site-packages（可用环境变量覆盖，便于将来环境搬迁）
_MAMBA_SITE = os.environ.get(
    "CASA_MAMBA_SITE", "/home/yqwang/miniforge3/envs/mamba_base/lib/python3.10/site-packages"
)

_SITE_INSERTED = False


def ensure_mamba_site_path():
    """把 mamba_base 的 site-packages 提到 sys.path 最前（幂等）。返回是否成功。"""
    global _SITE_INSERTED
    if not os.path.isdir(_MAMBA_SITE):
        return False
    if not _SITE_INSERTED:
        while _MAMBA_SITE in sys.path:
            sys.path.remove(_MAMBA_SITE)
        sys.path.insert(0, _MAMBA_SITE)
        _SITE_INSERTED = True
    return True


_STATUS = {}


def load_selective_scan_cuda():
    """返回可用的 `selective_scan_cuda` 模块（提供 `fwd`/`bwd`）。失败抛 RuntimeError。"""
    if "ssc" in _STATUS:
        mod = _STATUS["ssc"]
        if mod is None:
            raise RuntimeError(_STATUS["err"])
        return mod
    ensure_mamba_site_path()
    try:
        import selective_scan_cuda as _ssc  # type: ignore
    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
        _STATUS["ssc"] = None
        _STATUS["err"] = (
            f"[SCAN-BACKEND] 无法导入可用的 selective_scan_cuda：{type(e).__name__}: {e}\n"
            f"  已尝试把 {_MAMBA_SITE} 插到 sys.path 最前。\n"
            f"  排查：`ls {_MAMBA_SITE}/selective_scan_cuda*.so`；"
            f"`python -c \"import sys;sys.path.insert(0,'{_MAMBA_SITE}');import selective_scan_cuda\"`"
        )
        raise RuntimeError(_STATUS["err"]) from e
    _STATUS["ssc"] = _ssc
    return _ssc


def load_mamba_selective_scan_fn():
    """返回 mamba_ssm 官方的 `selective_scan_fn`（非 CUDA 扩展的 python 包装层）。"""
    if "fn" in _STATUS:
        fn = _STATUS["fn"]
        if fn is None:
            raise RuntimeError(_STATUS["fn_err"])
        return fn
    ensure_mamba_site_path()
    try:
        from mamba_ssm.ops.selective_scan_interface import selective_scan_fn  # type: ignore
    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
        _STATUS["fn"] = None
        _STATUS["fn_err"] = f"[SCAN-BACKEND] mamba_ssm.selective_scan_fn 不可用：{type(e).__name__}: {e}"
        raise RuntimeError(_STATUS["fn_err"]) from e
    _STATUS["fn"] = selective_scan_fn
    return selective_scan_fn


def load_selective_scan_oflex():
    """返回 `selective_scan_cuda_oflex`（TinyViM/Run1–Run7 生产同款，`casacd` 自带）。

    这是**本项目已验证**的路径（Run1–Run7 全部训练都走它，四向扫描 max_abs<2e-5 对拍过
    torch 参考实现），因此 VMamba 也优先用它，而不是引入未在本项目验证过的第二条内核。
    """
    if "oflex" in _STATUS:
        mod = _STATUS["oflex"]
        if mod is None:
            raise RuntimeError(_STATUS["oflex_err"])
        return mod
    ensure_mamba_site_path()
    try:
        import selective_scan_cuda_oflex as _oflex  # type: ignore
    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
        _STATUS["oflex"] = None
        _STATUS["oflex_err"] = f"[SCAN-BACKEND] selective_scan_cuda_oflex 不可用：{type(e).__name__}: {e}"
        raise RuntimeError(_STATUS["oflex_err"]) from e
    _STATUS["oflex"] = _oflex
    return _oflex


def backend_report():
    """只读诊断：一条字符串，供日志/预注册记录。"""
    out = [f"mamba_site={_MAMBA_SITE}", f"exists={os.path.isdir(_MAMBA_SITE)}"]
    try:
        oflex = load_selective_scan_oflex()
        out.append(f"selective_scan_cuda_oflex=OK({getattr(oflex, '__file__', '?')})")
    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
        out.append(f"selective_scan_cuda_oflex=FAIL({type(e).__name__})")
    try:
        ssc = load_selective_scan_cuda()
        out.append(f"selective_scan_cuda=OK({getattr(ssc, '__file__', '?')})")
        out.append(f"symbols={[a for a in dir(ssc) if not a.startswith('_')]}")
    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
        out.append(f"selective_scan_cuda=FAIL({type(e).__name__})")
    try:
        load_mamba_selective_scan_fn()
        out.append("mamba_ssm.selective_scan_fn=OK")
    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
        out.append(f"mamba_ssm.selective_scan_fn=FAIL({type(e).__name__})")
    return " | ".join(out)
